# 251: Chapter 248: Calyrex's Shinre Displays Divine Power

Calyrex sat atop Glastrier, gripping the reins tightly, as Glastrier seemed to resonate, raising its front hooves high and letting out a neigh.

A glow enveloped their bodies, and within that intense light, a green halo emitted from the massive crown on Calyrex's head.

The King's Cloak behind Calyrex gradually lengthened, turning into a pure white reminiscent of snow and ice, rustling majestically.

After a moment, everything returned to calm.

Calyrex held the reins, riding on Glastrier, gazing down at D with a look that's both authoritative and compassionate.

A vigorous energy radiated from Calyrex, indicating that this former King of Galar had regained its power.

"D, would you be willing to accompany me to the Crown Shrine?" Calyrex asked in a deep voice.

Hearing these words, D was momentarily stunned, but quickly understood Calyrex's meaning.

Having regained its strength, Calyrex now intended to head to the Crown Shrine to tame Spectrier once more!

Without hesitation, he nodded: "Of course!"

Calyrex slightly tugged at the corners of its mouth, revealing a smile: "Then follow me!"

With that said, it turned Glastrier around.

"Ice! Neigh!"

Glastrier raised its front hooves high, letting out an excited neigh, and trotted briskly across the snow-covered expanse toward the distance.

"So majestic!" D thought.

He quickly mounted Koraidon, "Let's follow!"

"Agaah!" Koraidon understood, and the moment D was seated firmly, it leaped high, pursuing the path Calyrex had taken across the snowy plains.

The sky began to sprinkle with tiny snowflakes once more.

Koraidon caught up with Glastrier, allowing D to ride alongside Calyrex.

Calyrex turned its head slightly, exchanging a nod with D, and the Pokemon beneath them picked up speed.

Twenty minutes later, the pair arrived at the northern mountains of the Crown Tundra.

The Crown Shrine stood at the mountain's peak, towering amidst the white wasteland of ice and snow.

This was an ancient temple long abandoned, surrounded by ruins and encircled by crumbling outer walls.

Tall, ancient, majestic…

Just looking at it, one could imagine how grand and sacred this old temple must have been.

But now, it had become a desolate ruin deprived of any living souls.

Glastrier exhaled two white breaths as its galloping hooves gradually slowed, finally halting before the shrine.

Calyrex, perched on its back, looked up at its Crown Shrine, softly speaking: "This was where I used to live."

Its gaze was both sorrowful and reminiscent: "I spent much time here with my beloved steed, and in those days, humans often came here to offer me tributes in gratitude."

D nodded silently.

He could roughly grasp Calyrex's previous thoughts.

Awakened from its slumber, Calyrex lost its power, and with both of its beloved steeds gone, it believed that humans had forgotten it, causing it to feel sorrow and despair.

Fearing to recall those beautiful memories, Calyrex had been deliberately avoiding the Crown Shrine, wandering the Crown Tundra instead.

"Is it an escape?" D thought.

At this moment, Calyrex retracted its gaze and turned back: "Like Glastrier, Spectrier was once defeated by me; it will not approach anything that poses a threat to it."

"Therefore, we should hide, and when Spectrier returns, I will step forward to subdue it."

The scale of the Crown Shrine was immense, making it easy to find a place to hide.

D nodded; there was nothing more for him to do, just to stay and watch Calyrex perform.

The two hid with Koraidon and Glastrier in a corner, awaiting Spectrier's return.

Time ticked by.

No one knows how long had passed before D felt restless and wanted to stretch when a Pokemon entirely black, with blue mane, pale blue tail, and limbs wrapped in a mist-like soul, walked through the shrine entrance.

D perked up instantly.

Spectrier had arrived!

The next moment, Calyrex beside him moved.

Calyrex, riding the white horse, flashed to appear in front of Spectrier.

Upon seeing Calyrex, Spectrier seemed startled, and in the next second, tried to turn and flee.

But Calyrex had long been prepared to respond.

It charged towards Spectrier on Glastrier, the earth instantly freezing under Glastrier's power, only to be shattered by its hooves again.

Calyrex raised its right hand high, blue light flashing in its eyes, using its spiritual power to condense Glastrier's cold aura.

Instantly, tiny ice shards emerged in the air, swiftly gathering together to eventually form a completely snowy white icy spear.

Calyrex held the ice spear, then used its psychic power to fling it at the Spectrier ahead.

"Whoosh!"

Spectrier was instantly hit, releasing a pained cry, its forelimbs buckling and it collapsed to the ground.

Calyrex reined Glastrier in, its hooves clapping loudly against the stone below, eventually halting in front of Spectrier.

Calyrex looked down at Spectrier, then flashed down from Glastrier, extending a hand to place a palm on its forehead.

Pulses of pure energy flowed out, gradually healing Spectrier's injuries.

The once fierce gaze Spectrier had towards Calyrex now softened significantly.

Once fully recovered, Spectrier stood, bowing its head in submission to Calyrex once more, standing alongside Glastrier to the left and right of Calyrex.

D stepped out with Koraidon, raising his eyebrows, "Is it done?"

Calyrex shook its head gently, "Not yet, there's still a rein missing."

"Hm?"

D paused, quickly realizing.

The Reins of Unity made from Glastrier's mane and the Radiant Flower can only merge Glastrier and Calyrex.

But for Spectrier, it needs a rein made from its own mane!

"Seems I have another favor to ask of you," Calyrex appeared somewhat embarrassed.

So far, the little disciple has helped it so much, yet it hasn't done anything in return.

This gratitude brings some shame to Calyrex as it recalls what the little disciple once told it, starting to think earnestly.

But before it could decide, the little disciple in front said, "Leave it to me, the Radiant Flower is used up, apart from the flower, we'll need Spectrier's mane."

Having done this once before, D believed he had mastered the technique of crafting the Reins of Unity.

Upon hearing this, Calyrex was slightly taken back, then nodded gently, handing over the materials, with a gentle and loving expression:

"This is the last task I entrust to you, when it's completed, I'll give you an answer to that question you asked the other day."

---

---

---

---

# 252: Chapter 249: D Stays Overnight in the Abundant Hall

Crown Shrine, noon.

Calyrex stood at the center of the shrine hall, with Glastrier and Spectrier by its side, occasionally breathing out white wisps.

They looked toward the entrance of the shrine, as if waiting for something.

No one spoke, and the surroundings were silent and tranquil.

At some point, Glastrier seemed a bit bored and began pawing at the ground with its hooves.

Suddenly, the annoying sound of clattering hooves echoed throughout the empty, silent shrine.

Beside it, Spectrier exhaled heavily, turning its head angrily to glare at Glastrier, apparently displeased by the noise.

"Hiss-huff!"

Glastrier was absorbed in the little pit it had dug and didn't hear Spectrier's voice.

Spectrier's expression grew sullen, with a glowing purple mist emanating from its body.

"Crown!" Calyrex noticed the little antics of its beloved horses and immediately spoke to stop them.

With this, Glastrier and Spectrier quickly calmed down, loyally staying by the king's side.

At that moment, the sound of running footsteps came from outside.

Accompanied by a lively voice saying "I'm back!", the three Pokemon saw D and Koraidon running in, carrying bundles.

With their arrival, the cold and silent shrine immediately became lively.

Calyrex seemed puzzled: "Why bring so many things?"

D and Koraidon placed the bundles on the floor, rubbed their hands, and replied:

"It's not that much, mainly carrot seeds for Spectrier and Glastrier, and some food and daily necessities I got from the village."

Saying this, D opened the parcels and took everything out.

On this trip, he nearly emptied the Freezington Village store of carrot seeds, and from the store, he also bought plenty of fresh ingredients like potatoes, fish, and steaks.

Calyrex floated forward, inspecting the items placed in the shrine, and began recounting past events.

"In the past, humans used to offer these things as tributes to I and my beloved horses, but I do not need them."

"Later, by observing the horses' preferences, they brought specially grown carrots for me and my companions."

D asked: "Is there anything you particularly like to eat?"

Calyrex reminisced carefully: "I differ from other Pokemon and don't need to eat to maintain this form, though if I encounter something I am interested in, I might taste it."

"Oh?" D raised his brow curiously: "What is it?"

"An orange-red food, similar in shape to the carrots the horses like." Calyrex said, "I have tried it once; it was brought by my followers from a distant city, as the tundra soil cannot grow such carrots."

Orange-red carrots...

A vegetable image floated in D's mind.

Isn't that a carrot?

However, there's a curious thing—the Crown Tundra soil can only grow Iceroot and Shaderoot carrots?

Is it due to the soil or environmental factors?

Thoughts flashed across D's mind as he pondered and said to Calyrex:

"That kind of carrot only grows outside the tundra; it's also abundant in the Paldea Region."

"I see." Calyrex said: "It seems the tundra's soil is different."

Calyrex looked quite puzzled and turned to its beloved horses, starting to ponder.

Meanwhile, D began handling ingredients, preparing today's lunch.

He peeled and chopped the potatoes, then placed them in a pot along with the sliced meat to simmer.

There aren't many ingredients on the tundra, not as abundant as those on farms.

Plus, with the cold climate and harsh environment here, a warm and hearty pot of potato stew would be perfect.

The potatoes and meat are cooked tender and flavorful, the broth rich; the air filled with the aroma makes one's mouth water.

D couldn't help swallowing, then lit a bonfire, hung the pot, and started simmering.

While waiting for lunch, he took out the needle and thread, Spectrier's mane, and the Radiant Flower he bought from the village, beginning to sew Calyrex's second set of Unity Reins.

Koraidon and Cyclizar sprawled beside D, opposite Calyrex and its beloved horses.

As D sewed the Reins of Unity, they quietly watched, observing the blue-purple mane transform into reins in D's hands.

The warm bonfire reflected in Calyrex's eyes, giving it an elegant look instead of its initial regal demeanor.

Soon, the reins were mostly finished.

"Next, singing with gratitude is essential." D thought.

Just as he raised his head, he saw Calyrex gracefully watching him.

This made D feel a bit embarrassed.

"Uh…there's the crucial next step, and I mustn't be disturbed during this process; I need to do it alone." D started spouting nonsense seriously.

Calyrex paused and then chuckled, nodding: "I understand; I shall take the horses for a walk."

With that, Calyrex mounted Glastrier, followed by Spectrier, and strolled away from the shrine.

Only then did D breathe a sigh of relief.

Once Calyrex's figure was out of sight, he proceeded with the final step—singing.

"The flowers round together~♬"

"Offer the mane to our King~♬"

After singing, D turned around to see Koraidon and Cyclizar staring at him.

D: "…Don't tell anyone, got it?"

Koraidon: "Ah-gah hiss..." (Got it...)

Cyclizar: "Gah-ao!" (Oh oh!)

Only then did D feel at ease.

When he looked again at the reins in his hands, they were already emitting a faint glow.

This marked the completion of the second set of Reins of Unity!

Five minutes later, Calyrex returned leisurely riding.

D handed the finished Reins of Unity to it.

Calyrex glanced down, showing grateful eyes.

It didn't speak but held the reins, riding atop Spectrier.

As a light flashed, a ghostly purple glow erupted from Calyrex and Spectrier.

When the Reins of Unity fully merged with Spectrier, Calyrex's cloak grew longer.

And Spectrier experienced a transformation; the mist on its limbs became denser, covering the shrine floor, making Spectrier appear to float on air, exuding a vivid purple glow.

After a while, the purple mist was drawn back into Spectrier's body.

Calyrex, atop its back, with the King's Cloak gently swaying, maintained a regal demeanor yet had grateful eyes looking at D.

"Thanks to you, I have now regained my full strength."

"I once thought I was no longer believed by humans and without my beloved horses, I wandered alone into the abyss of solitude. You pulled me from that abyss."

"So-called unending gratitude, unable to repay, that's this moment's sentiment towards you!"

"D, from now on, I ask you to guide me further."

---

# 253: Chapter 250: Not Becoming the King of Galar This Time

"D, from now on, please take care of me."

Hearing this, D was slightly taken aback, unable to react for a moment.

Calyrex looked at him and said, "This is my response. From this moment on, I am no longer the King of Galar."

This was a decision Calyrex had reached after much deliberation.

The current Galar Region had undergone earth-shattering changes, no longer the Galar they once knew.

And the former King of Galar had already become a thing of the past.

In Calyrex's mind, images of the place where the little believers were appeared, the humans there, the Pokemon there, the warm sunlight there.

This world is vast, with so many things that it had never experienced or understood.

Thinking of this, Calyrex's dignified gaze gradually became determined.

"Really?"

D finally realized what Calyrex was saying and was delighted, quickly asking.

Calyrex nodded slightly, "I never deceive others. D, take me to see the place where you live!"

D solemnly agreed, "Alright."

At this moment, the stew in the pot was ready.

With the bubbling sound, an aroma mixed with the fragrance of potatoes and meat spread through the air.

Smelling this aroma, Koraidon and Cyclizar's eyes lit up, looking expectantly at the pot nearby.

Even Calyrex was drawn to it, saying, "I have never smelled such an aroma."

D smiled and asked, "Would you like to try it?"

He thought Calyrex would agree, but to his surprise, Calyrex gently shook its head, "Forget it, I don't like... No, it's not that I don't like it, it's just that meat is not part of my and my favorite horse's diet."

D immediately understood.

So Calyrex and the two horses are vegetarian Pokemon!

He thought for a moment, then took out some bread made from flour from his backpack and handed it to Calyrex, "Want to try this?"

Calyrex reached for it and took a bite.

"How is it?" D asked eagerly.

Calyrex nodded slightly, "Though it doesn't taste as good as carrots, it's quite a nice food, I like it very much."

"That's good." As he spoke, D fed two pieces of bread to Spectrier and Glastrier before lifting the lid to check the hot stew.

[Potato Stew (B+): A high-level dish made by boiling diced potatoes and meat together in a large pot. The broth perfectly blends with the tender potatoes, making it rich and after eating a serving, it keeps the body warm all day long.

Cooking Effect: Resistance to cold, recovery level 2. After eating, the body will become warm, and stamina will greatly recover, lasting for 6 hours.

Review: A delicious meal that fills the stomach and warms the body, very suitable for humans and Pokemon who are frail and afraid of the cold. ]

Looking at this information, D was a bit surprised.

Resistance to cold recovery? Dual effects?

It can both keep you warm and restore stamina?

It seems like the hot noodle soup can retire now, D thought.

Calculating the time, the effect of the hot noodle soup they had previously eaten was almost gone, just in time to use this pot of potato stew to replenish.

D divided the potato stew for Cyclizar and Koraidon.

The two Pokemon immediately buried their faces in the bowls and ate heartily.

While eating, Calyrex suddenly asked, "When do you plan to return to the Paldea Region?"

D thought carefully and replied, "In a few days! I still have some things to do here, and I'll return once I'm done."

After finishing the work here in Crown Tundra, he still needs to go to other big cities to acquire some steel feathers from Corviknight to bring back to the ranch for Tinkatink to forge hammers.

Besides, there are a few other things in Crown Tundra to consider.

That is the white carrots and black carrots that Spectrier and Glastrier love most.

They certainly need to go with Calyrex to the ranch, but black and white carrots cannot grow outside of Crown Tundra, making them a unique specialty here.

After returning to the ranch, this means the two horses can no longer eat their favorite carrots.

D thought it would be great if he could find out why black and white carrots only grow here.

Then he could create a dedicated land at the ranch to grow white and black carrots for Glastrier and Spectrier.

Thinking of this, D shared his thoughts with Calyrex in front of him.

Upon hearing this, Calyrex also fell into contemplation, "Indeed, if we can grow vegetables they like there, they will certainly be very happy."

D nodded, "So, after dinner, I plan to go to Snowy Valley and Ancient Tomb to investigate."

To see what makes the soil and environment of these two places unique.

Calyrex: "I will accompany you."

*

After lunch, D packed his things, put on his backpack again, and climbed onto Koraidon's back.

Calyrex also rode on Spectrier, followed by Glastrier, walking alongside Koraidon out of the Crown Shrine.

When they got outside, Calyrex stopped and looked back at the ancient shrine perched atop the vast snowy mountain.

It would be impossible to say that there was no emotion inside.

After all, it had lived there for such a long time, the weathered walls of the shrine witnessing their rise and fall.

From being revered as a king by humans to being forgotten by the world.

Snow began to fall in the sky again at some point.

Calyrex stood in the snow watching for a long, long time.

Until a deep sigh echoed between heaven and earth, Calyrex withdrew its gaze and said to D, "I... Never mind, I am no longer a king. Since that's the case, let's go!"

D nodded, not saying a word, knowing that Calyrex must have made this decision with great determination.

On the way, Calyrex suddenly asked, "D, what does it feel like to be a farmer?"

D was slightly stunned, thought carefully, and then answered:

"Perhaps it's leisurely? Actually, everyone's perspective is different. Some people enjoy a bustling and lively life, they travel around the world, while others prefer a peaceful life, staying in one place, living an undisturbed, leisurely life."

Calyrex nodded lightly, "In that case, let me also start learning how to be a farmer! D, please teach me from now on!"

D: ?

Are you serious?

Perhaps seeing his confused expression was too obvious, Calyrex took the initiative to explain:

"The world is no longer the world I knew, the king has become history, and now, I no longer wish to be a king."

"I was wondering what I could do after not being a king, and seeing you, I thought of your ranch, I am very interested in it, perhaps I can start trying to be a farmer."

D: "..."

This is amazing, if Calyrex's former believers found out he's letting Calyrex do farming, they might just explode with anger!

But thinking about it, with Calyrex's abilities, it's indeed quite suitable for being a farmer.

"I'm looking forward to it," D said.

The corners of Calyrex's mouth lifted slightly into a smile, the same expectant smile on its face.

They rode Koraidon and Glastrier along the snow-covered mountain path, snowflakes falling gently onto their shoulders.

D and Calyrex walked side by side in the snow, heading down the mountain toward a new life.

---

---

---

---

# 254: Chapter 251: Special Farmland Cultivation Plan

Snowy Valley.

D and Calyrex's first stop on their journey was here.

The icy stream tinkled, meandering through the snow-white land, where the valley was serene and the scenery tranquil,

D and Calyrex stood together beside an empty field.

D crouched down, using a shovel to dig out a handful of soil, carefully checking the differences between this soil and that of his ranch.

"It feels very hard, it must be due to the climate here, frozen solid, unlike the loose soil on the ranch."

D sniffed it and then tasted a bit:

"Hmm, it just tastes like regular soil, but with a very strong scent of Ice Type energy."

Feeling the icy breath escaping from his mouth and nasal cavity, D spat out the soil and rinsed his mouth with mineral water, pondering:

"There's more Ice Type energy on this land than I imagined, is it because of the Ice Type energy?"

The abundant Ice Type energy caused the normal carrot seeds to mutate, the energy fused into the carrots, transforming them into snow-white radishes.

At this moment, Calyrex asked, "Have you discovered anything?"

D nodded and shared his hypothesis with Calyrex: "…I'm wondering if, in other places, injecting a large amount of Ice Type energy into the soil would cause the carrots to mutate and grow into white radishes."

Calyrex didn't quite understand these things, but still supported: "When I ride Glastrier, I can use Psychic Power to condense the ice breath on its body, then we can attempt to inject the ice breath into the ground together."

"Hmm." D nodded.

Glastrier stood beside them, entirely indifferent to their conversation, attentively focused on the land in front of it.

Seeing that D was slow to plant white radishes for it, Glastrier impatiently scraped the ground with its hooves, snorting two streams of white breath from its nostrils.

Seeing this, Calyrex seemed a little helpless: "My beloved horse has been this way for a long time."

D chuckled, then took out several packets of carrot seeds from his backpack, and together with Koraidon, they planted these seeds into the ground.

After the last seed was planted, Calyrex floated to the edge of the field and activated its power.

Instantly, a blue glowing tide erupted throughout the farmland.

When the tide subsided, the field was already filled with a bounty of white radishes.

Glastrier snorted happily, trotting over to the farm's edge to start eating the icy radishes in the ground.

On the other side, Spectrier saw this scene, its heart stirred, and a look of envy appeared in its eyes.

D looked at these white radishes, thought slightly, then stepped forward and pulled a radish from the ground, examining it carefully in his hand.

The white radish was completely snow-white, just like the pure snow, feeling icy and cool to the touch.

D took a bite and was surprised to find that the radish's flesh was also icy, with a very peculiar texture, and indeed it carried the aura of Ice Type energy inside.

This discovery further confirmed his previous hypothesis.

But more experiments are needed.

After Glastrier finished eating all the white radishes in the field, D and Calyrex rode Spectrier and Koraidon, heading towards their next destination, the Ancient Tomb.

Not all places in the Crown Tundra are cold snowfields; in the southern part of the tundra lies a vast, open wilderness.

At three in the afternoon, they traversed the boundless snowfield, arriving at this place full of grass, trees, lakes, hills, and caves.

Standing atop a hill overlooking the distant south, D vaguely saw an immensely tall tree, at least fifteen meters high,

There was also another meadow in the south, with a huge light-red berry tree.

Even though he had anticipated it, D couldn't help but be astonished upon seeing that giant tree.

The awe inspired by a real giant tree is beyond what a game can convey.

Calyrex's gaze also turned in that direction.

"That giant tree has been there for a long time, the berries it bears have nurtured the Pokemon of this land, and in the past, powerful Pokemon were drawn here, resting on the trunk."

Hearing this, D's heart was moved.

Was Calyrex referring to the Galar form Legendary Birds?

He remembered that in the game, Moltres, Articuno, and Zapdos had visited this giant tree.

Aside from them, there were other Legendary Pokemon.

Like Registeel, Regirock, Regice, Regieleki, and Regidrago.

They continued forward and soon arrived at the front of an ancient stone ruin.

Looking at this ruin, Calyrex spoke softly, "Here lie the slumbering giant Pokemon from ancient times, they have been sleeping here for a long time, and the ancient humans referred to this place as the Giant's Bed."

The slumbering giant Pokemon...

Referring to Registeel?

D's heart was eager: "Can I open the ruins and take a look at them?"

Calyrex thought carefully for a moment, then said, "I would not advise you to do so, the five giant Pokemon have been sleeping here, guarding this land, abruptly disturbing them might provoke the giants' wrath, which could endanger the nearby Pokemon."

"You're right." D nodded, thus abandoning the idea of unlocking the five pillars.

"We've arrived, and ahead is the land where you can grow Shaderoot Carrots." Calyrex cast its gaze forward.

D looked up and saw an overgrown, desolate field between the graveyards.

All around the field were scattered and incomplete gravestones, shrouded in a gloomy, ominous atmosphere, giving off a haunted feeling.

Ancient Tomb... The people buried here were likely ancient humans, D thought.

He silently apologized in his heart for the intrusion, then proceeded with his Pokemon to observe.

Gazing at the land before, Calyrex asked, "Do you wish to taste?"

D shook his head without hesitation: "… better not, humans can't sense the aura of Ghost energy."

Calyrex nodded: "I see, then how do you wish to investigate?"

D took out the remaining carrot seeds from his backpack: "First, let's try if this land can grow Shaderoot Carrots."

"I understand."

Once D and Koraidon had planted all the carrot seeds, Calyrex floated over and activated its power.

In an instant, from the pitch-black soil emerged dark purple carrots, their color appearing very eerie.

D looked at Spectrier and said to it: "Eat!"

Spectrier glanced at him, then snorted softly, calmly stepping forward, it approached the field and lowered its neck to start eating the shaderoot carrots from the ground.

Its manner of eating was unlike Glastrier's roughness, calm and composed from start to finish, seeming to thoroughly enjoy the experience.

D glanced at Glastrier, then at Spectrier.

So here's the question, for next year's horse racing tournament, which horse should he ride?

The places Glastrier passes automatically become icy surfaces, it possesses incredible strength and formidable destructive power, yet it's also fiercely proud and very combative.

Spectrier, on the other hand, is calm by nature and doesn't interfere with other creatures, stepping lightly on a fantastical, elusive purple mist, looking very handsome and elegant.

D found himself caught in a dilemma, it's really hard to choose!

---

---

---

---

# 255: Chapter 252: Friendship, Tears, Love, and Bonds (4k)

Spectrier continued munching on the Shaderoot Carrot in front of the field.

D began to observe the surrounding environment.

The simplest way to verify the cause of the Crown Tundra carrots' mutation is through comparative analysis.

The Ancient Tomb is already outside the periphery of the tundra. Although the temperature here remains cold, some cold-resistant green plants have sprouted nearby.

After pondering for a moment, D walked to the land facing the Ancient Tomb and then turned to ask:

"By the way, if you constantly use that power to accelerate crop Growth, will it affect your body?"

Calyrex gently shook his head: "No, thanks to your Helping Hand and the aid of the beloved horse, my powers have now returned to their peak state. This minor consumption can be quickly replenished."

"That's good." D nodded, then explained his thoughts to Calyrex: "I intend to plant carrot seeds here, away from the tomb, to compare it with that piece of land to determine the primary factors causing the carrot's mutation into Shaderoot Carrot."

Calyrex pondered briefly and nodded: "A fine idea, then let us begin the experiment!"

Hearing this, D let Koraidon dig a pit in the ground with his claws, then placed a carrot seed into it.

Once he was done, Calyrex floated forward and employed his power to accelerate the seed's maturation.

Accompanied by a flash of deep blue light, the seed buried in the soil swiftly underwent changes.

Once everything calmed down, there appeared a completely snow-white White Carrot on that piece of land.

D walked up to inspect it, "It's the White Carrot favored by Glastrier, although it seems somewhat different from the ones grown earlier."

Perhaps because the Ice Type Energy here isn't as abundant as on the tundra, this White Carrot isn't as pure.

"Is it due to the energy?" Calyrex mused alongside him.

D shook his head: "It's not entirely certain yet, but there is another validation method."

Saying this, he cast his gaze toward Calyrex and the Spectrier who had silently trotted over after finishing the Shaderoot Carrot.

Under Calyrex's questioning gaze, D asked, "I recall you can control the aura on Spectrier and Glastrier using Psychic Power, correct?"

Calyrex nodded: "Indeed, my abilities gradually merged with them over the years spent with my companion horses."

"When I ride Glastrier, I can use Psychic Power to concentrate its generated cold aura. The enormous ice lance you saw earlier was created by me; I call it 'Glacial Lance.'"

"And when I ride Spectrier, my Psychic Power can blend into its body, enhancing its strength. In an instant, I can create countless wisps of ghostly flames, directing them to fall like meteors from the sky; I call this move 'Astral Barrage.'

D nodded thoughtfully.

Glacial Lance and Astral Barrage are exclusive Moves corresponding to Calyrex when riding Glastrier and Spectrier.

The Psychic Power mentioned by Calyrex presumably refers to his innate Psychic abilities.

"Next, I'd like to ask you to do me a favor," D said. "Ride Spectrier and use Psychic Power to condense the Ghost Type Energy from it and gather it onto this field."

Calyrex immediately understood his intent and nodded slightly: "I'll take care of that!"

With that, Calyrex's form flickered, using Teleport to get onto Spectrier's back.

One king and one horse merged again, transforming into the Calyrex riding a black horse stance.

The next moment, D saw a mist of ghostly purple wafting from Spectrier's body, drifting onto this piece of land and merging seamlessly with it.

"It's done," Calyrex said.

D nodded, pulled out the White Carrot, and planted the second carrot seed.

Calyrex swung his left hand and activated his power to ensure a bountiful crop once more.

The miraculous blue light flashed past.

In the next second, D saw a familiar Shaderoot Carrot at the spot.

Succeeded? Just as I thought... D pondered.

"This also means Ice Type Energy and Ghost Type Energy are the key for the White Carrots and Shaderoot Carrots to grow," D murmured.

With this knowledge, he could confidently create experimental fields on the ranch.

Utilizing the Ice Type Energy and Ghost Type Energy from Glastrier and Spectrier, he could cultivate special fields dedicated to growing Shaderoot Carrots and White Carrots for both horses.

Allowing both horses to enjoy their favorite vegetables in the Paldea Region.

It's perfect!

D immediately shared his discovery with Calyrex.

Upon hearing this, Calyrex's face also showed a smile: "In this case, I don't need to worry about our beloved horses not finding their favored vegetables on the other side."

Suddenly hearing Calyrex planning to take them away from this snowy field, both Glastrier and Spectrier widened their eyes in disbelief.

To them, this place held significant meaning.

In the ancient Crown Tundra, Glastrier and Spectrier once reigned here as the kings of Pokemon.

Later, humans invaded their domain, and the enraged Spectrier and Glastrier relentlessly attacked humans and their partner Pokemon.

---

Until later when Calyrex appeared, after being defeated by Him, they established a deep bond with Calyrex, becoming His beloved horses, and together they ruled this kingdom.

But now, Calyrex was about to take them away together...

Noticing the thoughts of His beloved horses, Calyrex said to them with a dignified tone, "This place no longer belongs to us, and those humans who once revered us as kings have long vanished into the past."

Spectrier and Glastrier blinked their eyes, still a bit reluctant.

Seeing this, D spoke up, "When we get there, I will plant more Shaderoot Carrots and White Carrots for you to eat."

Upon hearing this, the reluctance on Glastrier's face instantly vanished. It snorted excitedly a couple of times, scraping the ground with its hooves, and started prancing around.

Spectrier also gazed at him with spirited eyes.

D: "..."

Calyrex watched this scene with a smile in His eyes.

Beside Him, Koraidon also let out a joyful growl, "Aga-s!" (How nice~)

*

Unknowingly, the moon had come out.

Night fell, and the hazy moonlight bathed the ground, overhead was a beautiful starry sky, and beneath was the vast earth.

D rode the cool Glastrier, alongside this temperamental and restless horse, under the stars, headed south towards the Crown Tundra.

On his left was Koraidon running in its ride form, and on his right was Calyrex riding Spectrier.

On this trip, D planned to visit the Dyna Tree Hill to see that pink giant tree which had nurtured countless Pokemon.

As they got closer, the towering giant tree suddenly appeared before them.

"So big!" D looked up at the scene and said to Calyrex walking beside him, "How did it grow so large?!"

Calyrex rode gracefully and upon hearing D's question, He gently raised His head, gazed at the pink giant tree, and replied:

"It was nurtured by this land."

D was stunned for a moment, "The land?"

Calyrex slightly nodded, "The land loves each of its children dearly and hopes they can thrive, therefore nurturing this tree to, in turn, nurture the beings living on this land."

D: "..." Couldn't understand.

Does the land in the Pokemon World have a will too?

D thought for a moment and concluded that Calyrex was probably referring to the will of nature.

Just like how Viridian Forest in the Kanto Region grants the blessing of the forest to certain humans, allowing them to use the Power of Viridian.

Or like in the Pokemon movie "Pikachu and Coco", where Coco, in order to protect his father Zarude, used the Pokemon move Jungle Healing with his human body.

Jungle Healing, Zarude's exclusive move, where the user merges with the jungle to heal the stamina of themselves and their companions using the power of nature.

Initially, D couldn't comprehend, but now it seemed that Coco must have been recognized by the forest too.

The bond between him and Zarude's father-son relationship moved the will of the forest, gaining its blessing to borrow its power.

"It really is an idealistic world…" D couldn't help but mutter.

He remembered that Ash had died several times in the anime, the first time being in the movie "Mewtwo Strikes Back" when he was petrified by an attack from Mewtwo.

Then the gathered Pokemon were moved by Ash and shed tears, and with the aid of the invincible artifact "Tears of True Emotion", Ash was successfully revived.

As a modern person who had traveled to this world, D felt he couldn't quite grasp these phenomena.

This is no different from those plots where people shout "Love!", "Bonds!", "This is our friendship!" and then defeat some overwhelmingly powerful demon lords.

Too cringy… thought D.

At this moment, they arrived at the lakeside of Ballimere Lake which surrounded Dyna Tree Hill.

Spectrier and Glastrier stopped.

D and Calyrex dismounted, intending to camp here for the night.

It was already seven o'clock in the evening. Although it was dark, they could still see those wild Pokemon active nearby.

Swablu flying in the air following Altaria,

Corvisquire with sharp eyes and agile bodies, hiding on branches shrouded in shadows,

Dubwool grazing in herds in the grass,

Fluffy Cottonee floating lightly in mid-air on the breeze.

And in the lake, Barboach swimming, the vacant-eyed Magikarp and Feebas, Basculin recklessly charging around.

The variety of Pokemon here was richer than any other place D had been to before.

With one glance, one could feel the vibrant life force emitted by this group of Pokemon.

Undoubtedly, these were the Pokemon nurtured by the giant tree Calyrex mentioned.

D was astounded by this beautiful ecology, feeling a slight emotional stir in his heart as he watched the Pokemon frolicking and playing on the earth, his mind wandering to the World Tree in his own ranch.

He mumbled to Calyrex next to him, "There's also a tree in my ranch that can grow really tall; if it matures, could it also nurture many Pokemon like this?"

Calyrex looked at him, "A tree?"

"I call it the World Tree," D told Calyrex the name of that tree, "It is a very magnificent tree, but it takes a hundred thousand years to grow."

"World Tree…" Calyrex had never heard this name before, He pondered carefully for a moment before speaking, "If it's only about time, perhaps My power can help it grow."

D thought of the three Gogoats who tended to the World Tree.

Each time after nurturing the World Tree, they seemed utterly exhausted yet displayed a completely satisfied expression on their faces.

If things went by the normal process, by the time he died of old age, he would still not see the World Tree fully grown, but…

D looked at Calyrex and said, "That might consume a lot of your energy."

Calyrex smiled, "And so what? You pulled Me from the abyss, spread My name on that land, providing Me with enough faith to withstand the harsh cold of the tundra."

D was very moved, "Calyrex…"

No doubt, this is a Pokemon with a heart full of compassion!

Calyrex was also deeply moved, "D…"

His little believer was truly good to Him!

Calyrex's affection level continually went up.

At this moment, Koraidon's tummy rumbling broke the touching atmosphere.

D turned his head to see Koraidon looking innocently over with a tilted head.

"Aga?" (What's up?)

D: "..."

Forget it.

"I'll start cooking now!"

Tonight's dinner, D had prepared grilled steaks and vegetable sandwiches.

The grilled steaks were prepared for Koraidon and Cyclizar, while the vegetable sandwiches were reserved for Calyrex.

As for Glastrier and Spectrier…

They had already eaten over a hundred carrots this afternoon, and their bellies were still bulging, indicating they hadn't digested them yet.

The preparation method for the vegetable sandwich was very simple; most of the ingredients D used were vegetables purchased from Freezington Village, such as carrots, lettuce, and broccoli.

Chop these vegetables, then place them between slices of bread, finally squeeze on ketchup and salad dressing, and a vegetable sandwich is done.

D glanced down and found that this sandwich only enhanced the power of the next Grass Type Move a Pokemon used, so he didn't pay much attention to it.

After making dinner for several Pokemon, he began preparing curry rice for himself.

During this, D suddenly thought of what the old village elder of Freezington had told him before and he turned to Calyrex, asking:

"By the way, the old village elder of Freezington, who is the descendant of those who used to make the reins, wants to know if you really exist. He previously asked me to tell him if I found anything. Should I tell him about you?"

Upon hearing this, Calyrex was calm. He thought deeply for a moment and then gently shook His head, "Forget it, I am no longer The King of Bountiful Harvests. Now, I am just an ordinary farmer."

D: "..." Goddamn ordinary farmer!

But if that's the case… fine! Then he wouldn't tell the old village elder of Freezington about this.

"If that's the case, then we'll set off tomorrow!" D said.

Next, he still needed to head to the large city in the Galar Region to purchase some of the fallen Steel feathers of Corviknight, which would also take some time.

Once that was done, he could take Calyrex back to the ranch to spend the winter together.

Calyrex nodded lightly, "Okay."

---

---

---

---

# 256: Chapter 253: King and King

The night blanket covers the earth, and the whole world soon falls into quietness.

Pokemon residing near Dyna Tree Hill gradually return to their nests, yawning and snuggling inside to sleep peacefully.

Occasionally, some nocturnal Pokemon pass by, their running stirring up soft rustling sounds in the grass.

Standing on the hill, one can see the distant sea of the Galar Region, where the ocean currents keep the temperature from being as cold as the northern part of the tundra.

It is precisely because of this that a rich ecology of wild Pokemon has been cultivated here.

D, along with a few Pokemon and Calyrex, sits around the campfire.

Upon hearing D is preparing to leave the Crown Tundra tomorrow, Calyrex casts his gaze towards the distant Galar Region and softly says to D:

"Before leaving, I wish to go to a place."

D is quite taken aback: "Where to?"

Calyrex: "The place where the King of Swords and the King of the Shield reside."

The King of Swords and the King of the Shield... aren't they the legendary Pokemon Zacian and Zamazenta from the Galar Region?

D hesitated for a moment, then asked in surprise: "Do you know Zacian and Zamazenta?"

Calyrex nods slightly: "I once faced the catastrophe that destroyed the Galar Region alongside them."

This must be referring to the Darkest Day caused by Eternatus, right?

D couldn't help but ask: "Can you tell me what exactly happened in the past?"

By now, the details of those events have long become blurry in modern times; people have forgotten Calyrex and remember neither Zacian nor Zamazenta—only recalling a hero wielding the Sword and Shield who saved the Galar Region.

In the games, this history isn't specifically detailed; players can only speculate based on scant words and clues.

Calyrex, with a dignified expression, nods and says: "Of course, since you wish to know, I shall recount everything to you!"

D immediately brings over a small stool, sitting side by side with Koraidon and Cyclizar, listening beside the campfire as Calyrex tells of the past events.

"A long, long time ago, I led my steed and the people who believed in me, living together on this land."

"One day, using Future Sight, I saw an unprecedented disaster would soon descend upon the Galar Region."

"To face this disaster, I called forth two other mighty beings who lived on this land, Zacian and Zamazenta."

"Upon hearing of the upcoming events, they agreed to stop the disaster."

"Thus, at the Crown Shrine, I conducted a coronation ceremony for them, granting them my 'King's Power,' and another Pokemon named Melmetal crafted weapons for them."

"Combining 'King's Power' with the weapons, Zacian and Zamazenta gained power sufficient to confront the disaster."

"Afterward, they went to stop the disaster, while I, together with my steed, used psychic power to relocate the Pokemon within the mountains and forests."

"My power was thereby exhausted; after ensuring the safety of those Pokemon, I was forced into slumber, not knowing what happened afterward."

"I see…" D murmured.

Calyrex, looking at him, continues: "However, I can sense remnants of disastrous power still lingering on this land, and perhaps the source of the calamity has not been fully eradicated."

"I intend to inform them during this trip to the King of Swords and the King of the Shield's domain, urging them to prepare."

D can feel Calyrex's continuous concern for the Galar Region.

He can't help but ask: "Will you not stay, and fight alongside them against the disaster?"

Calyrex gently shakes his head: "I am no longer needed here. When my power returns to its peak, I used Future Sight once more."

"This time, I saw the King of Swords and the King of the Shield will thoroughly repel the disaster's source with the help of two humans."

"I see…" D realizes.

The two humans mentioned by Calyrex must be the protagonists of Sword and Shield, Hop, and Leon's brother, Hop, right?

Yet, there's still one thing that particularly concerns him.

Zacian and Zamazenta's Sword and Shield were indeed forged by Melmetal?

That certainly is unexpected!

Having learned everything he wanted to know, D cannot help but sigh.

From Calyrex's account, he can almost envision how these legendary Pokemon united to deal with Eternatus during that era.

"Sigh."

Calyrex, puzzled, asks: "Why the sigh?"

D thinks Calyrex should know all this: "The disaster, called the 'Darkest Day' by humans, was successfully repelled by Zacian and Zamazenta after you fell asleep; afterward, they withdrew into seclusion."

"Once the Darkest Day vanished, humans began to thrive, establishing a kingdom; they stole Zacian and Zamazenta's heroic identity, misleading people to believe it was the Galar Royal Family who saved the Galar Region."

"To this day, people here remain oblivious, thinking that the royal family defeated the Darkest Day."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex immediately understands his little believer's grievance for them.

He gently shakes his head: "I do not care for such false fame; back then, I, along with the King of Swords, only wished to protect the Pokemon living on this land. To me, it is what a true king should do."

---

"Moreover, everything I know, those who believed in me, have long disappeared, and I no longer feel sorrow for this."

D: "..."

That's also true.

He sighed helplessly, "Alright."

*

The next morning, D and Calyrex rode their horses and Koraidon away from Dyna Tree Hill.

To D's disappointment, he didn't find any trace of the Galar Region's legendary bird Pokemon here; otherwise, he would have asked Koraidon to catch a few to bring home.

No Pokemon player can resist the temptation of rare Legendary Beast, and D is no exception.

Seeing the expression on his face, Calyrex asked in confusion, "Why do you look so disappointed?"

Koraidon, however, instantly understood; no one knows D better than it does.

Koraidon had an expression of disbelief.

Sure enough, the next moment, D said, "…I've read in a human adventure journal that three legendary bird Pokemon reside in that giant tree, but unfortunately, I didn't see them this time."

Hearing this, Calyrex immediately recognized which three Pokemon he was referring to.

"They don't actually reside here; they just occasionally rest on the treetops. If they encounter each other, they tend to engage in a battle."

"Looks like I picked the wrong time then," D said regretfully.

Koraidon sighed helplessly, "Gahhhh…" (Seriously...)

Calyrex smiled slightly, "If you wish to see them, I can accompany you here to search for them in the future."

"Never mind," D replied, "Let's wait until we have more time. There's plenty to do when I get back."

Like asking Keri to help build stables and prepare for reforesting next spring.

There are two horses, Spectrier and Glastrier, and they seem to have strong territorial instincts, so he has to build separate stables for each.

On the way back, D didn't return to Freezington Village but instead went straight with Calyrex to Slippery Slope.

The steam train to the Galar Region was stationed there.

On the outskirts of the snowfield, D stopped while riding Koraidon and asked Calyrex, "We're going to take the train to the Galar Region; we might encounter other humans there. Do you want to meet them?"

Calyrex thought for a moment and realized that humans today likely don't recognize him, so he answered indifferently, "Sure."

"Alright then," D turned his gaze to Spectrier and Glastrier, "There's something else, the train doesn't allow large-sized Pokemon, so I need to put them in Poke Balls, is that okay?"

"Poke Ball?" Calyrex looked puzzled.

D took out a regular red and white Poke Ball and explained:

"It's this thing, developed by humans to carry Pokemon. You just need to press the button to capture a Pokemon inside and take them anywhere."

He carefully explained to Calyrex about the relationship between humans and Pokemon in this era, including the roles of Trainers and Breeders, which are closely related to Pokemon.

Calyrex nodded while listening, and after D finished speaking, he said, "I understand. In that case, you can capture your beloved horses into them!"

D nodded, stepped forward, touched Spectrier and Glastrier's heads, and then took out two empty Poke Balls, pressing them against their heads.

With two flashes of red light, Spectrier and Glastrier's bodies were captured inside the balls.

Perhaps he had gained the horses' approval, the Poke Balls only shook slightly before completely calming down.

This indicates a successful capture.

Witnessing this magical scene, Calyrex's eyes widened in disbelief, filled with wonder.

"What kind of method is this?"

"Uh…" D thought for a moment, replying, "I think it utilizes Pokemon's tendency to shrink when they're weak, but I'm not really sure."

"Can I get inside too?" Calyrex asked on a whim, "To you humans, I should also be considered a Pokemon."

D: "I suppose?"

Calyrex seemed eager to try.

D could only take out a third Poke Ball, press the button, and capture Calyrex inside.

Perhaps it was due to Calyrex's favorable impression of him, the Poke Ball didn't even shake this time and quickly calmed down.

After a while, Calyrex emerged from the Poke Ball itself.

"Interesting," the former King of Galar remarked, "Humans truly are a very intelligent race."

Thinking of what D had mentioned earlier about Trainers and Pokemon's relationship, Calyrex asked, "In this way, I can be counted as one of your Pokemon, right? Trainer, please guide me well from now on!"

D: "..."

To avoid any misunderstandings, D explained, "I'm not a Trainer. I'm not skilled at training Pokemon. Like you, I'm also a farmer."

"Hahahaha!" Calyrex suddenly laughed out loud, "You are right, I'm not a king anymore either; from now on, we're both farmers!"

D: "..."

The two continued their journey, retracing the path back to the station at Slippery Slope.

Just as they were about to reach their destination, D suddenly saw a very noticeable caution line being set up on the road ahead, marked with red symbols indicating extreme danger, suggesting something dangerous had occurred here.

---

The doors of the station ahead were tightly shut, and the lights were dim.

"Hmm?"

D furrowed his brow.

Calyrex, standing beside him, also seemed to sense something unusual and said solemnly, "I sense an unusual presence..."

He turned his head and saw the distant mountains engulfed in flames, even the sky was painted scarlet by the firelight.

D and Koraidon also noticed that scene.

What happened? A fire broke out on a snowfield with subzero temperatures?

That's too bizarre!

At that moment, a low and eerie breathing sound suddenly came from the air.

Soon after, a red unusual creature resembling a humanoid mosquito descended from the sky, landing before D and Calyrex.

It was a creature covered entirely in bulging and terrifying muscles.

Behind it were two pairs of translucent bright red wings, a small flat head with black, sphere-like eyes that gleamed coldly, and a sharp, blade-like proboscis.

Ultra Beast!

Upon seeing the creature for the first time, its name immediately surfaced in D's mind.

Buzzwole!

But how could it be?! How did an Ultra Beast from the Alola Region appear in the Galar Region?!

Just as he was momentarily stunned, Buzzwole suddenly raised its robust arm and leaned it against its head, striking a perfectly executed Bulk Up pose.

Accompanied by its pulsating muscles, it was evident that Buzzwole seemed to be flaunting its explosive physique.

"Danger!"

A suddenly alert voice came from not far away.

D turned, seeing a man in a Galar League uniform, accompanied by a Snorlax, quickly approaching.

He directed his gaze at D, quickly saying, "Please leave here! This area has been blocked by the Galar League, the station is temporarily closed, please choose another route to the Galar Region!"

However, before he finished speaking, Buzzwole, as if disturbed, showed an angry expression.

Its entire body of muscles swelled rapidly, a massive buzzing sound emanated from its abdomen, and its red body moved with lightning speed, the gusts of wind lifting the man's hat.

The man was slightly startled, and before he could regain his composure, he heard his Snorlax let out a cry of pain.

The man turned and saw that muscular creature holding Snorlax tightly, ignoring its struggles, as it stabbed its pointed proboscis into Snorlax's neck.

And as Snorlax struggled, its once robust body rapidly shriveled before the naked eye until it was reduced to mere skin, weakly collapsing on the snow!

The man's eyes were filled with a murderous gaze.

Having finished draining, Buzzwole's muscles bulged as if molten lava flowed within, standing like a wall over the man, glaring down at him from above.

The sudden scene startled D.

Calyrex, witnessing the scene, also furrowed his brow.

Seeing Buzzwole about to attack the man, Calyrex spoke with an authoritative tone, "Impudent!"

Buzzwole indeed turned its attention towards him.

D also reacted at this moment, and seeing Calyrex preparing to act, he immediately took out a Poke Ball and randomly released a horse.

In a flash of white light, what appeared in front of Calyrex was Spectrier.

Buzzwole's aura surged, and it charged menacingly towards Calyrex.

Calyrex's figure flickered as he mounted onto Spectrier's back.

A powerful wave of spiritual power swept outwards, and the Ghost Energy on Spectrier surged turbulently.

Instantly, countless ghostly purple flames silently emerged from around Calyrex.

Calyrex waved his right hand, and a sky full of ghostly flames descended like meteors onto Buzzwole.

The man, dumbfounded by Buzzwole, was already awestruck.

What kind of Pokemon is that?!

---

---

---

---

# 257: Chapter 254: Father and Son (4k)

Buzzwole's muscles surged wildly, as if magma was flowing beneath its crimson muscles.

The powerful energy coursed through its muscular veins, rushing to its fists in an instant. Immediately, Buzzwole's aura skyrocketed! It soared! It surged!

It swung its fists directly at the endless mass of ghostly purple phosphorescent flames, seemingly attempting to punch them away.

However, the moment it made contact with the flames, Buzzwole suddenly widened its eyes, emitting a painful growl from its mouth.

"Ma-bu-suck!" (Pain!)

It looked down at its fists and found that the flames clung to them like bone-eating maggots, and its life force was rapidly draining away!

As more and more phosphorescent flames fell, ghostly purple flames began to burn on Buzzwole's back, arms, cheeks, and muscles around its abdomen.

A searing burning sensation spread throughout its entire body in an instant.

Buzzwole couldn't withstand such torment, gritting its teeth and crying out in agony, eventually rolling on the snow like a rogue trying to extinguish the flames using this method!

However.

Those flames were Spectrier's ghostly power, and ordinary methods were simply unable to extinguish them.

Calyrex, riding on Spectrier, looked down sternly at the crazy creature.

Gradually, Buzzwole's struggles diminished in intensity until it finally exhausted its strength, incapable of further action, and could only lie on the ground breathing weakly.

The man beside watched this scene, astonished at the overwhelmingly powerful and mysterious Pokemon capable of defeating Buzzwole in a single move.

At this moment, a tense voice came from the miniature communicator in his ear.

"Mark, what's happening?!"

The man called Mark quickly snapped back to reality, and as a League employee, he immediately went into work mode, reporting his situation:

"We've found the target! But... there's been a little accident here!"

"What accident?" The voice on the other end sounded slightly puzzled.

Mark: "That Pokemon that ran out from the glowing big hole drained my Snorlax, and at the crucial moment, a Trainer with several unknown Pokemon stepped in to save me!"

During the report, Mark suddenly saw the mounted Pokemon approach the creature from the Otherworld.

"Wait! That Pokemon is healing the unknown creature!" Mark exclaimed in shock.

Meanwhile, Calyrex's healing power had already begun restoring Buzzwole's injuries.

Mark hurriedly ran forward, shouting, "No, don't do that! It's dangerous!"

"Calm down." D glanced at the badge on the man's chest, realizing he was from the Galar League.

At this point, Buzzwole's injuries were fully healed, and it stood up slowly from the ground, looking fearfully at Calyrex.

Calyrex, with a stern gaze, looked directly at it: "I have forgiven your offensive actions, now, return to the place where you belong!"

Buzzwole suddenly halted in place, raised its arm to touch its head, showing a distressed and conflicted expression.

"Ma-bu-suck..." (I'm lost...)

Calyrex remained silent.

After a while, He asked: "Where do you come from?"

Buzzwole: "Ma-bu-suck!"

At this moment, the effect of the Translation Nuts Cake ended, and D could no longer understand Pokemon speech.

He didn't immediately take out a new cake, but instead turned to ask about the conversation between the two Pokemon: "Do you know where it came from?"

Mark was slightly stunned, then subconsciously replied: "From a complex cave..."

Upon hearing this, D nodded.

"As I suspected, this Buzzwole came from the Ultra Wormhole."

He recalled that whether in games or anime, there was indeed an Ultra Wormhole in the Galar Region.

And the Ultra Wormhole in the Galar Region was coincidentally located above the Crown Tundra, exactly in the depths of the cavern where players embark on their gigantamax adventures.

In the anime, Lillie's father, Professor Mohn, also entered the Galar Region through this Ultra Wormhole...

After a moment of contemplation, D said to the man: "It seems to be lost, we just need to send it back to the cavern."

"Lost?" Mark was quite surprised: "You... you can understand what Pokemon say?"

D neither nodded nor shook his head, casually picking a reason: "My Pokemon can use Telepathy."

"I see!" Mark suddenly realized.

But now didn't seem the time to discuss this, he glanced at Buzzwole, somewhat fearful: "Um, could you please accompany me to send it back?"

D took out a Translation Nuts Cake and ate it.

Calyrex watched his actions and said: "Certainly, I actually want to go there as I felt the abnormal aura emanating from that place."

"Alright." D nodded.

Then, Calyrex's gaze fell upon the drained Snorlax.

He rode Spectrier forward, raised his right hand, and a vibrant green energy was infused into Snorlax's body.

In the next second, the Snorlax that was drained to a skin suddenly inflated like a balloon.

Mark was already stunned by this scene.

He glanced at Koraidon beside him and then at Calyrex and the Spectrier beneath Him, unable to resist asking: "Are they all your Pokemon?"

---

D nodded, "Yes."

"Wow, so strong!" Mark's eyes widened.

As a League employee and Trainer, he knew very well that the Galar Region didn't have such Pokemon, which meant that this powerful Trainer in front of him came from another region!

As Mark led the way for a few people, he turned around and asked, "May I ask, where are you from?"

D smiled, "Of course, I'm from Paldea, and I've just arrived in the Galar Region not long ago."

Paldea! Mark instantly perked up.

That meant these two Pokemon he had never seen before were also Pokemon from the Paldea Region!

Seeing their spirited demeanor, it was obvious they were perfectly raised.

Mark couldn't help but praise, "Amazing! You must be a really impressive Trainer, right?"

D: "..." Here we go again.

He smiled politely, "No, actually I'm just a farmer."

"What?" Mark was dumbfounded, "A farmer? How is that possible?"

What kind of farmer could raise such powerful Pokemon?

Seeing he didn't believe it, D was utterly helpless.

Why does no one want to believe he's really just a farmer from the countryside?

Mark, not knowing what he imagined, kept glancing at him while walking, and after a while, he suddenly said, "I get it!"

D: "?"

What do you know?

At that moment, they reached their destination.

It was an area surrounded by cliffs, with various police cars and helicopters parked on the snowfield.

D saw some police cars and helicopters engulfed in flames, suggesting a big explosion had occurred not long ago.

Looking at this scene, Mark said, "These were all caused by that mysterious creature. It could send a car flying with one punch. Because of it, we dispatched a lot of people..."

At this time, a group of people gathered on the snowfield had already noticed their arrival.

Mark immediately brought Snorlax to report the situation.

The other people all turned their eyes towards D and the Koraidon and Calyrex beside him.

Seeing this scene, D initially thought someone might recognize Calyrex.

But it turned out no one did.

Calyrex looked at the seemingly bottomless cave not far away, and said, "I sense that the source of the power comes from this cave. It seems to be connected to another world unknown to us."

D was slightly taken aback and then realized Calyrex was likely referring to the aura of the Ultra Wormhole, and that world should be the homeland of the Ultra Beasts, Ultra Space.

"However, it's very weak now and will soon disappear," Calyrex said.

Upon hearing this, Buzzwole began to panic a little.

"Phew Mash?!"

If it disappears, won't it never be able to go back?!

D was utterly speechless, "Knowing you can't go back, why did you come over?"

Buzzwole: "Phew Mash..." (Curiosity...)

D: "..." Okay, it seemed this Buzzwole came to this world out of curiosity.

The only regret was that it wasn't Poipole who came; otherwise, he definitely would've taken it home.

As for why not catch Buzzwole...

D silently glanced at it; although Buzzwole was all muscle, its appearance couldn't compare with Incineroar or Urshifu of the same type.

And it might even take a chance to suck on the chickens raised by Pawmo in the ranch when he wasn't paying attention.

Forget it, D thought.

Just then, Mark had finished reporting.

He ran back and said to D, "Alright, now we can send it back underground!"

D was a bit puzzled, "What underground?"

Mark looked bewildered, "Isn't this Pokemon from the depths of the underground?"

D: "..." It seemed the Galar League still didn't know about the Ultra Wormhole.

Since that was the case, he wouldn't explain further.

Watching Buzzwole leave behind the others, he and Calyrex stood there for a while.

It was unclear how long had passed when Calyrex finally spoke, "I sense that the power has already disappeared."

D nodded, indicating that the Ultra Wormhole should be closed.

And at this moment, a group of Galar League staff rushed out of the cave in shock.

"It's gone!"

"That muscular Pokemon ran into a glowing hole and disappeared!"

"So it's not a Pokemon from the underground, but one from another world?"

Watching this scene, D said to Calyrex, "Let's go too; the train station should be operational again by now."

Calyrex nodded slightly.

He rode on Spectrier and followed Koraidon, leaving the place and heading towards the distant snowfield.

When Mark and others came to their senses, intending to thank the Trainer, they found that he had already left.

These Galar people, unaware of what they had missed, could only mutter to themselves:

"It's strange that this Pokemon would ride another Pokemon into battle."

"However, from your report, that Pokemon seems to be quite powerful, defeating the dangerous Pokemon in one move."

"To be able to catch such a Pokemon, that Paldean must be a very formidable Trainer, right? What a pity, it would have been great to have a chance to befriend him."

*

A day later, at Wedgehurst Town.

This was a quiet little town located in the suburbs.

Since Calyrex wanted to go to Slumbering Weald to find Zacian and Zamazenta, D and he got off at the Wedgehurst Town train station.

They planned to head south from here to Postwick Town, with Slumbering Weald right next to it.

D walked the streets with Koraidon and Calyrex, while Spectrier and Glastrier were stored in Poke Balls.

Koraidon looked around curiously, seemingly fascinated by everything here.

Calyrex also looked around at all the surroundings.

While looking, he said to D, "Compared to the world I am familiar with, a lot has indeed changed."

"Yeah!"

D, who had done some research on the ancient history of the Pokemon World, couldn't help but lament as well:

"Back in the day, humans still treated Pokemon as monsters and slaves, but now with the development of science, Pokemon have fully integrated into human life."

Calyrex gently nodded, and a smile appeared on his face, "I like the world as it is now."

D grinned, "I like it too."

At this moment, Koraidon seemed to suddenly discover something.

It widened its eyes and looked into the distance, "Ah-ga-skee!"

and saw a group of people gathered on the road some distance away, seemingly up to something.

"Huh?"

D immediately transformed into a curious onlooker and moved closer to see what was happening.

They pushed through the crowd and saw an Urshifu standing on the street!

The Urshifu was holding a few-year-old boy in its arms, looking very anxious.

"Ah-ga-skee!" (Let me see!)

Koraidon wanted to squeeze in for a better look as well.

And just then, an elderly woman with a full head of silver hair,

and a kind face walked up to the Urshifu's side.

D immediately recognized her identity.

She was Professor Magnolia, responsible for Dynamax research in the Galar Region!

Professor Magnolia first checked on the condition of the boy in Urshifu's arms, then asked Urshifu:

"Are you his Pokemon? The child has a fever and it's severe. We must get to a hospital immediately!"

Then, D heard the Urshifu respond, "Aoo!" (No, I'm his dad!)

Hearing this, D was slightly stunned.

What was that about?

But before he could gather more information, the Urshifu carried the boy away, following Professor Magnolia.

Looking at their leaving direction, it seemed they were heading toward the Pokemon Center.

The onlookers gradually dispersed, but several still cast curious glances at him, Calyrex, and Koraidon.

D asked Calyrex and Koraidon in confusion, "Did you guys hear what that Pokemon just said?"

Did he mishear it?

Koraidon nodded its big head repeatedly, "Ah-ga-skee!" (Heard it clearly!)

Calyrex gently nodded as well, looking at the back of the Urshifu, he replied, "It regards that human boy as its child."

Oh my god!

D immediately thought of Zarude and Coco.

No way?

Could it be that the boy was also raised by this Urshifu?

---

---

---

---

# 258: Chapter 255: Sword and Shield (4k)

Urshifu, huh! There are wild Urshifu in the Galar Region!

That's a Tier Two legendary Pokémon in the Galar Region!

D gazed at the Urshifu, feeling a tickle in his heart.

That tall, strong body, powerful arms, resolute and reliable gaze, and sleek, soft fur.

What a charming Pokémon!

D, from the Paldea Region, had never seen a live Urshifu before.

Driven by curiosity, he immediately took Koraidon and Calyrex, and after a bit of disguise, snuck into the Pokémon Center.

It seemed the boy was already taken into the emergency room.

The Urshifu was standing in the lobby, anxiously looking in the direction of the emergency room.

Professor Magnolia, sitting on a nearby sofa, looked at it and comforted, "Don't worry, the child will definitely be fine."

Urshifu looked worried, seemingly not comforted at all.

D acted as if he was a Trainer resting at the Pokémon Center. He put inconspicuous sunglasses on Koraidon and Calyrex, casually took a newspaper, and observed the Urshifu.

"Argghh..."

Koraidon felt speechless, knowing D's attention had again been drawn by a Pokémon.

Calyrex turned its head, looked around through its sunglasses, experiencing this peculiar feeling.

After a while, the light in the emergency room changed from red to green, and Miss Joy came out of the room.

D heard Miss Joy seriously ask, "What on earth happened here? Where are this child's parents? Sending him ten minutes later would have been dangerous!"

Speaking, Miss Joy looked at the Urshifu, asking, "Are you his Pokémon?"

The Urshifu opened its mouth, seemingly wanting to say something, but ultimately remained silent.

Professor Magnolia smiled, looking at the Urshifu, saying, "Alright, the child is already fine."

Miss Joy was still very angry: "His parents are truly irresponsible, not coming themselves at such a time, but letting a Pokémon bring him here!"

Upon hearing this, the Urshifu's mood sank, and it lowered its head in dejection.

Miss Joy looked at this Pokémon, sighed, and said, "The child is already fine. If you're worried, you can go to the ward to see him first."

Upon hearing this, the Urshifu hurriedly went in.

D watched all of this with interest.

If what he heard on the street just now was true, then what Miss Joy said would definitely be stabbing this Urshifu right in the heart.

It must feel very guilty, right?

Just then, Miss Joy noticed them sitting in the corner and adjusted her emotions, stepping forward to ask, "Hello, can I help you?"

Before even finishing her sentence, Miss Joy saw Calyrex wearing sunglasses and couldn't help but exclaim, "What... what a big head!"

Calyrex: "..."

D: "..." How impolite!

After speaking, Miss Joy hurriedly covered her mouth, realizing she had said her thoughts out loud, and quickly apologized: "I'm really very sorry!"

Calyrex looked helplessly: "It's fine, I don't blame you."

However, to Miss Joy, Calyrex's growl sounded like, "Crown, moo-gee-gee!"

D translated, "He says he forgives you."

Miss Joy looked apologetic, and after learning they were here to rest at the Pokémon Center, she personally provided them with three cups of nutritious Berry Juice.

"This Berry Juice can restore a Pokémon's energy and is very effective on Pokémon of all attributes!" Miss Joy smiled.

D inserted straws for Koraidon and Calyrex, handed them respectively, and then said to Miss Joy, "Another cup, please."

With that, he released Cyclizar.

Miss Joy understood, so she went to make another cup of Berry Juice.

A few Pokémon happily drank, and Calyrex also took the Berry Juice with his hand, holding the cup and sipping through the straw.

From its expression, the Berry Juice seemed quite tasty.

After finishing a whole cup, Calyrex remarked, "Indeed delicious, the temperature on the snowy plains is too low and not conducive to the growth of fruit trees, I haven't had this kind of drink for a long time."

D laughed, saying, "I grow many Berry trees on my ranch, so you can drink as much as you want when we go back."

Calyrex laughed heartily: "I am no longer a child, you don't need to use that sort of tone one speaks to children with me, your intention, I understand."

Though, it didn't really mind, actually.

D felt a bit awkward: "Haha, I'm just used to speaking like this with Koraidon and them."

At this moment, the Urshifu ran out of the room, decisively turned, and left the Pokémon Center.

Upon seeing this, D was a little stunned.

Where is it going?

Calyrex was also quite curious, and it suggested, "Shall we follow it and have a look?"

D nodded and took a few Pokémon to follow along.

They watched as the Urshifu went all the way forward, walking several streets without stopping, until it completely left Wedgehurst Town.

Seeing this, D was dumbfounded: "Is it trying to run away?"

At this moment, the Urshifu suddenly stopped up ahead.

---

It turned its head, looking over with a wary expression.

"Awoo?" (Who are you?)

This Urshifu's perception is sharper than he imagined! Its strength must be formidable!

D thought this to himself, but spoke aloud, "You left that boy alone at the Pokemon Center."

Upon hearing this, Urshifu's face showed a look of reluctance and pain, "Little Wu..."

D raised an eyebrow, "Little Wu?"

Suddenly, Urshifu realized, "You can understand what I'm saying?"

At this moment, Calyrex floated forward, asking with goodwill, "We mean no harm, I merely wish to understand your relationship with that human."

Urshifu looked at Calyrex, opened its mouth, internal pain and struggle evident.

After a moment, it spoke in a sorrowful tone, "He is Little Wu... my son."

D and Calyrex exchanged a glance, inwardly saying, just as they suspected.

Koraidon beside them was full of shock.

"Agassi?!" (Can a Pokemon give birth to a human?!)

D: "..." That Urshifu is obviously male, how could it lay an egg?

He greatly admired his Koraidon's fanciful thinking.

The Urshifu across from them glanced at Koraidon and explained, "He's not... I found him in the forest."

Speaking of his son, Urshifu's face was full of affection and pride; it was clear that it had devoted one hundred percent of its emotions to the human boy.

"At that time, I was practicing boxing under the waterfall when a human-abandoned boy drifted down from upstream in front of me."

"If I didn't take him in, he would die, so I adopted him, became his father, and named him Little Wu."

"I love him very much. I take him to the plains on the other side of the mountain to find milk, and he accompanies me by the waterfall to practice boxing all year round. Little Wu is a very intelligent child; he helps the Pokemon in the forest, and even some of the more fierce Pokemon become more docile when they see him."

Talking about the past, Urshifu's eyes were exceptionally gentle.

Calyrex listened silently, then curiously asked, "Then why did you leave him?"

Urshifu's expression turned somber, painfully answering, "Because Little Wu is a human, life in the forest isn't suitable for him."

"That human was right, I'm not a qualified father; I can't selfishly bind Little Wu to my side, keeping him away from the human world."

"Staying in the human world, Little Wu can grow up healthy, never get sick, always find food, live in a warm room, and not worry about rain and cold..."

Hearing this, D roughly understood Urshifu's thoughts.

He couldn't help but say, "You're already a qualified father."

Urshifu was slightly taken aback.

D: "But I think the child doesn't know about your departure yet, does he?"

Urshifu looked embarrassed.

Calyrex also understood D's meaning, "I can feel your heart. If you cannot let him go, why leave him?"

D: "When he wakes up, the first person he wants to see will surely be you, yet you're not by his side. Though it's the human world, everything is unfamiliar and unknown to that child."

Koraidon nodded its big head in agreement, "Agassi!"

Upon hearing these words, Urshifu was slightly stunned.

Imagining that scene, his heart raced, and he no longer cared about previous matters, hastily turning and running toward the town.

D turned to watch this scene, feeling slightly touched inside.

"I never expected that such a thing could happen in this world."

He liked Urshifu, this kind of Pokemon, even more.

Though regrettably, liking aside, the number of such Pokemon is really too scarce.

He certainly couldn't go and steal someone else's father, right?

Forget it! With Koraidon and Calyrex, he's already quite content.

The most important lesson in life is to understand the joy of being content.

Calyrex too saw new possibilities between humans and Pokemon.

This former King of Galar sincerely wished, "I hope that child can recover soon."

"Alright, we should continue on our journey!" D said.

Calyrex nodded.

Thus, the group continued, making their way toward Postwick Town.
