The sky was turning dark, the setting sun enveloping the entire land, as if the whole world were drowning in summer's orange soda.

Like Porto Marinada Town, Postwick Town also primarily engages in farm business.

Only here, the farm raises not Rock Pokemon or Miltank, but a large group of chubby, round Wooloo.

Watching them leisurely munching on grass across the sunset-lit fields brings a sense of peace and tranquility.

Looking at the scene here, Calyrex seemed to foresee its own future life.

A smile appeared on its face, softly saying, "I haven't experienced such life in a long time, and now, it does make one look forward to it."

Behind the vast plains lies a quiet, deep forest.

Calyrex's gaze fell upon the forest, "Let's go, King of Swords and King of the Shield are there!"

D felt a slight rush of excitement within.

Would he soon meet Zacian and Zamazenta?

This trip to Galar, he met six Legendary Pokemon, indeed making it all worthwhile!

With anticipation, D and Calyrex proceeded to the Slumbering Weald.

As the night descended, the entire forest appeared extraordinarily tranquil.

The river babbled along, dense plants thrived on both sides of the path, towering trees blocked out the sky, occasionally a beam of pure moonlight pierced through the gaps illuminating the woods.

In the distant thicket, a layer of enigmatic mist prevails, making the entire forest dreamlike, as if entering a fantasy.

The paths in the forest wind and diverge, unknown to D and Koraidon, so Calyrex led the way.

After walking without knowing for how long, gradually the surrounding mist grew heavier until visibility was less than five meters.

Suddenly, Calyrex at the front stopped.

D was about to ask what was wrong, then saw Calyrex stare proudly ahead.

D was slightly stunned, then he followed its gaze, seeing two agile shadows emerge from the thick haze.

The one on the left had a coat of blue fur with white patterns resembling scars, ears with braid-like extensions, and slender limbs.

The one on the right was covered in red fur resembling armor, had a tuft of hair on its head, sharp angles throughout, similarly marked with scar-like patterns, its face resembling a small shield.

With calm and composed eyes, Zacian and Zamazenta glanced at D behind Calyrex and then approached, bowing low like knights serving their king.

Calyrex spoke with authority, "No need for ceremony!"

Zacian and Zamazenta rose; they seemed to understand Calyrex's purpose, then raised their heads, letting out a howl akin to a canine.

"Woof—!" (We've successfully repelled the Darkest Day!)

Calyrex nodded slightly, "I am aware; however, I can still feel that force wasn't completely driven away, scattered across the land."

Upon hearing this, slight confusion flashed upon Zacian and Zamazenta's faces.

Calyrex's gaze turned to them, saying, "I am no longer the King of Galar. Soon, I will depart this land; from now on, everything here is in your hands."

Hearing that Calyrex would leave, Zacian and Zamazenta widened their eyes in surprise.

Zamazenta asked in confusion, "Awoo?" (Why? Is it because of this human?)

Calyrex glanced at D, "Yes and no, this land has no need for me anymore; humans have found their path."

Zacian, somewhat anxious, said, "Woof? Woof…" (But you just said the Darkest Day isn't over… If you leave, can we alone stop it? Furthermore, our weapons have long grown rusty…)

Calyrex lightly shook its head, "I have bestowed the King's Power upon you; it will provide answers, and I have glimpsed the future. This time, the disaster shrouding this land will be conclusively resolved."

Then Calyrex looked at the heroes before him and said to them:

"Until that day arrives, all you need to do is patiently await."

# 259: Chapter 256: Heroic Royal Court, Wyndon City

Deep in the Slumbering Weald, the Heroic Royal Court.

The broken archway is covered with moss, and below it stands an ancient stone tablet, the engraved characters long eroded by time, becoming blurred.

In front of the stone tablet are two rusted, broken weapons—one is a sword, the other a shield.

Moonlight streams through the forest gaps, gently illuminating the sword and shield.

This is the dwelling of Zacian and Zamazenta.

In the distant past, after driving away the Darkest Day, they came here to live in seclusion.

D and Calyrex stand before the court, gazing at the sword and shield bathed in ethereal moonlight.

Zacian and Zamazenta follow on either side of Calyrex, looking at their weapons, and solemnly say:

"Woof." (During the battle against disaster, our weapons were damaged by its power. After everything was over, we came here with our weapons and placed them in this court.)

D listens in silence.

He turns his head to glance at Zacian beside him; both Zacian and Zamazenta are still in their Hundred Battle Heroes form.

Without the sword and shield, they naturally cannot transform into the King of Swords and King of the Shield.

"Woof?" (King, when do you plan to leave?) Zamazenta inquires.

Calyrex shifts its gaze from the decayed sword and shield and replies: "Once D has finished his business, we will depart from this place."

D?

Zacian and Zamazenta blink in slight surprise, then realize this must be the human's name.

They look on, puzzled, wondering why the King would suddenly choose to follow an ordinary human.

Zamazenta calmly looks at the unfamiliar Pokemon beside D.

... No, perhaps not an ordinary human.

It can sense a deliberately concealed pressure from that Pokemon.

With just one glance, Zamazenta determines that this is a Pokemon whose strength rivals its own and its sister's, even far exceeding theirs.

Though unsure why the King would choose this human, Zacian still asks: "Woof?" (King, will we be able to see you again in the future?)

Calyrex does not give a clear answer: "Once your mission is completed, if by then you still wish to see me, then follow the traces I leave to another land to seek me!"

Hearing this, Zacian and Zamazenta feel happy in their hearts: "Woof!" (We understand!)

Watching Zacian and Zamazenta bow and salute Calyrex, D suddenly has a thought.

These two Pokemon... seem like they can't swim, right?

They can't fly like Groudon either, so how will they cross such a vast ocean?

Hmm, that's a problem.

The sky has darkened, and to allow Zacian and Zamazenta more time with Calyrex, D decides to set up camp on the spot, pitching a tent and lighting a bonfire.

Koraidon and Cyclizar also help out on the side.

Spectrier and Glastrier snort on the grassland to the right of the court.

Meanwhile, Zacian and Zamazenta lie by the fire like two large dogs, resting their heads on their front paws, calmly watching the flickering flames.

Watching this scene, D feels a stir in his heart.

What cute big dogs.

It's a pity they can't be captured; if they could be...

The Galarian Legendary Pokemon finding re-employment in the Paldea Region, with Calyrex becoming a farmer, and Zacian and Zamazenta taking on roles as sheepdogs and guard dogs.

Truly perfect!

However, capturing them is impossible, as Zarude and Zacian still bear the mission of repelling Eternatus.

D laments not bringing a camera.

If he had a camera, he could have taken pictures of these two Legendary Pokemon to keep as souvenirs.

D shifts his attention away from them and speaks to Calyrex by the campfire:

"Tomorrow morning, we will set off for the next place, and after completing things, we can return to the Paldea Region."

Calyrex has no objections and nods slightly: "Yes."

*

Early the next morning, D packs up, restores everything to its original state, and prepares to set off with his Pokemon.

Zacian and Zamazenta say their farewells to them in the morning light, watching the King of Galar leave.

Calyrex nods to the two Pokemon and then turns to leave the Slumbering Weald with D.

As they arrive in Postwick Town, D sees several young boys and girls playing on the street.

He stops and watches for a while.

Calyrex turns around and follows his gaze, inquiring puzzledly: "What's the matter?"

"Nothing." D shakes his head and sighs: "Just feels quite amazing..."

At this age, Yū and Hop are probably just like them, possibly still bedwetting and crying.

Who would've thought that in a few years, they would become the heroes who save the Galar Region?

"Let's go!"

They trace back the way to Wedgehurst Town, boarding the steam train to Wyndon City.

There is the headquarters of the Galar Flying Taxi fleet, where they can certainly get Corviknight feathers.

One or two feathers are not enough; as Tinkatink evolves, its strength will grow, so the previously made hammer won't suffice, and it will start crafting a new hammer.

Tinkatink's evolution has three stages, consuming quite a bit of metal during this time.

Besides that, there are also the hammers of Pawmo.

So this time, D plans to gather everything in one go, saving a future trip.

Trains in the Galar Region are very fast, and about eight hours later, they arrive in Wyndon City.

As they step out of the station, D and Calyrex are amazed by the sight before them.

As the largest city in the Galar Region, Wyndon City's scale seems even bigger than Levincia City!

Within the city, skyscrapers stand tall, two monorail tracks encircle the city, with various modern facilities in between.

The majestic Wyndon Stadium resembles a giant pink rose, elegant Gothic-Victorian style luxury hotels, a high-tech, modern tower, and a vast central park.

At the center of the park stands a giant pitch-black Corviknight statue, its steel wings spread wide, glistening in the sunlight, giving off an awe-inspiring aura.

Occasionally, flocks of Corvisquire can be seen flying across the sky.

On the streets, office workers carrying briefcases and wearing suits, leading their Yampers, and fashionably dressed high-society women can be seen everywhere.

D: As expected of the metropolis crafted by Chairman Rose!

Beside him, Calyrex looks up at the shimmering silver towers in the sunlight, softly murmuring:

"To think that humans can create such spectacular things, it truly amazes me!"

---

---

---

---

# 260: Chapter 257: The Commercialization of Pokemon Battles in the Galar Region (4k)

"Corviknight Flying Taxi General Company..."

D walked down the streets of Wyndon City with Koraidon and Calyrex, following the road signs in the city center to head to their destination.

Passersby came and went, almost everyone accompanied by one or two Pokemon.

Some were simply shopping, while others were Pokemon Trainers visiting Wyndon City for the first time, eyes full of wonder.

In this world, shopping with Pokemon was a common thing, so even with Koraidon and Calyrex, it didn't cause too much commotion.

The reason was simple: these two Pokemon were so rare that no one recognized them.

A few spirited young Trainers, after meeting D's eyes, ran over to block their way and challenged him to a battle.

Like right now.

A young Trainer wearing a red and white cap, followed by a Scorbunny, stood in front of them, looking at Koraidon and Calyrex excitedly.

"I've never seen these Pokemon before! You're a Trainer, right? Let's have a Pokemon battle!"

D looked down at the cute little bunny and couldn't help saying, "You must have just become a Trainer recently, right?"

The young Trainer held up three fingers: "It's already been three months!"

D showed a polite smile: "This might be a bit unfair to you, perhaps you should come back to challenge me when you're a bit older!"

The young Trainer persisted: "No, we've already collected three Gym Badges!"

"Three?" D was a bit surprised, but thinking about the strength of Koraidon and Calyrex, he still shook his head and said, "Still not enough, well, I wish you a successful journey, see you around!"

After saying that, he bypassed this new Trainer.

He hadn't walked a few steps before another Trainer ran over to invite him for a Pokemon battle.

D: "..."

As expected of a super metropolis where Trainer culture thrives!

He looked at the Sirfetch'd beside this young Trainer and thought it seemed much stronger than the previous one.

But he really wasn't here for battles this time!

Facing the young Trainer's invitation, D shook his head and said: "Sorry, I'm not a Pokemon Trainer, so I can't accept your invitation."

The young Trainer was taken aback, he seemed a bit older, about fifteen or sixteen.

"How is that? I can see you're clearly a Trainer... this Pokemon is so strong, it must have been through many tough battles!"

D continued to smile politely: "But we're really not!"

The young Trainer was quite perceptive to people's expressions and, hearing this, finally stopped insisting on the battle.

D then breathed a sigh of relief; he dared not leisurely stroll through the city anymore, so he directly mounted Koraidon and ran swiftly down the road.

After crossing several streets, they finally arrived at the headquarters of the Corviknight Flying Taxi Company.

The building was a skyscraper over thirty meters tall.

The first-floor lobby housed the ticket counter and a lounge area, and several staff members stood at the reception desk. As D entered, some guests who were about to take a flying taxi had just bought their tickets.

"It will be 1500 League coins. Please keep your ticket safe. Take the elevator to the rooftop terrace to board. Our Corviknight Flying Taxis are parked there."

The receptionist handed the ticket to the guest with a smile.

As the guest turned to leave, she turned her gaze to D, who had stepped forward, and greeted him with a polite smile: "Welcome! Hello, how can I assist you today?"

"Hello, I'm wondering if there are any Corviknight feathers for sale here?" D inquired.

The receptionist was slightly taken aback: "You want to purchase Corviknight feathers?"

D nodded.

The receptionist looked a bit troubled: "As far as I know, we've signed an agreement with a steel factory, where all the feathers that Corviknight shed while working here are sent for smelting to make various iron goods sold elsewhere. They've never been sold separately before..."

"Steel factory?" D pondered for a moment and quickly realized that Corviknight feathers are also an important resource in this world.

Seeing his keen interest, the receptionist thought carefully, then said: "Alright, I'll check with our company's boss."

D initially thought he wouldn't be able to buy any, but hearing this, he realized there was still a chance and thanked, "Sorry to trouble you."

The receptionist smiled: "No problem, I'm happy to be of assistance to you, Trainer sir."

The Galar Region greatly values each Trainer; after Chairman Rose became a league official, he implemented a series of policies favoring Trainers.

He hopes that every Trainer visiting the Galar Region will have a pleasant experience.

Because of his influence, everyone in this region is willing to help Trainers passing by.

These Trainers traveling through the Galar Region can bring them tangible benefits, whether by spending or battling Pokemon.

After all, the Galar Region is known for attracting people with Pokemon battles.

Regarding this, D wasn't very clear.

The receptionist didn't explain either; she picked up the phone at the front desk, dialed a number, and made a call.

---

The phone was quickly answered.

The receptionist truthfully reported the situation here.

"Hello, boss! A trainer has come to the company wanting to buy Corviknight feathers..."

"Yes, I see he has two very powerful Pokemon with him, which I've never seen before."

"What? You want to come in person?"

"Oh, okay, I got it, I'll arrange it!"

After saying that, the phone was hung up, and the receptionist showed an apologetic smile.

"Sorry, our boss wants to meet you after hearing the news. Please wait over there; he'll be here soon."

D was suddenly bewildered.

What's going on? He just wants to buy some Corviknight feathers; he's not some big shot, so is it necessary to make such a fuss?

After a brief contemplation, he sat in the lounge area waiting.

Koraidon curiously looked around, while Calyrex appeared indifferent and graceful, with a kind of invisible majesty in its gaze.

Soon, an elevator descended to the first floor.

Following that, a tall and strong middle-aged man dressed in casual wear stepped out of the elevator.

He held an old-fashioned phone in his hand, speaking loudly:

"What? That guy dared to say in public that my vision is not as good as his? And even affected the company's stock price?"

"Unbelievable! Absolutely unbelievable! Let him wait for me; our Galar Team company will definitely win the New Star Tournament!"

The man angrily hung up the phone and dialed another number.

"Where did that kid Komatsu run off to? Why hasn't there been any news until now? The New Star Tournament is about to start, doesn't he have any sense of urgency as the company-sponsored trainer?"

On the other end, someone said something.

The man was furious: "Forget it, I can't rely on him anymore. I'll see if I can find other trainers to help us; we can't lose to other companies in this New Star Tournament!"

After saying that, he hung up the phone and headed toward the reception desk.

The lady pointed in the direction of the lounge.

Then D saw the man walking towards him.

The man eyed the young man before him, then looked at the two Pokemon beside him.

The one on the left was huge, muscular, with fierce eyes, clearly not a Normal Pokemon.

The one on the right...

The man's eyes widened in surprise, how is this Pokemon's head so big?!

It seemed that Calyrex noticed the man's impolite gaze, raising its eyes, and instantly displayed the kingly majesty it once possessed, making others want to submit subconsciously.

The man was startled. Did this Pokemon see through his thoughts?

Though he didn't know what Pokemon it was, its gaze and aura undoubtedly marked it as extraordinary!

The man's eyes ignited with fervent intensity.

Luckily, fortune favors them, offering them an amazing trainer just when their sponsored player couldn't return. Luck was clearly on their side this time for the New Star Tournament; the Champion's title will definitely be theirs!

The man cleared his throat to appear polite: "Are you the one wanting to buy Corviknight feathers?"

D responded confidently: "Yes."

"That's easy, buying aside, I can even give them to you for free!" the man generously said.

D: "?"

"I just need you to help me out with something," the man added.

Help out? What's he planning? D pondered for a moment, asking: "Help with what?"

The man replied: "Represent our company in the upcoming regional New Star Tournament!"

Participate... in the tournament? D was mildly surprised.

Seeing his puzzled look, the man cheerfully explained:

"Don't worry, it's just a Normal Pokemon competition. You're a trainer from another region, right?"

D: "Yes."

"I thought so," the man boasted, confident in his judgement: "The regional New Star Tournament is a traditional competition in the Galar Region."

"Here, trainer battles have already been commercialized. Initially, some companies would invest heavily to invite famous trainers to endorse their products to fans."

"Later on, some companies started sponsoring talented trainers, cultivating them from scratch. If they perform well in championships, they bring considerable profits to the company."

"But championships are only held once per year, and some fans feel unsatisfied, craving more excitement. Thus, the regional New Star Tournament was born due to overwhelming requests, attracting many renowned trainers representing their supporting companies."

Hearing this, D catches on.

It's somewhat similar to celebrity matches? These companies invest funds, sponsoring those trainers and leveraging their fame and fan base for returns.

With this realization, a startling thought popped up in his mind: "You don't mean to let me represent your company in the tournament, do you?"

The man grinned: "Smart!"

He continued: "That's right, our company initially sponsored a trainer, who made it to the top four in the previous championship but lost to Leon."

"Last month, he went out for training but now, nobody knows where he went and we couldn't reach him. Seems like he won't return in time for the New Star Tournament."

"The neighboring wool sweater-selling company took advantage to mock us, claiming we're afraid to battle Toxtricity, speculating our first-round elimination this time. Too many sponsors withdrew early, leading to a significant drop in our company's stock!"

Speaking of this, the man was furious, nearly exploding: "That reprehensible guy, he only sells clothes!"

D: "... What was their sponsored trainer's rank in the previous championship?"

The man recalled: "Runner-up, I think? Hmph, just third place, nothing special!"

Being a runner-up beats making the top four, D silently critiqued.

The man glanced over at him, saying: "If you agree to represent our company in the tournament, I won't demand winning the Champion. Just eliminate that fellow's sponsored trainer; never mind Corviknight feathers, I'll get you a genuine set of Sinistea and Polteageist tea sets! All for free, not a penny from you!"

Hearing this, D's interest was piqued.

Genuine Sinistea and Polteageist sets? Those are incredibly rare!

He quite enjoys drinking tea.

Upon seeing his expression, the man knew he was interested.

With a charming smile, he asked: "What do you say?"

D: Intrigued.

Even though he's not a trainer, in crucial moments, he can be one.

Besides, Koraidon is incredibly strong; he can easily win by simply following along.

Yet, D had one concern: "Why do you believe I can definitely beat that trainer?"

"Because these two Pokemon of yours are clearly not Normal ones!"

The man had his logic: "Rare Pokemon are hard to encounter in the wild; even I've never seen them before."

"They exude mystery and strength, their eyes tell me they won't easily submit to humans like wild Pikachu on the roadside."

"With you conquering them, how could you be weak? You can't possibly tell me you're not a trainer but just a farmer growing crops, can you?"

D: "..." He actually is.

"Exactly!" The man spread his hands: "Such scenarios leave only two possibilities. One is what I just explained; the other is you're from a prestigious ancient family, entrusted with these Pokemon by elders."

"But you need to purchase Corviknight feathers externally, and not through family, so the second option is debunked, leaving only the first."

The "Great Mind" man showed a classic detective gesture: "Thus, there's only one truth! You are a truly powerful trainer!"

D: "..."

After saying that, the man awkwardly scratched his head: "Haha, sorry, I watched too much Detective Pikachu lately."

"Well? Will you help me out?" the man asked.

# 261: Chapter 258: Rising Stars Tournament

Should I agree?

D fell into deep thought.

His main purpose for coming here was to buy a batch of Corviknight Feathers.

But now, the man in front of him said that if he was willing to participate in the competition, not only could he give him the Corviknight Feathers, but he would also give him a set of genuine Sinistea and Polteageist teaware.

Ordinary Sinistea and Polteageist are not rare, but genuine ones are one in a million.

Sometimes, it's hard to find a genuine Sinistea and Polteageist even after traveling the entire region.

As a Pokemon player, D found it hard to resist the temptation of such rare Pokemon.

Sinistea and Polteageist have a unique move called "Tea Time," and coincidentally, he didn't have decent teaware at home to host a tea party.

If he brought them back to the ranch...

Images floated in D's mind of a sunny, flower-filled garden where a group of Pokemon gathered to host a tea party and share Berries.

Out of caution, D confirmed once more.

"As long as I help you participate in this competition and win, I can get the Corviknight Feather and the Sinistea and Polteageist?"

The man nodded: "That's right, I don't need you to be the Champion, just eliminate the Trainer supported by the opposing company!"

"If you're willing to compete, I'll give you the Corviknight Feather, but only if you succeed, I'll hand over the genuine Polteageist."

D's thoughts stirred, and he asked again, "Genuine Sinistea and Polteageist must be very valuable, right? Is it worth it?"

"Ha!" The man shrugged indifferently, "These are my grandfather's collected treasures from when he was young. After he passed away, he left me a bunch of these antique treasures. I don't need them, and I sold quite a few when I started my business. There are some left."

D: "..." So, this guy is actually quite a devoted grandson.

Then the man slammed the table in frustration, "I just can't swallow this! Whatever it takes to make them lose face, I'll do it!"

D: "..."

Not aiming for the Championship, just wanting to defeat the opposing company, and once he wins, he can obtain the Corviknight Feathers and Sinistea and Polteageist.

For him, there's everything to gain and nothing to lose.

Thinking of this, D nodded, "Okay, but I need to know about the opponent's strength."

The Championships in the Galar Region, like the League Competitions in other regions, require collecting eight Gym Badges to participate.

In such a competition, the top four Trainers are certainly not weak.

Seeing him agree, the man was very pleased, "That's easy, come with me! The data is in my office!"

D followed him.

While taking the elevator, the man didn't forget to belittle the opponent, "Don't worry, that guy just made it to the top four by luck, facing only weak Trainers along the way."

"And the time? When does it start?" D asked again.

Man: "The competition is this afternoon, with a total of eight Trainers participating. Your first-round opponent is that guy, and you won't need to battle others."

"This afternoon?" D was a bit surprised. It was already ten in the morning, meaning the competition would start in a few hours?

This was really like forcing someone into the ring.

The man shrugged, "So, because the time is too tight, and there wasn't time to find anyone else, I asked you for help."

Soon, the elevator stopped at the top floor.

The man returned to the office, took out a document from the drawer, and handed it over, "Here is the list of Trainers for this competition, noting the Pokemon they've used in previous matches."

D reached out to take it, and the first name he saw was Leon's.

He asked somewhat uncertainly, "Leon?"

"Yes, although Leon is the Champion, he's also taken on numerous endorsements and often participates in these commercial competitions," the man said. Seeing D's shock, he added reassuringly, "Relax, you don't have to face Leon. Look at the sixth row, the Trainer named Aoken."

Leon... D remembered that in the games, Leon was the strongest Trainer in the Galar Region before meeting Yu, undefeated.

For Leon, this should just be another regular endorsement competition, right?

D's mind was filled with thoughts, and he then reviewed the details of the Trainer named Aoken.

[Name: Aoken (Wooloo Sweater Clothing Company Representative)]

[Gender: Male]

[Number of Gym Badges: 8]

[Championship Ranking: 4]

[Pokemon Used: Rillaboom, Greedent, Drednaw, Appletun, Leafeon, Sudowoodo, Farfetch'd]

It seems... they're all Grass Type Pokemon?

After careful review, D found it feasible.

"How about it?" the man asked.

D nodded, "There shouldn't be any problem."

Koraidon knows Fire Type Moves, so Pokemon like Rillaboom and Leafeon are easily countered by Koraidon, getting knocked out instantly.

The man grinned, "That's great. Oh right, almost forgot to introduce myself. My name is Mike, what's yours?"

"D."

At this point, D suddenly remembered something, "Will they use Dynamax during the competition?"

Mike nodded, "Of course! This is the hallmark of our Galar Region! Oh, right, almost forgot, since you're not from Galar, you might not be able to Dynamax. Now, what to do..."

```

Mike frowned slightly, pacing anxiously back and forth.

In his view, a Pokemon becomes significantly stronger after Dynamaxing, so if an opponent Dynamaxes and their own Trainer can't, they'd be at a natural disadvantage.

Moreover, Dynamax Bands are controlled by the Galar League and are only given to gifted Trainers, so he couldn't get one.

Seeing Mike's demeanor, D was somewhat speechless; he paused for a moment and said, "It should be fine."

After all, there are instances where ordinary Pokemon defeat Dynamaxed Pokemon.

As it was getting late, D planned to take Koraidon out for a meal.

So, after inquiring about the match time, he left the place with Koraidon and Calyrex.

Fifteen minutes later, inside a restaurant selling fish and chips.

Looking at Koraidon devouring its food, D was a bit worried: "There shouldn't be any problem, right?"

Koraidon's demeanor often makes people forget it's a Legendary Pokemon.

Noticing D's thoughts, Koraidon confidently patted its chest: "Ah-ga-sii!" (No problem!)

No matter who the opponent is, it will surely win!

But first, it needs to fill its stomach to have enough strength to battle!

"Ga-oo..." Cyclizar was frightened by its gobbling display.

Seeing this, Calyrex showed an interested look.

Seated by the window, savoring human Sweet Red Tea elegantly, asking:

"So this is what you mentioned earlier, where Trainers command Pokemon to battle other Pokemon?"

D nodded: "Exactly. In this type of battle, Trainers direct their Pokemon's moves based on the situation, either defend or attack, using different strategies to win."

Calyrex got a rough idea of the situation and, thinking about the afternoon's match, asked: "Do you have any strategy prepared for Koraidon?"

"Ah-ga-sii?" Koraidon, hearing its name from Calyrex, paused its chewing and turned to look at D.

D thought for a bit and then said: "Kind of reckless?"

"Reckless?" Calyrex was somewhat puzzled.

D: "It's about overwhelming force, not needing tactics or strategy, just using the strongest moves to defeat the opponent."

Calyrex: "..."

Calyrex initially wanted to say this might be a bit too reckless, but then remembered using Glacial Lance and Astral Barrage directly in battle, and swallowed the comment.

"It is indeed a good strategy." Calyrex assessed, "It fits Koraidon's style well."

D laughed joyfully: "Isn't it?"

Koraidon also grew cheerful: "Ah-ga-sii!" (Yes!)

Watching the harmonious interaction between human and Pokemon, Calyrex pondered something and asked, "Currently, my steed and I count as your Pokemon, right?"

Upon hearing this, D was slightly stunned.

Truth be told, with a high-intelligence Pokemon like Calyrex, he always thought of Calyrex as a human, finding it hard to treat Calyrex as you would a Pikachu.

As for commanding it to battle… D couldn't even imagine that scene.

Perhaps Calyrex knows even better how to fight than him.

Calyrex seemed to have sensed his thoughts and gently said:

"You don't need to overthink; you brought me out from a solitary abyss, and now I simply want to offer you some Helping Hand."

"I get it." D joked: "If Koraidon gets defeated, then I'll have to rely on you."

Upon hearing this, Koraidon was somewhat displeased.

It exclaimed loudly: "Ah-ga-sii!" (Impossible!)

It would absolutely not lose!

"Hypothetically, just hypothetically, I was just throwing out an example, not seriously." D said with some resignation.

Seeing Koraidon stop eating, he took out a few napkins and handed them over: "Had enough? If so, wipe off the ring of ketchup around your mouth."

Koraidon had eaten its fill; it took the napkins with its paws, roughly wiped itself, and then let out a satisfied burp.

*

D arrived here with Koraidon and Calyrex to meet up with Mike.

"Is everything ready?" Mike asked nervously, since he casually found a Trainer and wasn't sure about the Trainer's exact strength.

D nodded cautiously.

Mike breathed a sigh of relief: "It's good that you're confident; the match is about to start. You should head to the contestant hall; your match is the first one."

"Got it." Although D hadn't participated before, he had seen quite a few anime.

Mike went to the stands to watch, while D headed towards the contestant hall.

Here, quite a few Trainers and Pokemon had already gathered.

At first, D was a bit worried that someone might recognize Calyrex.

But once there, he realized no one recognized Calyrex.

Trainers came for the competition, gathered around the hall, spread across different areas, taking care of their Pokemon soon to battle.

D glanced over and saw numerous Pokemon of various forms.

Grimmsnarl, Cinderace, Coalossal, Toxtricity, Grapploct, Falinks...

Tall, short, fat, thin, furry, bald, tame, fierce, almost all types were represented, making Calyrex and Koraidon blend in seamlessly.

---

Looking around, D didn't spot Leon in the crowd, but instead saw another familiar figure.

A dark-skinned, smiling, kind-looking young man.

Is that... Raihan?

D was quite surprised.

It suddenly dawned on him that as Leon's rival, Raihan must have come specifically to battle against Leon.

After all, this guy considers defeating Leon to be his number one priority in life.

Raihan also noticed him and looked in his direction, giving him a cheerful smile.

"Are you also a participant in the Rising Star Tournament?" Raihan came over, his gaze shifting to D's Koraidon and Calyrex.

D greeted him amicably, "Hello."

Raihan's gaze lingered on Koraidon.

As a Trainer specializing in Dragon Type Pokemon, he quickly judged that this unfamiliar Pokemon before him was indeed a powerful Dragon Type Pokemon.

D heard his name and apologized to Raihan, sidestepping him to enter the competitors' entrance passage.

"Please stay calm, the tournament is about to begin, let us welcome the two competitors who will be battling shortly!"

"First up, the Trainer sponsored and supported by Wooloo Sweater Clothing Company, Aoken!"

A cheer erupted from the audience seats.

Amidst this lively shouting, a composed and aloof-looking youth slowly walked out of the dim entrance passage onto the spotlighted stage.

Vivian: "Ah, as expected of Participant Aoken! As a top-four contestant in three consecutive Championship Cups, I believe everyone knows Aoken well, his perseverance and determination are truly inspiring!"

"Aoken! Aoken! Aoken!"

A chorus of cheers arose among Aoken's fans in the audience, rallying support for him.

Hearing the noise, Mike, who was seated in the front row, snorted coldly, "So noisy."

Ha ha ha!" At this moment, a chubby middle-aged man not far away laughed heartily, "Mike didn't get a good night's rest yesterday? Do you want to rest for a bit first, since today's Rising Star Tournament doesn't concern you anyway."

Mike replied with a sullen face, "It's not certain who will lose or win! You think your contestant will definitely secure victory? Just wait and see, Robbie."

With that said, Mike closed his mouth and said no more.

At this moment, Vivian's voice continued to come through the broadcast.

"Next up, the Trainer sponsored and supported by Corviknight Flying Taxi Company, Participant D!"

"Huh? Participant D, is he new? We've never heard of this name before!"

People in the audience showed puzzled expressions.

They all uniformly turned their gaze toward another entrance passage.

Then, they saw a robust young man with black hair emerge from it.

By his side were two Pokemon.

One was crimson all over, sturdy, its golden eyes full of pressure and intimidation.

The other one afforded a graceful posture, with its feet not touching the ground, instead using Psychic to levitate in mid-air, its head large but limbs quite slender.

More compelling were its eyes filled with unseen authority.

What are these two... Pokemon?

The audience fell silent, not a single person in the vast arena had ever seen these two mysterious Pokemon.

After a brief moment of bewilderment, Vivian quickly regained her composure as the host:

"Wow! Participant D sure is a mysterious Trainer! I wonder what kind of battle those two mysterious Pokemon will bring us. Let's wait and see!"

"The first match of the region Rising Star Tournament is now officially starting!"

The other end of the contestants' hall.

As the camera slowly moved, the lens automatically focused on those two mysterious Pokemon.

Leon looked at them on the screen with a look of surprise in his eyes.

As a Champion, his eyesight has always been very precise. Those two Pokemon, whether from their expressions or mental state, were clearly not ordinary Pokemon.

D... He had never heard of such a person in the Galar Region before.

Is this Trainer from another region?

"No matter who you are, we won't lose this match. Go, Greedent!"

D: "..." Boy, don't be so absolute in your statements!

"Agaaah!"

A mysterious power spread all around, and suddenly, the sunlight in the sky became even more intense.

"In the first battle, contestant Aoken chose to send out a Greedent, while contestant D sent out that red Pokemon! The sun has become stronger, is it because of that Pokemon?"

Vivian also noticed the change on the field, her commentary fast-paced.

In the arena, the battle had already begun.

"Covet!" Aoken calmly ordered.

As the command was given, the usually serious Greedent suddenly began acting cute, using Charm as it subtly approached Koraidon.

Facing this scene, Koraidon's eyes were puzzled, tilting its head to look: "Agaaah?"

Just then, D's voice came from behind: "Finish it quickly!"

Koraidon immediately snapped back to attention. Hearing D's words, its leg muscles powered up explosively, leaped high off the ground, transforming in mid-air, and crashed down on Greedent below.

Aoken sensed something was wrong, his heart skipped a beat, and he shouted quickly, "Danger! Dodge quickly!"

However, Greedent couldn't dodge at all.

The next second, Koraidon slammed into it, and the terrifying ancient energy immediately caused a violent explosion, sending Greedent flying.

Greedent didn't even utter a sound before collapsing and losing the ability to battle.

Instant defeat!

Robbie's face turned dark: "Damn, don't be happy too soon! The match isn't over yet!"

Before Vivian could finish, the audience in the arena saw the red Pokemon open its mouth and unleashing Flamethrower.

So this is a Dynamaxed Pokemon?

This power... resembles very much what they once faced during the Darkest Day.

Leon was ready for the upcoming battle with them.

In the audience seats, Mike was already tense with excitement, exclaiming, "Oh my God!"

Beside him, Robbie's face darkened, recalling his pre-match champagne celebration, and suddenly he flushed red with embarrassment.

```

He didn't even watch the match, just got up from his seat and turned to leave.

Once all the energy covering the battlefield dissipated.

"The match is over! Rillaboom has lost the ability to fight, and the winner of this match is Trainer D!"

A second later, Rillaboom's eyelids twitched, and then it stood up from the ground.

On the battlefield, Aoken looked in amazement at D and Calyrex as they walked toward him.

"You... what are you doing?!"

After witnessing Rillaboom's condition firsthand, Aoken nodded and released all his Pokemon.

Calyrex healed each one of them.

Once all the Pokemon had regained their ability to fight, Aoken looked at Calyrex and thanked it: "Thank you."

Calyrex smiled slightly, then retreated to D's side.

"Your strength is really impressive!" Aoken felt genuine admiration in his heart.

"Believe that one day, you can also reach such a level." D didn't know what to say, so he just said that.

Aoken immediately nodded with determination: "Yes, we will work hard too! Moving toward that peak!"

"Good luck!"

The purpose of this trip achieved, D didn't plan to continue battling. He intended to forfeit the upcoming matches and return to the Paldea Region with Sinistea and Polteageist.

But just as he was about to return to the players' lounge, Vivian suddenly ran over with a microphone.

"Trainer D, Trainer D! Please wait a moment!"

D stopped and turned his head in surprise: "What's up?"

"Thank you very much!" Vivian's eyes lit up: "Then the first question, where are you from, Trainer?"

D looked at the camera and replied: "The Paldea Region."

"The Paldea Region?" Vivian was a bit surprised: "Wow, that's really far from the Galar Region! I see, then the second question."

Saying this, she looked at Koraidon and Calyrex behind him: "It's about these two Pokemon; there's no record of them in the Pokedex. I'm sure everyone is really curious right now, wanting to know their names..."

Names? D was slightly taken aback, then instinctively looked at Calyrex.

Calyrex seemed to understand his thoughts, smiled, and gently nodded.

Seeing this, D understood, then looked back at the camera.

"This Pokemon is called Koraidon, and like me, it comes from the Paldea Region."

Koraidon happily exclaimed: "Aghh~"

Vivian was very surprised: "Wow! It seems so friendly! Not as ferocious as imagined!"

D smiled: "Koraidon is a very kind Pokemon."

"I see." Vivian nodded, then looked at the other mysterious Pokemon with a regal presence, like a true king: "And what about this one?"

D turned his head, exchanged a glance with Calyrex, and then in the arena that attracted the attention of all, announced its name to the camera:

"Calyrex, that is its name."

If he gets a chance to face him next, he will send out his ace Charizard, sparing no effort for an intense battle.

At their level, encountering such powerful adversaries is such a fortunate thing!

D is giving up the upcoming battles?

It's me, it's me, it's me! Mike, in the audience, was excited but also felt very disappointed.

Such talent, if willing to stay at their company...

Some unwilling spectators even came to like this powerful, polite Trainer just from this match where he swept his opponents.

Just like the strongest champion, Leon, everyone likes him, idolizes him, respects him, and sees him as their idol.

At this moment, the audience in the Galar Region sincerely hoped this powerful Trainer could stay and continue this rookie competition.

For the Pokemon to get enough rest, usually, after a match, they need to continue the next day.

This was a question he could answer, D smiled and said, "Next, I plan to return to the Paldea Region, back to my hometown."

The Galar people seeing him and Calyrex off, why does it feel so strange?

An hour later, at the Corviknight Taxi Headquarters.

Mike looked at the young man in front of him, feeling quite reluctant: "Really not staying?"

D smiled and shook his head: "No, there are people back home waiting for me."

Mike felt a pang of heartache.

Such talent... why isn't he a relative of mine?

Despite the heartache, this straightforward, hot-tempered uncle still handed over the promised reward to D in the end.

A large bag full of Corviknight Feathers was placed on the ground just like that.

"These feathers fell off Corviknight naturally. I don't know what you need them for, but here you go!"

Saying that, Mike took out two items from the office.

The item on the left looked like a broken teacup, with strange patterns resembling eyes and mouths.

The teacup was filled with purple liquid, almost like slime, and upon closer inspection, the liquid was slightly moving.

This is Sinistea.

The item on the right looked very much like a teapot, with colors similar to the teacup, making it clear they were from the same set.

Parts of the teapot's rim showed cracks and gaps, which were blocked by the purple liquid inside the teacup.

This is Polteageist.

Mike walked over holding a tray.

To the naked eye, Sinistea and Polteageist looked like an ordinary tea set.

But as D focused all his attention on Sinistea, the teapot lid of Polteageist was suddenly lifted by a small purple creature using its head.

D was slightly startled, turned his head, and locked eyes with the purple creature.

It was a small figure like slime, with eyes resembling patterns on coffee. At this moment, it was lifting the teapot lid, holding onto the rim with two small hands, cautiously observing the situation outside.

When it saw D, it seemed startled and quickly retracted like a hermit crab on the beach.

D: "..."

Mike also noticed this scene and burst into laughter: "These Ghost Type Pokemon are naturally timid and afraid. After taking them home, just leave them on the tea table. They'll get used to the environment in a few days."

Then he introduced them one by one:

"The cup on the left is Sinistea. Although the patterns on the cup look like eyes, the liquid inside is its real body, and even the handle is part of its body disguised!"

This was D's first time seeing a Sinistea in reality. Looking at the purple handle, he couldn't help but ask, "Can I touch it?"

Mike nodded: "Of course you can, but don't even think about drinking it like tea! If it's unfamiliar with you, it might take the chance to invade your body and drain your spiritual energy from the inside!"

"What if it's familiar with me?" D asked again.

"Even if you're familiar with it, you still shouldn't drink it," Mike shook his head. "Sinistea tastes terrible, and even people who aren't picky eaters would vomit it out right away after tasting it."

"So, most collectors keep it as a precious collectible. Real Sinistea holds incredible value among those who understand its worth!"

D roughly understood.

You can't drink Sinistea, but you can keep it at home as decorative tea ware.

Then, he looked over at Polteageist on the side: "If I'm not mistaken, Polteageist is drinkable, right?"

"That's right!" Mike continued: "Polteageist's red tea has a unique flavor and aroma. It has a special taste and fragrance, although drinking too much can cause stomach discomfort, some adventurous food enthusiasts still drink Polteageist daily."

Saying this, Mike looked disgusted: "I can't drink it; the tea inside feels like Polteageist's bathwater."

D: "..."

Well, that's a bit exaggerated, isn't it?

"Do you drink the Berry Juice that Shuckle brews in its body?" D couldn't help but ask.

Mike looked even more disgusted: "No way! That jar is full of Shuckle's sweat and slime! Who knows what other gross things might be in there!"

D felt slightly nauseated by his words and quickly said, "Alright, alright, let's change the subject!"

Mike's expression softened a bit before continuing the introduction: "There's one more thing to note; Polteageist can control the taste of its tea!"

"If you're on good terms with it and it trusts you, it might let you taste the red tea. But if you're on bad terms and try to force a taste, it'll inject its ghostly power into the tea before you drink it."

---

"Although I've never drunk it, I've heard from others that once you drink it, you'll feel a chilling coldness all over, and your body will feel weak!"

"Understood." D was enlightened internally.

He reached out and took the tray, examining the Sinistea and Polteageist on it.

The tea liquid of Sinistea had a swirling pattern, and it was hard to tell where its eyes were.

But judging from its slightly swaying body, it seemed capable of sensing its surroundings.

Polteageist, on the other hand, was much more straightforward.

It seemed to be aware that the tray had changed hands, and immediately used its head to cautiously lift the lid, creating a narrow gap to secretly observe the surroundings.

D once again locked eyes with it.

The next second, Polteageist shrank back into the teapot.

This scene reminded one of a hermit crab.

Speaking of which, the ecology of these two Pokemon really is similar to hermit crabs.

They also crawl into teapots and teacups from elsewhere.

For example, when Sinistea evolves into Polteageist, it crawls from an antique teacup into an antique teapot, and then transforms into Polteageist.

Many wild Sinistea and Polteageist blend into the piles of tableware in hotels and restaurants, living in a mimicking manner.

"Amazing." Unable to resist, D commented on this.

It's unclear whether Sinistea and Polteageist becoming such because they crawled into the teaware, or if this is simply their natural names.

So the question arises, what happens if the purple liquid accidentally crawls into the toilet?

"It is quite amazing." Mike nodded in agreement, "These antique teapots and tableware were collections my grandfather gathered from various places. Originally kept well in the warehouse, suddenly, one day, these two Pokemon appeared out of nowhere, sneaked inside, and merged with the teacup and teapot."

"The first time I went to inventory the antiques in the warehouse to sell them, I was scared by them! Later, seeing they were harmless, I let them reside inside."

"What's even more incredible is, they can survive even without eating anything at all!"

At this moment, a woman in uniform walked over with a machine in her arms.

She placed the machine on the table and said to Mike, "Boss, here's the microscope you requested!"

"Great, thank you, you can carry on now!" Mike waved his hand, then gestured to D to place the Sinistea under it.

D complied, putting Sinistea under the microscope.

Mike operated the microscope while explaining:

"The teacups used as residences by Sinistea and Polteageist are renowned antique tableware. There are many replicas on the market, but unlike real antiques, replicas lack the identifying marks on the bottom."

"This is a primary method enthusiasts use to authenticate Sinistea and Polteageist. Some collectors even prepare elaborate teacups and red tea beforehand, all in anticipation of Sinistea possessing them. Come have a look!"

D leaned forward and looked through the microscope, and sure enough, he saw a mark resembling a signature on the bottom of the cup.

This mark confirmed the authenticity of the Sinistea.

Mike hadn't hoodwinked him with a fake.

"Do you see it?" Mike asked.

D nodded.

"This is..." Mike started to say more, but suddenly noticed Polteageist, taking advantage of their distraction, using its head to lift the lid, sprouting small purple legs, and jumping off the table.

Mike was startled, quickly saying, "Polteageist's escaped with legs!"

D quickly turned to look.

Hearing this, Polteageist hastily accelerated its little legs.

The lid wobbled like a hat atop its head, blurring its vision.

Suddenly, Polteageist bumped into a furry body, the sudden contact causing its tea-like body to shake violently.

Polteageist immediately felt dizzy; once it calmed down, it noticed a figure standing before it.

Polteageist looked up, seeing a Pokemon with a large head, majestic posture, and dignified gaze looking down at it.

Startled, Polteageist quickly used "Shell Smash," its lid ringing as it sealed the teapot beneath it.

# 264: Chapter 261: Returning with a Full Harvest

"I am Calyrex, you need not be afraid."

Calyrex looked down at the teapot before him, speaking in a gentle tone.

He tried to project his goodwill toward the Pokémon residing in the teapot as much as he could.

Polteageist felt a wave of warmth and cautiously lifted the lid with its head, peeking at the unfamiliar Pokémon before it.

Calyrex gave it a slight smile.

Polteageist's eyes lit up, clearly less fearful than before.

It sensed that this Pokémon meant it no harm.

Not far away, the two observers watched this scene as Mike burst out laughing:

"Looks like Polteageist really likes Calyrex! In that case, you don't have to worry. Once we get back, let Calyrex look after them, and they'll quickly adapt to their new home!"

At this moment, Calyrex had already picked up Polteageist.

Initially, Polteageist was a bit uneasy.

D heard Calyrex say, "You shall accompany me for now!"

Polteageist paused for a moment and then happily agreed.

It wiggled its body, extended two pairs of purple legs from the bottom of the pot, and floated obediently beside Calyrex's grand crown, after being released from his hand.

It looked like a decorative teapot accessory that Calyrex had purchased with in-game currency.

D raised an eyebrow, feeling that Polteageist surprisingly suited Calyrex.

"Now, you should take out a Poke Ball and capture them!" Mike said. "However, I'm not very familiar with them, so I can't get them to obey. You'll have to catch them yourself."

D nodded; this was to be expected.

He took out two Poke Balls from his backpack and first tossed one toward the Sinistea on the table.

The Poke Ball successfully hit Sinistea, and with a click, Sinistea's small body turned into a red light, entering the Poke Ball.

The Poke Ball shook slightly a few times, then settled down. Whether this meant Sinistea had accepted him or was simply too weak to resist remained unknown.

Just like the bizarre capture methods of ancient Hisuin Region, most did not require Pokémon battles.

For example, sneaking up from behind and throwing the ball when the Pokémon is not paying attention.

Or physically fighting the Pokémon, dodging their moves, wearing them out, and quickly throwing the ball once they are exhausted.

And using various tools, including but not limited to Sticky Globs, Smoke Balls, Berries, and so on.

D pondered for a moment and then put away Sinistea's Poke Ball.

Next, he walked over to Polteageist and attempted to communicate with it.

"From now on, we're going to start a new life together. I will take you to my home; it's very safe there, and no Pokémon will harm you. I can also prepare delicious black tea for you."

Polteageist looked at him with spiral eyes, seemingly trying to judge whether his words were truthful.

At this moment, Calyrex also gently nodded, "I will also go to that place with D."

Polteageist pondered carefully for a while, and finally nodded.

It chose to trust this human and this Pokémon.

"Then please take care of me from now on, Polteageist!" D smiled gently, holding out a Poke Ball in front of the floating Polteageist.

Polteageist gave the Poke Ball a curious glance and then tentatively reached out to touch the button.

As soon as Polteageist touched the button, its body, along with the teapot, was drawn into the Poke Ball.

The button on the Poke Ball blinked twice and then stopped.

This signified the capture was successful!

Having acquired a complete set of genuine Sinistea and Polteageist teapots, D's spirits soared.

The next step was to return triumphantly to the Paldea Region!

Though he anticipated it in his heart, Mike couldn't help but extend an invitation:

"Would you consider staying in the Galar Region? If you do, I can offer you a generous annual salary."

A trainer with such skills is highly sought after anywhere, and if their company could sign someone like this,

the potential earnings were unimaginable, a perfect opportunity for their company.

D naturally understood what Mike meant; he smiled and shook his head, saying, "Thank you very much for the invitation, but I prefer my current lifestyle."

Upon hearing this, Mike knew they had missed their chance.

He felt a deep sense of regret: "Are you working with the Paldea League?"

Surely, the Paldea League wouldn't miss the opportunity to recruit such a wild trainer?

However, the next moment, Mike heard an answer that he couldn't believe.

"No." D shook his head. "In fact, I'm a rancher, running a ranch with my Pokémon."

What?!

Upon hearing this, Mike was stunned: "A rancher?" He must have misheard.

D: "Yes, it's about raising Pokémon and growing crops to sell to big cities."

Several words flashed through Mike's mind.

Rancher, raising Pokémon, farming... isn't that just a countryside farmer?

How is this possible?!

Mike was left bewildered by the wind, but looking at the Pokémon behind the young man before him, a bold guess came to mind.

---

Is there a possibility that the other party was once a powerful Champion-level Trainer, who in the long journey of a Trainer found this life too dull, so they gave up everything and returned to their hometown to farm with their Pokemon partners?

The rural reemployment of a retired Champion.

This is truly... leaving one a bit speechless!

To be able to give up fame and status, how firm must one's heart be to choose such a path!

Indeed, a Trainer with such a steadfast heart can't be swayed by shallow wealth.

You could tell from him abandoning the match at the New Star Tournament.

At this moment, Mike completely gave up the thought of persuading the other party. He could only sigh regretfully, "How wonderful it would have been if I'd met you sooner!"

D: "..."

Why did he suddenly not understand what the other person was saying?

After a moment of silence, D gave up thinking and instead began to bid farewell, "We need to go. If you have time, you can visit D Ranch in Porto Marinada Town of the Paldea Region. We will welcome you warmly then."

Paldea Region... D Ranch in Porto Marinada Town...

Mike silently etched this location in his heart.

D said no more, nodding to him before turning and heading downstairs with Koraidon and Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests.

Koraidon confidently hoisted that large bag of Corviknight Feathers.

The reason was simple, D was too weak and couldn't lift it.

Every time at moments like this, D couldn't help but feel that Koraidon was really reliable!

Before night fell, they reached Wyndon City Station.

To return to the Paldea Region, they first needed to head to Hulbury Town and transfer to a ship.

The steam trains of Galar Region were incredibly fast, taking only four hours to reach Hulbury Town Station.

By now, it was already five in the evening, just in time for the last ship heading to the Paldea Region to depart.

With Koraidon and Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests, D quickly bought the boat tickets.

As they ran to the deck, the sun was already setting, the evening glow spread across the sky, pouring onto the sea.

The ship's horn sounded melodiously, heading towards the distance as if chasing the sun.

Behind was the gradually receding Galar Region, accompanied by the ship's journey, the streets, ports, scenery, pedestrians, and lighthouse of Hulbury Town started to blur.

D stood on the deck, watching that scene. Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests and Koraidon stood beside him, together gazing towards Galar's direction.

"This journey to Galar truly brought a bountiful harvest!" D thought contentedly.

Not only did he capture the long-dreamt Glastrier and Spectrier, even Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests chose to leave with him.

Moreover, he witnessed the legendary Pokemon Zacian and Zamazenta siblings and received a complete set of authentic Sinistea and Polteageist tea sets.

Truly delightful!

However, what pleased D more was that he could finally go home, finally see everyone at the ranch again.

This time away took quite a while, he wondered how everyone at the ranch was doing.

Did any challengers come to challenge Gogoat's Grass Trial?

Has Tinkatink's hammer been crafted?

Has Naclstack evolved into a rock giant?

In winter, the ground doesn't grow pasture. Without fresh pasture, does the Miltank still eat comfortably?

Has their jointly established Pokemon Tavern had new Pokemon visitors?

Have the traveling Mew and Teddiursa sent new postcards and gifts back?

As the weather gets colder, Combee and Butterfree must have already retreated early into the warm Glass Greenhouse, right?

The answer to all this, D passionately wanted to know.

He missed everyone at the ranch, just as they missed him.

Calyrex also watched the gradually receding Galar Region.

All kinds of past events resurfaced before its eyes.

The once-prosperous Galar Land, humans offering tributes believing in it, the disaster that engulfed the entire region, the coronation ceremony at Crown Shrine.

Then its power depleted, falling into slumber, separating from its beloved horse, waking up to find itself having forgotten many things due to overusing its power, wandering alone across the vast snowy plains.

Calyrex's gaze was calm, looking into the distance like a Wise One weathered by the years.

In its early life, it experienced so much; even the harshest of times couldn't wash these memories away, just like the bricks of the Crown Shrine, retaining their freshness despite the storms.

What happened after that?

In Calyrex's mind, the past continued to appear.

One day, it suddenly felt a faint belief coming from a distant land.

At first, it was quite confused.

But as time passed, that belief grew from a small spark to a brilliant flame.

That was the sound of a devout little believer praising its name on that land.

Calyrex's expression was gentle and kindly.

It closed its eyes and then opened them again moments later.

So it is! The past cannot be pursued!

Now, it's no longer the King of Galar worshiped by humans.

From now on, it, just like the little believer, is simply an ordinary farmer.

All the kingly responsibilities faded away like a tide, at this moment, Calyrex felt an unprecedented ease.

---

It withdrew its gaze from the Galar Land, turned around, and began to look forward to the unfamiliar life of a farmer.

Paldea is its new home, but it will never forget Galar.

*

It takes three days to return to the Paldea Region from the Galar Region.

D had been rushing around during this trip, but tonight he finally had some rare free time.

He didn't wander around on the deck but instead took a package of tea provided for travelers from the ship's restaurant back to his cabin.

The night sea was very calm, with occasional ripples on the blue sea, appearing deep and mysterious under the full moon.

The cabin was not large, about twenty to thirty square meters, with a bed, a nightstand next to the bed, and a desk under the porthole for travelers to read and write.

D released all the Pokemon except Glastrier and Spectrier.

Calyrex's feet left the ground, levitating above the floor.

Cyclizar was curiously fiddling with a ship ornament.

"Aga-sis..."

Koraidon lay on the floor yawning, seemingly a bit sleepy.

Noticing this scene, D casually said, "If you're sleepy, go ahead and sleep!"

But as soon as he said this, Koraidon was no longer sleepy.

"Aga-sis~"

It energetically stood up, walked over to Calyrex, and watched D's actions with it.

D was brewing tea.

To be precise, he was brewing tea for Sinistea and Polteageist.

These two Pokemon are very magical; they can infuse their power into red tea, transforming it into a part of their bodies.

As the tea leaves steeped, the unique aroma of red tea spread throughout the room.

[Red Tea (C): Red tea brewed with lower quality tea leaves; it may taste slightly bitter.

Cooking Effect: Refreshing lv1, upon drinking, humans and Pokemon will be awakened by the bitterness, with no drowsiness.

Evaluation: Your cooking method enhanced the bitterness of the red tea, giving normal red tea the capability of coffee, a truly unexpected result.]

D: "..."

Divine Refreshing...

He originally intended to brew a cup of red tea for Sinistea and Polteageist to taste to improve their relationship.

But now, looking at the cup of red tea, he suddenly felt it might not be very suitable for them to drink.

Looking up, Polteageist was staring at the cup of red tea, and as for Sinistea... D didn't know where its eyes were.

The air was suddenly quite awkward.

D tentatively asked, "How do you find the taste?"

Sinistea floated back a bit, clearly not fond of the taste of this red tea.

While Polteageist cautiously leaned over.

Its internal red tea had been used up quite a bit in the warehouse before, now it needed to replenish some to increase its volume.

Seeing Polteageist's reaction, D was a bit surprised.

Could it be that this Polteageist prefers bitter red tea over sweet red tea?

He thought for a moment and pushed the red tea over.

Although hesitant, Polteageist still reached out from the pot, holding the cup with its small hands, and slowly poured it into the pot.

Soon, the small cup of red tea ran low.

Polteageist's originally somewhat petite body was rapidly changing.

It was integrating its power into the red tea to increase its volume!

"So amazing!" D watched this scene with great interest.

He remembered the Pokédex mentioning that red tea infused with Polteageist's power has a unique flavor and aroma, with a very distinctive taste and fragrance.

However, at that moment, the originally normal-spirited Polteageist suddenly became visibly low-spirited.

Its expression seemed downtrodden, as if it had tasted the bitterness of life.

"Phew..."

The small person of Polteageist sighed depressively, then with a bitter face stayed in the pot, laying its arms on the pot rim, supporting its almost invisible chin with its small hands, and gazing at the tabletop in a pose of deep contemplation.

However, there wasn't a thoughtful expression on its face, but one full of troubling matters, occasionally letting out a sorrowful sigh.

D: ?

Calyrex was also quite surprised, and curiously asked, "What is the reason?"

D: "…Don't know."

He tried hard to remember the relevant information about the Pokemon Polteageist.

Soon, an incredible hypothesis formed in his mind.

That is, Polteageist's temperament may change due to the flavor of the tea in the pot!

---

---

---

---

# 265: Chapter 262: Return to the Ranch (4k)

League Year 198, October 28, the ship finally arrived at the Paldea Region, docking at the port of Levincia City.

D got off the ship and hurried towards the ranch with Koraidon, who was carrying a sack of Corviknight feathers.

The Paldea Region doesn't compare to the Galar Region; trains don't cover all cities.

If you want to go out, apart from riding Pokemon, mostly, you take buses and other transportation facilities.

It's inconvenient to travel with so many things, so as soon as D left Levincia City, he released Glastrier and Spectrier from the Poke Ball.

Then he hung the sack of Corviknight feathers on Glastrier, who was stronger, while he and Calyrex respectively rode Koraidon and Spectrier, heading towards Porto Marinada Town.

They walked and stopped along the way, finally returning to Porto Marinada Town on the evening of October 31.

Looking at the familiar road leading to the ranch, D felt exceptionally excited inside.

Having left the ranch for so many days, he wondered how everyone was doing now?

He eagerly wanted to see those Pokemon, but just then, a large truck suddenly drove towards him on the road ahead.

D recognized that truck at a glance; it was Zack's truck, which he drove every day to collect goods from nearby ranches and farms.

At this time, it just happened to be five o'clock in the evening, which was Zack's collection period.

The rural road is not as wide as in big cities, so he quickly rode Koraidon to the side to let it pass.

Driving the truck, Zack also spotted a few familiar figures standing by the roadside.

He honked twice from a distance to greet them.

As he got closer, Zack stopped the truck, rolled down the window, stuck his head out, and showed a hearty smile: "Back already?"

D smiled back and replied: "Yes! The matter is settled, I can finally go home."

Thinking of D Ranch, Zack couldn't help but feel admiration:

"Your Pokemon are truly capable! While you were away, they managed to keep the ranch as neat as when you were here through mutual cooperation! It's really impressive!"

With that, Zack shifted his gaze downward, looking at Koraidon beneath D, and waved at it with a smile: "Long time no see, Koraidon!"

Koraidon also happily responded: "Aga Ssss～"

"You've grown stronger and stronger; looks like the ranch's food is really good!"

After speaking, Zack then turned his attention to the few unfamiliar Pokemon following D.

At first, he thought these were Pokemon D had just captured elsewhere, not paying much attention.

But when he saw the Pokemon riding on the horse, his face showed a stunned expression.

This familiar yet strange feeling!

Could it be...

Impossible, right?!

Zack was shocked and quickly jumped out of the truck.

"Is this Pokemon really?!"

He looked at Calyrex, who also gazed gently at this human.

Calyrex could feel that this human was also providing it with faith.

Its mouth slightly curled upward, and it nodded softly at Zack.

"Wow!" Zack couldn't help but shout.

This majestic look, this graceful posture, these loving eyes, aren't they the legendary Pokemon Calyrex?!

The legend is true!

D... actually captured Calyrex and brought it back to the Paldea Region!!!

After a brief moment of daze, Zack quickly reacted, immediately turned around, pulled D aside, and began whispering.

"What is going on here?!"

D looked innocent: "What do you mean, going on?"

Zack lowered his voice: "Isn't Calyrex the king of the Galar Region? How did you bring it here?"

Capturing the Pokemon symbolic of another region, if the Galar people find out, would they come here to seize it? If it escalates, it could trigger a war between the two regions!

Zack was very worried.

"It's not that exaggerated..." D showed an expression of speechlessness.

He recalled for a moment and suddenly realized he had previously only told Zack and others about the legend of Calyrex riding with its beloved horse across the Galar Land, bringing prosperity to humans, but not the subsequent events.

After some thought, D told Zack about the later events where Calyrex was forgotten and lost all its power.

"...There, people gradually forgot the King of Galar, the Crown Shrine turned into a ruin of broken walls, and the powerless Calyrex wandered lonely on the cold snowfield."

After hearing this, Zack couldn't help but frown and feel outraged inside: "That's too much!"

"Exactly!" D added: "When I went there, I almost froze to death on the snowfield. I really can't believe how Calyrex, a Grass Type Pokemon, survived all these years there."

"Luckily, you brought Calyrex back!" Zack no longer felt it would trigger a war between the two regions: "Since they don't cherish it, from now on, we will protect Calyrex!"

"Doesn't it need faith? Tomorrow, when I go out to collect goods, I'll spread the word for Calyrex in other towns!"

D: "..."

Great, from now on, Zack is missionary number two!

---

He stayed by Calyrex's side in service, while Zack went out to continue spreading the teachings, reviving the religion of Calyrex in this land.

However, to avoid misunderstanding innocent people, D finally said:

"Actually, it wasn't really the ordinary people of the Galar Region's fault. It was all because the Galar Royal Family, to maintain their rule, took credit for Calyrex's achievements and erased traces of His existence."

"Sigh!" Zack sighed, then added with some emotion, "It's a relief that the kingdom has long become extinct; now it's the era of the League."

"Indeed!" D agreed.

Once finished talking, the two turned around and walked back towards Calyrex, who was waiting nearby.

Zack looked at Calyrex with a gaze filled with both sympathy and respect.

But he had never spoken to such a Legendary Pokemon before. After hesitating for quite a while, he finally squeezed out a few words:

"Oh King of Bountiful Harvests, feel free to settle down in our town. We will join D in... in worshiping you!"

Calyrex nodded with a smile.

Seeing Him smile, Zack also started to smile.

But soon, he remembered he still had work left unfinished. Slapping his forehead, he ran back to the large truck.

Through the window, Zack bid farewell to D and Koraidon with Calyrex: "Almost forgot! I still need to go to work. You should also head back to the ranch soon! Dragonite misses you all!"

D nodded, watching the large truck disappear at the end of the road, then rode Koraidon in the direction of the ranch.

Soon, D saw the sign of D Ranch.

He rode Koraidon alongside Calyrex, while Glastrier followed with a sack of cargo.

"This is where we live," D said.

They passed through the ranch's main gate, as Calyrex curiously surveyed the place.

Neat rows of crops, withered flower fields, sparse and yellowing grass...

The view wasn't exactly impressive.

D felt a bit embarrassed: "Since winter is approaching, all these flowers have withered."

"I am aware." Calyrex nodded with a smile.

Looking at the orderly scene before Him, He could foresee the vibrant and fragrant sight that spring would bring.

In the ranch, a few Pokemon were listlessly lying on the grass, fiddling with weeds.

Those were Pawmo, Alcremie, Glaceon, and Naclstack among the Pokemon.

They sat with their backs to the ranch entrance, gazing idly at the blue sky.

Alcremie sighed: "Ma-ma..." (So boring!)

Naclstack were equally downcast: "Lei..." (Bored...)

Pawmo let out a gentle breath: "Ba-mo, Ba-mo." (D has been gone for a long time, I wonder when he'll be back.)

Beside them, Glaceon's mood was just as low.

Before coming to the ranch, it used to love winter, the snow-covered world, enjoying the life of playing freely in the ice and snow.

But now, it feels that life is too monotonous.

Compared to ice and snow, it prefers the fragrant flowers, running and playing with everyone on the grassy fields under the warm sun.

Thinking of this, Glaceon sighed: "Bui..." (Sigh.)

A few Pokemon looked up at the monotonous blue sky, and the next moment, they all sighed in unison.

"Sigh."

The days without D, even the sky seemed so repetitive.

"Aga..."

Seeing this scene, Koraidon was just about to say something, when D suddenly covered its mouth.

It looked puzzled, and saw D making a shushing gesture.

Though confused, Koraidon obediently closed its mouth.

D smiled, patted its head, and then tiptoed towards the Pokemon sitting with their backs to him, gazing up at the sky.

When he got close enough, he covered Pawmo's eyes with a hand: "Guess who I am."

Pawmo was startled by the sudden large hand, but upon hearing the voice, its face immediately lit up with excitement.

"Ba-mo!"

The last effect of the Translation Nuts Cake wore off, leaving D unable to understand Pokemon speech.

But it didn't matter, he could understand what his Pokemon meant, even without the Translation Nuts Cake.

D chuckled, lifted Pawmo under its armpits and hugged it: "I'm back!"

"Ba-mo~"

In Pawmo's eyes, there was a bright glow of joy and excitement. It warmly pounced into D's arms and used Nuzzle on him.

The next second, D felt a tingling sensation all over.

"Pawmo~"

Pawmot called out affectionately.

D smiled and patted its fluffy head, "Alright, alright, everything outside is settled, so now I can stay at the ranch with you all for the winter."

Hearing this, the nearby Pokemon were all especially happy.

D's return made them feel the sky was no longer so monotonous, and the whole world seemed filled with color.

Alcremie opened its arms and jumped in place, "Maa-Maa!"

D understood what it meant, so he put Pawmot down and then picked up Alcremie, "Alright, I'll hug you too."

"Maa-Maa~" Alcremie was overjoyed.

D then looked toward Glaceon, whose eyes were filled with longing, but it stood there stubbornly, not reacting.

D smiled slightly, put Alcremie down, and, ignoring Glaceon's struggling, picked it up.

Fortunately, Glaceon only made a token resistance before settling down, feeling a bit embarrassed to be held in D's arms.

"Have you been well these days?" D asked.

"Pawmo!"

"Maa-Maa!"

"Nacl!"

"Bui!"

The Pokemon all responded in unison.

D held Glaceon for a while, then put it down.

Just as he was about to take them to meet Calyrex, he noticed five Naclstack gazing at him with longing eyes, as if they also wanted a hug.

D: "..."

He decided that he would try to develop some Huge Power cooking!

If Ash can lift Nebby weighing up to 999 kg, he can definitely lift Naclstack!

At this moment, a "Baa" sound suddenly came from not far away.

D turned his head and saw other Pokemon on the ranch excitedly running toward him.

Three Gogoat, Butterfree, Dolliv, Dragonite...

The sound of their running was so loud that it attracted the attention of the Indeedee Sibling in the room.

The siblings opened the door and saw D returned to the ranch.

Their eyes lit up, and they happily came forward to greet him.

In the blink of an eye, D was surrounded by this group of Pokemon.
