He wasn't the least bit flustered, reaching out to pet each of them.

"It's indeed that familiar feeling! No wonder you're my Dragonite! This is great!"

"Poor Gogoat, you've all gotten so thin, don't worry, there's grass from a harvest a few months ago in the warehouse, I'll make you fresh Grassy Seed dumplings later."

"Dolliv, it's been a long time!"

"Butterfree, are you used to the environment in the Glass Greenhouse?"

The Pokemon responded one by one.

Dragonite hugged D enthusiastically, "Aoo~"

Gogoat nodded happily, "Baa~"

Dolliv squinted with joy, "Nyu~"

Butterfree also happily nodded, "Fui~"

Finally, D looked at the Indeedee brother and sister, smiling and thanking them, "Thank you for your hard work during this time!"

The Indeedee brother stood seriously behind his sister, like a little adult.

The Indeedee sister happily nodded, "Ai yi~"

After greeting them, D turned around and led them to Calyrex, Spectrier, and Glastrier.

Meanwhile, he released Cyclizar, Sinistea, and Polteageist from the Poke Balls.

D introduced them one by one, "This Pokemon that looks like a cup is called Sinistea, and this somewhat morose-looking Pokemon is called Polteageist."

"This totally white Pokemon here is called Glastrier, and this black one is called Spectrier, and this is Calyrex."

"From now on, these Pokemon are our new companions; they will live with us at the ranch."

The Pokemon on the ranch looked at Calyrex, blinking, and then collectively made welcoming cries.

Seeing this, a smile appeared on Calyrex's face.

He looked at the group of Pokemon in front of him, eyes full of kindness.

"I am Calyrex, like D, a farmer adept at cultivation. From now on, I ask for your guidance!"

---

---

---

---

# 266: Chapter 263: Winter Tea Party (5k)

D Ranch, seven in the evening.

Night had fallen, and after enjoying a reunion dinner with all the Pokémon, everyone got busy with their own activities.

Sister Indeedee headed to the Pokémon Tavern to attend to the Pokémon guests who visited daily.

Brother Indeedee handed over a stack of postcards and several items to D.

D glanced over and found that these were sent back by Teddiursa and Mew who were traveling.

The front of the postcards were photos, with little paw prints on the back. At first glance, they seemed to depict the scenery and Pokémon the two had encountered on their travels.

The items included a Star Piece that shone with a red glow, a heart-shaped colored scale, a peculiar bone, and a hard stone.

No doubt, these were all picked up by Teddiursa using the "Pickup" ability.

D briefly scanned them before setting them aside, intending to read them slowly as bedtime reading later.

For now, he had some tasks to attend to.

First, he needed to thaw the batch of fresh pasture harvested in autumn and turn it into Grassy Seed snacks.

The Grassy Seed snacks he made last time were all eaten by the three bleating Gogoat, so he needed to make more as soon as possible.

D mixed the pasture with the dough and then put them into the pot to steam.

As D was busy, the other Pokémon congregated in the living room, spread throughout the house.

Occasionally, when D looked up, he saw Alcremie and Pawmot climbing up and down the stairs.

Thinking about it made sense; in this season, once night falls, the temperature outside plummets. Compared to the cold and darkness outside, no Pokémon could resist a cozy indoor space with a burning fireplace, bright lights, and unlimited Berries and snacks.

D smiled, checked on the Grassy Seed snacks, and then set an alarm clock.

When the alarm sounded, it meant the Grassy Seed snacks were ready.

While waiting, D brewed a few cups of his favorite Kitakami Sweet Red Tea.

He carried the tea back to the living room, placing one cup in front of Calyrex seated on the sofa, and two more cups in front of Sinistea and Polteageist.

After drinking that bitter cup of red tea on the ship, Polteageist had been gloomy until now, without a hint of a smile on its little face.

Thus, D decided to swap the tea for Polteageist.

"Want to give it a try?" D pushed the two cups towards Sinistea and Polteageist.

The two Pokémon looked down at the tea before them.

Sinistea swayed its body and floated forward, using Giga Drain on the tea before it.

In no time, the Sweet Red Tea was emptied.

Sinistea, content, let out a satisfied burp, its expression visibly growing more cheerful.

Clearly, it enjoyed the taste of the red tea.

Meanwhile, Polteageist looked at the red tea before it, unsure of what it was thinking, and after a while, let out a soft sigh.

D: "?"

Just then, two little heads peeked out from under the table.

Pawmot and Alcremie looked innocently towards Polteageist.

Alcremie happily said, "Maa maa!" (The tea D brewed is delicious! Sweet!)

Pawmot nodded in agreement: "Bamo bamo!" (That's right, go ahead and try it!)

The little Polteageist person gazed dazedly at the two Pokémon before it.

It could feel the kindness and friendliness in the atmosphere.

This brought a touch of emotion to the hearts of Polteageist and Sinistea, who had always stayed alone in the dilapidated warehouse.

"Pol…" Polteageist swayed its head and then, as if making a firm decision, extended two slender little hands to pick up the cup of Sweet Red Tea and pour it into its body.

A moment later, Polteageist's body seemed fuller, spilling some purple tea liquid onto the table.

Polteageist felt a bit concerned.

However, D just smiled, took a cloth, and wiped the spilled tea.

"It's okay, as long as it's cleaned up quickly."

Polteageist looked at the human in front filled with kindness, and it seemed like flowers blossomed atop its head.

Its mood became cheerful and lively, and there was a joyful tone in its voice: "Pol!"

"Feeling better now?" D asked.

The Polteageist before him had completely shed its previous gloomy vibe from the ship.

Polteageist happily nodded: "Pol!"

Calyrex beside them also smiled at the scene, feeling moved and spoke:

"I never would've thought a young one's temperament could change due to the taste of a cup of tea. In all my years of living, I have never witnessed such a wondrous thing!"

"Indeed!" D nodded in agreement and then looked at Calyrex before him: "Would you like to try? Not sure if this red tea suits your taste."

Calyrex nodded gently, picked up the cup of tea, and elegantly sipped it at its lips.

After carefully tasting it for a while, Calyrex nodded slightly: "Quite delicious, I like its taste too."

"That's great," D grinned.

Looking at the elegant Calyrex before him, he couldn't help but picture a scene in his mind—underneath a warm sun, Calyrex sitting in the flower field, elegantly tasting afternoon tea.

"Hmm, if there were a slice of Green Grass Cake, it'd be perfect, Afternoon tea paired with Green Grass Cake, a quintessential match."

---

D remembered that Calyrex is also a Grass Type Pokemon, so wouldn't that mean that the Grassy Seed and Green Grass Cake he developed earlier are also effective for Calyrex?

The Grassy Seed can help Calyrex comprehend Grass Type Moves and increase the Grass Type Energy within its body.

While the Green Grass Cake can cause Calyrex's Grass Energy to mutate, allowing crops to undergo mutations during cultivation.

For example, the gigantic watermelon that was birthed earlier...

Besides that, there's also the World Tree on the farm that needs a hundred thousand years to grow.

D wondered if Calyrex's power could accelerate its growth.

D's heart was stirred, but it was already late, and these things could only be done in the future.

Next, he had a lot of work to do.

Such as building individual stables for Spectrier and Glastrier,

and finding suitable land on the farm to use the power of Calyrex and the two horses to create special Snow Field and Ghost Field there.

To grow the Shaderoot Carrot and Iceroot Carrot that Spectrier and Glastrier love to eat the most.

While pondering, the sound of the alarm clock came from the kitchen.

D quickly stepped forward to turn off the alarm, turn off the stove, and took out the steamed Grassy Seed.

He called Koraidon over and handed some of the Grassy Seed to it: "Help me deliver these Grassy Seeds to Gogoat and the others!"

"Ahgasi!"

Koraidon happily agreed, holding the basket of Grassy Seed, and went out the door.

Meanwhile, D took two plump and soft Grassy Seeds and brought them to Calyrex, asking, "Want to try? This food is specially developed by me and is very suitable for Grass Type Pokemon to eat."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex lowered its head to look at the seed placed on the white porcelain plate.

The next second, one of the Grassy Seeds emitted a blue light.

Covered in light, the Grassy Seed floated into the air and hovered before Calyrex.

Calyrex reached out to catch it, then opened its mouth and took a gentle bite.

After chewing slightly, the King of Bountiful Harvests immediately opened its eyes in disbelief.

This food... actually contains a trace of faint energy!

Was it a hallucination?

Calyrex took another taste.

No, it wasn't a hallucination.

It clearly felt a trace of faint Natural Energy merging into its body!

But, how can this be?!

Calyrex immediately cast an inquisitive glance at its little follower.

Upon seeing this, D knew that Calyrex had sensed the effect of the Grassy Seed.

He grinned and asked, "You felt it, didn't you?"

Calyrex nodded slightly: "I felt a trace of faint Natural Energy within it. This power... where does it come from? No, from your look, you've known about this all along."

D nodded: "Because this is my ability, to tap the potential of food and give them all sorts of magical effects, including but not limited to replenishing energy, healing injuries, and comprehending moves."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex was slightly startled but soon accepted it.

"Truly worthy of being my follower! To have such power, you are chosen by nature. I am truly proud of you!"

D: "..."

Isn't that a bit exaggerated?

Calyrex seemed to have misunderstood something, but he didn't know where this ability to create culinary wonders came from.

Explanations would be complicated, so D could only respond with a smile.

At this moment, Koraidon returned with the Grassy Seed.

D took the basket from its hands and went back to the kitchen to clean the dishes.

After finishing, he couldn't help but yawn.

After days of traveling, D felt a bit tired. When he glanced at the time, it was already nine o'clock in the evening.

D wanted to Sleep, but before that, he needed to arrange a place for Calyrex to stay.

After thinking for a moment, D asked, "By the way, do you have any specific requirements for your residence?"

Calyrex understood his intention and shook its head lightly: "No requirements, you can Sleep first. I want to go outside for a look and will find a place to rest later."

Since it planned to live here for a long time, Calyrex intended to take a walk outside to learn about the situation and environment near the farm.

"Alright, if there's anything, feel free to call me anytime," D said.

Calyrex nodded gladly.

Seeing this, D bid it farewell and waved to the other Pokemon:

"I'm going to Sleep, you guys can continue playing here!"

"Ahgasi!"

Resting on the carpet, Koraidon immediately followed.

D glanced around but didn't see Pawmot, guessing it might be playing with its friends on the second floor, so he gave up on the idea of taking it to Sleep. Instead, he turned and went back to his bedroom with the postcards and items sent by Teddiursa.

He placed those letters beside the bed and then went to the bathroom to wash up.

*

After D left, Calyrex's form flickered, and it appeared outside the house.

It Levitate in the night sky, overlooking the farm below, enveloped in a serene and peaceful atmosphere.

It saw Spectrier, ghost-like in form, hiding in the shadows, and Glastrier snorting on the grass;

Saw the brightly lit, bustling little house, where a group of Pokemon gathered, joyfully drinking water and chatting;

Saw transparent glass buildings full of beautiful blooming flowers, where several Bug Type Pokemon were resting with their eyes closed;

---

In the orchard, I saw those Pokemon peacefully sleeping in little houses made of wood.

And in a fenced area made of wooden stakes lived three sheep Pokemon.

At this moment, they were crowding around a feeding trough, devouring the Grassy Seeds inside.

Calyrex sensed a surprising energy wave emanating from them.

This left Him quite astonished.

Calyrex watched the three Pokemon for a while.

Before long, they seemed to have filled their bellies and, satisfied, walked over to a tender sapling, lay around it, and began to sleep.

As a Pokemon, Calyrex could see things that many humans could not.

For example, right now, He clearly saw a weak Natural Energy being released from that sapling, continuously nourishing the three sheep Pokemon, circulating among them.

"This is..."

*

In the living room.

Glaceon, Pawmot, Alcremie, Cyclizar, Dolliv, Dragonite, and Indeedee gathered around the Levitate Sinistea and Polteageist, cheering happily.

Dragonite clapped joyfully: "Awoo~" (Welcome to this place~)

Following its lead, the other Pokemon let out a chorus of Growls.

"Nyu~"

"Eevee~"

"Gao~"

"...."

Sinistea and Polteageist felt the friendliness emanating from everyone, as if they were very welcome here.

The two Pokemon were in a daze, especially Polteageist.

For a long, long time, it had lived by itself.

One day later, the teapot it once inhabited broke, prompting it to migrate in search of a new dwelling.

Eventually, it accidentally wandered into that antique-filled warehouse.

There, it found a beloved antique teapot and moved in.

One year, two years...

Over the long years, Polteageist gradually felt lonely.

To have someone accompany itself, it separated a part of its tea liquid and infused it into a matching antique cup.

Thus, Sinistea was born.

But Sinistea was in a daze too and was not very talkative.

The two Pokemon relied on each other in that dark warehouse for a long, long time until they were discovered by that human and sent to the hands of this person named D.

Initially, Polteageist was very worried about its future.

But over time, it watched as that human personally brewed tea to replenish its tea liquid. Though it was a bit bitter, it still drank it.

The bitter tea made its inner self bitter as well, and affected by the tea liquid, it had been immersed in loneliness alone for days.

Perhaps it was too apparent, that human surprisingly noticed and even brewed a new sweet and delicious black tea.

After drinking it, Polteageist immediately felt the bitterness of the past few days vanish, the world seeming brighter, even its body feeling warm.

Now, not only were their bodies warm, but after feeling the kindness and friendliness released by everyone, even their hearts felt warm.

After a brief moment of daze, Polteageist and Sinistea let out joyous Growls.

"Polte~!"

It gently shook its body and floated in mid-air, musical notes representing joyful emotions popped out from above its head.

A magical power lingered around, and the gathered Pokemon below looked up at the happily floating Polteageist, their eyes lighting up.

Then somehow, they found a Berry, held onto it, and under the lead of the hopping Polteageist, began to eat joyfully.

In the room, D had just finished bathing when he heard the lively Pokemon Growls from outside, thinking they were fighting, so he opened the door to check.

But before he got close, he saw a group of Pokemon seated by a warm fireplace, taking out Berries and holding a tea party.

Seeing this beautiful scene, D was slightly taken aback.

Was that a... tea party?

A tea party, Polteageist's signature move.

It is said this move is only used when Polteageist is happy, inviting other Pokemon to hold a tea party to enjoy Berries and gourmet food.

"What a wonderful move!"

D stood in place, unable to help but admire.

He didn't step forward to disturb them, knowing this was the Pokemon's tea party.

"Ah ga sse?"

At this moment, Koraidon ran out of the room.

D looked at it and said softly, "You should join them too!"

Koraidon gazed over, then licked D, and ran toward the fireplace to gather with its Pokemon companions.

D smiled slightly, and after leaving Koraidon to join the tea party, he turned back to the room, sitting by the bed.

D quietly listened to the joyful laughter of the Pokemon outside, then took out the postcards sent by Mew and Teddiursa to examine them one by one.

There were four photos in total; the first photo showed a group of Clefairy dancing around a bonfire, with a round moon overhead, seemingly performing some ceremony.

While Teddiursa held a Star Piece, poking half its head out from the lens, appearing happy to have found the Star Piece.

"Clefairy from Mt. Moon?" D was a bit surprised.

He picked up the Star Piece that Teddiursa had sent back and looked at the Star Piece in Teddiursa's hand in the photo.

Undoubtedly, the Star Piece in the photo had already been sent back by Teddiursa.

The second photo depicted endless rolling mountains.

"Could it be the famous Mt. Silver Range?" D couldn't help but speculate.

Have Teddiursa and Mew gone so far?

Just wondering if Red might be at the top of Mt. Silver.

The third photo showed mother and child Cubone and Marowak, each holding a bone.

The young Cubone reached out to gift a bone as a present to Teddiursa.

Seeing this scene, D speculated, combining Mew's previous help to Teddiursa:

"Mew and Teddiursa must have helped this Marowak mother and child, so Cubone gifted its cherished bone to them."

The fourth photo shows a shimmering lake.

On the lake's surface, a beautiful Pokemon moved gently under the sunlight.

Is that... Milotic?!

This is the Pokemon hailed as the most beautiful in the world!

Coming to this world, D has seen Milotic for the first time. Even through the photo, he could feel the Milotic's charm.

The scales shimmering with rainbow-like light under the sun are truly beautiful!

---

---

---

---

# 267: Chapter 264: Dye Berry, World Tree (5k)

Early the next morning, with the arrival of dawn, the ranch welcomed a brand new day.

The first thing D did upon waking up was to look for the whereabouts of Calyrex to see where it slept last night.

Living room... not there.

Second floor... not there.

Kitchen... also not there.

D searched for a long time but couldn't find it, so he returned to the living room.

On the coffee table, the Polteageist and Sinistea from last night's tea party were snoozing on top, and Cyclizar was also dozing off by the fireplace.

Even big brother Indeedee looked drowsy.

Seeing this, D said helplessly, "If you're sleepy, go take a nap. I'll handle all the ranch work today!"

Big brother Indeedee shook its head vigorously to wake itself up, quickly saying, "In...deedee!"

It could still hold on!

Alright! D nodded, turned, and prepared to check out the wine cellar below, but there was still no sign of Calyrex.

It seemed that Calyrex must have spent the night outside yesterday.

After a brief thought, D decided to check outside.

But when he returned to the living room, he saw big brother Indeedee dozing off again.

Little sister Indeedee covered her mouth and giggled from the side.

D: "..."

Just then, a Butterfree landed in front of the large floor-to-ceiling window next to the living room.

D instructed, "Butterfree, use Sleep Powder on big brother Indeedee!"

"Bzzzz?!"

Butterfree was suddenly startled, but after confirming D wasn't joking, it flew to the sleepy big brother Indeedee and fanned out the Shield Dust that could induce sleep.

Soon, big brother Indeedee fell asleep.

"Alright, little sister, take your brother upstairs to rest!" D exhaled a sigh of relief.

"In...deedee~"

Little sister Indeedee eagerly agreed, using Psychic to take her brother back to his room.

Pawmo, Alcremie, and Glaceon were also sleeping.

D didn't disturb them and turned to take Koraidon outside the ranch.

Although it hadn't snowed yet this season, the temperature had already dropped below ten degrees. In this environment, some cold-fearing Pokemon rarely ventured outside to play.

As soon as D and Koraidon stepped out the door, they saw Calyrex stroking its beloved horse on a spacious lawn.

Hearing the commotion, Calyrex turned its head and looked warmly at its little follower: "Moo crown chirp!"

D: "..."

He almost forgot he had eaten all the Translation Nuts Cake, so now he couldn't understand Calyrex anymore.

"There are still some sunflowers harvested from last autumn at home, I'll have to make another batch of Translation Nuts Cake later," thought D, feeling it was quite troublesome, "It's a pity there's no dish that allows direct communication with Pokemon."

The power to let humans and Pokemon communicate is extremely rare in this world.

Aura, Power of Viridian, Triumphant Light...

And dishes with such effects must be of very high grade and quality, which he probably couldn't make yet.

After a brief thought, D greeted Calyrex, "Good morning! Did you sleep well last night?"

Calyrex nodded slightly: "Crown chirp!"

Beside it, the ill-tempered Glastrier hoofed the ground discontentedly and snorted twice.

Even without translation, D could tell what Glastrier meant.

It was hungry and wanted to eat the Iceroot Carrots from the Crown Tundra.

On the other hand, Spectrier showed no reaction, standing silently with a serene gaze.

Seeing this, D brought a few Berries over and placed them in front of the two horses.

They first sniffed at them half-heartedly, but perhaps due to hunger, they eventually lowered their heads and started eating.

"Looks like Spectrier and Glastrier don't really like regular Berries..." D thought.

This means I need to move up the plan to create a special field.

"Wait for me a bit, I need to milk and feed the Gogoat first, then I'll come and plant carrots for you," D said to the two horses.

Finally, Glastrier settled down and began eating the Berries tastelessly.

D nodded to Calyrex and then carried a milk bucket with Koraidon to the Gogoat pen.

In the pen, the three Gogoat were already energetically waiting.

They obediently bent down for D to milk them and then went on to eat their favorite Grassy Seed.

Calyrex watched the scene from a distance.

About the three sheep Pokemon and that mysterious tree, it had a lot to say.

But it seemed its little follower couldn't understand it now...

Calyrex's eyes showed a sign of confusion.

Before coming here, it thought this was just an ordinary ranch, but now it seemed otherwise.

Everything here was filled with secrets.

After finishing milking the three goats, D went to the Chicken House to collect the eggs laid by the hens last night.

Then, he and Koraidon went to the Pokemon House to move the MooMoo Fresh Milk collected by the automatic milker from the Miltank to the warehouse, placing it with the goat milk, waiting for Zack to come and pick it up in the afternoon.

During this time, the two Dragonite and five Dragonair from Dragonite Island came over to start work.

Seeing D return, they happily ran over to greet him.

D greeted each of them and then watched as the two working Dragonite took the goat milk from little sister Indeedee and flew to town for delivery.

---

The five Dragonair were eagerly following D around.

Wherever D went, they'd follow.

D regarded them as though he had bought five Dragonair pendants.

It's winter now, and the pastures' fields are temporarily idle, no need for watering.

So, D took the Corviknight Feathers he got from the Galar Region to the Berry Garden located in the southern part of the ranch.

In a month's time, Tinkatink had crafted its first hammer.

However, this hammer seemed a bit rough.

When D arrived, Tinkatink was under a fruit tree, inspecting its hammer.

The Greedent was perched on a branch, gnawing on a Berry.

Not far away, in the forest, Maushold offspring, who had grown quite a bit, were playing hide and seek amidst the thick fallen leaves.

The addition of these wild Pokémon brought a lot of vitality to the Berry Garden.

Tinkatink frowned, looking very dissatisfied with its creation.

D approached and curiously asked, "Is it finished?"

Upon hearing the voice, Tinkatink and the Greedent up above both looked up to see D approaching.

Their eyes lit up, and then Tinkatink lifted the rough hammer for D to see: "Waaah..."

D inspected it closely and, unsurprisingly, found that the quality of the hammer was rather poor.

There were even rusty spots on it...

Undoubtedly, it was because of the poor quality of the materials Tinkatink used.

Tinkatink knew this as well; if only it had better metal.

But it had already scoured the area many times, finding no metal suitable for making a hammer.

Without a hammer, Tinkatink lived each day without a sense of security.

Thinking of this, its mood inevitably fell a little: "Waaah..."

Just then, D smiled and pulled out a box of Corviknight Feathers from behind: "Ta-da! Look what this is?"

Tinkatink was slightly stunned, looking down to see the box full of dark, metallic feathers gleaming.

This, this, this is?!

Tinkatink's eyes widened in disbelief.

Isn't this the rare metal that only the mightiest Tinkaton in their tribe could find?

In its previous home, there were several Tinkaton, who wielded immense strength and often swung their hammers at flying Corviknight.

When those Corviknight were struck, they would drop some metal feathers resembling steel.

The Tinkaton said that using this metal to craft a hammer could smash anything.

Thus, owning a Corviknight Hammer became a dream for every Tinkatink in the tribe.

"It's for you," D said.

Tinkatink was utterly shocked: "Waaah?!"

"That's right," D patiently explained, "These feathers are a gift I brought back for you on this trip, and you can use them to forge a hammer that's uniquely yours."

"Waaah..."

Hearing this, tears welled up in Tinkatink's eyes, its heart filled with emotion.

This human named D... is so wonderful!

Not only did he aid them and provide food and shelter, but now he's also gifted it a box of precious Corviknight Feathers!

Tinkatink was so moved it almost cried, biting its lip, teary-eyed: "Waaah!"

Never had anyone treated it so kindly!

D was a bit puzzled: "...Alright, alright, it's just a few feathers, as long as they're useful to you. Also, once you've completed your hammer, I have a favor to ask: to craft a suitable hammer for Pawmot."

Hearing this, Tinkatink vigorously wiped its tears and nodded earnestly.

It was determined to craft the best hammer in the world for D using these feathers!

With the Corviknight Feathers, Tinkatink bid farewell to Greedent and D and went off to train in seclusion.

D, touched by Tinkatink's serious demeanor, smiled reflectively, indeed, there are many diligent Pokémon in this world!

However, when he looked up, he saw the Greedent, round and plump from eating, clearly fatter than before.

D: "..."

Alright, he took back what he said earlier, not all Pokémon are that diligent.

The Pokémon in the Berry Garden remained as they were.

D strolled around, greeted Pokémon like Maushold, Surskit, Hoppip, and Shroomish, and checked on their wooden houses for any damage.

When it was Slakoth's turn, Slakoth was lazily lying on the thick fallen leaves, munching on a leaf.

At first, D didn't feel anything was amiss until Koraidon let out a puzzled growl beside him.

"Argh?"

"What's up?" D turned his head, following Koraidon's gaze to the Slakoth.

The next moment, realization dawned on him.

The fur color of this Slakoth had turned light purple!

Light purple... isn't this the color of a shiny Slakoth?

No way! He remembered clearly this Slakoth was an ordinary one.

After a brief moment of shock, D stepped forward to check Slakoth's condition.

He felt around Slakoth's fur and soon found a spot where the fur was shorter than elsewhere.

---

This is a place that was once scorched bare by a great fire.

This also means that the Slakoth remains the same, not replaced.

But... why did it suddenly change color?

Could it be that the Grafaiai dyed its fur?

With doubt in his heart, D found the Grafaiai, who was scribbling on the trunk of a Berry tree, and asked, "Grafaiai, do you know what's going on with the Slakoth's fur?"

"Zee zee?" Grafaiai was a little confused at first, but quickly responded, "Zee zee!"

It stopped its graffiti and led D to Slakoth's side, searched around the canopy, then reached out and pointed to a Berry.

D looked up and saw a pink fruit he's never seen before hanging quietly on the branch.

[Dye Berry: A special ingredient, when consumed, a Pokémon's color will change accordingly. Can be eaten directly or cooked before consumption.]

D was startled.

Did the Berry tree produce mutated Berries?

Upon reflection, when this orchard was still growing, the Gogoat came every day to aid their growth with Grass Energy.

Dye Berry, does this mean it could create Shiny Pokémon?!

If given to Koraidon, it would turn black, and if given to Pawmot, it would become an ultra-rare pink Pawmot...

D imagined what a pink Pawmot would look like.

Even cuter...

A bit moved, he quickly searched the nearby fruit trees carefully.

Unfortunately, it seemed that only two Dye Berries were produced this batch.

One of them had already been eaten by Slakoth as food.

D picked and kept the remaining Dye Berry.

At this moment, his sister Indeedee had prepared breakfast and called him back to eat.

D responded and bid farewell to Grafaiai and Slakoth before heading home for the meal.

At the dining table, D glanced around, pondering whom to use this Dye Berry on.

"Before using it, I should ask the Pokémon how they feel." D thought silently.

In this world, some Shiny Pokémon are marginalized and discriminated against as outcasts by their own kind.

"Should I use it on Koraidon?"

If he really suggested this idea, Koraidon likely wouldn't refuse him, and with its strength, it probably wouldn't care about such matters.

But there's only one Dye Berry, and once used, it can't be undone. Who knows when the next one might grow on the tree.

So it must be used wisely.

After a brief thought, D decided to keep the Dye Berry stored until he discussed it with the Pokémon.

After breakfast, D began roasting Translation Nuts, finally adding syrup to make nut brittles.

When everything was finished, it was already 10 AM.

D ate a few pieces of the nut brittle, then went to the ranch to find Calyrex to discuss creating special fields.

"In half a month, it will snow, and the weather will be too cold for crops to grow, so I don't plan to plant other crops until spring arrives."

Calyrex realized its little follower could understand it again.

"Why?" It asked, puzzled. "With my power, no matter how cold the snowfield, crops can grow as usual."

D: "...Okay, actually I want to take a break."

Calyrex was slightly taken aback. "A break?"

D replied honestly, "Yes, I've been busy from spring to autumn. If I farm in winter too, it would be a year-round rush. I don't want such a hectic life; a proper rest is necessary."

Calyrex nodded slightly. "I see, that kind of life indeed feels exhausting."

D smiled slightly, deciding to reserve this plot for seasonal crops and to open a separate field for the two horses' carrots.

Thus, he tapped the hole beside the farm that the Diglett had dug out.

Soon, the Diglett in the Underground Cave heard the noise from above, crawled out along the tunnel, and peeked at D, "Zee zee?"

"I have something to ask of you, help me open up two more plots of land behind this area!" D said.

"Zee zee!" The Diglett expressed its understanding, immediately burrowing underground, reaching the area D indicated, and diligently cultivating the land.

In no time, two neat plots of land took shape under Diglett's efforts.

Calyrex watched this in surprise, unable to help but exclaim, "You are indeed welcomed by these little ones!"

After finishing its work, the Diglett returned excitedly, and D, while feeding it the food in his hand, said:

"I provide them with food and shelter, and they stay here to help me. It's a mutually dependent relationship, I suppose?"

"More than that." Calyrex said, "I can sense that these children genuinely like you."

D smiled slightly, then took carrot seeds prepared in advance from Koraidon's hands and planted them in the two plots.

Once he finished this task, Calyrex mounted Glastrier, using its spiritual power to control the Ice Type Energy on Glastrier, injecting it into the plot on the left.

In an instant, the edge of that plot was covered with a thick layer of frost.

The Snow Field was complete.

Then, with a flash, Calyrex used Teleport to switch to Spectrier.

Vast spiritual power infused the Ghost Type Energy from Spectrier into the ground.

The previously brown earth was soon covered with a layer of dusky purple at a visible speed. Wisps of purple mist churned in the soil, looking dreamlike.

The Ghost Field was complete as well.

Watching this scene, Calyrex began the final step.

The enormous bud on its head bloomed with blue light, and soon, the two fields before it erupted with a strong blue glow.

Surrounded by light, the carrot seeds buried deep in the soil quickly broke through the earth.

When the light faded, both fields were filled with Shadyroot and Iceroot Carrots.

The Diglett watching this scene from the side widened its eyes in shock, revealing a dumbfounded expression.

"Zee zee!"

Glastrier snorted impatiently, seeing the grown carrots, immediately rushed forward eagerly, lowering its head to feast in the field.

Meanwhile, Spectrier glanced at the small Diglett before slowly walking to the Ghost Field, leisurely eating its favorite Shadyroot Carrot.

Watching this scene, D inexplicably felt a sense of accomplishment welling up inside.

"Alright, now I can plant carrots for them anytime in the future!"

Calyrex nodded slightly.

Taking this opportunity, it inquired with its little follower about that tree.

Calyrex: "Actually, beyond this matter, there's another thing that concerns me more."

D was a bit surprised, "What?"

Calyrex turned around, casting its gaze towards the distant Gogoat, and the budding seedling they were nurturing with Grassy Terrain.

"Where did that seedling come from? I feel an incredibly strong life force within it, as if the vitality of the entire nature is laid out before me."

---

---

---

---

# 268: Chapter 265: Seizing Every Minute

Following Calyrex's gaze, in a vast grassy plain, a tender seedling stood between heaven and earth.

It didn't lose its color due to the cold winter, nor did it break under strong winds and heavy rain.

[World Tree Seedling: A seedling that has absorbed a significant amount of the Power of Grass. Due to Gogoat, it underwent a mutation during growth, nurturing a vast Life Force, and finally broke through the soil. It will take another 99995.5 years to reach maturity.]

"That's the World Tree," D replied.

"World Tree?" Calyrex was slightly startled and then slowly shook its head, "I have never heard of such a thing."

The two walked forward, coming to the seedling.

Seeing D, the three Gogoat taking care of the World Tree seedling using Grassy Terrain raised their heads.

"Baa ah~" (The tree is growing well~) one of the Gogoat said happily.

"Thank you for your hard work!" D reached out and patted its head, then began to tell Calyrex about the World Tree.

"As far as I know, somewhere in this world, there exists a matured World Tree, to be precise, it's called the Tree of Beginning."

Located in the Kanto Region, the story of Sir Aaron and Lucario happened on that tree.

"Tree of Beginning..." Calyrex murmured the name.

D nodded, "It is said that the tree existed at the dawn of the world, living in symbiosis with a Mew. The tree is home to many ancient Pokemon and is guarded by a legendary giant Pokemon."

"As for the origin of this tree... well, it's complicated." D fell into thought, considering how to explain the origin of the World Tree seedling to Calyrex.

Calyrex gazed at the seedling with eyes filled with majesty and compassion.

Unable to resist its curiosity, it used Future Sight on the seedling.

The next moment, a blue aura enveloped its eyes.

Through the distant time and space, Calyrex saw visions of the future.

By then, the seedling had grown into an enormous tree, larger than a pasture.

The place seemed unable to contain its presence anymore.

With a rumbling of the earth, the tree rose into the sky with a piece of land, and upon reaching a certain height, it mysteriously floated there.

In the hazy aura, Calyrex saw many Pokemon attracted by this World Tree.

A mysterious bird Pokemon with fiery gold and red feathers, so vibrant and splendid that under the sunlight, they radiated like a rainbow. Wherever it flew, a beautiful rainbow appeared.

A mysterious dragon-type Pokemon with a pitch-black, gigantic body, exuding an overwhelming aura that swept across the sky like a dragon, then coiled at the top of the World Tree.

And a humanoid Pokemon with strong spiritual power, a large tail behind it. It turned to look around, and then Calyrex saw a pink cat-like Pokemon and a little flying bear chasing each other as they flew past the humanoid Pokemon.

The blue aura gradually faded, and the duration of Future Sight ended, preventing Calyrex from seeing more.

At this time, D had thought about how to explain the origin of this tree to Calyrex.

To understand this matter, one must first learn about the effect of the dessert he made, the Green Grass Cake.

D organized his words in his heart, then slowly began to speak:

"In the past, I once used my ability to make a cake, which had a magical effect."

"That is, when eaten by Grass Type Pokemon, it could cause a certain probability of inducing some magical changes when they used their Grass Energy to cultivate plants."

"Green Grass Cake?" Calyrex thought of the previous Grassy Seed, "Is it also a food made using grass?"

"That's right," D nodded, "I'll make one for you to taste in the afternoon."

"I look forward to it," Calyrex said with a gentle smile.

D continued, "This World Tree was originally three ordinary Miracle Seeds. Do you know what Miracle Seeds are?"

Calyrex had not heard of it.

D explained, "They are seeds that contain magical power. When held by a Pokemon, they enhance the power of Grass Type Moves."

Calyrex, being a very wise Pokemon, combined with these words, quickly understood what happened next.

Calyrex turned its gaze to the three Gogoat, "Then, those three seeds were planted in the soil, and the Gogoat, after eating the cake, used Grass Energy to nurture them, causing the seeds to break through the soil and become the World Tree."

"Am I right?" Calyrex asked with a smile.

"Exactly!" D nodded, internally awed.

Worthy of once being a King, ruling an entire region! Calyrex could foresee future disasters and quickly come up with perfect response strategies, with wisdom and foresight that surpassed ordinary human kings by far!

"I now know the origin of this tree, but I can sense that it is still in its infancy and will need a long time to mature," Calyrex added.

---

One hundred thousand years... A number emerged in D's mind.

If this World Tree were allowed to grow freely, by that time, he might already be dust returning to dust, earth returning to earth.

At this moment, a sudden glimmer erupted in Calyrex's eyes beside him, and slightly excited, it said:

"In that case, let me help it grow!"

After speaking, a powerful aura burst forth from Calyrex.

The former King simultaneously raised both his left and right hands.

Strong energy stirred up a gust of wind; humans within it would not be harmed, instead feeling as if they were bathed in a spring breeze.

D looked towards Calyrex.

At this moment, the energy released by Calyrex had already transformed into a green light ball. With Calyrex at the center, it began to expand outward as Calyrex waved its right hand.

A warm spring breeze blew, and the originally wilted grass due to the season suddenly transformed into a lush green scene.

Tender and vibrant little yellow flowers bloomed across the grass.

Until that light ball enveloped the World Tree, it finally stopped expanding.

Then, Calyrex waved its left hand again.

The next moment, the long unchanged World Tree began to grow at a visibly accelerated pace.

Small branches sprouted tender green leaves; the originally fragile-looking trunk grew increasingly upright and became much sturdier compared to before.

One minute, two minutes, three minutes...

D's gaze turned towards the World Tree and saw the numbers above it quickly decreasing.

[World Tree Seedling: A seedling absorbing massive Power of Grass, mutated due to Gogoat, where immense life force brews within, ultimately breaking through the earth, now needs 99,989 years to mature.]

[99,970 years.]

[99,885 years.]

[99,600 years.]

...

[98,567 years.]

[97,995 years.]

Eventually, the numbers stopped at 97,995 years.

"Suddenly reduced by 2,000 years!" D was genuinely shocked.

This means, within his lifetime, he could really witness the World Tree fully grown!

Calyrex helped him conquer time!

The surrounding anomalies gradually faded, and Calyrex, looking weary, lowered its hands.

D quickly asked, "How do you feel?"

Calyrex's expression couldn't hide its exhaustion: "The life level of this World Tree far exceeds my understanding. With my power, this is all I can do. The energy consumed needs a month to recover."

Hearing this, D couldn't help but feel somewhat guilty: "If it weren't for me, you wouldn't have to be so tired..."

However, Calyrex shook its head, a glimmer in its eyes, and said:

"No need to be remorseful; this is my own choice. Being able to participate is our honor!"

"Calyrex..." D felt deeply moved.

He decided, later on, he would prepare something nice to nourish Calyrex!

Weary Calyrex now wanted to rest a bit.

So, D led it to the Glass Greenhouse.

The heating in the Glass Greenhouse was quite sufficient, with flower racks filled with vibrant blooming flowers all around, and the room was permeated with a sweet fragrance.

Before winter arrived, D had already arranged everything inside the Glass Greenhouse.

On the left side against the wall stood a bookshelf, filled with various books.

Below the bookshelf was a lazy sofa for resting and a wooden coffee table.

Further inside, at the center surrounded by flower racks, stood an iron tea table.

Calyrex sat by the tea table, and D brought it a cup of red tea and some small sweets to revive energy, letting it rest here.

Calyrex turned its gaze towards the green vines hanging beside and the blooming flowers, couldn't help but marvel:

"Human wisdom astounds me; I never thought even without my power, they could make flowers and grass bloom in this season."

D sat beside the tea table chatting with Calyrex: "Indeed! The world keeps changing incessantly; who could have imagined thousands of years ago humans and Pokemon would develop the kind of relationship they have now?"

"Indeed!" Calyrex took a sip of Sweet Red Tea.

Bathed in this warm environment, surrounded by floral scents, it felt its body and mind involuntarily relaxing.

The Dragonairs followed D into the greenhouse.

They coiled on the sofa, showing their white bellies, seemingly very fond of this warm place.

Looking at this scene, D calculated in his mind the time for the World Tree to fully grow.

One time, Calyrex could shorten its growth period by 2,000 years but would need to rest for a month afterward.

One month, 2,000 years, and 100,000 years would need 50 months.

And with a year having 12 months, it means even with Calyrex's Helping Hand, the World Tree would still need about four years to fully mature.

Four years...

D was already satisfied with this number.

After all, with the original timeline, he might be dead before he could see the World Tree grow big.

After a while, Calyrex seemed to have fallen asleep in this warm and comfortable environment, indeed easy to become sleepy. The five Dragonairs also slept crookedly, in disorder.

D did not disturb them, rising quietly and leaving the Glass Greenhouse.

---

He returned to the house and checked the household supplies,

finding that during his absence, some seasonings and ingredients had run out.

So, taking this opportunity, D called upon Cyclizar and rode it to Porto Marinada Town.

Due to the temperature, the people of Porto Marinada Town were already wearing warm winter clothes. Although it hadn't snowed yet, some who feared the cold even had their scarves on.

After replenishing the household supplies from the store, D went to the town's seed shop.

Seed Shop Owner Elo greeted him with a cheerful smile: "Hi D, it's been so long since I've seen you!"

D smiled and replied, "I was out for a bit recently, just got back."

"I see!" Elo said in realization, "So, take a look, what seeds would you like to buy this time?"

"Carrot seeds, do you have them here?" D glanced around, searching the shelves.

Now with the Snow Field and Ghost Field, he didn't have to worry about food problems for Glastrier and Spectrier.

Just plant normal carrot seeds in them, and they would soon grow into Iceroot and Shaderoot Carrots that the two horses love to eat.

Besides that, D remembered that Calyrex said it wanted to eat carrots.

Luckily the ranch still had plenty of normal fields where he could plant some for Calyrex and later cook them into a dish to see if carrots had any magical effects.

Upon hearing his needs, Elo replied quickly, "Yes, yes, we have them!"

He left the counter to help retrieve the goods, expressing his gratitude to D while picking them up:

"Thanks to you, my daughter Bell now dares to leave her room!"

D was a bit surprised; he recalled the girl who was afraid to go out, worried that something outside would hurt her.

He remembered suggesting giving Bell a harmless Eevee Egg so she could raise a Pokemon from a young age, and thus wouldn't be afraid.

"How is she now?" D asked curiously.

Elo grinned and explained, "At first, Bell was very worried. But after Eevee hatched, the situation gradually improved."

"Earlier when Eevee accidentally ran outside, Bell mustered the courage to leave the house to find Eevee. Speaking of which, Eevee played a huge role in Bell overcoming her fear of being harmed!"

"That's great." D laughed; he could almost imagine the situation at the time.

A girl who developed paranoia about being harmed after a Pokemon incident was eventually healed by a Pokemon.

Such a perfect ending.

After paying for the seeds, D bid farewell to Elo and headed to the small cabin Keri had built on the forest edge.

When he arrived there, Keri was inside drawing house design plans.

Upon hearing movement behind her, Keri turned and said in surprise, "What a rare guest!"

D: "..."

Keri smirked and stood up to inquire, "What would you like to buy this time?"

D replied concisely, "Stables, I want to build two spacious stables."

Keri was quite surprised: "Have you caught a horse-type Pokemon? Which one is it? Mudsdale or Rapidash?"

D smiled mysteriously, "Neither!"

Seeing this, Keri couldn't help but frown: "Couldn't be Slowbro, right? Oh, I almost forgot, Slowbro has been changed to Slowbro by the League."

D said speechlessly, "Slowbro can't be ridden at all, can it? As for which horse, you'll find out at the ranch."

"Alright." Seeing his secretive demeanor, Keri didn't ask further. She turned to take out stable designs, asking, "Come and see, what kind of stable do you want to build?"

D stepped forward to check.

The design had two sheets.

The first sheet showed a connected stable with a large space inside, enough for several horse-type Pokemon to live.

The second sheet showed an individual small house-like stable suitable for a single horse.

Keri explained, "The first design is for horse farms where many horse-type Pokemon are generally raised."

Upon hearing this, D immediately rejected the first design in his mind: "Let's not go with the first one."

Because Glastrier and Spectrier's relationship was neither close nor distant, and one was quiet while the other was restless, they weren't suited to live together.

"Then the second one." Keri continued, "This kind of stable has a larger inside space, covered in a thick layer of hay, ideal for one horse."

D was satisfied, so he nodded: "Then the second one, I want two."

"Two, a total of two horses?" Keri asked in surprise.

D nodded.

"Alright." Keri was merely a bit surprised; she didn't ask much: "In that case, shall I visit your ranch in the afternoon?"

"Sure."

The two agreed to meet in the afternoon, and then D rode Cyclizar back to the ranch.

---

---

---

---

# 269: Chapter 266: Flash, Stable, Afternoon Tea (5k)

After returning to the ranch, Calyrex and Dragonair still hadn't woken up.

Taking advantage of this time, D tidied up the fields at home and planted the carrot seeds he had bought.

Seeing that the Iceroot Carrots and Shaderoot Carrots in the Snow Field and Ghost Field had been mostly eaten by Glastrier and Spectrier, he went into the field and planted some more carrots.

This time, there was no need for Calyrex's power to accelerate growth; they could just grow slowly here.

After everything was done, it was already twelve noon.

His sister Indeedee had already prepared a hot lunch.

Though it was chilly outside, it was very warm inside the room, with the fire in the fireplace burning brightly. As soon as D stepped in, he sighed contentedly.

As he changed into his cotton slippers, he surveyed the room.

The sleepy Indeedee and Pawmot, who had slept through the morning, were now awake and standing somewhat sheepishly in the living room.

"Why are you standing there? Come over and eat!" D said nonchalantly, pretending not to see them, and waved them over.

A group of Pokémon immediately cheered, stepping forward to their respective dining tables.

While eating, D glanced at Pawmot and couldn't help but think of the cute image of a pink Pawmot, so he asked:

"Pawmot, do you want to change your fur color?"

"Pamo?!" (What?!)

On hearing this, Pawmot was taken aback.

This brought back memories of a midnight movie D had watched not long ago, along with Glaceon, Alcremie, and others.

The movie's protagonists were a few Pikachu.

They originally lived happily together in a forest, but one day, an evil demon came to the forest.

The demon was a Pokémon that had been harmed by humans; before it died, all its fur was stripped off by villains.

Thus, its resentment turned into a demon, wandering the earth, and upon seeing beautifully furred Pokémon, it would capture them and ask if they had seen its fur.

Regardless of the answer, the demon would mercilessly strip the victim Pokémon of its fur, leaving them bloody and horrific!

Even the Pikachus couldn't escape the demon's clutches and all lost their fur!

To this day, recalling that movie still made Pawmot break into a cold sweat.

And now, D was asking it the same question? Could it be that D had turned into a demon as well?

Pawmot: =͟͟͞͞=͟͟͞͞(●⁰ꈊ⁰●|||)

D noticed Pawmot's shocked expression and asked skeptically, "...What's with that expression?"

So, following this, D heard from the Pokémon about the midnight demon that stripped furry Pokémon of their fur.

"That's terrible!"

After hearing the story, D slammed the table and stood up.

Pawmot and Cyclizar huddled together, nodding repeatedly, "Pamo!" (That's right!)

"No way! I need to report this! This isn't a movie; it's a Pokémon snuff film!"

D promptly made this decision, planning to stay up late to conduct a live capture and see just how extreme the movie was.

Pawmot and the others were very frightened, and even Dragonite shivered at the thought.

Having not seen the movie, Cyclizar was utterly confused, while Koraidon displayed an expression of speechlessness.

"Agass..."

Seeing the Pokémon so scared, D said, "Don't worry, it's just a movie. When I mentioned changing your fur color, I didn't mean stripping off your fur, but rather changing its color."

Hearing this, Pawmot was a bit doubtful, "Pamo?"

D asked, "Do you want to try it?"

Seeing D's expectant expression, Pawmot thought for a moment, then found itself a bit curious.

It nodded, deciding to give it a try.

"Great." Just as lunch was about to wrap up, D took out the Dye Berry and placed it in front of Pawmot.

Staring at the berry, which exuded a fragrant aroma and resembled a Berry, Pawmot opened its mouth wide and took a bite.

As soon as it swallowed the whole Dye Berry, its body color began to change.

Starting from an orange hue, it transformed into a cute pink.

Seeing this transformation, the watching Pokémon gasped in surprise, with both admiration and delight.

Pawmot looked down at its new self, feeling a bit unaccustomed initially.

But soon, it became giddy with everyone's bright eyes and envious looks, standing on the sofa shyly.

"Ma-ma..."

Alcremie's eyes shone brightly; in this household, it loved pink things the most!

The pink Pawmot looked like delicious fresh cream!

It also wanted to become that color!

Alcremie immediately turned to D.

D recalled the color of shiny Alcremie, which was like coal black...

That color just didn't suit a Pokémon like Alcremie at all.

"But if you eat that berry, you'll turn black." D said honestly.

Alcremie instantly felt disappointed, deciding against it!

D comforted, "Your current color is beautiful, like a creamy light pink."

Alcremie felt comforted, happy, and no longer wanted to change to another color.

Meanwhile, Dragonite, looking at the pink Pawmot, couldn't help but think of the pink Dragonair on Dragonite Island.

---

D seems to really like this color... If he turned this color too, would D not be attracted away by other Dragonite?

Thinking of this, Dragonite immediately leaned in front of D, looking at him eagerly: "Oh!"

The Translation Nuts Cake's words had run out, but seeing Dragonite's expression, D immediately guessed its meaning.

"... You want to change your color too?"

Dragonite nodded repeatedly, happily: "Oh!"

A green, plump image appeared in D's mind, and for a moment he didn't know what to say.

After thinking carefully, he started to explain to the Pokémon about Shiny Pokémon knowledge.

"Shiny Pokémon are the most special existence among all Pokémon species."

"It's extremely rare to encounter one, possibly only one out of thousands or even tens of thousands of Pokémon."

"As its name suggests, a Shiny Pokémon has a body color that greatly differs from others in its tribe."

"So far, you should have seen a Shiny Pokémon, it's the pink Dragonair that visits the ranch every day."

Upon hearing this, the Pokémon immediately recalled the five Dragonites that come to play every day, one of which is pink, different from the others.

D continued: "Different species of Shiny Pokémon also have different body colors, for example, Shiny Pawmot is pink, whereas Shiny Alcremie is black."

"Oh?" Dragonite eagerly asked, what color is its Shiny form?

D picked up a piece of green vegetable, pointing at the color on it and said: "Green."

"Oh!" Ugly!

Dragonite let out a disdainful growl, immediately losing interest in changing colors.

"Indeedee is dark green, Psyduck is blue, Cyclizar is gray-white, and Koraidon is black."

D's glance swept across the group of Pokémon in front of him, introducing the Shiny colors of the ranch Pokémon one by one.

Finally, he asked: "Understood?"

The Pokémon nodded collectively.

D smiled, continuing: "Those berries grow from the fruit trees in the berry garden, and maybe we can get more of them in the future. Then, if you want to experience it, you can eat them."

"Just one can turn you into a Shiny, and if you eat another, you'll change back to your original appearance, so don't worry about not being able to change back."

*

In the afternoon, Keri drove her car over to help D build the stable.

Upon arriving at the ranch, she saw a pink Pawmot with a handsome cape flying around the ranch.

Keri was puzzled: "Why has Pawmot turned pink?"

"It ate a berry called Dye Berry, which can change a Pokémon's color," D explained with a smile.

"I see!"

At this moment, Pawmot heard someone calling its name and flew over happily.

Upon seeing the pink Pawmot, Keri's eyes lit up instantly.

She felt her girlish heart being touched and couldn't help but reach out to Pawmot.

But quickly, she realized what she was doing and stopped, apologizing to D: "Sorry, I've never seen a Pawmot like this before."

D grinned, holding Pawmot in his arms and squeezing its little paw pads.

Pawmot made a charming noise: "Pa-mo~"

"Cute, isn't it?" D asked.

"Yes." Keri swallowed: "Very cute."

Pawmot used Growl, and poor Keri's attack power decreased.

D thought that with such a cute appearance, it might provide a significant advantage in Pokémon battles.

Anyone seeing Pawmot for the first time would likely think it's an adorable, harmless-looking Pokémon.

But in fact, Pawmot could swing a hammer with fire and lightning to knock opponents flying.

Tinkatink was already busy making Pawmot's hammer, and it wouldn't take long to complete.

Corviknight's feathers have good electrical conductivity.

By then, Pawmot could evolve into its complete form and become a true "Thunder God."

D released Pawmot: "Go play!"

Pawmot flew away.

Keri's gaze followed the cute Pawmot until she remembered the real business at hand.

She looked around and asked: "Have you chosen the stable's location yet?"

D nodded, took her to the side of the house, and pointed at the empty space: "Let's build it here!"

Building it next to the house would be convenient for going out later.

Right now, he had too many Pokémon mounts, and he didn't know which one to choose each time he went out.

"Okay."

Keri said no more, she stepped forward to measure the distance, then had Machamp unload tools and wood from the car, starting to hammer and construct.

The construction of the stable was relatively small, taking about a few hours to complete.

D looked around but couldn't find traces of Spectrier and Glastrier.

Taking advantage of this time, he strolled around the ranch and eventually found the two horses near the berry garden.

They were lingering by the river, lowering their heads to drink the clear water.

At that moment, Calyrex suddenly appeared beside the two horses, accompanying them in drinking water.

---

Seeing that Calyrex had woken up, D said, "I didn't want to disturb you while you were sleeping, so I prepared food just for you."

Calyrex smiled and said, "Crown!"

D: "..."

Oh dear, he couldn't understand what Calyrex was saying again.

It's not great to keep snacking on seeds forever. If only Calyrex could use Telepathy!

Unfortunately, it couldn't.

D took a deep breath, and suddenly thought of Calyrex's type.

It's well-known that Calyrex is a Grass + Psychic Type Pokemon.

And the ability of Telepathy is something even some Normal Psychic Pokemon can master.

So the question is, can Calyrex learn Telepathy?

With that thought, D immediately looked at Calyrex.

Seeing the little follower looking at him, Calyrex's face showed a puzzled expression.

D: "Calyrex, have you heard of the ability called Telepathy?"

Telepathy? Calyrex looked confused, shaking its head to indicate it hadn't heard of it.

"It means communicating heart-to-heart without talking directly or needing someone else as a mouthpiece."

Recalling the Pokemon in anime that can use Telepathy, D speculated on its principles:

"Essentially, it's using Psychic abilities to transmit your thoughts into another's brain, and then using Psychic power to sense their thoughts, enabling conversation without speaking."

Psychic is indeed a magical ability.

If anyone masters this ability well, it would be Mewtwo.

In anime, it used Psychic powers to delve into human brains to explore memories, implant false memories, and even erase parts of human memories.

Upon hearing this, Calyrex fell into deep thought.

It opened its mouth, as if to say something, but then closed it again.

Heart-to-heart communication?

Until now, Calyrex had never tried this method, nor heard of using it to communicate with humans.

Previously, when needing to talk with humans, it would often communicate its intentions through their bodies.

But now…

Calyrex closed its eyes, carefully sensing, gradually exploring the method D described.

It harnessed its Psychic powers, incorporating its thoughts, and transmitted them into D's mind.

The next moment, D heard Calyrex's voice in his heart—

"Is it like this?"

D's eyes lit up, he nodded quickly: "That's right!"

Calyrex, truly a wise Pokemon, learned it right away!

"I comprehend now… Incredible… I never imagined communicating with humans in such a way! Now I understand!" Calyrex expressed through Telepathy.

Seeing Calyrex had learned Telepathy, D felt relieved.

His inflamed mouth finally could get some rest.

Then, D started to talk business: "I just hired people from the town to build stables for Spectrier and Glastrier at the ranch, give them a place to live, check later if it's suitable."

"Okay." Calyrex nodded slightly, lovingly looking at its two cherished horses, and said gently: "My beloved horses, having lived upon the cold snowy plains, find this place more habitable. I can sense they quite like your ranch."

With these words, D looked at Spectrier and Glastrier.

At this stage, his relationship with Glastrier was significantly better.

This is because Glastrier loves the Iceroot Carrot he grows and was indifferent to being ridden by him.

As for Spectrier…

D raised his head to see Spectrier staring deeply at him.

Their gazes locked for a moment, D smiled amicably and said: "Almost forgot, I'm baking Green Grass Cake at home! I'll go get it, you can wait for me in the Glass Greenhouse!"

Calyrex nodded kindly.

D turned back to the house, retrieving the baked Green Grass Cake from the oven. Looking down, the cake's details surfaced in his mind.

[Green Grass Cake (A): A cake made with ripe summer grasses, flour, MooMoo Fresh Milk, and Alcremie's cream.

Cooking Effect: Flower Trick, having a small chance to cause slight mutations in crops tended with Grass Type Energy by Grass Type Pokemon.

Evaluation: Perfectly blends flavors of different ingredients, greatly appreciated by Grass Type Pokemon, who may thank you with surprises after eating it.]

D carried the cake, taking along the tea and utensils to the Glass Greenhouse, surprised to find Sinistea and Polteageist followed him inside.

Upon opening the door, Calyrex was already seated at the ironwork tea table.

D placed the Green Grass Cake on the table, preparing to brew two cups of tea, when Sinistea and Polteageist floated to the tea table.

Curious, D asked: "You want some too?"

"Polt!"

Polteageist shook its head, the little figure inside the pot smiling happily, loving this place and feeling bliss from the tea D brewed.

Hence, it wanted to share its carefully blended tea with Calyrex and D!

Polteageist circled the teacups on the table.

Soon, a clear, fragrant purple tea was poured into the cups.

When the cups were full, Polteageist stopped and joyfully spun among the flowers, inviting with delight: "Polt!"

Seeing this, Calyrex hesitated briefly but soon understood.

It explained to D: "This child is inviting us to taste its tea."

D was equally surprised.

Because he knew well what the sharing of tea from Polteageist meant.

Every tea enthusiast knows that Polteageist's tea possesses a unique taste and aroma, exceptionally rare and precious.

Generally, Polteageist wouldn't share its tea liquid with others.

If one attempts to drink it forcefully, Polteageist would angrily spray its tea-like body into their mouth.

This tea, filled with negative emotions, would make any human shiver with cold.

But now, Polteageist was genuinely inviting them to savor the tea.

This meant that this Polteageist had begun to trust him and Calyrex!

# 270: Chapter 267: Moon Dew Rose, The King's Beloved Steed

Inside the greenhouse, warm like spring.

Under Polteageist's expectant gaze, D and Calyrex picked up the teacup in front of them, focusing their attention and holding their breaths as they began to taste.

The purple liquid in the cup swayed gently, and the tea inside was so clear and bright, like a pool of purple clear spring water.

D bowed his head, sniffed first, and immediately caught a unique tea fragrance that was refreshing and delightful.

He raised the cup to his lips and took a gentle sip. The next second, he was astounded by the tea's deliciousness.

It was like traveling to the gourmet shows he had watched before, drinking this tea truly made one feel as if they were in a dreamland!

D's eyes lit up, his mind couldn't help but ponder the possibility of using Polteageist as a special version of Shuckle that excels at brewing Berry Juice.

Although Polteageist cannot directly turn tea leaves into tea like Shuckle, its power can enhance the quality and flavor of the tea.

Thinking of this, D looked up, wanting to know Calyrex's opinion on this kind of tea.

At this moment, Calyrex was elegantly holding a cup, sipping amidst the flowers.

It closed its eyes, moving slowly, as if carefully savoring the taste of the tea.

After a long while, Calyrex opened its eyes and looked at the waiting Polteageist with a gentle gaze.

"Your tea is the finest in the world."

Polteageist's little eyes lit up, then looked at D.

D also smiled and nodded, "I've never tasted such delicious tea."

Upon hearing this, Polteageist excitedly spun around, and Sinistea, feeling its emotion, also started swaying behind.

D and Calyrex smiled, watching this scene.

Then, D pushed the slice of Green Grass Cake towards Calyrex.

"Want to give it a try?"

Calyrex glanced down and saw a pastry emitting a strong grassy aroma and a sweet scent placed in front of it.

D demonstrated how to use the knife and fork for it.

Calyrex understood and gracefully used a silver knife to cut a small piece of the Green Grass Cake, then used a fork to lift it into its mouth to savor.

As soon as the cake entered its mouth, Calyrex felt a strong grassy aroma, and the cream and caramel's sweetness wiped away the original bitterness of the grass, creating a very unique taste.

Coupled with the strands of Grass Type Energy mixed in, Calyrex felt that no Grass Type Pokemon could resist such a dessert.

It genuinely liked it, even more than the orange carrot its followers offered long ago.

"This is the most delicious dessert I've ever eaten." Calyrex praised.

Seeing this, D immediately laughed, "That's good."

However, Calyrex seemed to have thought of something and asked, "You previously mentioned that the Green Grass Cake can cause changes in crops when consumed. Does that mean I possess such an ability?"

D thought carefully and replied, "In theory, yes."

He glanced again at the effects of the Green Grass Cake, seeing no restrictions for Legendary Pokemon, he nodded affirmatively, "Correct."

Calyrex's eyes slightly brightened, feeling curious, "Is it effective on these flowers too?"

D nodded again, "Yes."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex said, "I wish to give it a try."

D was slightly taken aback, "Won't that overly exhaust your energy?"

Calyrex shook its head, "My power comes from faith. I can feel my faith continuously flowing on this land. Additionally, now with the companion of my steed, I was able to recover within a month."

"The energy required to make the flowers bloom and to tend to the World Tree is entirely different. Now I only need a thought to turn the whole pasture into verdant grass and blooming flowers."

D understood, Calyrex meant that tending to the World Tree consumes 90% of its power, while making flowers bloom and crops mature in a small area only consumes 0.5% of its energy.

"Then shall we give it a try?" D was also a bit curious.

Calyrex gave a gentle nod with a warm gaze.

It floated from the wrought iron tea table into the air, facing the flowers that hadn't fully bloomed yet, and gently waved its right hand.

The next second, a magical scene unfolded.

In the vast greenhouse, regardless of whether they were planted in pots or hung on flower racks, all the flowers and plants amazingly bloomed beautifully.

Seeing this, Calyrex put down its hand and turned to ask, "Is this sufficient?"

"I need to take a look," D said, then began searching among the flower racks.

His gaze swept over each blooming flower.

Roses… not yet.

Penny… not yet.

Pansies… not yet.

After wandering around, D finally found a flower that had mutated.

It was a rose with a color as pure and gentle as moonlight.

[Moon Dew Rose: A rose that evolved under the nourishment of Grass Energy, originally an ordinary red rose, now possesses the power of the moon. When made into a dish, it can impart the effect of a Moon Stone to the dish.]

Seeing D pause, Calyrex, Polteageist, and the Pokemon like Butterfree and Combee living in the greenhouse all flew over.

Calyrex looked at the rose and immediately identified it as the transformed flower.

"Is it this one?" Calyrex asked.

D nodded, "It transformed from a regular red rose into a Moon Dew Rose. Once made into a dish, the dish will also have magical effects."

---

Rose cake, flower cake, rose jam... Various dishes that could be made with roses immediately popped into D's mind.

As for the Moon Stone, it should have the same effect as those that evolve certain Pokemon, right?

Unfortunately, there isn't a Pokemon at the ranch that can use it right now.

"This is truly amazing!" Calyrex looked at its hand.

It never imagined that it could possess such power.

At this moment, Keri's voice came from outside.

"D? Are you in the greenhouse? The stable's finished, bring your two horses over to take a look!"

"Looks like the stable's finished," D said.

"A home for beloved horses... I shall go with you as well," Calyrex said.

D nodded and turned to lead Calyrex outside.

Not far away, two Machamps were loading construction tools and leftover wood onto a truck, while Keri inspected the stable for any issues.

Seeing D come out, she looked up and said, "It's ready, come take a look!"

However, the next second, Keri saw the Pokemon hovering beside D.

That is...

Fragments of memories flashed through Keri's mind.

D Ranch had grown a giant watermelon, inviting townspeople to taste it, and he showed everyone a statue called The King of Bountiful Harvests.

During the Autumn Gratitude Festival, Mayor Thomas had crafted a massive statue of The King of Bountiful Harvests, placing it in the Festival Plaza.

Additionally, her home was adorned with several handmade wooden carvings, each personally crafted by her, and then sold to the townspeople.

That familiar large head, that long royal cloak, that majestic gaze, and that slender, graceful posture.

That Pokemon...

It was Calyrex!

It was as if a loud thunderbolt exploded in her mind; Keri's eyes widened, staring in disbelief at Calyrex.

"Oh my gosh!" she exclaimed.

A long time ago, she had heard the legend of The King of Bountiful Harvests from D, and now, this legendary Pokemon had come to D's ranch!

Did D capture it?

"Keri?" Seeing Keri in a daze, D stepped forward and called her.

Keri snapped out of it, and at that moment, she noticed Calyrex looking at her, even giving her a kindly smile!

"Ca...Calyrex?" Keri couldn't help but ask out loud.

Calyrex nodded slightly and spoke through telepathy: "Indeed, I am Calyrex."

"Oh my gosh!" Keri was even more shocked, unsure as she looked at D: "Is it Calyrex talking to me?"

D: "...Yes, calm down Keri, there's no need to be so surprised."

Keri finally realized she was a bit overly shocked and quickly responded, gaining her composure.

D explained: "From now on, Calyrex will be living at my ranch permanently."

It must be true that D captured it!

D actually captured a legendary Pokemon!

Keri was internally shaken, but then she remembered D's recent travels: "Did you go away recently to find Calyrex?"

D nodded: "Yes."

Just then, at Calyrex's call, Spectrier and Glastrier came running over from two different directions.

Keri looked at the two Horse-type Pokemon she had never seen before and, after a pause, asked: "Are these the Horse-type Pokemon you brought back?"

The running Spectrier and Glastrier came to a standstill beside Calyrex, directing their gaze at the only unfamiliar human present.

D watched the scene and replied: "Yes, these two are Calyrex's beloved horses; they came to the ranch together with Calyrex."

Calyrex's beloved horses...

Keri was stunned.

D calmly stepped forward, examining the luxurious separate stables in front of him, while explaining to Keri:

"The left one is for Spectrier and the right one is for Glastrier, both have been with Calyrex for a long time. In Galar legends, Calyrex once rode them across the Galar Land."

"The legendary king's beloved horses..." Keri felt a bit dreamy.

No wonder she had never seen these two horses before; they were Calyrex's royal steeds!

After the initial shock, Keri gradually calmed down.

She glanced at the snorting Glastrier and Spectrier a few times, then began introducing these two stables to D.

"These are the largest stables available, perfect for Glastrier and Spectrier. The wooden walls below shield from wind and rain, and the back can be lined with soft hay as beds for them."

D nodded, hay... fortunately, the ranch had a haystack, harvested during a past autumn with Koraidon.

Keri continued: "At the front are troughs for food and water. Simply place food and water inside, and these Horse-type Pokemon can just reach their heads out to eat, it's quite convenient."

"Alright, I got it; let's leave it like this for now." D was very satisfied with the two stables.

Hopefully, Spectrier and Glastrier wouldn't dislike them, given they used to dwell in the cold, dilapidated Crown Shrine.

Comparatively, the stable could be considered exceedingly warm and comfortable.

The ranch is so vast, they can roam during the day, munch on carrots, and when night falls, they can return to the stable, sleeping on the soft, warm stacks of hay.

---

"Well, if there's nothing else, we'll take our leave. Elif Cherry Garden ordered a wine rack from my shop yesterday; I need to deliver it to him," Keri said.

"Alright." D nodded, watching Keri's figure depart from the ranch.

It was getting dark.

D pushed a wheelbarrow and, together with Koraidon, arrived in front of the haystack and used a hay fork to load a cart full of hay to spread in the stable.

Next, he collected some water and poured it into the trough.

Finally, he placed a heap of Berries and the few remaining Iceroot Carrots and Shaderoot Carrots into the feed trough.

With this, the stable for Spectrier and Glastrier was completely finished.

"Want to try going in? Choose a room you like."

D held the hay fork and stood alongside Koraidon and Calyrex, looking at the two horses beside them.

Upon hearing this, Spectrier and Glastrier silently exchanged glances before stepping forward and each choosing a stable to enter.

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised. He initially thought the two horses would have a disagreement over temperament and fight, but they each chose their own in a very harmonious manner.

Upon reflection, the two horses had known each other for several thousand years, so they must have already adjusted to each other's temperaments.

The dusk was dim; in the stable, Spectrier and Glastrier stood in front of the feed trough, lowering their heads to nibble the Berries and carrots inside.

Calyrex looked at this scene with gentle eyes and said to D, "They really like the place you've prepared for them."

"As long as they like it."
