D smiled slightly, gazing at the sunset, unable to help letting out a long breath of relief.

Finally settling Spectrier and Glastrier, now he could stay at home and enjoy life!

It's already November; in a few weeks, it should start snowing.

By that time, the ranch would be covered in white snow, and he planned to build snowmen and have snowball fights with the Pokemon on the ranch.

For now, he needed to go back and prepare dinner!

D returned to the Glass Greenhouse to pick that Moon Dew Rose, intending to try making Flower Cake for the Pokemon to taste.

But before he could enter the house, a purple vortex suddenly appeared over the lake on the ranch.

The Gate of Space-Time slowly opened, and Calyrex immediately sensed something amiss.

Its expression suddenly turned solemn, and in a dignified tone, it asked, "Who dares trespass?"

D already knew who it was; he shook his head at Calyrex and said, "It's fine, just a... Pokemon living in another dimension."

Calyrex gradually relaxed.

D then recalled when the emotions of Giratina were reversed, and it entrusted him to grow some flowers in the Reversed World.

Now that Calyrex was at the ranch, the question arose: with Calyrex's power, could the seeds planted in the Reversed World grow quickly?

"This requires a test," D thought to himself.

But the normal Giratina was still too dangerous, and its temperament was irritable, making negotiations difficult. If he were to discuss it, he'd first have to reverse its temperament.

Just as well, there was still some unused Water of the Reversed World left in the fridge.

D first placed some 'offerings' into the lake's mirror-like surface, resolving to bring the Reversing Cuisine to that ruined world tomorrow.

And along the way, see if he could turn Giratina's domain into his own vegetable garden and back garden, since the ranch's lake connects to that place, making visits convenient.

---

---

---

---

# 271: Chapter 268: Blossoming and Bearing Fruit

The process of making flower cake is not complicated, but there's only one Moon Dew Rose, and there are so many Pokemon in the ranch that it won't be enough for everyone.

To ensure every Pokemon gets a piece, D went to the Glass Greenhouse to pick a whole basin full of flowers; the greenhouse almost went bare from his picking.

This made Butterfree and several Combee watch with tears almost falling, eagerly eyeing the petals being picked off.

D: "..."

Next to him, Calyrex watched the scene with a smile. It gently raised its hand, and colored flowers bloomed once again on the bare stems.

Butterfree and Combee were immediately drawn to this scene, gazing with admiration at Calyrex.

"Fwee!" Amazing!

"Buzz!"

In response to Combee and Butterfree's remarks, Calyrex remained still, elegantly seated at the wrought iron tea table. Amidst the admiring glances of the Pokemon, it calmly lifted a cup of tea to its lips and took a gentle sip.

This demeanor made Butterfree and Combee admire this impressive Pokemon even more, capable of making flowers bloom.

They flew beside Calyrex, happily vocalizing.

Seeing this, D breathed a sigh of relief.

It's great to have Calyrex around.

He nodded at Calyrex, then carried the flowers back to the kitchen.

"Aiiee?"

Sister Indeedee curiously looked at the flowers in the basket, approaching to ask D if he needed help.

D shook his head and said, "I can handle it myself. It's my first time making this dessert, so I'm not sure if the method is correct. I need to explore a bit first."

To make the flower cake, one needs to first prepare the rose jam.

However, the roses must ferment in a container for a month before they can turn into jam.

This means there's temporarily no way to make flower cake today.

After pondering for a moment, D decided to start with the rose jam.

He removed and cleaned the petals, left them aside to dry, then added white sugar, gently kneading them with the sugar until they became sticky.

Next, he placed them into transparent glass jars, poured a layer of honey on top to seal them, and finally sealed the jars, waiting for the petals inside to ferment.

D made three jars of rose jam in a row.

As he looked down, the information about these rose jams surfaced in his mind.

[Unfinished Rose Jam: Made from freshly bloomed rose petals, needs another 30 days to fully ferment.]

"As expected, just the way I remember, the fermentation time is thirty days," D murmured.

He placed the jams in a cool, dark corner of the kitchen to let them ferment naturally.

Then, D took out the frozen Water of the Reversed World from the fridge.

He thawed the ice cubes, then boiled them in a pot, poured the boiling water into a teapot, and began to brew tea.

There are three types in the reversed cooking series: heart, body, and skill.

And to calm down the temper of the irritable Giratina, the heart is needed.

After brewing three cups of tea in succession, D finally obtained what he wanted.

[Reversed Red Tea·Heart (S): A red tea made with river water from the Reversed World combined with tea leaves.

Cooking Effect: Reversing the Universe, after consumption, the inner nature of humans and Pokemon will be reversed, with the kind turning dark, the dark turning kind, the timid becoming brave, and the selfish becoming generous.

Evaluation: Drink cautiously!]

"Next, I just have to have Giratina drink it mixed among the offerings tomorrow."

D planned his next steps in his heart.

First, he needs to reverse Giratina's personality, and then take Calyrex and Koraidon into the ruined world.

Afterward, he would search for suitable farming land, plant seeds there, and use Calyrex's power to make them grow.

If the seeds can grow, it means the world can support agriculture.

And the task Giratina previously commissioned him with could be completed.

Transforming a destroyed world into a new world full of floral scent and lush green grass is a fulfilling experience for a farmer.

*

Early the next morning, after finishing the daily chores at the ranch, D informed Calyrex of his plans.

"Yesterday, that Pokemon lived in another world, a world full of floating islands, devoid of life, a shadow world brimming with the aura of destruction."

"Previously, I accidentally stumbled into there and met the Pokemon named Giratina. It loves flowers and commissioned me to plant some there."

"But that world is peculiar. It's on the backside of the world we live in, full of shadows, with no sun or earth, so we need to see if seeds can grow there first."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex nodded slightly and readily agreed: "I understand, I am glad to help with this task."

Even though the aura it felt yesterday was fierce and powerful, a Pokemon that loves flowers isn't a bad Pokemon.

Seeing Calyrex's agreement, D was happy and said, "Then I'll leave it to you."

Being cautious, D did not immediately enter the Reversed World.

He still vividly remembers when Giratina bombarded him for ten minutes straight in the past.

---

Therefore, D first brought an offering mixed with Reversed Red Tea to the lake by the pasture.

Perhaps it was because Giratina was watching him from another world, as soon as D arrived, a purple vortex-like portal appeared on the lake surface automatically.

D threw the offering in and waited for a moment by the shore.

After a while, a voice suddenly came from the vortex:

"My friend, you may come in."

The reversal was successful!

D stood up, nodded to Calyrex and Koraidon, then rode on Koraidon, with Calyrex following behind, and jumped into the purple vortex in front of them.

They were in the ruined spaces of Floating Islands.

A large Pokémon with golden pupils, its body covered in sacred pure white with blue stripes, was slowly levitating in the void.

"Nice to meet you." The reversed Giratina revealed a 'grin' on its face.

D: "..."

He was still somewhat unaccustomed to this friendly Giratina, pondered for a moment, then spoke:

"Regarding the flowers you entrusted me to plant here before, I think I've found a way."

"Oh? Really?" Giratina's tone revealed a hint of surprise: "Do I need to do anything?"

D shook his head, thinking it would be fine as long as it didn't attack them, leaving the rest to them.

After receiving Giratina's permission, D rode Koraidon, took Calyrex, and arrived on a small fragment of Sky Island.

And Giratina followed suit, its massive body floating on the small sky island, calmly and curiously staring down at D's actions.

"What are you planning to do? Are you going to dig like you did outside?" Giratina asked.

"...It's called farming," D explained while observing the soil below.

The soil on this sky island was reddish-brown and somewhat hard, clearly unsuitable for farming.

He used a hoe to break it a couple of times, only to find that the soil turned into chunks, impossible to plant.

Thinking briefly, D decided to borrow the power of Pokémon and asked Koraidon to fetch Diglett from the ranch.

"Ah gahs~" Koraidon happily complied.

It flew back to the ranch at top speed, and soon returned with Diglett.

"Squeak?!"

Upon seeing Giratina, Diglett was startled by its intimidating aura, quickly hiding behind Koraidon, trembling.

"Ah gahs..."

Koraidon pushed it out speechlessly.

D explained: "Don't worry, it's fine. I brought you here to ask you to help cultivate the land."

Upon hearing this, Diglett timidly looked at Giratina,

"Squeak..."

Seeing that Giratina truly showed no signs of attacking, Diglett's heart finally settled a bit.

Under the gaze of a group of powerhouses, it bravely drilled into the inside of the sky island, starting to cultivate the land as usual.

Following Diglett's actions, the soil on the sky island quickly became visibly looser.

"Now we can start planting," D thought to himself.

He only came to verify if this place could be used for planting, so he didn't bring many seeds, just a few leftover Berry cores.

However, just as he was about to bury the Berry seeds in the soil, Giratina's aura behind him suddenly changed drastically.

D turned around and saw Giratina's eyes had turned blood red, the stripes on its body reverted to its original black and red color.

Oh no, the time for the Reversed Red Tea has expired!

D's heart skipped a beat, worried that Giratina might get angry.

However, although Giratina emitted a ferocious and irritable aura, it neither spoke nor shouted angrily.

When D looked at it, it pretended nothing had happened, awkwardly saying things it would normally never say:

"My friend, why are you looking at me like that? Quickly, plant the seeds!"

D: "?"

Is Giratina pretending that the effect hasn't expired?!

D was a bit perplexed, wondering what this creature was up to.

Giratina naturally knew about the things this human did to it.

Even though its temperament was altered, it was still itself, retaining all memories from then.

The first time it was reversed and revealed its true inner thoughts, it even scared itself.

So when the effect time ended, Giratina was embarrassed and enraged.

Giratina was no fool; it knew this human came to fulfill its request for planting flowers on those Floating Islands.

This time, Giratina was also keenly curious to know if flowers could actually bloom here.

Therefore, after the effect ended, it pretended the effect hadn't expired to avoid scaring the human and his weak Pokémon.

Yet, Giratina didn't realize its return had already been noticed and was unaware that its body would change appearance and color when the temperament shifted.

Seeing Giratina pretending to still be in reversed time, D didn't know what to say.

Rarely did the original Giratina not shout or rage, so D simply pretended he hadn't seen anything.

He gave Koraidon and Calyrex a look and continued to turn around, burying the Berry core into the loosened soil.

Then, D stepped aside, leaving the rest to Calyrex.

Calyrex's flower bud on its head bloomed with a blue light, and the next second, the Berry seed buried in the soil quickly grew.

With a flash of light, a tall Berry tree appeared before D and the Pokémon.

Without sunlight or water sources, solely relying on Calyrex's power, the Berry tree sprouted lush green leaves, and its branches were laden with ripe and heavy Pecha Berries.

Success! D was internally elated.

The Berry tree successfully sprouted, meaning things could be grown in this world!

Giratina's gaze was captivated by this vibrant fruit tree, staring dumbfounded with its scarlet eyes at this scene.

Its realm, this world filled with desolation and destruction, could actually bear fruit!

Giratina's heart felt immensely complicated.

Since the dawn of its creation, it was exiled to this ruined world for its harsh nature.

It raged, fumed, resented, spiraling in an empty world, gazing at Arceus, which made them, lively in sunlight through a water ripple.

Until after countless years, everything settled into calmness.

Arceus and Dialga left the world, vanishing into the void.

After experiencing the initial emotions, Giratina also regarded this place as its realm, occasionally gazing through the surface at the sunshine, green grass, and flowers of another world.

Now, its world has grown identical vibrant trees.

Giratina felt complicated as it turned and quietly looked at this human and his Pokémon, pondering silently.

D didn't notice Giratina's expression; he was focusing entirely on the Berry tree before him.

More precisely, on the Berries hanging from the tree.

He discovered that these Berries differed significantly from the Pecha Berries he was familiar with.

[Pecha Berry: Toxic Berry, consuming it leads to poisoning, but is very popular among specific Pokémon, possessing great appeal to Poison Type Pokémon.]

Moreover, even the Berry tree itself seemed different.

[Pecha Berry Tree (Eternal): An ordinary Pecha Berry tree, influenced by antimatter allows it to maintain this form indefinitely, never wilting, growing, or dying.]

"Wow!"

Witnessing this, D couldn't help but exclaim in amazement.

Now, he finally understood what "regular conventions in the Reversed World differ from the real world" meant.

---

---

---

---

# 272: Chapter 269: Sky Island Development

"Eating a Pecha Berry causes poisoning... has the effect been reversed?"

D plucked a soft pink, juicy Pecha Berry from the branch.

When you bring it close, you can smell the sweet peach scent wafting from the Pecha Berry.

However, it's a shame that this Berry is poisonous. Poison Type Pokemon might like it, but unfortunately, there aren't any Poison Type Pokemon on the farm right now.

Since his purpose for this trip was achieved, D decided to return to the farm first, intending to go to town and buy a batch of flower seeds and seedlings for planting.

So, he turned to say goodbye to Giratina: "It seems you can grow things in this realm of yours, but I didn't bring many seeds this time, so I need to make a trip back to the farm first."

Giratina quietly stared at him. After a moment, the temperamental Dragon of Destruction nodded, letting out a roar that was hard to interpret.

A flash of eerie purple light appeared, and the portal to the real world opened.

D mounted Koraidon and, along with Diglett and Calyrex, left the place.

Once back at the farm, he immediately rode Koraidon to Porto Marinada Town to purchase a batch of grass seeds, flower seeds, and Berry Seedlings from the Elo Seed Store.

Seeing D buy so many things at once, Elo was quite surprised:

"Isn't it the wrong season to plant these things in the field? It's going to snow in a few days, and these seedlings might freeze to death!"

D chuckled and shook his head: "Don't worry. I know. I'm planning to plant these seedlings in a warmer place where they definitely won't freeze to death."

A warmer place? Elo suddenly remembered that some farms build greenhouses, where things can be planted year-round.

"Alright then!" Elo smiled, and then he thought of some new crops he had just gotten in, and asked, "We just got a batch of tea saplings in the store, perfect for planting in a greenhouse!"

"Tea saplings?" D was a bit surprised.

Elo nodded: "That's right! Once the tea saplings grow, you can harvest the leaves and brew fragrant green tea!"

D thought about it for a moment and agreed that they would be good for planting in the Reversed World, and said: "Sure, let me have twenty of them to try planting."

"Got it!"

Elo grinned, and after moving all the tea saplings over, he asked:

"By the way, we also received a batch of local specialty apple trees from Kitakami; Kitakami's apples are famous for being big and round. Would you like a few of them as well? Plant them on your farm, and you might even attract some Applin Pokemon!"

Apple trees?

Seeing he was buying so many items anyway, D decided to purchase a batch of apple trees as well.

Since he bought so much, the store offered delivery service, so D wasn't worried about whether he and Koraidon could carry everything.

But before leaving, he couldn't help asking about Kitakami: "Are the apples from Kitakami really that famous?"

Before he crossed over, the news about Kitakami hadn't been announced yet, and D knew nothing about it.

Hearing this, Elo nodded with a laugh: "Yes! The apples there are well-known throughout all of Paldea! The people of Kitakami work at sunrise and rest at sunset, living by planting rice, making it a very simple and pastoral town."

"Is that so..." D became interested in Kitakami, and perhaps after the farm matters were settled, he could visit with a few Pokemon for an agricultural exchange.

Once everything was purchased, D rode Koraidon back to the farm.

Not long after, Elo sent someone to deliver all the seeds he had bought from the store.

D stood by the lake, and as the purple glow shimmered, the entrance to the Reversed World was automatically opened by Giratina.

D tossed the seeds and seedlings in one by one before jumping in with Diglett and Calyrex.

Giratina was already waiting there early.

It looked at D without the previous temper and rage, or staring dangerously.

This legendary Dark King Dragon simply watched D silently, observing the things he brought from outside.

To be precise, it was looking to see if there was anything that could change its disposition.

There wasn't that kind of thing, and it didn't even know how to communicate with this human.

However, after looking for a long time, Giratina still couldn't find the target.

It couldn't help but wonder why this human wasn't using that ability on it.

Seeing that D really hadn't brought that stuff, Giratina pondered for two seconds and thought that the food it ate should still be effective, so it decided to continue pretending that the food hadn't expired yet, smiling a terrifying smile that made its already menacing face even more sinister.

D noticed this scene, and a question mark popped up above his head.

Giratina: "With these things, can we make this place the same as the outside?"

"That's right." D nodded, looking up at the floating Sky Islands in the void, and continued: "It's just that the land in this world is scattered, so I have to plant on each island slowly."

---

"Then I'll leave it to you." Giratina felt more and more at ease. Finally, it added modestly, "Thank you, human. I will remember everything you have done for me."

D: "..."

Doesn't Giratina still think the effects of the meal haven't worn off?

Oh my gosh, this is so awkward!

If I expose it now, Giratina might get embarrassed and angry, and give me a swipe.

After pondering for a moment, D ultimately decided to pretend he hadn't seen anything.

He rode Koraidon to the recently planted Pecha Berry tree on that Sky Island, then buried a layer of grass seeds in the land that Diglett had tilled.

Then, Calyrex activated its ability.

The next moment, the broken Sky Island was covered with a touch of greenery, transforming into a vibrant scene.

The first Sky Island was completed.

D named it the Initial Sky Island. He looked around, then his gaze settled on another, larger Sky Island above, which had a waterfall.

"Let's go there!" he mounted Koraidon.

"A-ga-sii!"

Koraidon leaped gently and landed on that Sky Island with D.

D first surveyed the terrain and then said to Diglett, "I'll leave this to you as well!"

"Squeak squeak!" Diglett squealed happily and continued its work on the island's construction.

For this island, D planned to cultivate an apple-themed Sky Island, with lush apple trees bearing large, rosy apples.

If Giratina ever feels like a snack, it can come to this island and have a red apple.

The waterfall on the island tumbled down, forming a clear pool.

The water rippled, reflecting the real world on its surface.

D looked at the water and thought that Giratina might be peeking at the real world through places like this.

What might it be watching?

The sunlight, flowers, and trees of the real world?

Seeing these things, what might Giratina be thinking?

D glanced at Giratina, only to see it innocently watching Diglett till the land.

D couldn't help but smile, thinking that, in a way, this legendary Pokemon, living in solitude, must have a worldview and experiences similar to a human child.

So, it's curious about Diglett's land-tilling work.

Soon, Diglett had tilled most of the island.

D and Koraidon brought apple saplings, planting them across the island, and sprinkled grass and flower seeds on the land.

Finally, Calyrex activated its power again.

With their teamwork, the Sky Island was renewed in the blink of an eye.

From its formerly ruined and desolate state, it became lush and vibrant.

The entire Sky Island's ground was covered by green grass, with small yellow flowers blooming in the grass.

Robust apple trees formed a flourishing apple garden, with large, round red apples hanging from the trees.

D looked down, and the information about these apples surfaced in his mind.

[Sweet Apple: A delicious sweet apple, influenced by Calyrex's power, which erased the bad traits, allowing it to bring a taste of happiness to those who eat it.]

[Apple Tree (Eternal): A normal apple tree, influenced by antimatter, allowing it to maintain this appearance indefinitely, never withering, never growing, never aging.]

"Indeed, even in the Reversed World, the apple trees maintain their eternal state," D murmured.

He stepped forward, picked a few red apples, gave one to Calyrex, tossed one to Giratina, and hand-fed the apples to Koraidon and Diglett.

The tossed apple, unaffected by gravity, gradually floated in front of Giratina.

Giratina gazed at the rosy apple, opened its mouth, stretched out its head, and took a big bite.

In the next moment, it felt a burst of sweet and tasty juice explode in its mouth.

Giratina's eyes lit up, its voice filled with joy and delight: "Delicious!"

"As long as you like it," D smiled slightly.

And just then, not far away, the apple-eating Diglett seemed to have discovered something and suddenly let out a puzzled cry.

"Squeak squeak?"

"What's wrong?" D looked at Diglett and followed its gaze, soon spotting a large, dark mushroom under the dark and damp waterfall.

This is...

D paused, and in the next second, information about the mushroom emerged in his mind.

[Destruction Mushroom: A unique ingredient, a mushroom growing from dark, damp corners, filled with destructive forces and antimatter, usable in cooking, but if brought to the real world...]

What is this?

Staring at the dark mushroom, D suddenly felt it resembled the Doom-shroom from Plants vs. Zombies.

Oh no, will it explode?

Calyrex and Koraidon were also drawn to the mushroom.

With just one glance, Calyrex saw through the mushroom's essence: "I sense it contains a force akin to this realm."

"We'll make it into mushroom soup later," D stepped forward, picked it, and kept it well, knowing from past experience that it could possibly be a potent cooking ingredient.

The Pokemon had no objections.

---

Giratina really didn't care about these things, and since D was helping it modify realms, it could even give away a Sky Island without a second thought.

Giratina regarded D as a little brother and made a decision in its heart—from now on, it would take care of this human!

The second Sky Island was completed, and D looked at the exhausted panting Diglett, deciding to take a break first.

The workload of constructing the Reversed World was just too enormous, and they weren't in a hurry, so they might as well take it slow; there was still a long time ahead.

When D told Giratina this idea, Giratina readily agreed and opened the portal to the real world for them.

"See you later then!"

D and Giratina waved goodbye, taking the Destruction Mushroom back to the ranch.

However, D had barely taken two steps when he suddenly felt the Destruction Mushroom in his hand heating up.

An uneasy feeling rose within him, and D's heart skipped a beat, quickly throwing the mushroom away.

In the next second, the mushroom flying through the air exploded with a loud "boom."

Koraidon was startled, thinking danger was approaching, and alertly stood in front of D.

"Holy crap!"

D was stunned; what was happening?

After the explosion ended, the mushroom in mid-air was completely obliterated, leaving no trace behind.

Meanwhile, Calyrex sensed the surroundings using psychic power and then thoughtfully said:

"I can sense two completely opposite forces clashing, mutually offsetting each other, and then the mushroom's presence vanished."

Upon hearing this, D was slightly taken aback.

He immediately recalled the description of the Destruction Mushroom—filled with destructive power and antimatter.

Could it be that the collision between the antimatter from the Reversed World and real world caused the annihilation phenomenon?

This is too strange! How is he supposed to use the Destruction Mushroom in cooking?

The moment this question popped up, D found the answer in his heart.

And that was to cook directly in the Reversed World, not bringing the mushroom into the real world!

But is it really safe to eat this kind of mushroom?

D pondered as he walked towards home.

It's already ten in the morning, and Dragonite and the others had already delivered their goods, returned to the ranch, sitting under the tree munching on Poffin.

Just then, D suddenly noticed that the number of Dragonite in the ranch seemed a bit off.

"One, two, three, four... Four Dragonite?! How come there's an extra Dragonite?" D was somewhat confused.

Could it be that some of those Dragonair evolved into Dragonite?

Seeing this, D wanted to check the situation, but before he could get closer, he saw his own Dragonite standing in front of the unfamiliar Dragonite, loudly accusing it with a raised claw.

"Roar roar, roar!" (Who let you come here! This isn't the place you should be, go back to Dragonite Island!)

The innocent-looking Dragonite didn't turn back, still eagerly watching the Poffin box held by the other two Dragonite working in the ranch, drooling uncontrollably.

"Roar!" (It smells so good, I want to eat too, give me a piece!)

Upon seeing this, the two Dragonite immediately turned around with disdain, clutching the Poffin box.

"Roar!" This is what they earned from their hard work, and they certainly won't share with other dragons!

"Roar?" (Work?) The Dragonite looked completely bewildered.

The two working Dragonite nodded, "Roar!" (If you want to eat, go work yourself!)

---

---

---

---

# 273: Chapter 270: Let There Be Light

Dragonite Four had noticed long ago the two Dragonites and five Dragonairs who left with the sunrise and returned with the sunset every day.

Every morning, they would wake up from their sleep as the sun rose, then fly towards the distance in the early sunlight.

When the sun was about to set, Dragonite Four would see them return to Dragonite Island, bringing back fragrant and extremely enticing food for dragons.

In the end, it couldn't resist the temptation and secretly followed them this morning.

It originally thought that the delicious food was picked from trees in the wild by the two Dragonites, but it turned out they landed in a place with many Pokemon living there.

Not only that, even the Dragonite that was once the most foolish on the island was here.

Dragonite Four became curious about this place.

Watching the two Dragonites eating Poffins, it naively asked, "Rawwr?" (What is work?)

The two working Dragonites thought for a moment, and the one sitting on the left side of the soil answered, "Rawwr, rawwr." (Work is helping humans, who in turn give us tasty Poffins.)

So this delicious thing is called a Poffin! Dragonite Four thought to itself.

At this moment, the Dragonite sitting on the right side of the soil noticed D walking over.

It waved its paw disdainfully, indicating that this guy should stop disturbing them while they were eating, as his drooling was almost hitting their Poffins.

"Rawwr." (D is coming, if you need something, go ask him.)

Upon hearing this, Dragonite Four turned its head and saw a familiar figure walking towards them.

At a glance, it recognized the other party's identity.

This human was the one who had visited Dragonite Island before!

At this moment, D also guessed the origin of this unfamiliar Dragonite.

Clearly, it had come from Dragonite Island, and as for why it had come, there was only one possibility.

Thinking of this, D's gaze turned to the Poffin Box held by the two working Dragonites.

"Another gluttonous Dragonite..." D secretly criticized in his heart.

He smiled at the newly arrived Dragonite and then asked his own Dragonite, "Have you told it the precautions for the ranch?"

Seeing this, Dragonite sighed in resignation and shook its head helplessly, "Rawwr..."

"Then it's up to you." D smiled and patted his Dragonite's head, then turned around to prepare to head back to the house to make some fatigue-relieving dishes for the Diglett.

Being patted on the head made Dragonite very happy, and it nodded its big head, gladly accepting the task.

D didn't know what the Dragonites were talking about, but early the next morning, he discovered that there was an additional Dragonite working in the ranch.

That morning, Dragonite stood on the grass like a leader, directing the three Dragonites in front of it to pack bottled fresh milk into satchels, then arranging their work and precautions for the day.

For instance, who was in charge of which street, forbidding them from sipping the goods midway, forbidding fights with guests' Pokemon, and returning to the ranch after delivering the milk and wine.

Once Dragonite finished issuing instructions, the four Dragonites flapped their little wings in unison, flying towards the direction of Porto Marinada Town.

D stood on the ground watching this scene, feeling somewhat sentimental.

There were more and more Dragonites in the ranch!

Unfortunately, the ranch was currently in the development stage and didn't have much work for them to do.

This also meant that after delivering goods every morning, the Dragonites would turn into idle beings lying around the ranch all day.

D shook his head with a laugh, sometimes really wishing the information age would arrive sooner.

Then, he could equip these Dragonites with better equipment, fully utilizing their strengths to transport ranch goods further away.

Watching the Dragonites' figures vanish over the horizon, D turned around and continued his new round of Sky Island development endeavors.

As the entrance to the Reversed World slowly opened, D, along with the Diglett and Calyrex, rode on Koraidon and jumped in.

Upon entering this special space, D saw a familiar figure appearing before them.

It was none other than the domain's master, Giratina.

Giratina gazed at them with those terrifying blood-red eyes.

Just as D thought it was going to roar and shout aggressively as before, Giratina unexpectedly opened its mouth calmly and asked, "What are you planning to plant today?"

D was slightly taken aback and then looked over in disbelief.

Giratina pretended not to notice his impolite gaze, and as its goodwill towards this human increased, so did its tolerance.

Moreover, having bombarded this person for a long time before, Giratina's heart was filled with remorse.

At the same time, it also felt very fortunate.

Luckily, this human was somewhat special and wasn't blown to bits back then.

Otherwise, it would never have had the chance to see this destruction-laden place bloom with beautiful flowers.

After a brief daze, D realized and responded, "Today, we're planning to plant some flowers."

Yesterday they had created Birth Island and Apple Hills, so today it was time to try Flower Island.

However, before heading to the new Floating Island to plant flowers, D rode on Koraidon once again and made a trip to Apple Hills.

---

Yesterday, that exploding Destruction Mushroom caught his attention.

D tried searching for traces of other Destruction Mushrooms on this apple and waterfall-laden island.

However, he didn't see any Destruction Mushrooms, but he did notice some apple trees had been eaten bare.

It was obvious without even thinking that it must have been Giratina eating them after he had left yesterday.

D imagined Giratina shrinking to a little over two meters, landing on the grass of Apple Hills, plucking apples with its mouth and devouring them.

At that time, what kind of mood would Giratina be in?

D curiously looked at Giratina.

Sensing D's gaze, Giratina felt a bit uneasy and glared back at him with wide eyes.

Pretending not to see, D, unable to find the Destruction Mushroom he wanted, set off to a new Sky Island.

Ultimately, they chose a Sky Island with relatively flat terrain, no backward waterfalls, and no scattered boulders, appearing quite normal.

Once again with Diglett's help, D turned the soil, scattering grass and flower seeds before handing it over to Calyrex for cultivation.

With a surge of blue light, in the blink of an eye, the Sky Island before them transformed dramatically.

The barren, deep land was covered with green grass, which bloomed with a variety of flowers.

Roses, lilies, roses, lilies of the valley, sunflowers, and some unknown small flowers.

D looked at this sea of flowers, feeling extremely satisfied.

However, at that moment, as if suddenly discovering something, Calyrex gazed at a special sunflower and said, "I sense an unusual aura."

"What?"

D was slightly taken aback and followed Calyrex's gaze to see a peculiar sunflower emitting a faint glow amidst the dense Sunflora Field.

The next second, information about that sunflower surfaced in his mind.

[Sunflower: Special crop, originally an ordinary sunflower that underwent magical changes after Calyrex's cultivation. It can periodically produce sunlight to illuminate a certain area, providing both warmth and light. Dishes made from its fruit will have the effect of a "Sun Stone."]

This is...

D was stunned on the spot.

Just then, the sunflower's head suddenly brightened at a speed visible to the naked eye.

When it reached a certain level, the light gradually detached from the head below, gathering into a golden sun that rose above this Sky Island.

Instantly, the surrounding environment became warm and bright.

Giratina, seeing this scene, was also slightly taken aback.

If it wasn't mistaken, that thing was... the sun?

Giratina felt a bit unsure, so it turned its gaze to D.

After a brief shock, D quickly accepted the situation and said to Giratina, "Alright, you can come here to bask in the sun from now on."

Giratina still looked bewildered. It couldn't believe its eyes while staring at that sunflower, exclaiming, "What is that thing? Humans can actually grow such plants!"

D: "... Not everyone can grow sunflowers like this."

Giratina looked over.

D explained, "To be precise, it's called a Sunflower, a special plant born from the combination of my ability and Calyrex's ability."

Giratina seemed to half-understand and then, in an admiring tone, said, "That's amazing!"

D inspected the other flowers carefully, and seeing no other mutant plants besides the Sunflower, he took a few Pokemon to the next Sky Island.

This time, he planned to plant tea seedlings here and transform this place into his exclusive tea garden.

*

Meanwhile, in Mesagoza City, at Paldea News TV Station.

A blonde woman in an office lady outfit sat at a desk, reviewing letters sent in from across Paldea.

Generally speaking, these letters would record strange and unusual events happening across Paldea.

For example, rare Pokemon appearing in certain areas, a specific Pokemon causing disturbances in a town, or a Trainer witnessing ghosts somewhere.

When these events occur, Paldea News TV Station would dispatch specialized reporters to investigate and interview, then produce programs to air on TV, thereby captivating the audience.

"Let me see if there's any big news worth uncovering lately." Alice's heart was filled with anticipation.

If they encountered interesting news, the viewership ratings of their show would rise by several points.

Opening the envelope, the first thing that caught her eye was — [A large number of Magnemites causing chaos in the town, leading to a massive city-wide blackout, their purpose in draining electricity was actually for...]

"Magnemite? A pretty normal event!" Alice sharply commented, "This kind of stuff happened many years ago, nothing surprising at all."

Alice put this letter aside and continued to open the second one.

[A man fell in love with a Gardevoir, dressed Gardevoir in a wedding gown, intending to hold a wedding at a church. What will be the outcome of this forbidden love between humans and Pokemon?!]

Alice: "..."

At this moment, a male colleague wearing glasses approached: "Hey Alice, have you discovered any interesting news?"

---

Alice handed the envelope about the forbidden romance to the man: "Here, take a look yourself!"

The man reached out to take it, and after seeing the information on it, his eyes behind the glasses lit up instantly:

"Wow, this is definitely a major news story worth digging into!"

"...In that case, I'll leave this matter for you to investigate." Alice straightforwardly assigned the task to the man, then continued to open the third letter.

[In a rural town, sightings of the rare Pokémon Dragonite have emerged! Someone is actually using Dragonite as a delivery Pokémon!]

"Huh?" Seeing the content of the letter, Alice immediately became interested and continued to read further.

[Not long ago, I traveled to Porto Marinada Town. One morning, while having breakfast with my Pokémon, I suddenly saw several Dragonites with satchels flying into the town.]

[The locals said those Dragonites came from a rural farm, and it was a farmer directing them to deliver goods to the townsfolk!]

"A farmer?" Upon seeing this, Alice's first reaction was disbelief.

A farmer in the countryside capturing such a powerful and rare Pokémon like Dragonite—it sounds rather implausible.

While it's true that Dragonites have a gentle disposition, it doesn't mean they would choose a weak human as a companion.

Moreover, each Dragonite has a different personality, and some seek strength and power.

The male colleague assigned to investigate the forbidden romance between humans and Pokémon saw Alice's expression and curiously asked, "What's up? Found another big story?"

Alice answered with a question: "Mark, do you think a farmer running a farm could capture a Pokémon like Dragonite?"

Upon hearing this, Mark's first response was to shake his head: "How could that be possible? A farmer isn't a Trainer, and the two seem completely mismatched. If it were Geeta, I might believe it!"

"Here." Alice handed over the news letter in her hand: "See for yourself."

Mark took it, skimmed through it, then firmly shook his head: "Absolutely impossible! It's fake; there's no way such a thing could happen in this world!"

He initially thought it was just one Dragonite, but it turned out there were several.

This is absolutely impossible!

"Too exaggerated." Mark shook his head: "This person is clearly trying to lure us there for an info fee!"

Alice thought the same.

Not paying much attention, she set the letter aside and continued to look through other newsworthy stories.

But surprisingly, the next letter was still about the farmer and the delivery Dragonite.

[Hello, I am a traveling Trainer! Recently, I passed by a strange farm... Many Pokémon live there, but strangely, there's not a single human, just a group of Pokémon.]

[There's also a tavern that only serves Pokémon, run by two Indeedee. Even stranger, several Dragonites live there, and they deliver milk to the town every morning.]

[Strange, right? Oh, by the way, that farm is near Porto Marinada Town. If you're going to investigate, don't forget to send me the info fee!]

Alice: "?"

The same Porto Marinada Town, the same farm and delivery Dragonites—this was getting peculiar.

Alice appeared pensive. If that's the case, the place is definitely worth a visit.

Thinking of this, Alice, being action-oriented, immediately began to pack her things.

Seeing this, Mark was somewhat puzzled: "Where are you going?"

Alice already had her camera and luggage ready, answering without turning her head: "Business trip!"

---

# 274: Chapter 271: Man and Dragon

[Tea Tree: A special crop, influenced by the power of Calyrex, from which trees grow. The leaves from these trees can be used to make delicious green tea, but cannot be consumed directly.]

In the Reversed World, D looked at a Sky Island before him that had been cultivated into a tea garden, feeling a deep sense of satisfaction.

From now on, whenever he wanted to drink tea, he could come here directly to pick the tea leaves.

"Good job!" Having tilled the land on three Sky Islands in a row, Diglett was panting heavily. Looking at the exhausted Diglett, D patted its head before saying to Giratina, "Let's call it a day for now!"

Giratina nodded its massive head, glanced at D and the few Pokémon, and then opened the exit to the Reversed World.

Standing at the exit, D waved goodbye to Giratina and then returned to the ranch with Calyrex, Koraidon, and Diglett for some rest.

As their figures disappeared at the end of the Reversed World, the spatial rift gradually vanished.

Giratina levitated in the void, its blood-red eyes sweeping in all directions.

Until now, it still found it hard to believe.

In just two short days, its realm had undergone a drastic transformation.

Not only was there grass and flowers, but even this place, unique to that world, had sunlight.

Filled with inner shock, Giratina slowly descended onto that Apple Hills.

It turned its head and saw those red apples hanging from the branches.

Giratina approached, opened its mouth, plucked an apple from a branch, and took a gentle bite. Juice burst into its mouth, accompanied by a rich fruity aroma, and Giratina immediately felt as if it was infused with new life.

The scene of vibrant growth in the Reversed World was still fresh in its mind.

After eating the apple, Giratina flew to the Sky Island filled with green tea trees.

It looked down on these low green tea bushes, sniffed at the leaves with its massive head.

Thinking of how D valued these tea trees, it assumed they might be something delicious and curiously took a bite.

The next second, the bitter leaves twisted its face, making its already fierce appearance even more ferocious.

Giratina spat several times, then spread its blood-colored wings and flew back to Apple Hills, eating several sweet scarlet apples before feeling somewhat better.

"Phew..."

Feeling the sweet apple juice in its mouth, Giratina couldn't help but sigh with relief.

Looking at the Sky Island filled with tea trees not far away, it unhesitatingly decided to blacklist everything on it.

Having done all this, Giratina flew to the Sky Island blooming with flowers.

It stood amidst the sea of flowers, looking up at the bright but not dazzling, warm but not scorching golden sun above.

This was a sensation Giratina had never experienced since coming to this destroyed world.

In a daze, its mind seemed to conjure scenes of long ago when Arceus and Dialga were active under the sunlight.

Back then, it often watched the other sunlit world through calm water surfaces.

Was Arceus feeling the same way then as it is now?

Giratina stood dazedly amid the sea of flowers. Gradually, a warm feeling enveloped its entire being.

This made Giratina involuntarily relax completely. At this moment, it no longer wanted to cause trouble to gain Arceus's attention; it just wanted to lie quietly here, basking in the sun, going nowhere.

Thinking this, Giratina's massive body slowly lay down in the sea of flowers.

Its huge head rested gently on its forelimbs, smelling the flowers in front of it while basking in the warm sun.

At this moment, Giratina felt an unprecedented sense of contentment in its heart.

It closed its eyes and slowly drifted into slumber in the sunlight.

*

Though he called it resting, D actually didn't just stay at home, transforming into a couch potato.

Compared to a few Pokémon exerting their powers, he wasn't very tired.

Back at the ranch, Calyrex went to the Glass Greenhouse, enjoying the winter sun while savoring tea served by Polteageist.

Diglett returned to its underground nest, happily carrying a bunch of snacks given by D.

Koraidon, on the other hand, followed D back home.

D took a basket of Berries from the cabinet and said to Koraidon, "Go play for a while; I need to make Poffins for Dragonite."

"Aghhh..."

Seeing D couldn't play with it, Koraidon drooped its head, feeling a bit deflated.

But soon, it was distracted by Glaceon and Alcremie's growls outside, and ran out to see what they were up to.

Watching this scene, D silently chuckled, then focused entirely on preparing the Poffin mixture.

When the Dragonites returned to the ranch after delivering goods, two freshly baked batches of Poffins were just ready.

D donned oven mitts and carried the tray to the living room.

The two Dragonites in the first batch were already very familiar with the process.

Even though they had gone through it many times, around this time every day, they still felt excited.

The two Dragonites stared unblinkingly as D placed the freshly baked Poffins into their Poffin Box.

---

"Glug!"

The newly arrived Dragonite number four couldn't help but swallow.

Its eyes were fixed on the Poffin in D's hand, that was the flavor!

So fragrant!

Dragonite number four's drool poured down like a waterfall.

Dragonite disdainfully looked at the scene; compared to it, the Dragonites on the island were really too inexperienced!

"Here you go, this is for you two! Thanks for your hard work today!" D said with a smile as he handed two boxes of Poffins to the working Dragonites.

"Roar!"

They let out happy growls, reached out to take the Poffin Boxes, and ran out of the room, heading to the farm to enjoy the Poffins.

Dragonite number four stared longingly at the remaining Poffins.

Under its pitiful gaze, D took out a new Poffin Box from the cabinet, filled it with Poffins, and handed it over: "This is yours. Welcome to the farm, Dragonite!"

Hearing this, Dragonite number four's eyes lit up.

Holding the Poffin Box stuffed with Poffins in its arms, it felt happy about its decision for the first time.

Thank goodness it had secretly followed them this morning!

Dragonite number four happily bid farewell to D and left the house with the Poffin Box.

Watching its back, D and Dragonite walked out of the room and stood under the eaves, watching the three Dragonites eating Poffins under the tree in the distance.

They leaned against the big tree, using claws to take Poffins from the box and stuff them into their mouths, happily chewing away.

"Phew..."

Watching this scene, D let out a long sigh, feeling a bit emotional.

The Dragonites at the farm were increasing!

It felt like he could rename the farm to "Dragonite Ranch."

*

Days passed routinely, and in the blink of an eye, it was November 6, in the 198th year of the League Calendar.

That morning, the farm welcomed a new guest.

Looking at the blonde lady returning to the farm with four Dragonites, D was a bit bewildered.

He asked uncertainly, "You mean you're a reporter from the Paldea News TV Station, wanting to shoot a program about me and the Dragonites?"

Even at this moment, Alice had not recovered from the shock.

She looked at the four Dragonites wandering under the tree, her face filled with astonishment.

Just last evening, she had arrived at Porto Marinada Town located in the western part of the Paldea Region.

Not knowing the exact location of the destination farm, she stayed overnight in town, planning to observe the next morning to see if, as the letter said, Dragonites would come to town every morning to deliver milk.

Alice had thought it was just some dastardly person deceiving them for information fees, but unexpectedly, the next morning, she indeed saw Dragonites with satchels circling above the town.

Even more shocking was that there wasn't just one Dragonite!

There were four!

What kind of family could afford to raise four Dragonites!

So, to uncover the truth, Alice quickly grabbed her camera and followed these Dragonites to this farm.

She found out there weren't just those four Dragonites, but also many rare Pokemon she had never seen before!

Although she didn't know them, just by looking at these Pokemon, she could sense they were extraordinary.

Were they Pokemon from other regions?

At this moment, Alice looked at the young man in front of her and couldn't help but swallow.

Who exactly was this man?

To have captured so many seemingly strong Pokemon, he must be a powerful Trainer!

Elite... or Champion?

But what Alice couldn't understand was why such a promising and powerful Trainer would come to such a remote countryside to become a farmer?

Seeing the reporter in a daze, a question mark popped above D's head.

?

Only then did Alice come to her senses, nodded, and replied, "That's right! I'm from Mesagoza City, where the headquarters of the Paldea News TV Station is located!"

"I came to Porto Marinada Town to investigate the truth behind the mysterious urban legend about the [Dragonite Delivery]!"

D: "..."

Seriously, an urban legend!

So it turned out that on some unknown occasions, sending Dragonites to deliver milk had already become a mysterious urban legend?

D internally ranted.

Yet at this moment, Alice was filled with anticipation.

Would this man named D agree?

Although the program hadn't started filming yet, she had already thought of a name for this episode.

Let's call it "Man and Dragon."

Why did rare Pokemon like Dragonites appear in the mysterious town, and why did they circle above every morning? What is the mysterious connection between the man and Dragonites? Stay tuned for the evening news "Paldea Secrets: Man and Dragon"!

D pondered deeply.

Honestly, he didn't mind being featured in a program.

After all, he planned to establish the Dragonite Delivery Company in the future.

Instead of promoting it laboriously then, why not prepare in advance, boost the fame of the Dragonite Delivery Company, and use this program to advertise for the farm?

Thinking of this, D's brow relaxed.

He smiled and replied, "Of course!"

Hearing this answer, Alice's eyes brightened: "Thank you very much!"

---

If this program can be broadcasted, their show's ratings will definitely increase by several percentage points!

Compared to those boring events caused by Pokemon, this kind of thing naturally carries a certain level of buzz!

After all, just having a group of Dragonite deliver goods can attract a large audience.

However, before the program started filming, Alice had some things she wanted to know.

Thus, D invited her to the living room, and his sister Indeedee served the guest a cup of tea.

Alice took a sip, cleared her throat, and casually asked, "When did Mr. D start running this ranch?"

D thought for a moment and replied, "In January this year!"

"Pfft!" Alice was surprised and asked uncertainly, "In January? So you're saying you only started managing the ranch this year?"

"Yes." D nodded slightly: "I have been in other regions before, and only recently returned to the Paldea Region."

"So, the Paldea Region is your hometown?"

"That's right." D answered, "This ranch was left by my grandfather. After he passed away, the ranch was abandoned. I traveled for many years and wanted a place to rest, so I came back to this place to start managing the ranch again."

So he was a Pokemon Trainer from another region! No wonder she hadn't heard of such a figure in the Paldea League before, Alice thought.

Traveling outside, becoming a strong trainer and then finding city life dull, giving up a great future to return to the countryside for a slow-paced farm life?

Alice had a rough impression of the man in front of her.

All along, whether boy or girl, everyone has been working hard to become the best Pokemon Trainer in the world, some even with gray hair still haven't given up on their dreams.

Thinking of this, Alice couldn't help but sigh, "It's quite rare for a young and powerful trainer like you, Mr. D, to make such a choice!"

D: "..." When did he become a trainer again?

Alice was puzzled, "Huh? Isn't that so?"

D: "…No, as you can see, I'm just a rancher."

"You're quite the joker." Alice shook her head and said, "Where in the world does a rancher capture four Dragonite and have them willingly deliver goods?"

At this point, Alice suddenly understood, "Oh, I see! You're saying that now you're just a rancher!"

D: "..."

D simply gave up explaining. At first, he was a bit unaccustomed, but now that he's experienced it more, he can face this kind of situation calmly.

Alice: "So next, let's talk about how to shoot the film!"

It finally reached the part she was best at, and Alice brightly introduced her filming plan to D.

"First, we need to showcase the Dragonite's powerful abilities from multiple angles, and then demonstrate how incredible it is to have such strong Pokemon deliver milk to guests."

"Once viewers feel that strong contrast, it will lead them to the man behind the Dragonite, which is you, Mr. D."

"As for the program's title, I've already thought of it in advance."

D was a bit curious, "Oh? What's it called?"

"Man and Dragon!"

---

---

---

---

# 275: Chapter 272: Sensation Caused by a TV Program

Man and Dragon...

It's quite similar to The Old Man and the Sea!

D had no objections and said to Alice, "You decide, Dragonite and I will cooperate with your work."

"That's wonderful!" Alice smiled.

She continued explaining the upcoming photography work to D, "Actually, you don't need to do anything special, just act as usual. As a competent journalist and photographer, I'll find the best angles myself."

"No problem," D said.

Having said that, D resumed his ranch work as usual.

Meanwhile, Alice stayed nearby, searching for the right angles.

The Pokemon on the ranch didn't pay much attention to the visiting guest.

At this time, Calyrex was resting in the glass greenhouse with Sinistea and Polteageist, not coming out.

Spectrier and Glastrier were eating the pile of carrot Berries D had poured into their feeding troughs.

In the cold weather, Naclstack and others didn't come to the Ground much, Alcremie and Cyclizar remained by the fireplace playing with the toys D bought them.

At this time, the only Pokemon outside were Pawmo greeting the hens, Gogoat cultivating the World Tree, and the few Dragonite and Dragonair.

Alice had already seen those four Dragonite, as they guided her to the ranch.

She was fascinated by observing the ecology of those four Dragonite, taking pictures of their harmonious interactions with her camera.

After a while, Alice began to search for D's figure in the ranch.

She was in for a shock once she found him.

D was standing in front of a lake, surrounded by five slender Dragonair!

One of the Dragonair was pink, an extremely rare Shiny Dragonair!

Alice widened her eyes in disbelief at the scene, exclaiming, "Oh my god!"

Five Dragonair, on top of the previous four adult Dragonite, meant there were nine Dragonite in this ranch!

"Hiss!"

The overwhelming number made Alice gasp in astonishment.

Could it be that Mr. D was a Trainer specializing in Dragon Type Pokemon?

Raising nine Dragonite, along with the red Dragon Type Pokemon, seemed even Dragon Elite Hassel couldn't have so many Dragon Type Pokemon.

Moreover, if she remembered correctly, Hassel was supposed to be the young master of the Dragon Tamer family in the Paldea Region.

But now, the number of Dragon Type Pokemon around D had far exceeded the young master of the Dragon Tamer family.

Between these two, who was the real Dragon Elite?

Alice looked in disbelief at D interacting harmoniously with the five Dragonair, observing their responsive and affectionate behavior towards him.

"This is truly astonishing!"

Even after a day of shooting, Alice couldn't quite recover, her face showing a dazed expression akin to that of Xianglin's Wife.

At that moment, D was packing Poffins into a box for the Dragonite.

Once the Poffin Box was filled, the Dragonite hung it around their necks, waved claws to bid farewell to D, and flew toward the evening sky with five Dragonair.

Witnessing this, Alice was puzzled, "Where are the Dragonite going?"

D looked towards their disappearing direction, smiling as he replied, "They're, of course, returning to their homes."

Alice was bewildered, "What do you mean?"

"Literally," D answered. "I've told you before, I never caught them, they came over on their own."

"How is that possible?" Alice was incredulous.

How could such a thing happen in this world?

D shrugged helplessly, "But that's how it is, I'm just as surprised."

"Then what about this Dragonite?" Alice turned her gaze to the only remaining Dragonite on the ranch.

"It?" D chuckled, "Only it is my Dragonite, my partner with whom I live."

Hearing this, Dragonite was delighted, knowing it was different from the others.

Dragonite nodded repeatedly in agreement with D, "Roar!" That's right!

Alice was slightly taken aback, then shook her head, saying, "Even though you say you haven't caught them, I can tell they've chosen you!"

D smiled without answering.

This man, just what kind of charm did he possess to attract so many Dragonite and Dragonair? Alice couldn't help but feel perplexed.

Her intuition told her if D threw a Poke Ball at them, those Dragonite and Dragonair definitely wouldn't refuse.

Perhaps deep down, they were still hoping, hoping that D would throw a Poke Ball at them.

This truly is... enviable!

"It's getting late, let's wrap up today's shooting!"

Regarding the urban legend of the "delivery Dragonite," Alice's heart had already grasped the gist.

Looking at the sunset in the sky, she continued, "I will come to the ranch tomorrow morning to shoot the content of the Dragonite's work, and then it can be concluded."

---

"Later, I'll return to the TV station for editing, and then it can be broadcast. I'm sure this episode will cause quite a sensation, it's really exciting!"

"I hope this episode will bring me more customers," D said with a smile.

Hearing this, Alice was also very excited: "Maybe it'll attract some powerful Trainers too!"

D: "I'm looking forward to it. If the goods from the ranch can be favored by these Trainers, everyone will be very happy."

*

League Year 198, November 10th, winter.

That evening, a show named "Man and Dragon" was officially broadcast on Paldea's most renowned news TV station.

As the program began, the scene showed a group of Dragonites with satchels circling over a small rural town.

The moment these Dragonites appeared, they instantly grabbed the attention of a large group of Trainers in front of their TVs.

"It's Dragonite!"

"These are very rare Pokemon! How could they appear in this place?"

After a brief shock, a series of questions emerged in the viewers' minds.

Soon after, the scene on the screen began to change.

Those clever Dragonites, upon reaching the town, immediately flew off in different directions, landing in the town.

The camera zoomed in slowly; a Dragonite with a red satchel had just landed where the photographer was.

The Dragonite extended its chubby paw and knocked on the door of the house in front of it.

Then the door opened, and a kind, amiable old lady came out.

The Dragonite took two bottles of fresh milk from its bag and handed them to her, then turned and flew away with a smile.

The Dragonite's figure gradually receded in the camera.

And this scene thoroughly aroused the curiosity of a group of viewers.

"Did I see that right? Was that Dragonite delivering milk to that old lady?"

"This is unbelievable! Like someone specifically trained it for this!"

"This Dragonite is delivering milk, what about the other Dragonites? Could they also be playing the role of delivery workers?"

"Oh my gosh! Isn't this like a luxury version of Pelipper used to deliver mail? This is so extravagant!"

"Who could it be that managed to tame so many Dragonites and have them deliver goods!"

At that moment, a question arose in all the viewers' minds.

And then, the image on the screen gradually transformed from the town to a rural ranch.

A young man with a mysterious red Pokemon stood under the eaves, gazing at the sky.

Soon, some silhouettes appeared in the clear sky.

It's Dragonite! The group of delivery Dragonites is back!

Is it this man who raises this group of Dragonites?

"Hello everyone, I'm Alice!" Alice appeared on the screen, smiling and greeting: "I'm sure some viewers have heard the urban legend of 'Delivery Dragonite' during their travels. Alice is here today to investigate the truth behind this urban legend!"

"And as you can see, the legend is true! And the place I visited today is the birthplace of the legend, Porto Marinada Town!"

"And these delivery Dragonites are all the Pokemon raised by Mr. D on this ranch!"

"It's not just four Dragonites! There are also five Dragonairs on this ranch, which means there will eventually be nine Dragonites in total, an astonishing number! Even Alice is very surprised."

Upon hearing this, the audience in front of the TVs all showed shocked expressions.

Nine... Dragonites?

How is that possible? They didn't hear it wrong, did they?

As everyone knows, Dragonite is a very rare Pokemon, and some people may never encounter one in their lifetime. They usually dwell deep in the oceans, far from human civilization.

Many Trainers have set out to search for traces of this Pokemon over the sea, but all returned empty-handed.

And now, that rural ranch is actually raising nine Dragonites at once!

Who in the world is that man?!

Meanwhile, in the ancestral home of the Dragon Master clan.

An elderly man with gray hair was brought in front of the TV by the younger generation of the clan, and upon seeing the young man sending Dragonite to deliver goods, he couldn't help but reveal a shocked expression.

Who is that guy? How can he be better at taming Dragon Type Pokemon than our Dragon Master family?!

Even if our whole family were combined, we wouldn't have that many Dragonites!

No, that's not right!

The old man quickly came to his senses, grabbed his cane, and forcefully tapped the floor, shouting:

"This is utter nonsense! How can this kid let a Pokemon like Dragonite do that kind of job!"

Dragon Type Pokemon are so noble and strong; they are naturally meant to be active on the battlefield.

Every member of the Dragon Master clan takes pride in taming Dragon Type Pokemon and is proud of raising powerful Dragon Type Pokemon.

And now, someone is so wastefully squandering them!

"Speaking of Mr. D and Dragonite, there was once a very touching story between them! It was because of this incident that the Dragonites chose Mr. D and stayed at the ranch to help him work."

With that, Alice slowly began telling the audience the story of how D helped Dragonite become stronger, and how Dragonite grew from a weak Dragonite into a powerful one.

---

At the end of the story, the transformed Dragonite did not leave after becoming stronger as agreed.

On the contrary, as the human and the Dragonite lived together, they gradually grew accustomed to each other's presence, and from then on, the Dragonite stayed at the ranch.

"I must say, Mr. D is indeed a very charming person!" Alice exclaimed, "The other Dragonite were also drawn by his charm and came to the ranch because of it."

"Alright, that's the full content of the mysterious urban legend 'Dragonite Delivery Service'! Thank you very much for watching!"

Alice smiled slightly at the camera, and then the screen image changed.

In the shot, the young man named D tenderly distributed Poffins to the Dragonites, and every Dragonite standing before him was filled with anticipation and happiness.

This harmonious scene of humans and Pokémon gradually faded to present the word "end."

This signifies that today's episode has come to an end.

Meanwhile, in a mansion located in Cabo Poco Town.

A bright-eyed girl about six years old, with a ponytail and a cheerful, lively personality, excitedly watched the images on the TV.

So many Pokémon! A human who can capture so many Pokémon must be a very strong Trainer!

"I've made up my mind!" Nemona suddenly shouted, "I want to go to that place and challenge that Dragonite!"

Upon hearing this, the old butler beside her showed a helpless expression: "Miss Nemona, you are only six years old this year and have not yet reached the age to become a Trainer."

Nemona: "But I've already learned how to battle with Pokémon! Plus, I already have ten Pokémon!"

With that, Nemona couldn't wait to dash out the door.

The old butler helplessly followed behind: "Wait for me, Miss Nemona!"

"This child is truly headstrong..." On the other end of the sofa, a woman about thirty years old shook her head helplessly, "I don't even know whom she inherited this personality from."

The man reading the newspaper on the other side wore a doting expression: "If Nemona wants to go, then let her. In four years, she will become a Trainer and embark on her own path, so why not let her get an early experience now."

*

At the Cabo Poco Lighthouse, Arven and Mabosstiff were nestled on the sofa, leaning on each other while watching the television in front of them.

On the other side, there was a call from the depths of the Paldea Giant Pit.

Professor Turo and Professor Sada's faces appeared on the screen. They looked at Arven and asked, "Have you had dinner yet?"

Arven buried his head in Mabosstiff's fur, not looking at his parents, and answered in a muffled voice, "I had dinner; I made sandwiches."

"That's good," Professor Sada praised, "Arven is so capable, already able to make sandwiches. With this, your dad and I can feel at ease."

Arven: "..."

As expected, it was the same pair of research-obsessed parents he was familiar with!

Young Arven sighed inwardly, feeling a bit sad.

At this age, other people's parents could accompany them, but he could only live with Mabosstiff.

He had to eat by himself, dress himself, sleep by himself; compared to other peers, he seemed almost like an adult.

Yet, he didn't want to become such an adult...

Seeing Arven like this, both Professor Sada and Professor Turo exchanged glances, feeling somewhat helpless.

"Arven, be good. Our research is in a critical phase, and we cannot stop now. However, we promise that when it's finished, we will immediately return to find you and take you to the amusement park." Professor Sada assured.

At this moment, Professor Turo noticed the program playing on the TV.

Looking at D and Koraidon on the screen, he displayed a look of surprise.

Professor Sada also saw that scene and felt a bit taken aback, but knowing D's character, she wasn't entirely surprised.

Instead, she smiled and suggested to her son, "If you find nothing to do, you can head to Porto Marinada Town and help D at the ranch! I heard he recently caught some rare Pokémon."

"If you go there, I believe he'd be happy to introduce the Pokémon at the ranch to you."

Hearing this, Arven couldn't help but envision the image of D and the lively and warm atmosphere at the ranch, and he felt a bit tempted.

Seeing this, Professor Sada smiled, "Then it's settled, we'll get in touch with D."

Without responding, Arven acquiesced.

Both Professor Sada and Professor Turo ended the call, preparing to contact D far away in Porto Marinada Town.

*

In Levincia City, after a full day of Gym challenges, Lono returned home, exhausted, and collapsed onto the sofa.

The Magnemite hairpieces on her head hung weakly to the side, as devoid of energy as their owner.

Lono turned on the TV and listened to its sounds with her eyes closed.

"...I must say, Mr. D is indeed a very charming person!"

"I believe these Pokémon living at the ranch must have felt his warmth and charm, which is why they chose to stay there."

Upon hearing this, Lono opened her eyes just in time to see the gentle man distributing Poffins to several Dragonites.

"Yawn!" Lono yawned tiredly, watching the man's expression and actions on the screen.

She immediately saw that he was also someone who loved Pokémon.

"Huh? A Trainer who raises nine Dragonites?" Recognizing the content of the program, Lono couldn't help but show surprise.

As a Gym Leader, she's well aware of what this signifies.

Lono suddenly became interested.

This guy is no ordinary person! To raise such precious Pokémon as Dragonite to deliver goods to guests, that's quite interesting!
