# 276: Chapter 273: Red Cape and Meowth Hammer, Superhero and Pawmot!

After dark, D checked the facilities in the ranch and then stayed at home with Pokemon like Koraidon, turning on the TV and casually tuning into the Paldea News TV Station.

As expected, it was airing the program "Man and Dragon" filmed by Alice.

D watched the TV with great interest. This was his first time experiencing something like this, and it was quite new to him.

Seeing himself on the screen, D couldn't help but think: Does this mean he's on TV now?

"I hope this documentary will spread the name of the ranch," D murmured to himself.

A journey of a thousand miles begins with a single step.

Although the ranch is not well-known yet, D believes that one day the name Dragonite Delivery will spread throughout the entire Paldea Region!

*

The next morning, life returned to its usual routine.

After returning from the Galar Region, there wasn't much else outside that needed D's attention.

So he stayed at home, working and playing with the Pokemon.

When he got up in the morning, the first thing D would do was milk the Gogoat, then bottle the freshest milk and hand it over to the Dragonite delivery service to deliver it to the town's customers.

After milking, the Indeedee sibling prepared breakfast, and then D would dine with everyone on the ranch.

After breakfast, the Indeedee brother and sister began tidying up the room messed up by Pawmot and Cyclizar, while D went out to do the ranch work.

Pouring drinking water and Grassy Seed into the Gogoat's trough, filling the stable with Berries and carrots, collecting MooMoo Fresh Milk from the Miltank.

Collecting honey from the Combee hives, gathering Rock Salt from Naclstack in the Underground Cave, and picking up eggs laid by the hens last night at the Chicken House.

After tending to the Pokemon in the ranch, D returned to the kitchen to make Grassy Seed for the Gogoat and check the progress of fruit wine and rose jam in the cellar.

When all the work was done, it was lunchtime.

D would dine with the Pokemon, then go to the Glass Greenhouse for a short rest and have afternoon tea with Calyrex.

Around three or four o'clock, he would go to the Reversed World to continue developing Sky Island, transforming it into the ranch's backyard.

The Pokemon in the ranch also had their own things to do.

Playing, frolicking, running...

The weather was quite good today, and D basked in the sun, listening to the sound of the Pokemon frolicking outside, feeling that life was complete as it was.

"Woo wah~?"

Just then, the voice of Tinkatink came from outside.

D opened his eyes, walked out the door, and saw the petite Tinkatink holding a hammer and looking around, seemingly searching for something.

Seeing D, Tinkatink's eyes lit up, and it quickly ran over, energetically raising the hammer in its hand.

"Woo wah~!"

D's eyes lit up, and he quickly reached out to take the hammer Tinkatink handed over.

"Is it finished?"

Tinkatink nodded repeatedly: "Woo wah!" That's right! This hammer is the Final Hammer it poured all its heart and soul into crafting!

D looked down at the heavy hammer in his hand.

The hammer gleamed with a silver metallic sheen, distinct from Tinkatink's own hammer.

The hammer Tinkatink held was small and delicate, just like Tinkatink itself.

But the hammer given to D was larger, with a square head, and it emitted a captivating silver glow.

Clearly, this hammer was crafted by Tinkatink specifically for Pawmot.

"Amazing!" D couldn't help but exclaim.

Even though he had heard tales about Tinkatink Pokemon, witnessing their hammer-making ability was still a marvel to D.

No need for any auxiliary tools or high-temperature smelting; just relying on those tiny hands and the Steel Type Energy within could create such an excellent hammer.

Tinkatink looked over expectantly.

Noticing its expression, D smiled and praised, "I really like this hammer. Thank you for making it, Tinkatink!"

"Woo wah~"

Hearing this, Tinkatink's eyes brightened, looking very happy.

The Tinkatink of today no longer had the frail, dispirited look from the forest fire days.

After recreating its hammer, its spirit and energy seemed to have fully recovered.

Perhaps, this was the true Tinkatink.

D was genuinely happy for Tinkatink's ability to emerge from the shadows.

He reached out to pat Tinkatink's head, then turned to the roof of the Chicken House and said, "Let's go, let's deliver it to Pawmot!"

"Woo wah!"

Tinkatink nodded excitedly, then followed D with the hammer.

They arrived below the Chicken House, and D called out. Pawmot, who was sunbathing on the roof, immediately flew down.

"Pawmot?"

D handed the hammer to it, expectantly saying, "Want to try it?"

Pawmot took the hammer, surprised to find it surprisingly fitting for its size.

But after the surprise, Pawmot was puzzled. It looked at D, confused: "Pawmot?"

Is this a gift for it? But how is it supposed to use it?

Seeing this, D remembered that Pawmot didn't know about Thunder God, so it naturally didn't understand the meaning of the gift.

He cleared his throat, sat down, and prepared to tell them the story of Thunder God.

Noticing this, the two Pokemon in front of him also sat down.

D began to speak slowly, "A long time ago, there was a Pokemon named Thor, the Thunder God. It wore a Red Cloak symbolizing hope and carried Mjolnir, traveling the world to punish evil and help weak Pokemon."

"It could transfer the Spark within its body to the hammer, enveloping it in electricity to attack opponents, using this method to punish many bad people."

"Those helped by it, both humans and Pokemon, called it a superhero. Over time, that figure in the Red Cloak with Mjolnir flying in the sky became everyone's beacon of hope."

"Pawmot..."

"Woo wah..."

After hearing the story, both Pawmot and Tinkatink seemed filled with longing.

Tinkatink suddenly had an epiphany, remembering how during the forest fire, Pawmot, much like the Thunder God in the story, flew around in the sky in a Red Cloak, searching for Pokemon in need.

Tinkatink looked admiringly at Pawmot: "Woo wah?!"

Seeing Tinkatink's reaction, D also recalled the scene from then, and with Pawmot showing a slightly shy expression, he nodded with a smile:

"That's right, Pawmot is a superhero too."

Hearing this, Pawmot's eyes instantly brightened.

Recalling the Pokemon in the story named Thor, Pawmot tried to emulate it, gripping the hammer tightly and diligently summoning its Electric Type Energy.

The next moment, zapping currents surged from Pawmot and, under its control, flowed into the hammer.

Sparks flew, and the hammer in its hand burst forth with a powerful lightning.

Pawmot couldn't believe what it was seeing, exclaiming in amazement.

"Pawmot?!"

---

---

---

---

# 277: Chapter 274: Is This Pawmot Legal? (4k)

"Pa Mo, Pa Mo!" (I am the Thunder God Pawmo, for the glory of the ranch!)

Five minutes later, Pawmo was flying around with the hammer above the ranch, much like a child with a new toy.

D and Tinkatink stood below, watching this scene.

After the initial surprise, Pawmo quickly fell in love with the hammer.

To play the superhero called Thunder God Thor that D had just described, Pawmo even went back to its room to change into its red cape.

Looking at it now, it really does resemble something like that.

In D's mind, the image of Pawmo electrifying the hammer just now appeared, stirring thoughts within him:

"Judging from how the hammer performed after being electrified, the conductivity of the Corviknight Feather is quite impressive and can perfectly adapt to Pawmo's Electric Energy."

"The Electric Energy has enhanced the power of the hammer, and as it stands, Pawmo seems to have instinctively learned to use the electrified hammer to attack without any guidance... What is this? A self-created move, Mjolnir?"

"I wonder if, according to the Paldea League's battle rules, Pawmo would be allowed to enter battles with the hammer."

"But, it should be allowed, right?" D speculated.

After all, Tinkaton and similar Pokemon also wield hammers in battles.

Moreover, there are even Pokemon like Maushold and Dragapult that join battles with their families.

In a sense, this hammer can be considered Pawmo's exclusive weapon.

And from today onwards, Pawmo would no longer be just a Normal Pawmo, but a special individual among the Pawmo tribe, named the Thunder God Pawmo of the Pokemon.

D had a faint premonition deep down that what he was doing now wasn't in vain, but was, in fact, the creation of a new legend.

It's truly exciting to think how far the Pawmo who ate the Lightning Steamed Bun could go in the future!

At this moment, his sister, Indeedee, came out of the house and called out to D: "Ai ee!"

D realized that his sister Indeedee needed something from him, so he said to Pawmo and Tinkatink:

"You two play here, by the way, Pawmo, the Tinkaton family is very skilled in using hammer-type weapons. If there's anything you don't understand, you can consult with it!"

Upon hearing this, Pawmo's eyes lit up and it looked towards Tinkatink expectantly, asking, "Pa Mo?"

Being looked at by its idol with such an expectant gaze, Tinkatink felt a little shy but nodded nonetheless.

"Woo ah."

D was right; every Tinkatink is extremely skilled in using hammers, as if these methods are innate within their minds.

Seeing this, Pawmo quickly asked Tinkatink to teach it.

Tinkatink, delighted to finally be of help since being rescued and having freeloaded here, immediately agreed.

The two hammer-wielding Pokemon joined hands and ran to a nearby open space to practice hammer techniques.

D then turned around and went back to the living room with his sister Indeedee.

As soon as he entered, he heard the sound of a video call.

Huh? Who could be calling at this hour?

D was somewhat surprised as he went forward to answer the call, and the next second, Professor Sada's spirited face appeared on the screen.

"Long time no see, D." Professor Sada greeted him with a smile.

After a brief moment of daze, D quickly regained his composure: "Long time no see, Professor. Koraidon is outside basking in the sun; I'll go get it."

However, Professor Sada shook her head helplessly: "No, this call isn't for Koraidon; I need to ask a personal favor."

Hmm? D was slightly taken aback, then immediately said: "Please, go ahead."

"It's about Arven." Speaking about her son, Professor Sada showed a hint of guilt: "The research in Zone Zero is at a critical stage, and Turo and I can't leave for long."

"For all these years, Arven has been living alone. We owe him a lot. Yesterday, during my communication with him, I saw the child in a dark room by himself. Compared to his peers, he is too lonely and mature."

"Turo and I have always hoped that Arven could go out more and engage with interesting things, but he has always been sullen and uninterested."

"When I mentioned your ranch yesterday, Arven seemed a bit inclined, so I wanted to ask you a favor, to take care of Arven for a few days and introduce him to the world outside."

"As a token of my gratitude."

Here, Professor Sada paused slightly, then smiled and said:

"Last time you didn't get to capture your desired Paradox Pokemon. Recently, there have been new achievements at the Pokemon Research Institute regarding Paradox Pokemon. When the time comes, if there are Paradox Pokemon compatible with this era, Turo and I will do our best to give you the opportunity to care for it."

D: "..."

He showed a helpless expression: "I have no problem with this, but Professor, you know this might only be a temporary solution."

Professor Sada sighed: "I know, this is where we as parents haven't fulfilled our responsibility, but there's nothing we can do…"

---

Upon hearing this, D couldn't help but sigh.

So be it!

If Arven is destined to have such a tragic fate in the future, then let him create some memorable childhood moments for Arven now!

He didn't say much more, simply agreeing silently to Professor Sada's request.

"Thank you very much," Professor Sada said gratefully. "In a week, the lab will be on break, and then Turo and I will return to the Paldea Region to pick up Arven."

"Okay," D nodded.

After hanging up the phone, D stood there thinking for a while, then went up to the second floor to prepare the guest room.

"In the next couple of days, there will be a guest coming to the ranch," D said as he made the bed, speaking to the Indeedee Sibling behind him. "He's the son of the two professors, named Arven."

"Indeedee~"

The younger Indeedee nodded happily, ensuring to take good care of the guest!

D smiled; he had met Arven before. Arven wasn't a brat; on the contrary, he was very well-behaved and sensible.

According to the professors, Arven should arrive at the ranch by tomorrow afternoon, taking the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi from Cabo Poco Town.

During this time, D meticulously cleaned the rooms on the second floor and then waited with a group of Pokémon for Arven's arrival.

The following afternoon at three, D was in the wine cellar pouring fruit wine when a commotion suddenly erupted in the ranch.

D initially thought Arven had arrived, so he stopped what he was doing, preparing to go out and greet him.

But when he went outside, he discovered it wasn't Arven; instead, a low-key but luxurious black car had arrived.

D: "?"

The car stopped in front of the ranch.

In D's bewildered gaze, two individuals emerged from the car.

One was an elderly man with graying hair, dressed in a black suit with a white shirt underneath, looking like a butler.

And a short girl with a ponytail and bright, spirited eyes.

As soon as the little girl got out of the car, her gaze locked onto D from a distance.

Then, she pulled out a red and white Poke Ball from her pocket, energetically saying, "Let's battle!"

D: "???"

The old butler beside the girl said helplessly, "Miss Nemona, you're too eager."

Ni... Nemona?!

D was a bit shocked. Was this little girl really Nemona? No, that couldn't be right. How could Nemona be here?!

At this moment, the old butler stepped forward and politely said to D:

"Sorry to intrude, Mr. Rancher. This is Miss Nemona. We came from Cabo Poco Town. Miss Nemona saw you with those Dragonites on TV and rushed over to challenge you to a Pokémon battle."

Upon hearing this, D instantly realized what was going on.

He looked at the little girl, thinking it was typical of Nemona's "anything goes" personality!

But...

D opened his mouth, about to explain he wasn't a trainer, when a pink figure suddenly flew over the ranch.

The pink figure, adorned in a majestic red cape, descended from the sky holding a hammer, the wind making its cape rustle loudly.

That Pokémon was...

Nemona's gaze was instantly captured.

The old butler beside her was even more shocked, his mouth agape.

A flying Pawmot?!

Could it be that his old eyes were failing him?

And it was a shiny Pawmot!

Pawmot was so excited to stop in front of D.

"Pawmo!"

It had already learned how to use the hammer!

With that, Pawmot eagerly demonstrated to D.

A surge of blue lightning erupted from its body, enveloping it entirely, even turning its eyes a pale blue.

A terrifying amount of Electric Type Energy surged madly into Pawmot's hammer.

Pawmot swung the hammer with great force, flying high into the sky; the hammer's spinning speed increased until it burst with terrifying lightning and sparks.

At a certain height, with an emphatic "Pawmo," Pawmot's pink figure descended from the sky, delivering a lightning hammer strike to the ground.

Suddenly, intense sparks scattered everywhere, and the previously flat ground was even smashed into a large pit.

D widened his eyes, staring in disbelief at the scene.

What was that move, and why was it so powerful?!

This exceeded all his expectations...

Across, the old butler's face mirrored astonishment; he was stunned, unable to say a word, just staring blankly at the extraordinary Pawmot.

That move... what move was that? He had never seen it before!

What kind of Pokémon was this? Was this pink, girlish Pokémon really the Pawmot he was familiar with?!

Beside him, Nemona's eyes sparkled as she gazed at Pawmot in the distance.

She clenched her fists excitedly, her eyes shining with a fierce light: "Amazing!"

"Pawmo?"

Pawmot finally noticed the visitors, turning to look at the old butler and Nemona behind.

Nemona had already pressed the button on her red and white ball.

With a flash of white light, a tiny, fluffy Pawmi appeared before Pawmot.

Nemona's desire to battle was burning hot: "Mr. Rancher, please have a Pokémon battle with me!"

Hearing this, D instinctively lowered his head to look at the young Pawmi.

Pawmot also looked down at the Pawmi, so much smaller than itself.

Nemona's Pawmi also looked up at Pawmot, amazed as her fluffy face showed surprise at Pawmot's flight.

"Pawmo?"

Pawmot was a bit unsure, turning to D for guidance.

D looked at Nemona: "...This doesn't seem appropriate, right?"

Nemona was momentarily taken aback.

D continued: "If the power difference is too big, forcing a Pokémon to battle won't yield results and might even put the Pokémon in danger. That's not what a competent trainer should do."

Nemona, although passionate about Pokémon battles, didn't want to see this happen. She glanced at the Pawmi before her and at the Poke Ball in her hand, ultimately returning Pawmi to the ball.

The old butler finally snapped back to reality, noticing other Pokémon drawn by the commotion and realizing this wasn't an ordinary ranch.

Nor was the man before him an ordinary person; he was likely a powerful trainer hiding in seclusion.

His long-standing butler training allowed him to remain calm even in this situation.

Seeing Nemona abandon the idea of battling, the old butler first spoke to her:

"Miss Nemona can wait until she grows up to have a Pokémon battle with this trainer."

Upon hearing this, Nemona's eyes lit up, no longer as disappointed as she was earlier.

Seeing this, the old butler smiled slightly, then stepped forward, politely inquiring:

"Mr. Rancher, may we tour your ranch? We've never seen so many Dragonites. Miss Nemona and I are willing to pay the appropriate entrance fee."

D was slightly taken aback, then understood their purpose for coming.

They wanted Nemona to learn about Dragonite through personal exposure, providing her the best educational environment.

As expected of a young lady from a wealthy family!

Since it was no big deal, D shook his head, saying, "No need for the entrance fee; if you'd like to visit, then follow me."

The old butler responded courteously, "Thank you very much."

With that, D prepared to show Nemona and the old butler around the ranch.

Just then, a voice sounded from behind them.

"Uh... Excuse me!"

D turned to see Arven, who had somehow arrived at the ranch, standing shyly nearby, raising his right hand towards the group.

Whoa!

Seeing this, D raised an eyebrow.

What a coincidence! Nemona had come, and now Arven had arrived too.

All that's missing is Penny, and the trio of childhood friends would be complete.

---

---

---

---

# 278: Chapter 275: Traveling Koraidon Is Back and Brought You a Gift (4k)

"Mr. D..."

Arven clutched the strap of his backpack somewhat apprehensively, looking towards D not far away.

D gave him a slight smile and said, "Welcome, Arven, don't worry. Professor Sada has already talked to me. For the next few days, just make yourself at home here."

Hearing this, Arven felt a sense of relief.

Growing up alone had taught him a lot early on.

Being a guest on others' ranches made Arven worry that he might cause trouble for Mr. D.

Arven walked over to D with his backpack.

The old butler and Nemona noticed this quiet, reserved boy.

"Professor Sada..." Nemona picked up on the key information from their conversation.

As a reserve trainer, no one in the Paldea Region didn't know Professor Sada's name.

The cutting-edge Pokemon Doctor, who developed the Terastal phenomenon, allowing Pokemon to Terastalize during battles.

"Are you Professor Sada's son?" Nemona asked in surprise.

Upon hearing this, Arven looked at the girl who was a bit younger than him and nodded silently.

"Wow!" Nemona said excitedly, "Professor Sada and Professor Turo are the most amazing professors in the Paldea region! By the way, as their son, do you know how to battle Pokemon? Would you like to have a Pokemon battle with me?"

Arven: "..."

As expected from Nemona, who loves Pokemon battles! Seeing Arven a bit at a loss for words, D timely interjected:

"Alright, Nemona. Arven just arrived at the ranch and must be tired from the journey. He needs to rest for a bit."

While speaking, a peculiar thought rose in D's mind.

How should I put it? It feels a bit like looking after relatives' children...

"Alright then." Nemona looked a bit disappointed.

The old butler seemed helpless, saying, "Miss Nemona, you should rest a bit too."

Nemona shook her head immediately, "I'm not tired! Rancher, where are the Dragonite? Can I see them?"

"Of course." D nodded, "The Dragonite are sunbathing under the tree over there."

With that, he led the way.

The group crossed the fields and reached an open pasture.

By then, many Pokemon had already gathered there.

Besides the four Dragonite and five Dragonair, there were Glaceon and Cyclizar running on the grass.

Not far off, Koraidon lounged on the grass, leisurely enjoying the beautiful afternoon.

Looking further, in a grassy area surrounded by a wooden fence, three Gogoat could be seen.

On the other side of the fence, two majestic black and white horses galloped around the pasture, with a mysterious Pokemon with a huge head riding one of them.

Upon arriving, Nemona was instantly captivated by these Pokemon.

"Dragonair, Dragonite, Cyclizar, Glaceon, Dolliv, Gogoat... and so many Pokemon I've never seen before!"

Wow, wow, wow!

This place... is this paradise?

Nemona immediately rushed excitedly towards the group of Pokemon.

The old butler tried to stop her, "Miss Nemona!"

But was unsuccessful.

The elderly white-haired man sighed helplessly, "Miss Nemona is always so full of energy."

D smiled as he watched this scene, "I can see that."

Then, D looked at Arven beside him and said:

"There's been a lot of change in the ranch recently, and quite a few new Pokemon have joined. Would you like to take a look?"

Arven looked intrigued but didn't rush forward like Nemona. Instead, he politely bowed and thanked, "Thank you, Mr. D!"

"Let's go then!"

D led Arven to catch up with Nemona.

Nemona was eagerly studying Koraidon, observing this large, muscular Pokemon exuding a wild aura.

Koraidon, resting with eyes closed, sensed the commotion nearby, opened its eyes slightly, and upon seeing the human in front, closed its eyes once more.

Oh, it's this human!

Koraidon remembered her name. Wasn't it Nemona?

The reason it remembered her was due to the next few years when this human, who loved Pokemon battles, would often come to the ranch.

Whether to challenge the Gogoat's Grass Trial or Pawmot's Thunder Trial, she even challenged "it" once.

"Rancher, what kind of Pokemon is this?" Nemona asked.

"This one?" D introduced with a cheerful smile, "This is Koraidon."

Nemona: "Koraidon?"

D nodded, "That's the name Professor Sada gave it. It's a Pokemon discovered by Professor Sada."

"Amazing!" Nemona exclaimed excitedly, "It must be very powerful, right?"

D: "That's right, Koraidon is a very powerful Pokemon. It has always protected this ranch, and thanks to it, the ranch has developed into what it is today!"

"Such a powerful Pokemon... I would love to battle it!" Nemona's eyes were full of hope, but she also knew she was not a trainer yet.

---

Thus, Nemona made a decision in her heart — when she becomes a Trainer in the future, she must come here to challenge this Pokemon, and that Pawmot!

After seeing Koraidon, Nemona ran over to the Dragonites.

D followed behind with Arven, and he said to Arven and Nemona, looking at the Dragonites:

"These Dragonites are currently working as delivery personnel on the ranch. They come to the ranch every morning and help me deliver goods to the customers who ordered."

"Rawr~" The Dragonites greeted D happily.

"So cute!" Nemona's gaze was instantly drawn to the adorable appearances of the Dragonites.

Beside them, the old butler looked at the young man next to him in shock.

Pokemon like Dragonite are so rare, yet this person not only raises so many, but actually uses them for deliveries!

This is simply too extravagant!

Being able to do such a thing, this young man is no ordinary person!

At this moment, Calyrex riding on Spectrier passed by in the distance.

As they drew near, he deliberately slowed his pace and nodded toward D from a distance, greeting him.

Another Pokemon I've never seen before!

Nemona felt her vision overwhelmed; she quickly asked, "Ranch owner, sir, what Pokemon is that?"

It can even ride a horse!

"That is Calyrex," D introduced, "The Pokemon he is riding is named Spectrier, his royal steed."

Royal? Nemona did not react, but the old butler beside her understood.

Using the term royal... that's a word only a king can use!

He paused for a moment, then looked at the dignified and majestic Pokemon, uncertainly repeating: "Royal?"

D nodded: "That's right, precisely, his name is Calyrex, the legendary King Pokemon."

"King Pokemon?" Nemona and Arven were also drawn to this term.

D nodded slightly, then slowly began to tell them the legend of Calyrex.

"A long, long time ago, Calyrex used to ride his beloved steed across the land. His power was immense, and legend has it that when he waved his left hand, crops would instantly mature, and when he waved his right hand, flowers would bloom."

"In a distant era, he used this ability to bring abundance, making the entire region prosperous."

"How amazing!" Nemona exclaimed.

Arven seemed to realize something, and asked somewhat incredulously: "A... legendary Pokemon?"

Legendary Pokemon, with great power, rarely seen in the world, exist only in legends passed among humans.

In the Paldea Region, there are also quite a few legendary Pokemon, like the four calamity Pokemon that were once imprisoned in the kingdom era.

But that time is far too remote from now, and whether those four calamity Pokemon truly exist remains unknown.

Yet now... Mr. D has actually captured a legendary Pokemon?!

D understood Arven's meaning and nodded, saying: "That's correct."

Arven was a bit confused: "But... I don't recall hearing this legend in the Paldea Region?"

D smiled slightly: "That's because everything I just said comes from the ancient legends of the Galar Region, and Calyrex is something I encountered in the Galar Region."

Galar Region? No wonder! Arven suddenly understood.

This is extraordinary! He knew it!

The old butler, who followed Nemona to the ranch, was equally shocked at heart.

He was right to guess! This man is no ordinary person!

Who would have thought such a place was hiding such an impressive trainer.

The old butler had a vague premonition that this man, and his ranch's name, would soon be renowned across the Paldea Region!

At five in the evening, with a heart full of shock, the old butler led a satisfied Nemona on their journey back.

Nemona happily sat by the window, looking outside. Though she didn't get to battle Dragonite on this trip, she saw so many impressive Pokemon.

However, she doesn't plan to give up.

Looking into the rearview mirror at the gradually fading ranch, Nemona made a firm resolution — soon she would return here to battle Dragonite, Pawmot, and that Pokemon named Koraidon!

The old butler watched the spirited young lady and encouraged her: "Miss Nemona, one day, you will also become an impressive trainer."

"Yes! I will work hard!"

*

At the ranch, D stood at the door and watched as the car carrying Nemona gradually drove off into the distance.

"I never thought Nemona would suddenly come to the ranch."

"On this trip, Nemona got to know Koraidon and Arven, and if the story unfolds normally, who knows what it will be like then."

D imagined, thinking it quite interesting.

"Alright, we should head back to sleep!" D withdrew his gaze and headed home with Arven.

As night approached, the Pokemon also returned to the house.

Arven stood in the living room, looking at all the Pokemon, and became even more restrained.

Seeing this scene, D smiled helplessly: "Don't be so nervous, Professor Sada and I are friends, just treat this place like your home."

---

"Thank you." Arven silently expressed his gratitude.

D smiled and continued, "Indeedee has prepared a room for you, it's on the second floor, I'm not sure if you're comfortable with it."

As he spoke, he called over his brother, Indeedee, to take Arven upstairs to have a look.

Brother Indeedee calmly nodded and agreed, "Indeedee."

D added, "Go ahead, I'll prepare dinner!"

The fireplace burned warmly in the house, filling the room with a cozy ambiance. Feeling D's thoughtful care, Arven had a sudden urge to cry.

He quickly bowed his head, holding back tears, and apologized to D, before following brother Indeedee upstairs.

Seeing this, D chuckled and shook his head, turned on the TV, listened to the news broadcast, and began preparing dinner for the Pokémon.

Outside the window, the cold wind howled, while the Pokémon gathered on the soft carpet and sofa, basking by the fire, comfortably exposing their soft bellies.

The voice of the weather news host came from the television.

"The Paldea Region is expected to receive snowfall in the early hours tonight, citizens please prepare in advance to prevent the impact of the cold weather."

Huh? It's going to snow?

D was a bit surprised, but after thinking carefully, he realized it was about time.

Snowfall is great! Once the snow covers the ground, he could take the Pokémon to the ranch to build snowmen and have snowball fights!

D hummed a little tune, eagerly anticipating the snowfall.

Meanwhile, on the other side of this planet, on an island nestled in the deep blue sea.

Mew and Teddiursa held a bunch of leeks, waving goodbye in front of a Farfetch'd.

Not long ago, they arrived on this island and met the Farfetch'd in the forest needing assistance.

After hearing about the Heroic Expedition Team's tales, Farfetch'd immediately sought their help.

Farfetch'd said a Pokémon that loves eating plants came to the forest recently, eating away the roots and leeks growing from the ground.

Unable to find leeks to arm itself, Farfetch'd felt insecure and thus asked the Heroic Expedition Team to help drive away the leek-loving Pokémon.

Mew and Teddiursa accepted the task, and upon reaching the destination, they found it was a Stantler hungry for a long time.

The Stantler migrated from another forest where a lack of food was caused by humans logging trees, thus it had to come here to eat leeks.

To help Farfetch'd complete its task, Mew and Teddiursa gave it their treasured Berries to fill its stomach.

Once fed, Stantler promised not to eat Farfetch'd's weapons anymore.

The matter was resolved perfectly, and Farfetch'd gifted a bundle of leeks to Mew and Teddiursa.

After Farfetch'd left, Teddiursa looked down at the leeks in its hand, "Yummy..." (Strange taste...)

Mew tilted its head, "Miu?" (Should we eat them?)

Teddiursa sighed, "Yummy..." (Not tasty...)

It thought of Farfetch'd using these leeks as weapons, feeling they might be quite useful tools for some Pokémon.

Decided!

Teddiursa, a sudden idea sparkling in its mind.

It decided to send these plant stems back to the ranch, giving them to D to see if any Pokémon there might need them!

Speaking of D...

Teddiursa's mind envisioned his tall figure and grew melancholic.

Unknowingly, it had left there for quite a long time.

Teddiursa missed D, missed the ranch, missed D's delicious snacks.

Mew observed the melancholic Teddiursa and couldn't help but tilt its head, "Miu?" (What's wrong?)

Teddiursa replied truthfully, "Yummy..." (I miss D...)

Mew knew not what missing someone meant, never having missed anyone like that.

Seeing Teddiursa's state, it thought it over carefully, then cheerfully suggested, "Miu!" (Then let's return to the ranch!)

---

---

---

---

# 279: Chapter 276: Snow Maze, Calyrex’s Sentiment

In the 198th year of the League calendar, on November 14th, the Paldea Region welcomed its first snowfall of the year.

The snow fell for a whole day and night, and when it finally stopped, the entire world was enveloped in a magnificent snowy landscape.

As soon as the snow stopped, the Pokémon, curious about the snow, asked D to help them put on warm scarves.

With the door opened, a large group of various Pokémon swarmed outside.

Instantly, the once smooth snow was covered with all sorts of plum blossom-shaped footprints.

The Pawmo, running in the middle, was so excited that it accidentally tripped over a stone hidden in the snow, falling with a thud and leaving a "Pawmo" imprint in the snow.

The surrounding Pokémon stopped running when they saw this and gathered around to check on it.

Pawmo, with a face full of snow, got up from the ground, showing a silly smile: "Pawmot..."

D and Arven followed behind, and upon seeing this scene, smiled helplessly and said, "Take it slow."

"Pawmot!"

Pawmo cheerfully responded and quickly got up from the snow.

Upon seeing this, the other Pokémon also smiled, laughing and playing in the snow like a group of children seeing snow for the first time.

Arven's Mabosstiff stood by his side, looking a bit envious at the lively scene.

D noticed this and said, "Mabosstiff can also join in and play!"

"Woof?" Mabosstiff looked up.

Arven reached out and patted Mabosstiff's head, saying, "You can go play with everyone! I'm right here, it's fine."

"Woof!" Mabosstiff got excited.

Seeing this, D called over Alcremie to play with Mabosstiff.

Alcremie, being cheerful and lively, happily agreed and took Mabosstiff to join the others.

Gazing at Mabosstiff's back, D thought about Arven's situation and asked:

"Has Arven always lived alone?"

Arven answered honestly, "Not alone, I've always had Mabosstiff with me. It's a gift from my father and mother, and has been by my side since I was born."

Upon hearing this, D nodded, thinking it was no wonder Arven traveled all over the Paldea Region in the game to find a way to heal Mabosstiff.

A kid like Arven must be very sentimental.

"Have you always lived over in Cabo Poco Town?" D asked.

Arven truthfully replied, "Not always. My father and mother used to take me to the Area Zero Research Institute, and I occasionally stayed there for some time."

"I see!" D thought for a moment, then remembered something: "Speaking of which, are you turning ten next year?"

Arven looked surprised: "How did you know?"

"You forgot? The professor mentioned it last time you came." D said with a smile.

Arven thought carefully and finally remembered.

D: "Do you have any plans for next year? Are you planning to become a Trainer and travel around, or attend Paldea Academy?"

"I... I'm not very good at Pokémon battles," Arven said hesitantly. "I don't know what I want to do in the future either."

D: "..."

In the game, Arven, who isn't good at battles, can still take out six Pokémon over level 60 to battle with the player. If he were good, what would that be like?

However, D remembered that Arven's dream wasn't to become a strong Trainer, but to be a chef who fills Pokémon with energy.

What is this called? A chef who isn't a Trainer isn't a good chef?

D thought cynically but outwardly told Arven, "If you don't know what to do, you should visit Paldea Academy. Maybe you'll find the answer there."

"Okay!" Arven nodded seriously.

At this time, the Pokémon in the snow under Koraidon's lead had begun to build snowmen.

However, Koraidon's technique wasn't very good, resulting in snowmen with big heads and small bodies.

Seeing this, D speechlessly took Arven and walked forward: "You should do it like this, the snowball on top shouldn't be too big..."

Saying this, D suddenly pictured Calyrex in his mind.

D: "..."

He quickly changed his words, "It can also be bigger, just model the snowman after specific Pokémon's appearances."

"Kjira!" Koraidon instantly understood.

Then, it started digging up the snow with its large claws.

The other Pokémon copied, and soon enough, a group of oddly shaped snowmen appeared on the snow.

D: "..."

Forget it, after all, they're just Pokémon. Can't ask too much.

D turned his head to look around and when he saw the Indeedee Sibling, he suddenly recalled the hedge maze he once saw in that manor.

At that time, he remembered a group of Pokémon playing hide-and-seek inside.

So the question is, could he create a small ice maze here for the Pokémon to play in?

Thinking of this, D immediately found the smiling, quietly standing nearby Glaceon, watching everyone play in the snow.

Compared to other Pokémon, this snow wasn't new to Glaceon. It used to live in Glaseado Mountain, where the snow is the same everywhere.

---

Seeing D walking towards it, Glaceon turned its head, "Gla?"

"Do you have a moment? I want to ask you to help me make something." D asked with a smile, then explained his idea to it.

After listening, Glaceon pondered for a moment, then its eyes lit up, it nodded gently, responding with, "Gla!"

"Great, shall we give it a try then?" D asked.

Glaceon nodded.

So, D and Glaceon went to a wide-open grassy field.

D stood behind Glaceon and gave it instructions, "First, use Ice Beam to create an ice wall!"

"Gla!"

Glaceon opened its mouth and released a beam of frosty light.

Rich Ice Type Energy swept across the snowy ground, and where it passed, an ice-blue wall immediately appeared.

"Great, just like that!" Seeing that the method worked, D was delighted and continued, "Do it again, connect several ice walls together!"

"Gla!"

This was very easy for Glaceon, following D's instructions, it continuously used Ice Beam to create wall after wall of ice over the snow.

The outermost four ice walls formed a huge rectangular area together.

Inside the area, there were countless small ice walls of varying lengths, together forming a huge ice maze playground.

The maze had two exits, among them were numerous crisscrossing paths, but there was only one true path that could lead to the exit.

When the maze was completed, other Pokémon were also drawn by the scene, staring in shock at the structure that rose from the ground.

D also looked up at the scene, these ice walls were taller than him, and a single Glaceon could achieve this.

It seemed that summer's cream ice was not eaten in vain!

"Great job!"

D patted Glaceon's head, then called the other Pokémon to introduce the rules of hide and seek to them.

"... Hide and seek is divided into two sides, one side is responsible for hiding, and the other side needs to find the hidden Pokémon."

"If all the hiding Pokémon are found, then the searching side wins, and the first Pokémon to be found must be the seeker in the next round."

"Do you understand?"

The Pokémon were listening in a semi-understanding way, but soon, the clever Pawmo's eyes lit up, "Paw!"

It got it!

Pawmo flew up and spoke to the other Pokémon, "Paw!" It wanted to be the seeker in the first round!

D: "… Unfortunately, during the game, you can't use flying abilities or moves."

Upon hearing this, Pawmo immediately showed a shocked expression.

In that case, how could it find the other Pokémon?!

"Use your eyes," D said.

Pawmo pondered and then suddenly understood.

It got it!

Seeing no objections from the other Pokémon, D said, "Then, you can start now, go hide in the maze! Pawmo will start looking for you after counting to fifty, be careful not to be found!"

Upon hearing this, the Alcremie and Cyclizar among other Pokémon hurriedly ran into the ice maze, each looking for a place to hide.

Even Koraidon was attracted by the game and dashed into the ice maze.

Pawmo closed its eyes and began counting as D instructed.

"Paw!" (1!)

"Paw!" (2!)

…

"Paw!" (35!)

"Paw!" (36!)

Pawmo's counting voice was so loud that even the Pokémon in the maze could hear it.

As the time approached, Pokémon in the maze felt an intense sense of urgency.

Inside the maze, Alcremie frantically looked around, searching for something to hide behind.

Suddenly, it noticed the snow on the ground, and its eyes lit up.

Then, it dived into the snow, hiding its body, not noticing the clear trail of small footprints behind it.

…

In a dead-end corner of the maze.

Cyclizar nervously stared at the wall in front, then ran to a corner and curled up facing the wall, trying to blend in with the wall, thinking Pawmo wouldn't see it.

…

"Paw!" (50!)

Time was up, Pawmo eagerly stepped into the maze.

Pawmo is a very clever Pokémon, quickly noticing footprints in the snow.

"Paw!" (I've found you!) Pawmo intentionally said.

It targeted a set of footprints, and followed them all the way forward, finally stopping where the footprints continued into a dead end.

And in the snow ahead, there was an unidentifiable bulge.

"Paw…" Pawmo carefully moved forward, and then smacked the bulging snow pile.

The next second, a startled scream came from beneath the snow.

"Maaa!"

*

"Looks like Alcremie was the first Pokémon found by Pawmo."

Outside the maze, hearing Alcremie's voice, D couldn't help stretching.

Let the Pokémon play by themselves here, while he turned to check the honey preparation by the Combee in the Glass Greenhouse.

"Arven, do you want to come along?" D asked.

Arven shook his head, "No, Mr. D, I want to stay here with Mabosstiff."

"Okay." D nodded, "If you feel cold, head back to the living room, the fireplace is lit, and you'll warm up quickly."

"Got it!" Arven said gratefully.

D chuckled, then turned to the Glass Greenhouse.

Calyrex wasn't here, D guessed he might be familiarizing himself with the nearby pasture areas.

He didn't think much of it, and after greeting Butterfree, went to the Combee hive.

The stored amount of Honey in the hive wasn't much, and the Combees didn't look very spirited.

D guessed they might be akin to some Pokémon requiring hibernation, becoming less active during this season.

For Combee-like Pokémon, this was quite normal.

Seeing this, D returned to the house, took some honey prepared in advance to help the Combee through winter, and placed it in the hive as reserve food.

After doing all this, Calyrex returned.

He appeared directly in the Glass Greenhouse, and upon seeing D, Calyrex seriously inquired:

"I sensed an aura of deathly silence from the mountains, devoid of life. What is the situation there?"

D immediately realized what Calyrex was referring to, he thought for a moment and replied:

"Before you arrived, there was a wildfire on the back mountain, which burned everything on the mountain, and many Pokémon lost their lives there."

"The Pokémon living in the Berry Garden are those who survived the wildfire, I took them in, promising to help them plant grass, flowers, and trees on the back mountain when spring arrives."

"I see," murmured Calyrex.

Upon hearing about the loss of many Pokémon lives, the kingly Pokémon with a heart full of love felt a wave of sorrow.

He let out a mournful sigh, "If only I could have come sooner, I could have used my power to relocate them to safety."

"Alas!" sighed D, "What we can do now is help the surviving Pokémon rebuild their homes."

Calyrex closed his eyes to mourn for the Pokémon who had passed away, then reopened them moments later.

"In that case, I shall come to help as well!"

"Yes." D nodded, planning the purchase of seedlings and grass seeds in his mind.

Besides the Pokémon on the farm, some who survived the wildfire were rescued by the Paldea League and live in a shelter in town.

By then, the Paldea League will also send people to participate in the activities of rebuilding the back mountain, in efforts to help the forest recover as much as possible.

# 280: Chapter 277: The Path to Fame, Big Root (4k)

If the Calyrex were to perform a miracle and make the trees on the back mountain grow instantaneously, then its existence would certainly be discovered by the Paldea League.

In that instant, many thoughts crossed D's mind.

Like whether there would be people coveting the existence of Calyrex and how the Paldea League would treat a Pokemon of such significant meaning.

Would they treat it like the Johto League treats Ho-oh and Lugia, by building towers and erecting shrines for worship and homage?

Or would they trap it like Chairman Rose of the Galar League did to Eternatus, using it as a regional power source?

D wasn't worried about the latter.

The current Calyrex has regained its peak power, with Spectrier and Glastrier by its side, ordinary people would likely be reduced to dust by Astral Barrage and Glacial Lance.

As for whether Paldea's chief champion would behave like Galar's Chairman Rose...

D believed that with Geeta's character, she wouldn't do such a thing.

He had met Geeta several times, and she was a champion with great wisdom and charm. Given her character, she would probably prefer to recruit Calyrex into the Paldea League rather than subduing it...

Then, only one possibility remains.

—The Paldea League would strive to promote the deeds of Calyrex to the people of the Paldea Region, establishing buildings like shrines to ensure everyone in Paldea believes in the King of Bountiful Harvests.

This way, it wouldn't annoy Calyrex and would increase its goodwill towards the Paldea Region, thus making Paldea more prosperous.

However, the only downside is that Porto Marinada Town might attract a bunch of unseasoned trainers...

After a moment of inner conflict, D made a decision.

Since it's unavoidable, just let it be!

With Calyrex's help, Gogoat wouldn't need to expend as much energy as before to cultivate the World Tree.

This is just the right opportunity to involve those visiting trainers in the Grass Trial to let Gogoat stretch its legs.

Then, D remembered Pawmot wielding a hammer and dealing blows.

"Hmm, besides the Grass Trial, perhaps we could add a Thunder Trial to increase Pawmot's experience points, refine its combat skills, and make it a stronger Pokemon."

"As for Dragonite, currently, its basic training seems to have reached a bottleneck. To progress further, it needs a lot of practical battle experience."

And where does practical battle experience come from? Undoubtedly, it's from battles.

Remembering Dragonite's ability to summon storms, a new trial name emerged in D's mind—Storm Trial.

This way, the ranch could develop into a complete industry chain.

Trainers and travelers attracted by the Legendary Pokemon Calyrex—participating in the three major trials—boosting the ranch's reputation—drawing more people in.

By the time the ranch becomes famous, the Rotom Phone would probably have been launched.

At that time, the world will officially enter the information age.

New and exciting things like the live streaming industry and online shopping would likely sprout up like mushrooms after the rain.

By then, he could officially establish Dragonite Delivery Company and invite all Dragonite from Dragonite Island.

Equip each Dragonite with a Rotom Phone that has navigation, positioning, and communication functions, and commission them to deliver ranch goods to various places in the Paldea Region.

And the first step to realizing this plan is to wait for those trainers and travelers to arrive.

These people can enhance Calyrex's faith and help Dragonite and Pawmot, among other Pokemon, to exercise and hone their abilities.

Just the right opportunity for killing many birds with one stone, maximizing benefits.

A truly perfect plan!

Perhaps the smile on D's face was too "sinister," which drew a glance from Calyrex nearby.

"What are you thinking?" Calyrex asked.

"I'm thinking about what I'll do next year!" D answered with a bright gaze.

"Next year?" Calyrex had little concept of time's passage.

"It's almost next year." Afraid Calyrex wouldn't understand, D added, "For us humans, a year has three hundred sixty-five days, going through spring, summer, autumn, and winter."

"Just like now, snow falling signifies the year's end. When the ice and snow on the earth melt, life recovers, and flowers and grass grow again on the meadows, it means spring has arrived, and a new cycle begins."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex's mind cleared: "I see, the Crown Tundra snows all year round, always showing the same scene, and it has been many years since I last saw spring."

During that time, he once rode with his beloved steed across Galar Land, witnessing countless beautiful sights.

The blossoming spring, the scorching summer, the refreshing autumn, and the snow-covered winter.

Calyrex couldn't help but fall into memories.

Indeed, as his memories showed, the last time he saw spring was many years ago, so long that he couldn't recall how much time had passed.

"You can see it every year from now on." D said with a smile.

If nothing unexpected occurs, they would still live together for many years.

For D, meeting so many Pokemon, living together with them and watching them eat meals he made, makes this life worthwhile!

---

"I'm really looking forward to it," Calyrex said with a hint of a smile.

He looked forward to the spring that was just around the corner, as well as spending it with D.

"Alright, I still have to find Dragonite and Pawmot to talk to them about something."

Calyrex nodded slightly, watching D leave.

Once out of the Glass Greenhouse, D intended to go directly to Pawmot and Dragonite to talk about the Thunder Trial and Storm Trial.

But upon seeing a group of Pokemon joyfully playing in the Ice Maze, D stopped in his tracks, deciding to wait until they were tired from playing.

As for now...

D planned to return to the house to warm up some Berry Milk for the Pokemon so they could drink it when they came back tired and cold.

D turned around and walked towards the house.

He was about to open the door when he suddenly heard a familiar Growl from behind.

"Ema!"

D froze for a moment, then turned around incredulously, seeing the familiar little Teddiursa behind him, thinking he might be hallucinating.

"Teddiursa?" D asked uncertainly.

"Ema Ema!"

Teddiursa nodded repeatedly, then dropped the bundle of green stuff it was holding onto the ground and ran towards D.

As it got closer, Teddiursa leapt up forcefully, its furry little body jumping into D's arms.

D instinctively reached out to catch it.

Teddiursa lifted its head, its dark, bright eyes sparkling with light: "Ema~"

"Miu~"

At that moment, another Pokemon landed from the sky.

D looked at it and recognized it at a glance as the Mew named "Hero."

Surprised at Teddiursa's unexpected return, D felt delighted inside: "Why did you guys come back all of a sudden?"

"Ema!"

D didn't understand but was still very happy, rarely seeing Teddiursa, he quickly carried it into the living room: "Let's talk inside, Hero, come along! I'll bring some tasty treats for you."

"Miu!" Hearing this, Mew exclaimed happily and followed D into the living room.

D put Teddiursa on the sofa and went to the fridge to fetch some sweets and drinks.

When he returned to the living room with food, Teddiursa was curiously observing its surroundings while sitting on the sofa.

"Ema..."

D understood Teddiursa's intention; it seemed to be saying that things were different here now.

He chuckled and explained, "The old house was too small, after you set out on your journey, I asked the town's craftsmen to build a new one."

"Ema!" Teddiursa realized.

D placed the platter full of Poffins and small cakes on the table, adding a pot of honey that Teddiursa loved most.

Seeing the honey, Teddiursa's eyes lit up immediately.

It picked up the honey and started using its little bear paws to dip the golden honey inside, licking it.

Mew used its tiny hands to pick up a Poffin and ate happily.

"Miu!"

Taking advantage of this moment, D grabbed a few Translation Nuts Cakes to eat, then sat on the sofa and chatted with the two Pokemon.

"Why did you guys come back all of a sudden? I was really surprised!" D asked.

Teddiursa: "Ema, Ema!" (We wanted to see D, so we came back!)

Wanting to see him? Hearing this response, D was initially taken aback, but then felt joy from the bottom of his heart.

It seemed Teddiursa still cared about him a lot.

After that, D said nothing, patiently watching Teddiursa and Mew eat.

After they finished eating, Teddiursa started inspecting its surroundings.

Catching sight of the photo hanging on the right wall, it exclaimed: "Ema!" (That's the one Pelipper sent back!)

D glanced at it and nodded, "Yes! The Pokemon you asked were very reliable, always delivering things to the ranch on time."

Teddiursa happily said: "Ema~" (Everyone's such good Pokemon~)

D chuckled slightly, then asked, "Did you encounter anything interesting on your travels?"

Mew was beside constantly eating Poffins, its little cheeks puffed.

Having filled up, Teddiursa replied: "Ema!" (Lots!)

It excitedly started sharing stories from its journey with D.

Including Zapdos, including Lugia, including Mewtwo, including Ninetales, and including the lone big Dragonite.

Teddiursa recounted that they met Zapdos at an abandoned human power plant.

The poor Zapdos was injured; they gave it some healing Berries and became friends with it.

Upon leaving, it discovered Zapdos feathers on the ground that glowed, so it picked them up and sent them back to the ranch.

D: "..." So the Zapdos feather he made into a Lightning Steamed Bun wasn't forcibly plucked using Teddiursa's "Super Luck" and "Run Away" skills?

"Zapdos didn't attack you?" D couldn't help but ask.

"Ema!" (Of course not!) Teddiursa shook its head, looking puzzled.

D: "I've heard Zapdos is usually very temperamental."

Teddiursa carefully recalled and slowly said: "Ema..." (This Zapdos is a good Zapdos, it's a very good Pokemon, but later Hero and I met another really bad-tempered Zapdos...)

A second Zapdos?

D immediately thought of the movie "The Birth of Lugia."

If he remembered correctly, this world also experienced that storyline, and the Storm Steamed Bun Dragonite ate was obtained from this encounter.

Talking about this memory, Mew and Teddiursa seemed a bit embarrassed.

"Ema..." (That Zapdos was guarding a shiny Treasure Pearl, and Hero and I thought nobody wanted it, so we planned to pick it up and send it back to the ranch. But that bad-tempered Zapdos chased us really far...)

It ended up not catching up...

Teddiursa recalled that Zapdos nearly got infuriated.

But they didn't know that Treasure Pearl was so important!

Thinking of this, Mew and Teddiursa exchanged an embarrassed glance.

D: "..."

Just as he was about to say something else, Mew, having finished its meal, scanned the surroundings, seemingly searching for something.

Not finding what it was looking for, Mew turned its gaze to D and asked: "Miu?" (Where's Mewtwo?)

"Mewtwo?" D realized immediately that Mew seemed to want news about Mewtwo, he answered: "Mewtwo came once, after resting one night with a group of Pokemon at the ranch, it left again, saying it wanted to find a place they could call home."

"Miu..." (What a pity...)

Mew looked regretful; it wanted to see Mewtwo again!

"Don't worry," D consoled, "Mewtwo is still in this world, as long as you keep traveling, one day you'll reunite."

Upon hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa blinked, then immediately filled with fighting spirit.

"Miu!" (That's right!)

"Ema!" (Yes!)

Seeing they wanted to know what happened to the Pokemon who visited the ranch, D then talked about the giant Dragonite settling on Dragonite Island and Ninetales visiting the ranch.

Upon hearing all ended well, Mew and Teddiursa were very pleased.

To Teddiursa, the journey still felt like a dream until now.

It looked at D and said: "Ema..." (Hero and I formed the Heroic Expedition Team and helped so many Pokemon! I love that feeling!)

D smiled warmly, listening to the stories from their journey, he couldn't help but sigh: "That's wonderful!"

The uniqueness of Pokemon friendship and bonds devoid of humans, just like a fairy tale.

Unexpectedly, the door opened, and Indeedee came in carrying a bundle of leeks.

It wondered, "Ai ee?" (Whose stuff is this? Left at the door?)

Witnessing the scene, Teddiursa immediately remembered it hadn't given its gift to D.

Quickly standing up, it took the bundle of leeks from Indeedee and handed it to D.

"Ema!" (To you, D!)

D was somewhat surprised; glancing down, information about these green leeks flashed through his mind.

[Leeks: A special stem plant, extremely rare, delicious, can be used for cooking, long used by Farfetch'd as a weapon, the large root at the bottom can also be carried as an item by some Pokemon.]

# 281: Chapter 278: Mew and the World Tree

Teddiursa: "Immu immu!" (This is a gift from the Farfetch'd that Big Hero helped! It's his favorite leek!)

It was indeed well known that Farfetch'd loved such leeks, and D nodded knowingly.

Farfetch'd, the Wild Duck Pokemon, according to the Pokédex, they only live in places where stalk-like plants grow.

D looked down at the bunch of leeks in his arms.

The so-called stalk-like plant was actually these leeks. For some reason, the leeks in this world were so large, much larger than the ones he ate in his past life, large enough to be used as swords.

Thinking of this, D pulled out a leek and swung it like a sword twice.

The stem sliced through the air, producing a whooshing sound.

Beside him, Teddiursa watched this and nodded repeatedly: "Immu!" (That's right! Farfetch'd swings leeks just like that!)

D imagined the scene of a Farfetch'd fighting with a giant leek and found it inexplicably amusing.

But speaking of which, due to the rarity of these wild-grown leeks, the number of Farfetch'd has been dwindling in recent years.

Because Farfetch'd is a very unique Pokemon, without leeks, they can't survive. So, when facing opponents that try to steal their leeks, they will fight with all their might.

In some places, to protect the dwindling number of Farfetch'd, people have even started specifically growing these leeks to attract Farfetch'd to settle and nest nearby.

"Farfetch'd, huh?" This rare Pokemon, he quite wanted to raise a few, but Farfetch'd only lived where stalk-like plants grew.

D started to brainstorm.

"If I plant these leeks on the ranch, would it attract wild Farfetch'd to settle here?"

It's just that... it seems like the Paldea Region doesn't have Farfetch'd.

D felt a sense of regret.

He placed the bunch of leeks aside, then turned to see Indeedee brother curiously looking at Teddiursa and Mew.

D then remembered that when Indeedee brother came to the ranch, Teddiursa had already set off on a journey, and they hadn't met yet.

Thinking of this, D began introducing the current state of the ranch to Teddiursa and Mew.

"After you left, many new companions have joined the ranch."

"Immu?" Teddiursa now noticed Indeedee brother, blinked, and happily greeted it: "Immu!" (Hello!)

Indeedee brother paused, apparently surprised by the friendly nature of Teddiursa, and responded clumsily, "Ai eee..." (You...hello!)

D smiled slightly: "This is Indeedee brother, he and his sister are our family's caretakers, always managing the household chores. They are very capable Pokemon, even when I'm not at the ranch, they manage everything meticulously."

Hearing D's straightforward praise, Indeedee brother felt a bit embarrassed, after all, these were part of their responsibilities.

And upon hearing these words, Teddiursa's eyes filled with admiration: "Immu!" (Amazing!)

Just like humans!

This made Indeedee brother feel even more embarrassed.

"Besides brother, there are many other new companions, but they're all playing hide and seek in the maze right now, I'll introduce you to them later."

D paused, then asked: "After traveling for so long, you must be exhausted, right? Want to take a nap?"

Teddiursa and Mew indeed felt somewhat tired.

After deciding to return to the ranch that day, they rushed back nonstop from the distant Johto Region.

They crossed the ocean, climbed mountains, and after countless hardships, finally made it back here.

Both Teddiursa and Mew had expended a lot of energy, and having just filled their bellies and basking in the warm environment, the fatigue from the journey surged all at once.

"Immu..." (So tired...)

"Miu..." (Really want to sleep...)

Seeing this, D smiled and said, "Then go take a nap!"

He led the two little ones back to his room, letting them rest in his bed.

Teddiursa lay on the soft bed, inhaling D's scent from it, the room was very warm, which gave Teddiursa an unprecedented sense of security.

It drowsily half-opened its eyes to look at D, muttering hazily: "Immu..." (So glad to have met D...)

D didn't quite hear: "What?"

When he looked over, Teddiursa had already closed its eyes and fallen asleep.

Mew beside them also let out a string of meaningless murmurs: "Miu...Miu..."

Mew: zZZ

Then, it too drifted into slumber.

Watching Mew and Teddiursa's adorable sleeping faces, D's heart softened, then quietly adjusted the temperature for them, and only then tiptoed out of the room, gently closing the door.

At this time, Dragonite and the others, who had been playing all morning, also returned to the living room.

Seeing them, D remembered the main task.

He poured a cup of hot Berry Milk for all the Pokemon and Arven to warm up, then looked at Pawmot and Dragonite.

"Do you want to get stronger?" D asked.

"Roar?"

"Barmo?"

The two Pokemon instantly turned their heads to look over.

The effect of the Translation Nuts Cake had already worn off, D could no longer understand their speech.

But seeing Dragonite's excited eyes and Pawmot's eager expression, even without translation, he could understand what the two Pokemon meant.

---

"I have an idea." D then detailed to the two Pokemon about the Thunder Trial and Storm Trial.

"...Just like Gogoat, they battle with visiting Trainers to gain experience from battling their Pokemon, making you stronger."

"Awoo... Awoo!"

"Pawmo!"

Both Dragonite and Pawmot had seen Gogoat's Grass Trial before.

They knew how powerful Gogoat was, and the thought of being like the three Gogoat made the two Pokemon happily agree.

"Great." D nodded with satisfaction, knowing Dragonite and Pawmot wouldn't refuse.

However, the specifics of the Trial, as well as the tactics and move combinations for Dragonite and Pawmot, still needed to be decided later.

Although he wasn't a professional Trainer, the experiences he had in games could also provide some tactical guidance to the two Pokemon.

At this moment, the other Pokemon in the living room also heard the conversation between D and Dragonite.

Alcremie stood up expectantly, "Mamma?" Would it have its own special Trial too?

"Uh..." This put D in a bit of a dilemma.

After all, Alcremie wasn't a battle Pokemon, and if it were to have a Trial...

A Cream Firing Trial?

Or a luck-based Metronome Trial?

Neither seemed quite right.

After pondering for a moment, D replied, "I haven't thought of something yet. Give me some more time to think it over."

To this, Alcremie was very excited, "Mamma~" It wanted to use its best Metronome to defeat its opponent when the time came!

That said, D felt that Alcremie was just joining in the fun and wasn't seriously intending to battle.

"Oh well, since Alcremie's interested, let it give it a try."

Then D noticed Cyclizar also looking expectantly.

D: "..."

Thinking carefully, Cyclizar didn't seem to have any outstanding qualities at the moment.

Even though it ate the birthday cake that could boost its species strength, it was still the first year, and its specific performance wasn't clear yet.

But seeing Cyclizar's expectant look, D, after some thought, still said, "I'll think of something."

"Gao~" Cyclizar was very happy.

As for Koraidon...

D looked at the lazy Koraidon lying aside, the legendary Winged King didn't seem to want to battle like the other Pokemon.

But that's okay, as with Koraidon's demonstrated strength, defeating it would be like a fairy tale for ordinary people.

*

When Mew and Teddiursa woke up, it was already three in the afternoon.

The two Pokemon opened their eyes on the bed, momentarily thinking they were still in the outside tree hollow.

Only when they took in their surroundings did they realize—oh, they had already returned to the ranch!

"Eema?"

Teddiursa immediately looked around, starting to search for D's figure.

It jumped off the bed and ran out of the bedroom with Mew, and seeing D wasn't in the living room, they continued running outside.

Sure enough, they saw D in the ranch.

D was leading a group of Pokemon to clear the snow on the ground.

You couldn't feel it now, but in a while, the snow would freeze into ice blocks due to the low temperature, making the roads slippery and easy to fall.

So they had to clear a path in advance to the Pokemon House, Chicken House, Berry Garden, Glass Greenhouse, tavern, stables, and Underground Cave.

Arven was also helping out, secretly observing D. Arven noticed that Mr. D seemed to understand what the Pokemon were saying.

"Mr. D is really impressive!" Arven thought.

At this moment, he saw Mew floating not far away, and his eyes couldn't help but show a look of surprise, "Mr. D, that Pokemon is?!"

D turned to look and replied with a smile, "Them? They are Mew and Teddiursa."

Arven had seen Teddiursa in the Pokedex but had never seen a Mew.

Looking at that unfamiliar pink figure, he couldn't help but mutter, "Mew?"

"Yes." D nodded and said, "The legend is that Mew is an extinct Pokemon, said to have existed in this world for a long time. Some scholars believe Mew is the ancestor of Pokemon."

Hearing this, Arven incredulously asked, "Ancestor?"

D explained, "Because Mew's genetic code contains information on all Pokemon in this world, it can use any move."

"That's... that's amazing!" Arven was tremendously shocked, there was such a Pokemon in this world!

"However, that's not necessarily true, as those are just rumors." D said with a smile, "This Mew is not my Pokemon, it's a friend Teddiursa made."

While speaking, Mew and Teddiursa had already come up to them.

"Eema!" Teddiursa looked up with its bright, black eyes.

D patted its head and said to it, "I'm clearing the snow! I left some sandwiches for you in the living room, you can go eat if you're hungry."

"Eema~" Teddiursa nodded happily.

Meanwhile, Mew, who was glancing around, suddenly seemed to discover something, tilting its head and looking in a direction with its innocent blue eyes.

---

"Miu?"

"What's wrong?" D was slightly surprised.

Mew had already turned around and flown over to that area.

D and Teddiursa looked in the direction it was flying and saw a sapling with green leaves sprouting on the snow-covered land.

That is... the World Tree!

Mew seemed very curious about the World Tree, circling around it and making sounds of wonder and excitement.

"Miu!"

"What?" Teddiursa looked confused at this scene.

D was also slightly surprised. This Heroic Mew seemed to really like that World Tree?

D's mind was stirred, thinking of Mew and the World Tree in the movie "Lucario and the Mystery of Mew".

Perhaps in places unknown to him, there is some connection between Mew and the World Tree?

"Miu!"

At this point, Mew gave a joyful call.

D ran forward with Teddiursa, and Mew looked at them, happily saying, "Miu!"

Teddiursa widened its eyes in disbelief: "What?"

D couldn't understand at all and quickly asked Koraidon to bring him some Translation Nuts Cake as fast as possible.

"Aga-sigh..." Koraidon complied reluctantly.

Soon, the Translation Nuts Cake was delivered, and D ate a piece, hearing Mew say to Teddiursa:

"Miu!" (There's abundant life energy in the tree! I like it here!)

However, Teddiursa couldn't sense it. It looked confusedly at the sapling, mumbling, "What..." (What kind of tree is this...)

Mew shook its head: "Miu." (I don't know either.)

But it could sense the tree had abundant life energy, which is great for them and very suitable for living!

Seeing that Mew also didn't know what tree this was, Teddiursa looked at D: "What?" (Does D know what kind of tree this is?)

Mew also looked over, expecting his answer.

D could only reply, "This is the World Tree."

"Miu?" (World Tree?)

Mew had never heard of this name.

Indeed... not all Mew have a connection with the World Tree, it seems this Mew was attracted by the World Tree probably because, like Calyrex, it sensed the life energy contained in the tree, D thought.

"That's right, the World Tree." D continued, "It will take a long time to grow, it will grow very large, larger than the entire ranch, then all of you can live on it."

Hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa's eyes instantly lit up.

Mew thought to itself, it must use Secret Power to build the Heroic Expedition Team's secret base here in the future!

Teddiursa said happily, "What what!" (Then we can invite Zapdos over as a guest!)

Mew: "Miu!" (Exactly!)

D raised his eyebrows, a bit surprised: "Zapdos?"

Teddiursa nodded: "What what!" (It was the Zapdos we met at the power plant! Zapdos told us living at the power plant, humans often come to disturb it and attack it with Pokemon, it finds this troublesome, saying it would be nice if there was a place without such disturbances.)

Listening to this, D's heart was stirred: "Of course, you can invite it over as a guest!"

Teddiursa was very happy about D willing to let their newfound friends visit the ranch.

D smiled and said, "However, you'll have to wait until the World Tree grows, it will take about four years, when the house becomes big, if you make good friends during your journey, you can invite them to live in the World Tree."

Like Zapdos, Farfetch'd, and other Pokemon.

The World Tree is very large, with vast areas inside, able to accommodate many Pokemon.

If you could gather Pokemon like collecting a Pokedex to live in the World Tree, enriching its ecology, the thought alone is exciting!

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa and Mew's eyes brightened.

"What!" (D is so great!)

D continued smiling.

"Aga-sigh..."

Off to the side, Koraidon looked a bit speechless.

D's plans were so loud, it could practically hear them.

---

---

---

---

# 282: Chapter 279: Magical Fruit Wine

World Tree, Zapdos...

Arven was getting more and more confused by the side. What is the World Tree? And what is Zapdos? Are they Pokemon?

No, that's not right!

Arven opened his mouth, his face showing an expression of surprise.

As expected, Mr. D can understand Pokemon's cries!

He couldn't hold back and finally asked, "Mr. D, um...can you talk to Pokemon?"

D slapped his forehead, then remembered that Arven was still there with him.

He pulled out a piece of Translation Nuts Cake and handed it to Arven while explaining:

"This is a dish made using Secret Power. After eating it, you can understand Pokemon speaking, but the effect only lasts for a while."

Hearing this, Arven was a bit shocked. He reached out to take it, looking incredulously at the snack he had never seen before.

D, with a serious expression, bluffed, "That's the power of food. A good chef can develop the hidden effects of ingredients. I've heard some ingredients growing somewhere in this world can even restore Pokemon suffering from incurable diseases to health!"

Arven's mouth gaped in shock, so amazing!

D smiled and said, "Want to try? Maybe after eating it, you can understand what Mabosstiff is saying!"

Arven looked at Mabosstiff beside him, and upon hearing D's words, a gleam of light shone in Mabosstiff's slightly cloudy eyes.

"Woof!"

Arven picked up the cake in his hand and put it in his mouth.

Immediately, a sweet and irresistible fragrance filled his mouth.

Arven's eyes lit up, so delicious!

Only after he'd swallowed the whole piece did Mabosstiff make another sound and ask:

"Woof?" (Arven?)

Hearing this familiar yet unfamiliar voice, Arven immediately widened his eyes in shock, "Mabosstiff? Is it you talking?"

Mabosstiff nodded happily, "Woof!" (It's me, Arven!)

"You can really understand Pokemon talking..." Arven stood there dumbfounded, unable to describe his feelings at that moment.

D stood by with a gentle smile, watching this scene, and glanced at Mabosstiff, speaking:

"Mabosstiff, do you have anything you'd like to say to Arven? You can take this opportunity to tell him!"

Arven was slightly stunned, then came to his senses and looked down at Mabosstiff in front of him.

And Mabosstiff looked at him with an affectionate gaze.

This child... it watched him grow up.

When it was still a Fidough, Arven was born.

At that time, Professor Sada and Professor Turo brought it to Arven and told it that Arven would live with it from then on.

So, from that day on, Fidough stayed by Arven's side, watching him grow from a lively and cheerful little boy to a quiet, very lonely young boy.

And it evolved from Fidough to Mabosstiff.

Thinking of this, Mabosstiff couldn't help feeling very sad.

Arven is a very unfortunate child. His father and mother are always busy with work, which led to Arven living alone from a young age.

Sometimes, even during the New Year, he would be alone.

Looking at Arven, Mabosstiff slowly spoke, "Woof!" (I will always be with you, Arven!)

"Mabosstiff..." Arven's eyes swelled with emotion, and then he sincerely said, "Thank you!"

D and Koraidon and the other Pokemon stood quietly by, watching this scene.

"Arven and Mabosstiff's bond is indeed strong...no wonder Arven would travel the whole Paldea Region to cure Mabosstiff." D thought to himself.

At this moment, Arven stood up, wiped his eyes, and said to D, "Thank you too, Mr. D."

"Alright!" D took a deep breath, then smiled, "Since we're at the ranch, let's forget all the unhappy things for now and enjoy the winter countryside life! It's hard to experience in a big city!"

"Hmm!"

Arven nodded, then went off into the snow to play with Mabosstiff.

While D began introducing the new companions at the ranch to the returning Mew and Teddiursa.

After going out for a while, Teddiursa became a completely different bear from before.

Perhaps because of the experiences it encountered and the many Pokemon it met, Teddiursa now was lively and cheerful and could quickly make friends with any Pokemon.

Is this perhaps a kind of talent?

After dinner, the outside sky darkened, and in this cold snowy night, D gathered everyone by the fireplace to warm up by the fire.

The orange light of the fire illuminated the faces of a group of Pokemon, and the room was warm and cozy.

Koraidon lay on the carpet, resting on its arm, eyes peacefully closed.

Cyclizar stretched comfortably, happily wagging its tail.

Pawmot and Alcremie ran circles around D, playfully chasing each other.

Mew and Teddiursa kept eating the honey pudding and honey tea that D prepared for them.

Arven was also there, tidying up Mabosstiff's fur.

D looked at Teddiursa eating honey pudding and smiled, saying to it, "You've changed a lot now!"

Teddiursa looked up, a bit confused, "Huh?" (What do you mean?)

D reflected nostalgically about the first time they met, "At that time, you pretended to be a lost, pitiful little bear who appeared at the ranch, wanting to steal honey when I wasn't looking."

---

"Uh..."

Hearing this, Teddiursa felt very embarrassed.

D laughed heartily, then revealed to Teddiursa:

"Actually, before I met you, I had already heard about you from other ranchers nearby. They told me to be careful of a Teddiursa."

The nearby ranchers... they must mean the ranches it had visited.

Thinking of this, Teddiursa was astonished: "Uh?!" (So D knew I was coming to the ranch to steal honey?!)
