D nodded truthfully: "That's right."

"Uh..." Then, a look of confusion appeared in Teddiursa's eyes: "Uh?" (Then why didn't you drive me away at the time? And why did you let me stay?)

If it were any other human, they probably would have driven away this "little thief" at first sight.

Teddiursa really wanted to know the answer to this question. It put down the honey pudding in its hand and looked up at D.

D thought carefully before answering: "Perhaps it's because we were destined to meet? It was fate that brought us together."

Just like the phrase players can see in the Pokemon's data in the game—alluding to Teddiursa crossing vast time and space to meet its destined match.

"Uh..."

Teddiursa didn't quite understand, but those words sounded impressive!

A smile appeared on its face as it happily said: "Uh!" (I am also very happy to have met you, D!)

D smiled slightly and then asked: "Are you and the others going to continue your journey?"

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa and Mew exchanged a glance.

Mew nodded softly: "Miu!" (That's right!)

Teddiursa also said: "Uh!" (The Heroic Expedition Team's journey isn't over yet!)

However, just after saying that, Teddiursa's tone became downcast: "Uh..." (That means I have to leave D again...)

"Go on without worry!" D reached out and patted Teddiursa's small head.

Teddiursa looked up, staring dumbly at him.

D showed it a gentle smile: "If you want to do it, then go ahead! I'll always be waiting for you here at the ranch. If you get tired, you can come home anytime, and I'll prepare a sumptuous dinner for you then."

"Uh..." Upon hearing these words, Teddiursa was deeply moved; its eyes glistened with tears of gratitude.

Teddiursa wiped its tears and resolutely said: "Uh!" (I will always remember D!)

No matter where it goes in the future, it will remember D and miss him.

D smiled slightly, letting out a yawn as he felt a bit sleepy: "Alright, it's getting late, time to rest!"

"Uh~"

*

League Year 198, November 25th.

On this day, Professor Sada returned from the Paldea Giant Pit to take Arven home from the ranch.

"Thank you for taking care of him during this period." Professor Sada expressed her gratitude to D.

D smiled and shook his head: "It wasn't any trouble, Arven was very well-behaved, and he helped me a lot with the work."

Professor Sada smiled, then bid farewell to D with Arven.

"We're going back now; we'll visit you again when we have time."

D nodded, looking at Arven: "Goodbye, Arven! Feel free to come play at the ranch anytime!"

Arven and Mabosstiff had enjoyed themselves these days. They nodded and waved to D: "Goodbye, Mr. D!"

D stood at the ranch, watching their figures fade into the distance.

Only after they disappeared from view did he turn and head back to the living room.

Today was already November 25th; next month would be the new year.

Reflecting on it, he felt quite emotional; it had almost been a year since he came to the Pokemon World.

The weather forecast indicated that there would be continuous snowfall in Porto Marinada Town for the next half month, possibly even burying the roads in snow.

To be on the safe side, D thought he should stock up on supplies for the new year beforehand.

When the time comes, he won't go out but spend the whole day at home with the Pokemon, keeping warm by the fireplace and reading books.

D checked the stock at home.

"There aren't many steaks left, and we're running low on flour and seasonings. We have plenty of eggs, though, as the ranch can produce them, so no need to go to town for those."

"It's a pity the greenhouse isn't up yet, otherwise, we could have fresh home-grown vegetables."

"But it's okay, building it next year will be just as good."

"Besides these supplies, I need to purchase another batch of Berries, for making Poffins and also as snacks for Koraidon and the others."

After pondering for a while, D took out paper and pen to write down the items he needed to buy.

That afternoon, he rode Koraidon and took three Gogoats with him to Porto Marinada Town.

In winter, Porto Marinada Town took on a completely different appearance.

The rooftops of the buildings in town were covered with a layer of white snow, and both the people and Pokemon on the streets were dressed in thick winter clothes and scarves.

Despite the cold temperature, Porto Marinada Market was still bustling with activity.

D bought the items he needed according to the list, then hung the big bags on the Gogoats to help carry the goods.

Once the three Gogoats were fully loaded, they ended this shopping trip and returned to the ranch.

D called on Cyclizar and other Pokemon like Pawmot to help carry the goods back home, while he also hurried back with several bags in hand.

---

After all this was done, the time had reached four o'clock in the evening.

D looked at the calendar and suddenly remembered that the fruit wine he had put into the wine cellar for brewing should be ready now.

So, he went to the wine cellar, found the oak barrel where the fruit wine was brewed, and as soon as he opened the wooden stopper, a rich wine aroma mixed with fruit fragrance wafted out.

Smelling this aroma, D's eyes lit up, and he hurriedly used a beer glass to pour a cup.

This was fruit wine brewed from Pecha Berry, and under the dim light, the liquid of this fruit wine was as clear as a spring, with a light pink color, just like the color of a Pecha Berry.

D looked down, and the information about this cup of fruit wine floated into his mind.

[Pecha Berry Wine (B): A fruit wine brewed from Pecha Berry, perfectly combining the fresh scent of the Berry with the rich aroma of the wine, tasting sweet and perhaps sought after by some Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Detoxification, with the effect of removing the poison status and toxic condition from Pokemon and providing some poison resistance to their bodies, the effect cannot be stacked.

(Poison Resistance: When Pokemon fall into a poisoned state, it can reduce the damage caused by poisoning, and there is a slight chance to be immune to the poisoned state.)

Evaluation: Though the wine is good, do not overindulge!]

Seeing the effect of this fruit wine, D couldn't help but look surprised.

Poison resistance? That's a very exaggerated effect! Especially effective against Poison Type Pokemon's special attacks.

However, upon seeing the following "some" and "slight chance," D no longer thought it was exaggerated.

Still, overall it was quite good, at least after drinking this cup of fruit wine, one can permanently reduce some damage caused by poisoning.

This is great, the beverages in the tavern can finally be updated.

Then, D opened the next oak barrel.

The last time he brewed fruit wine, he used a total of three types of Berries, namely Pecha Berry, Cheri Berry, and Sitrus Berry.

Now that the Pecha Berry Wine has detoxifying effects, what about the other two?

Thinking of this, D hurriedly poured a cup of Cheri Berry Wine to check it.

[Cheri Berry Wine (B): A fruit wine brewed from Cheri Berry, with a spicy taste that perhaps Fire Type Pokemon would greatly enjoy.

Cooking Effect: Paralysis Resistance, after drinking, it can relieve paralysis from Pokemon and provide some paralysis resistance, reducing the chance of falling into a paralyzed state, the effect cannot be stacked.

Evaluation: Though the wine is good, do not overindulge!]

"As expected, it can also permanently increase paralysis resistance!"

D decided to let all the Pokemon on the ranch drink a cup each, although the resistance increase is small, sometimes it might just be the little bit of resistance that makes the difference.

Next, there was only the last Sitrus Berry Wine left.

D opened the oak barrel stopper, poured a cup of fruit wine, and looked down.

[Sitrus Berry Wine (B): A fruit wine brewed from Sitrus Berry, tasting sour and sweet, possibly sought after by some Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Stamina restoration, after drinking, will restore stamina to the drinker.

Evaluation: Though the wine is good, do not overindulge!]

D: "..."

Why did it become so lackluster when it came to Sitrus Berry?

Well, it can't be said to be lackluster, it can only be considered normal in effect.

Looking at the three oak barrels in front of him, D fell into deep thought.

The Pecha Berry that cures poison can provide Poison resistance to Pokemon, and the Cheri Berry that relieves paralysis can provide Paralysis resistance to Pokemon.

Then the question arises, if it was replaced with the Speed-increasing Salac Berry, the Attack-increasing Liechi Berry, what effects would these fruit wines have?

---

---

---

---

# 283: Chapter 280: The Greedy Coin Pokémon

That night, D moved those three oak barrels of fruit wine into the Pokemon Tavern.

As business hours arrived, Pokemon from the town gradually came to the tavern.

D was wiping the glasses nearby, while his sister Indeedee enthusiastically greeted the guests.

"Ai y~" (Welcome~)

The two Machamp saw D coming here today and couldn't help but show a surprised expression.

D glanced at them and asked, "The tavern has some new arrivals today. Do you want to try them?"

"Nika?" The Machamp exchanged looks with each other.

D then introduced the newly arrived three barrels of fruit wine to them:

"These are wines made from berries. The one on the left is Pecha Berry Wine, it's sweet and also has the effect of curing poison."

"The one in the middle is Cheri Berry Wine, with a spicy flavor. Drink a cup in winter and you'll quickly warm up. It also cures paralysis; if you get stunned by the Stun Spore of Grass Type Pokemon or the Thunder Wave from Electric Type Pokemon, a glass of this will heal you right away."

"The barrel on the right is Sitrus Berry Wine, it has a sweet and sour taste. If you're exhausted, drinking a cup will quickly restore your stamina!"

"Nika!"

Upon hearing these effects, the Machamp showed expressions of surprise.

They looked left and right, unsure of what to drink today.

Seeing this, D asked, "Did you go out to work today?"

"Nika."

The Machamp shook their heads. Recently, due to heavy snow, Keri took a holiday and has been resting at home, not going out to work.

"I see!" D turned around and picked up two wine glasses, "Then I recommend you try the Cheri Berry Wine. It's perfect for this kind of weather."

"Nika~"

The two Machamp happily agreed. The distance between the ranch and the town wasn't close, and they had to walk a long way every day. Their muscles had long been frozen stiff by the cold wind and snow.

D poured them two glasses of Cheri Berry Wine, and the Machamp took out a coin from beneath their gold belts and placed it on the table.

Undoubtedly, this was their payment for the wine today.

After paying, the Machamp took their Cheri Berry Wine and went to the table to savor it slowly.

D observed their expressions.

After taking the first sip of the fruit wine, the faces of both Machamp quickly turned visibly red.

Then, they began to breathe heavily.

"Nika!" (Spicy! Very spicy!)

Yet at that moment, one of the Machamp suddenly felt its body warming up rapidly.

And the spicy sensation in its mouth was not as strong as before, instead leaving a feeling of wanting more.

The Machamp was taken aback, "Nika...!" (Body... warming up)

D smiled at the scene and said to his sister Indeedee next to him, "Looks like they've grown accustomed to Cheri Berry Wine."

"Ai y~"

D then picked up the coin given by the Machamp.

Initially, he thought it was a small-denomination League coin, but upon closer inspection, he realized it was not a League coin at all but an ancient gold coin!

The gold coin was embossed with uniquely gorgeous patterns, and it looked very valuable.

If it's a gold coin, it must be worth quite a sum!

D was astonished, wondering where the Machamp had gotten it from?

Thinking of it, D asked them.

Upon hearing the question, one of the Machamp, feeling warm all over, happily responded:

"Nika." (We found it in the corner of the warehouse.)

"Warehouse? Keri's warehouse?" D immediately thought of the big house behind Keri's workshop.

The Machamp nodded: "Nika~" (That's right~)

D was speechless: "...Could it be something passed down from Keri's ancestors?"

Upon hearing this, the two Machamp understood D's hint.

They quickly shook their heads: "Nika Nika!" (No, it wasn't there before! We just found it in the warehouse yesterday, and Keri gave them to us so we could buy wine at the tavern!)

"I see..." D nodded thoughtfully.

This seems odd, it wasn't there before, it just appeared yesterday...

Suddenly, a Pokemon's name came to D's mind.

Gimmighoul!

It's a Pokemon that is born from the passion infused in coins!

They always wander around with a coin, hidden in various places in the Paldea Region, but whenever someone approaches, this Pokemon will quickly flee.

That's why no Trainer in the Paldea Region seems to have successfully captured it yet.

Could this coin be Gimmighoul's coin?

D had never seen this Pokemon, so he didn't dare to jump to conclusions.

After pondering for a moment, he placed the coin in a prominent spot on the wine rack.

If it truly is Gimmighoul's coin, then that Gimmighoul would surely come chasing after it.

Just then, more Pokemon guests arrived at the tavern.

D stopped thinking and began to help his sister Indeedee in entertaining the Pokemon guests.

Once the tavern got busy, the matter of the coin slipped from his mind.

Gradually, as night deepened.

The guests began to leave the tavern one after another, and D closed up the tavern, taking Koraidon and his sister Indeedee home with him.

---

After they finished washing up, they turned off the room lights and went to bed.

When all the lights in the ranch were out and everything returned to silence, a tiny figure, only ten centimeters tall, emerged from the shadows.

It cautiously surveyed its surroundings, and only after ensuring there was no danger did it approach the large wooden door of the cabin.

It bent down to slip through the door crack into the darkness of the room, immediately spotting the gleaming gold coin placed high on the wine rack.

"Ding!"

This Pokemon let out a joyful growl and then painstakingly climbed onto the table, leaped upward, and landed on the wine rack.

It hoisted the coin onto its back and, after looking around, vanished into the darkness.

*

Early the next morning, while having breakfast, D suddenly remembered the coin he received from Machamp yesterday.

After breakfast, he hurried to the tavern.

He pushed open the door and looked towards the now-empty wine rack.

Gone!

The coin is gone!

D widened his eyes, distinctly recalling placing the coin there when he closed up last night!

And now...

It must have been that Gimmighoul who took it away!

Just then, the Mew and Teddiursa accompanying him noticed a set of tiny footprints in the snow.

They quickly called D over, "Imma!"

D turned around and noticed a trail of tiny footprints leading from the pasture to where he stood.

The prints indicated someone had entered the tavern but left no footprints upon exit.

This means the Gimmighoul must still be inside the tavern!

"Just as I thought!"

Gimmighoul, whether in chest form or roaming form, loves lurking in warehouse or item shop corners where it's less likely to encounter people or Pokemon.

Roaming form Gimmighoul is manageable, but chest form Gimmighoul is often mistaken for an antique, leading people to take or sell it to antique shops.

Mew and Teddiursa continued examining the footprints.

Meanwhile, D deciphered who left them.

Clearly, it's a roaming form Gimmighoul.

Speaking of Gimmighoul, it's an exceptionally unique Pokemon in terms of both ecology and evolution.

They came into being about 1500 years ago during the kingdom era, born from the fervor of ancient explorers and treasure hunters embedding coins in chests.

Its evolution method is equally distinctive.

To evolve into Gholdengo, one must collect 999 ancient gold coins from the Paldea Kingdom.

Those 999 ancient coins create such a harsh evolution condition that most find it impossible; even the wealthy find the task arduously demanding.

Curious at heart, D wanted to see this special Pokemon, Gimmighoul.

He stepped into the tavern, scrutinizing every corner, but saw no trace of Gimmighoul.

Thereupon, D sought Mew's Helping Hand.

"Mewtwo, can you use your Psychic powers to help me scan the room? I have a hunch there's an unfamiliar presence hiding here."

Hearing this, the helpful Mew readily obliged.

"Miu!"

It flew around the tavern, closed its eyes, and emanated a Psychic Mind Wave from within.

Next, transparent ripples undulated throughout the air.

Mew's Psychic ability enveloped the entire tavern,

leaving nothing imperceptible under its insight.

Moments later, Mew opened its eyes, flipped around, and flew toward the cupboard under the counter.

"Miu?"

In there?

D instantly stepped forward, gingerly opening the cupboard door under Mew and Teddiursa's watchful eyes.

Inside, they saw a small figure, a Pokemon tightly clutching the coin, half-hidden within the darkness.

"Wow!" It's really a Gimmighoul!

D's eyes lit up instantly.

Mew and Teddiursa, too, looked at this previously unseen Pokemon with curiosity.

In contrast, the Gimmighoul appeared panicked.

Startled, it dropped the coin and attempted to flee.

D: "..."

Now he finally understood how players in the game obtained their coins.

Seeing Gimmighoul flee, both Teddiursa and Mew were similarly startled.

They looked puzzled at Gimmighoul, and Teddiursa even tried to call after it, "Imma!"

Alas, to no avail.

D: "...It's useless; Gimmighoul's ability is Run Away. If it doesn't want to get caught, it'll definitely escape."

"Miu..."

Mew seemed puzzled as to why this Pokemon would flee.

Meanwhile, D had already dashed out the door, shouting,

"Gimmighoul, you don't need to be afraid. We mean no harm! I won't take your coin; it'll stay right here! You're welcome to come back anytime to get it, and you're more than welcome to stay in the tavern if you'd like. If not, we won't bother you any further!"

D wasn't sure if Gimmighoul heard him, but after returning the coin to its place, he joined Koraidon in shoveling today's snow.

Meanwhile, Mew and Teddiursa began playing in the pasture.

They decided to rest a while at the ranch before resuming their adventures.

D mentioned that the New Year was approaching.

For humans, it's a time for family gatherings when everyone returns home to reunite with family.

Teddiursa loved such holidays and decided with Mew to continue their expedition after the New Year.

As D and Koraidon worked, Mew and Teddiursa ventured into the Glass Greenhouse for exploration.

There, they encountered a Pokemon with a large head and an affectionate gaze.

Yesterday, Calyrex was absent from the ranch, riding Spectrier to the rear mountain to pray for the souls of departed Pokemon to find peace.

Thus, Teddiursa and Mew hadn't met Calyrex yet.

Upon seeing these two, Calyrex was initially surprised, then smiled at them.

"I am Calyrex, a friend of D's, very pleased to meet you."

"Imma?" Teddiursa blinked, tilting its head with a curious gaze at the Pokemon in front.

On the other hand, Mew sensed a hint of the World Tree from the Pokemon before it.

It instantly understood: "Miu" (Did you nurture the World Tree!)

At this, Calyrex was somewhat astonished, not expecting the small Pokemon to notice.

Calyrex nodded slightly, "Indeed, I did. The World Tree is the source of life. I am privileged to assist in its growth."

Teddiursa was amazed: "Imma!" (So awesome!)

Mew's eyes widened, then a joyful grin spread across its face, "Miu!" (We'll help too!)

Calyrex smiled gently. Just as Mew could sense its power, it could sense the ancient, vibrant energy emanating from the Pokemon before it.

This Pokemon named Mew was clearly not as weak as its small, adorable exterior suggested.

Quite the contrary, it was an ancient Pokemon, a long-lived presence in this world.

With this in mind, Calyrex called upon Polteageist and Sinistea, extending a tea party invitation to Teddiursa and Mew.

"Would you like a cup of tea?"

Mew and Teddiursa blinked, then cheerfully accepted.

"Imma~"

"Miu~"

*

In the afternoon, D took a moment to visit the tavern.

He opened the cupboard under the counter, only to find the ancient coin he left there had vanished without a trace.

Evidently, Gimmighoul had returned and taken the coin away.

Whether it heard what I said earlier...

With a brief thought, D again sought Mew's help for a scan.

Mew once again unleashed its Psychic powers.

Moments later, it reopened its eyes, this time focusing on a more hidden corner.

D quietly asked, "Is it still there?"

Mew nodded: "Miu!"

D finally exhaled a sigh of relief, relieved it was still there.

This at least meant Gimmighoul heard what he said earlier.

---

---

---

---

# 284: Chapter 281: Tree of Beginning Leaf

Alliance Year 198, late December.

The Paldea Region was hit by a snowfall that lasted for nearly half a month.

A thick layer of snow covered the roads, blocking the path to the farm, and even Zack's big truck couldn't drive in.

With no other option, Zack had to take a few days off, planning to resume deliveries once the snow subsided a bit.

Early in the morning, D took his tools and headed out with Koraidon and Cyclizar to shovel the snow.

Standing in the snow, he gazed in the direction of Porto Marinada Town and inwardly thought, "As expected."

Just as he predicted, the road was buried in snow, making it impassable for trucks.

"Luckily, I had prepared supplies for winter in advance, so there's no need to brave the snow to go to town," D thought to himself.

In this kind of weather, people stayed indoors, and it was rare to see anyone outside, even the Trainers and Travelers who usually roamed around were nowhere to be seen.

The road to the town was blocked by snow, and with no one coming, the farm seemed isolated from the world, like a paradise.

As far as the eye could see, everything was a vast expanse of white.

Being here, it felt as if one's soul was cleansed.

D enjoyed the leisure, standing with his eyes closed in the snow, savoring the pure atmosphere of solitude.

However, the next second, a series of hoofbeats clattered past him.

D opened his eyes to see Glastrier galloping wildly through the snow.

D: "..."

The peaceful atmosphere was instantly shattered, forcing D to detach from the tranquility he was enjoying.

Not only was Glastrier running around the farm, but with the cessation of snow, the entire farm came to life.

Smoke rose from the snow-laden rooftops, as his sister Indeedee prepared breakfast for everyone.

Pawmot flew out of the living room and into the Chicken House to care for the hens.

Glaceon, together with Dolliv and Alcremie, played a game of drawing with their footprints in the snow.

Dragonite and Dragonair, who were working, also arrived at the farm. Led by Dragonite, they strapped on their saddlebags and set off for Porto Marinada Town, carrying fresh milk to deliver to the customers there.

D couldn't help but sigh deeply; although the farm was cut off from the outside world, it was hardly lonely here!

Koraidon and Cyclizar stood on either side of D, watching the lively scene and letting out joyful growls.

"Ah~ Garsis~"

"Gao~"

D noticed Cyclizar's scarf was coming loose, so he went forward to re-tie it.

Seeing this, Koraidon also stepped forward, chest puffed out.

"Alright, alright, I'll tie yours again too," D sighed helplessly. "Seriously, like a kid."

"Ah Garsis~" Koraidon let out a growl that sounded like a charm.

With that expression, it really resembled an enthusiastic golden retriever.

D gently pushed its head aside and continued the day's snow-shoveling work, checking the other facilities around the farm as well.

Back when the temperature dropped last month, he had turned on the heaters in both the Chicken House and the Pokemon House.

During this time, both the hens and the Miltank stayed inside the heated rooms all day and night without venturing out.

Arriving at the Pokemon House, D saw the Miltank, and greeted them with a smile, "Good morning, everyone! A new day is starting!"

"Moo~"

The Miltank lay on their soft straw beds, responding happily.

"Good."

D picked up a brush to groom their fur, then filled their feed troughs and water basins with Berries and hay, before saying goodbye to the Miltank and leaving the place.

Next, they arrived at the Chicken House.

Pawmot was here chatting with the hens.

Standing with arms akimbo in front of the hens, it smiled while constantly chirping, "Pamo, Pamo Pamo!"

Seeing D come over, Pawmot flew to his side, "Pamo~"

D curiously asked, "Can they understand what you're saying?"

Pawmot nodded repeatedly, "Pamo!"

These chicks are its good friends!

Huh? Can Pokemon and ordinary animals communicate? D looked surprised.

Having lived in this world for so long, D had gradually learned some information.

As far as he knew, there were also many ordinary animals in this world.

But it was somewhat different from what he initially thought.

There were no large carnivores like tigers, elephants, lions, or sharks; instead, there were only edible animals like chickens, ducks, geese, cattle, pigs, fish, and shrimp.

As for how people in this world distinguish between ordinary animals and Pokemon, perhaps it's by seeing if the ordinary animals can use Moves?

Though some people do use parts of Pokemon as food...

For example, Slowpoke's regularly shed tails, Veluza's shed flesh, and Klawf's shed claws.

However, D, since arriving, had never eaten any of these things.

It's not that he's overly compassionate, he just feels that Pokemon's emotions and intelligence are quite close to humans, with some people treating them as companions and family.

Under such circumstances, eating them gives a certain indescribable feeling.

After contemplating for a moment, D bid farewell to Pawmot and turned to leave the Chicken House.

---

The Indeedee sibling has already prepared breakfast.

D took Koraidon and Cyclizar to wash up, then joined everyone for breakfast.

After breakfast, snowflakes began to fall outside again.

D had nothing to do, so he moved a rocking chair in front of the fireplace, sat there warming himself while reading a book.

The Pokémon also stayed inside, having nothing to do, they joined D to rest fireside.

Snow fell outside the window, while the fireplace warmed the room, even the air was cozy.

D glanced at the group of Pokémon beside him, his lips curling into a slight smile.

This feeling… truly wonderful!

"The New Year is just days away; oh, how time flies!" D mumbled to himself.

Unknowingly, this year has almost passed.

The first New Year in the Pokémon World holds special significance for D.

He thought hard, wishing to do something special for the Pokémon to remember in the future.

But after thinking for ages, he was stumped.

The only thing he could do was to personally prepare a sumptuous New Year's Eve dinner for the Pokémon around him.

"Luckily, the house still has ample ingredients; since that's the case, I might as well remake all the dishes from this year!"

*

In the afternoon, Calyrex appeared before D.

With excitement, it said to D, "My strength has returned, I can start the Second Nurture!"

Hearing this, D's eyes lit up and, despite the snow outside, hurriedly went with Calyrex to the World Tree.

Seeing them seemingly about to do something to the World Tree, Mew and Teddiursa curiously followed.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmot, Alcremie, Dolliv, Glaceon, Gogoat, Indeedee Sibling...

A group of Pokémon surrounded the World Tree in the center.

D and Calyrex stepped forward, Calyrex glanced around with a smile, and then a powerful aura erupted from it.

Vital energy swept outwards, forming a hemispherical shield covering the World Tree.

In the next moment, the snow on the ground melted completely, sprouting tiny green grass from the vibrant energy storm.

The World Tree began to change along with it.

Its trunk grew thicker, reaching higher, sprouting more green leaves on its previously bare branches.

D's gaze locked onto the World Tree in front of him.

Before his eyes, the tree's growth rate visibly accelerated!

[World Tree Seedling: A seedling absorbing significant Power of Grass, altered by Gogoat during growth, brewing immense Life Force, ready to emerge, needing 97995 more years to mature.]

[97980 years.]

[97500 years.]

[96980 years.]

…

[96001 years.]

[95995 years.]

Finally, the number settled at 95995 years.

And before them, the World Tree had grown into a tall, human-sized tree reaching about 1.8 meters!

The tree's branches were laden with lush, dense leaves, with the gentle flow of luminous green energy, just by looking, one could sense the tree's extraordinary nature.

The Pokémon nearby noticed this scene.

Mew and Teddiursa's eyes widened in disbelief.

"Eee!" (The leaves are glowing!)

"Mew!" (Calyrex is amazing!)

Calyrex smiled, looking at the tree before it with satisfaction, then marveled:

"Unbelievable! In just over four thousand years, this World Tree has grown into such a form, who knows what it will become in ten thousand years, it really is intriguing!"

D recalled the theatrical version of the World Tree, and using it as reference, replied: "Probably a few hundred or even thousand meters tall, right?"

But after speaking, D suddenly realized something.

If this World Tree could grow that tall, the ranch might be engulfed by it!

That's not good news.

D couldn't help but ponder.

"Several hundred meters, even thousand meters..." Calyrex compared this with the future premonition it had seen, intending to say something but saw D troubled with a furrowed brow.

"Why are you worried?" Calyrex asked puzzled.

D glanced at it and shared his concerns:

"I'm thinking, if the tree grows that big, the ranch will be doomed by then."

This place is his home, he doesn't want it to vanish...

Upon hearing this, Calyrex recalled the future scene it had seen through time.

"You needn't worry," Calyrex said, "I foresaw the future of this tree, after a long time, it will leave the ground, soar to the sky, and finally float above the clouds."

"What?" D was taken aback.

The World Tree... flying?

"How does that work?" he asked in astonishment.

Calyrex shook its head gently: "I don't know, Future Sight only lets me see the outcome, not the process."

Just like when it foresaw Eternatus descending upon the Galar region.

"Alright..." D was still baffled.

How does such a huge tree fly to the sky?

It's so unscientific, doesn't it fall due to gravity? Or has someone installed an anti-gravity device on it?

In his contemplation, D noticed fatigue on Calyrex's face.

"Oh well, we'll cross that bridge when we get to it, since that's the future, we'll surely discover how," D said to Calyrex. "You've worked hard, quickly go rest! I'll prepare Grassy Seed and Green Grass Cake for you!"

Calyrex nodded slightly, and then vanished from sight, returning to the Glass Greenhouse.

D, along with a group of Pokémon, returned to the living room; he let some Pokémon play on their own while he went to the kitchen to prepare energy-restoring snacks and afternoon tea for Calyrex.

On the way to deliver the snacks to the Glass Greenhouse, D saw three Gogoat surrounding the World Tree, resting on the grass germinated by Calyrex, chewing something unknown.

"...What are you eating?" D couldn't help but ask.

"Meee~"

Upon D's inquiry, one Gogoat opened its mouth, revealing a vibrant green leaf inside.

D's eyes widened immediately.

Oh no! Gogoat is starting to munch on the World Tree!

Just as he thought Gogoat was attracted by the World Tree, actively munching on its leaves, three leaves unexpectedly fell from the lush green foliage.

The three leaves glittered with green gleam, spiraling down to the Gogoat's feet.

"Mee!"

The Gogoat's eyes lit up, lowering their heads to eat the leaves.

D: "?"

What's going on?

D stared in astonishment at the scene.

Does the World Tree possess consciousness? Is it the will of nature? Or perhaps the will of the world? Has it chosen Gogoat?

D pondered deeply; soon, he remembered the World Tree in the theatrical version.

It was equipped with a defense system, deploying the Guardian Three Pillars to halt intruders when sensing external invasion.

"Could it be?"

D approached closer, getting to the World Tree.

Then, the World Tree seemed to sense something.

Suddenly, another leaf slowly fell from the tree.

Witnessing this, an unbelievable thought sprang to D's mind.

He used to water the World Tree with Gogoat, fertilizing it too! So, the World Tree considers him friendly!

Thinking this, D instinctively reached out to catch the leaf.

He looked down, the next moment, information of the leaf surfaced in his mind.

[Unripened Tree of Beginning Leaf: Mythological item, a leaf containing Nature Power, a coveted treasure for Grass Type Pokémon, extraordinarily rare, specific effects unknown.]

---

---

---

---

# 285: Chapter 282: Year After Year

This is unbelievable!

A Mythical Level item, this is on the same level as Dialga and Palkia's Adamant Orb and Lustrous Orb!

D stared at the shimmering green leaf in his hand, deeply shaken.

What does this mean? It means that this World Tree is nurturing them!

The three Gogoat chewed on the leaves in their mouths. They looked at the snowy ground not far away, then at the distinct circular area near the tree.

The area was filled with thriving life, covered with fresh, soft grass as if spring had arrived.

The Gogoat looked bright-eyed and then lay down around the World Tree.

D's attention was drawn by the actions of the Gogoat.

Clearly, the three Gogoat were the first Pokémon to receive nurturing from the World Tree.

He thought of Regice, Regirock, and Registeel within the Tree of Beginning.

Will the Gogoat grow to their level in the future?

This question remains to be answered.

D stood there for a while, gazing at the World Tree before turning around with the snacks prepared for Calyrex and the leaf heading to the Glass Greenhouse.

He placed the snacks on the wrought iron tea table, then took out the Tree of Beginning Leaf.

"Calyrex, can you see the difference in this leaf?" D asked.

Calyrex looked down, showing a surprised expression: "Such rich Nature Power! Where did you obtain it?"

As soon as the words fell, Calyrex realized, saying, "No, I am familiar with this aura. It's the World Tree!"

D nodded: "That's right. Just now while I was on my way here, the World Tree dropped this leaf for me, and it is also nurturing the Gogoat with the leaves."

Upon hearing this, the thought of "nurturing?" flashed through Calyrex's mind.

"Exactly!" D nodded, confirming the matter.

"No wonder…" Calyrex murmured.

D felt a stir in his mind: "Did you discover something too?"

Calyrex slightly nodded, slowly speaking, "When my power leads its growth, a strand of natural aura enters my body."

"At first, I thought it was an illusion, but after a while, the energy within my body becomes stronger. Now it seems, that's the reason!"

D was somewhat surprised: "It wasn't an illusion?"

Calyrex nodded: "Of course."

"So that's how it is…" D couldn't help but murmur.

He thought of the Gogoat that once nurtured the World Tree; could it be that the World Tree started nurturing them back then?

Such terror!

Who would have thought, back then when he caught Skiddo from Casseroya Lake, it was just to bring them over as milk goats?

To what extent will the Gogoat grow in the future, D couldn't even dare to imagine!

His heart filled with shock as he looked at Calyrex before him, unable to help but murmur:

"What will this World Tree eventually become?"

Calyrex's heart also sought to know.

Thus, right in front of D, it used Future Sight by consuming its internal energy again toward that distant future.

In an instant, Calyrex's gaze traversed countless times, witnessing scenes centuries later.

The World Tree soared, reaching towards the sky.

The earth was enveloped with greenness, and the plains flourished with vitality everywhere.

The branches were laden with heavy Berries, Pokémon freely ran across the land.

This land finally became a truly fertile place.

Moments later, Calyrex slowly opened its eyes, recounting the future events to D.

"I see the World Tree will become the foundation of the world, its power will shroud this planet. By then, more Pokémon will emerge on the earth, and nature will bear more fruit."

Does that mean the World Tree enhances the world's level of life?

D pictured that scene, considering the current progress, the World Tree would mature in four years.

Four years... luckily, he could witness all that.

However, as a Transmigrator, D knew clearly that this cosmos doesn't only contain one world.

Beyond this world, there are other worlds, other universes, other parallel dimensions.

And the essence of Arceus exists outside these worlds, observing the development of humans and Pokémon silently.

So the question is, won't everything they've done attract the attention of Arceus' essence?

D pondered for a moment, concluding with an answer even he didn't know.

After all, the thoughts of the Creator God are something a mere Normal human like him can't fathom.

Rather than dwelling on this intangible matter, D now wishes to know the effects culinary dishes made from the Tree of Beginning Leaf will have.

Thinking of this, D bid farewell to Calyrex and quickly ran back home to the kitchen.

"Cooking with the leaf…"

Tea eggs, World Tree Leaf scrambled eggs similar to stir-fried toon eggs, or perhaps Leaf Cake like a Green Grass Cake?

With a quick thought, D reached a decision.

A single leaf was too little to make tea eggs or World Tree Leaf scrambled eggs.

Since that's the case, he'll make Leaf Cake like a Green Grass Cake.

Thinking of this, D called Alcremie and went to the cake workshop with it.

---

Use a blender to turn the Tree of Beginning Leaf into a paste, then add ingredients like eggs and milk, pour into molds to shape, and finally bake in the oven.

Once it's baked to perfection, take the cake out of the oven, spread cream on it, and cut it into small pieces.

With that, a Leaf Cake is complete.

D couldn't wait to check the information on this Leaf Cake.

[Leaf Cake (Incomplete Edition) (S-): A cake made by mixing Tree of Beginning Leaf with eggs and MooMoo Fresh Milk, etc. Listen closely, and you might sense the Breath of Nature within.

Cooking Effect: Breath of Nature.

1. Permanently increase the power of Grass Type Moves by 20%, effect limited to Grass Type Pokemon, cannot be stacked.

2. After consumption by Grass Type Pokemon, it can respond to the rhythm of nature. When using Grass Energy to cultivate crops, it can slightly shorten the crop growth time, effective only for Grass Type Pokemon, effect cannot be stacked.

Additional Effect: Master of the World Tree, you have been recognized by the Tree of Beginning. As an existence that nurtured the World Tree with the mounting goat, you may enter all regions inside the World Tree in future, this effect only applies to D.

Evaluation: Listen to the Breath of Nature! Perhaps once the World Tree is fully grown, dishes made from its leaves will have even better effects.]

D was dumbfounded.

Wait! What does this additional effect mean? It's valid only for him?

D calmly analyzed, pondering a bit.

Initially, he had personally brought back those three Miracle Seeds from the town, intending them as items for the mounting goat, which unexpectedly got planted in the ground and nurtured with Grass Energy.

Then, those three Miracle Seeds took root and sprouted in the soil...

Thinking of this, D suddenly realized something.

All along, he considered the Gogoat as merely a Pokemon that nurtured the World Tree, ignoring the underlying conditions.

But from the effects of this Leaf Cake, isn't there a possibility that the World Tree regards the Gogoat as a mother?

And Gogoat's daily act of nurturing the World Tree with Grass Energy, isn't it like feeding milk to a child?!

"So, could it be that the World Tree is influenced by Gogoat and considers me its owner too? Or perhaps its grandfather?"

The more D thought about it, the more absurd it seemed.

If this were true, then wouldn't he and the Gogoats be like young people freeloading off their children?

It's too absurd...

D quickly shook his head, dismissing this bizarre speculation.

After all, the World Tree represents nature's will and shouldn't be so ridiculous.

Of course, all of this is just D's conjecture, the World Tree's own intentions remain unknown.

Ignoring the additional effects, D began to carefully examine the two specific effects above.

The first is to increase the power of Grass Type Pokemon moves.

"Increase by 20%... it's about the same value as the items Pokemon carry." D mused.

This is just the effect of Tree of Beginning Leaf before it's mature, if once the World Tree grows completely, the increase in move power should be much greater.

The second is the reduction of crop growth time, which is currently a necessity for the ranch.

Whether it's the everyday vegetables and melons, or the carrots fed to Glastrier and Spectrier, or the World Tree, all need Pokemon with this capability.

D even considers the second effect more critical than the first.

Because the future ranch can't always rely on Calyrex's power, with the ability to shorten crop growth times, the Gogoat, Dolliv, and other Grass Type Pokemon can handle it themselves.

Just like the Shaymins' ability to make flowers bloom.

And once Calyrex eats the Leaf Cake, the World Tree's growth time will likely shorten further.

"Once the World Tree is fully grown, the extent of the reduction in crop growth time will likely increase from slight to significant." D thought.

Overall, the effects of this Leaf Cake far exceeded his imagination!

D first ate a piece himself, then placed the remaining five on a tray and brought them to the ranch to feed them to three Gogoats, Dolliv, and Calyrex respectively.

After eating, none of the Pokemon reacted significantly, no exaggerated glowing effects, just going about their usual activities, playing and sleeping.

Only Calyrex perceived a subtle change.

After eating the Leaf Cake, it immediately felt the changes occurring within its body.

"Was that miraculous cuisine made by you as well?" Calyrex asked curiously.

D grinned: "That's right, that's Leaf Cake, able to boost the power of Grass Type Pokemon moves and shorten their crop cultivation growth time."

"Such cuisine exists in the world..." Calyrex murmured in awe, then smiled: "Thanks to you, my power has grown again."

When leaving Galar, it came here with the intention of seeking a change in life and mood, and also to see if its little follower needed assistance, which it would offer to the best of its ability.

Yet now it seems, the little follower not only refrained from seeking too much help but rather kept creating snacks and afternoon tea to enhance its power.

---

"You've done so much for me, and I've never done anything for you. I truly feel ashamed," said Calyrex.

D just smiled and shook his head, saying, "No, you've already done so much for me."

Sharing the Spectrier and Glastrier, helping him cultivate the World Tree, exploring Giratina's Reversed World together…

But Calyrex felt that these weren't significant to him.

Seeing this, D comforted, "Alright, there's no need to dwell on these things now. Besides, you've helped me tremendously. Helping each other without counting gains or losses—that's what friends do!"

Calyrex was slightly taken aback, then revealed a relieved smile:

"Indeed, we are friends! I understand now, D. I will no longer feel lost!"

Seeing this, D smiled slightly—Calyrex is truly wonderful!

Calyrex also showed a gentle smile, thinking his little follower is wonderful!

At this moment, Calyrex's affection for D surged up, reaching the max and stopping there.

*

The time quickly came to the last day of the year.

In the evening, D started preparing the New Year's Eve dinner in the kitchen with his sister Indeedee.

Grassy Seed and Green Grass Cake, which Gogoat loves the most.

Pan-fried steaks, Koraidon, and Cyclizar's favorites.

Poffins and sandwiches, Dragonite loves these.

Cream smoothies that Glaceon enjoys.

Honey pudding and honey lemonade that Teddiursa likes.

Rice, Pawmot's favorite.

Salted fish and fried soil, Naclstack's favorites.

…

D prepared plenty of each, finishing up after nightfall.

On this day, the Pokemon Tavern stopped its operations. D, along with Koraidon, pushed the food cart and shared the food with the Pokemon on the ranch.

"Happy New Year!" he said to the Pokemon on the ranch.

Though Gogoat and the others didn't know what day it was, their spirits were uplifted by the celebration and the numerous steaming dishes, feeling joy and happiness from the bottom of their hearts, and crowded around the troughs to enjoy the food.

Miltank, knowing the significance of the new year, showed happy expressions.

One of the Miltank mooed loudly at D.

D was a bit surprised, then ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake.

Then, he heard words of happiness expressed by the Miltank.

The Miltank said they loved it here and were very glad to have D taking care of them, wishing to stay and live with D here forever.

It's impossible not to be moved by such words.

D took a deep breath, patted each Miltank in front of him, and said to them, "Please continue to take care of me next year!"

"Moo~"

In the Underground Cave, D piled up dried salted fish on the ground, all salted by him, Naclstack's favorite food.

The salted fish and salted meat formed a small mound, and Naclstack was thrilled to see it, eagerly nudging D, pushing him onto themselves.

"Stack~"

"Slow down!" exclaimed D, helplessly sitting on Naclstack, and patting each one's head.

Bellibolt residing here looked on enviously.

Attracted by the noise, Diglett came over too.

Seeing this scene, D walked out from the circle of Naclstack, grabbing food prepared for them from Koraidon's small cart.

"Diglett, Bellibolt, this is for you! May you have a Happy New Year as well!"

Bellibolt and Diglett didn't expect to have food too, and sheer delight sparkled in their little eyes.

"Eat up!"

D smiled slightly, then said to a group of Pokemon, "I still need to deliver food to other Pokemon, see you later!"

After leaving the Underground Cave, D arrived at the Berry Garden.

Tinkatink and Greedent saw D and Koraidon coming, feeling quite joyful, and ran out of their small nests.

Koraidon used its large paw to give them food. D, touching Greedent's fluffy large tail, said to them:

"Winter is almost over, soon I'll be able to help you rebuild your homes. Don't worry, the hillside will be restored to how it used to be!"

Hearing this, a group of Pokemon instantly widened their eyes, showing hopeful expressions.

D smiled slightly and then left the Berry Garden, heading to the area where Psyduck lived.

He shared Berries with the silly Psyduck and then turned to go home with Koraidon.

The snowy night was peaceful, and the ranch was enveloped in a warm atmosphere.

Looking at the house with lights on at the snowy end, D felt an unprecedented tranquility and peace inside.

Because he knew, there were a bunch of adorable Pokemon waiting for their return to enjoy the New Year's Eve dinner together.

---

---

---

---

# 286: Chapter 283: The Best Day (End of Volume 4)

"Happy New Year!"

"Cheers!"

D and the Pokémon raised their Berry Juice for a clink, then tilted their heads back, gulping it down.

Snowflakes started drifting outside again, while inside the fire in the fireplace burned brightly, and the table was laden with steaming hot food.

Pawmot, dressed up by D as Santa Claus, happily hugged a bowl, scooping Berry-mixed rice into its mouth.

Cyclizar, wrapped in a scarf, was delightedly holding a sandwich, opening its mouth wide, and took a big bite.

Having eaten several pieces of Alcremie's Poffins before dinner, it wasn't very hungry now. Alcremie lay lazily on the couch, touching its plump belly with its little hands.

"Vee～"

Glaceon sniffed the creamy ice smoothie filled with the scent of milk in front of it, then happily buried its face into Eevee's bowl, licking the delicious smoothie with its pink tongue.

Dragonite grabbed a string of pan-fried bass with Dragon Claw, ignoring the oil on its claws as it opened its mouth to bite.

The bass, pan-fried, had turned crispy and golden on the outside, but the flesh inside remained tender and juicy, offering a distinct, wonderful texture with each bite.

Feeling the deliciousness burst in its mouth, Dragonite's eyes instantly lit up.

"Roar!" So tasty!

Dolliv quietly sat to the side, seemingly liking the lively atmosphere, with a constant cheerful smile on its little face.

Mew and Teddiursa, two small-sized Pokémon, sat directly on the table.

In front of them was a honey feast that D had specially prepared.

It included honey pudding, honey buns, honey grilled fish, honey milk, honey cookies, honey roasted meat, and Berry mixed with honey.

Surrounded by the honey feast, Teddiursa and Mew thoroughly enjoyed the feeling of being enveloped in happiness.

Compared to the other Pokémon who were eating heartily, the Indeedee sibling pair ate with much more elegance.

Judging from their expressions, the pair also liked this lively atmosphere very much.

In fact, before arriving at the ranch, the Indeedee siblings had worried about their future.

At that time, they didn't know what the family they were heading to would be like or how their new owner would treat them.

With a sense of unease, the siblings kept diligently following their parents in learning various etiquettes and doing chores.

It wasn't until they arrived at the ranch and met D that they settled down.

The two Pokémon were very happy to have met a master like D and were keen to care for everything for him.

What delighted the siblings even more was the atmosphere of the ranch.

Everyone lived together harmoniously, without bullying because of being strong or weak.

Thinking of this, the Indeedee brother couldn't help but gaze at their owner.

D drank a cup of hot juice, feeling warm all over; he was watching the Pokémon's diverse eating styles when he suddenly sensed a gaze directed at him.

Turning his head, he saw the Indeedee brother looking at him.

"What's up?" D asked.

The Indeedee brother quickly lowered his head, calmly replying, "Ee-yip."

The Indeedee sister, seeing this scene, unhesitatingly exposed her brother: "Ee-yip!"

D noticed the Indeedee sister seemed to have something to say to him, so he stuffed a piece of Translation Nuts Cake into his mouth.

Then he heard the Indeedee sister say: "Ee-yip～" (Brother is embarrassed～)

The Indeedee brother urgently interrupted: "Ee-yip!" (Don't say it!)

But it was too late.

The Indeedee sister stepped back several steps, distancing herself from her brother, before saying to D: "Ee-yip!" (Brother actually likes D, he's just too shy to say so!)

"Ee-yip!"

The Indeedee brother hung his head, having given up struggling.

Although he had a vague feeling, hearing it directly from the Indeedee moved D deeply.

"I see!" He looked at the Indeedee brother and said with a smile, "I like you guys too. Here's to the new year! Let's keep up our good work!"

The Indeedee brother paused, looking at D, then nodded firmly in agreement: "Ee-yip!"

"Enjoy the food!" D said.

The Indeedee sister giggled as she returned to her brother's side, and the Indeedee brother glanced at his sister, sighing helplessly like a little adult.

Watching the Indeedee siblings' little actions, D's face showed a smile.

He suddenly noticed Koraidon next to him also zoning out.

D couldn't help but turn to look: "What's up? Not hungry?"

Koraidon's feathers drooped, and light sparkled in its golden eyes, a gentle look that was nothing like the fierce and domineering wing king described in the Book of Scarlet and Violet.

D tilted his head, patiently waiting for its response.

"Awg…" Koraidon said straightforwardly and warmly: "Awg-sys!" (I like D too!)

"I know." D petted its head.

Koraidon thought for a moment, feeling different from the other Pokémon, then added: "Awg-sys!" (I like D the most!)

Upon hearing this, Dragonite stopped eating roasted bass and immediately leaned in to express its affection.

"Roar!" (I'm the Pokémon that likes D the most!)

---

Pawmot was also drawn over: "Pa-mo!" (Me too!)

Alcremie, who had eaten its fill and was now lying on the sofa, sprang up energetically upon hearing this:

"Ma-ma!" (D's favorite Pokemon is me! He even built a cream workshop just for me!)

Dragonite argued, "Gwaa-gwaa!" (It's me! D went to great lengths to raise me and help me become the strongest Dragonite!)

Upon hearing this, Pawmot joined the discussion: "Pa-mo!" (D also got me a cape and a hammer!)

The Pokemon were endlessly debating over who was D's favorite Pokemon.

However, after calling out for a long time, they couldn't come to a conclusion and eventually all turned to D.

Though Cyclizar didn't participate in the debate, it quietly cast an expectant look in this direction.

D: "..."

It feels like this isn't really a good question!

"You guys..." he said.

The Pokemon widened their eyes, all waiting for the answer.

Even Calyrex glanced slightly over.

D: "You are all my favorite Pokemon."

Light sparkled in the eyes of all the Pokemon.

"Pa-mo..."

"Gwaa..."

"Waa-gwaa..."

"Let's eat up!" D started to change the subject: "After we finish dinner, I'll read the letters from the town residents to you!"

The Pokemon's attention was immediately drawn, and they began to continue with dinner.

Seeing this, D secretly breathed a sigh of relief.

At this moment, he suddenly remembered there was a Cufant hiding in the tavern.

Thinking of this, D stood up and took some Poffins and Berries, went to the tavern, opened the door, placed the dish of food in the dark, and then turned to leave.

It was unknown how much time passed before a small Pokemon cautiously emerged from the darkness.

It approached the fragrant Poffins, looked down at this food, then looked up towards the direction the human had left, not knowing what it was thinking in its heart.

*

After dinner, the Pokemon were playing in the living room, and brother Indeedee cleaned up the table and the hygiene. Meanwhile, D and sister Indeedee were washing the dishes.

When all the chores were done, the time had already reached eight in the evening.

D wiped his hands and then went to the bedroom to get a stack of letters sent by Allen.

These were all sent by the townsfolk and nearby farm owners over the last two days.

He opened and checked two of them this morning and found they were all New Year blessings, so he decided to leave the rest to read with the Pokemon in the evening.

With these letters, D sat in front of the fireplace and waved towards the living room.

Soon, the Pokemon playing and frolicking around quickly ran over and gathered around him.

The flames in the fireplace flickered gently, casting long shadows of the Pokemon and D.

After ensuring everyone was present, D opened the first letter.

"I'm going to start reading now!"

"Ah-ga-sse!" Koraidon happily nodded in response.

D smiled slightly, while stroking Pawmot lying on his lap, he began to read the content of the letter.

"Hey, D, Happy New Year! You've unknowingly been on this ranch for a year now. If your grandfather saw how well you're running the ranch, he'd surely be very pleased, right? The year is now drawing to a close, so keep working hard next year too! Lastly, give my regards to Cyclizar—Mayor Thomas."

D waved the letter in his hand towards Cyclizar and said, "Did you hear? Mayor Thomas is wishing you a Happy New Year!"

"Waa-gwaa～"

Cyclizar nodded its big head happily.

D said, "Tomorrow we'll go together to give Mayor Thomas a New Year's gift."

Cyclizar gladly agreed.

Next, D read the second letter.

"Happy New Year! D! This is Keri's Forest Workshop, thanks for your hard work this year! Please continue to take care of us next year! If you need anything, feel free to order furniture from this side anytime—Keri."

"Just right, after winter ends I'm planning to build a greenhouse on the ranch so we can enjoy the freshest vegetables, no matter the season." D eagerly shared his plans with the Pokemon.

The Pokemon listened carefully; although they couldn't quite understand, seeing D happy made them feel happy too.

"Next, let's see—this is a letter from Mr. Elif. Do you remember Mr. Elif? He runs a cherry garden next door."

Koraidon and Cyclizar remembered, but Indeedee and Glaceon were a bit confused.

"In spring, Mr. Elif will host a Sakura Blossom Wine Party with many people and Pokemon attending, enjoying wine and sharing food," D explained, "Last time it was held, you all hadn't come yet, but after some time, I'll take you to experience it."

"Ai-ee～"

Sister Indeedee gladly agreed.

D smiled slightly and continued reading the letters slowly, at first with the Pokemon listening attentively.

But later on, they all began to feel sleepy.

With full stomachs, soft carpet, and a warm fireplace, such an environment indeed makes one drowsy.

Seeing this, D paused, placed the letters to the side, then patted Pawmot next to him, saying to all the Pokemon:

---

"If you're sleepy, go to sleep! I'm getting sleepy too! I will go to sleep after taking a bath."

The drowsy Pokemon agreed in a daze, then obediently returned to their sleeping places.

"Goodnight, everyone!" D said softly.

Alcremie returned to its little nest in the cabinet, Dragonite transformed into a red light and went back to its Poke Ball, the Indeedee Sibling went upstairs, Cyclizar stretched lazily on the ground, then rested its head on its arm and lay down right there...

D watched this scene and then brought Calyrex to the Glass Greenhouse: "See you tomorrow, Calyrex."

Calyrex nodded slightly: "See you tomorrow, D."

D smiled gently, his heart as clear and bright as a cloudless sky.

After bidding farewell to Calyrex, he turned and hurried back.

On the way, D suddenly remembered there was one Pokemon he had neglected.

He slapped his forehead, quickly ran back inside to take out a few boxes of Poffin and headed to the central lake of the pasture.

The Reversed World is as vast as the real world.

As the ruler of the Reversed World, Giratina does not stay in one area for too long.

Just like the wandering photographer Ho-oh, Giratina also roams around the Reversed World.

But this situation only lasted until not long ago.

After he changed those Sky Islands in the Reversed World, Giratina stopped wandering, staying in this area all day.

When D and Calyrex later went to develop Sky Island, Giratina was either lying on Flower Island sunbathing or on Apple Hills nibbling on apples hanging from the trees.

It seems this Underworld Dragon really liked the transformed Sky Islands.

Thus, D guessed that Giratina would still be around here at this time.

And indeed, just as he predicted, shortly after arriving at the lakeside, a purple vortex surfaced on the lake.

D immediately hugged the Poffin and jumped in.

Before, he brushed up Giratina's favor when helping it develop the Sky Island; now, Giratina is no longer the bad dragon that would bombard him for hours.

Inside the Reversed World, it has completely transformed after their modifications.

In this corresponding area of the pasture, all the Sky Islands are now covered in greenery, with the scent of flowers and fruits wafting through this heaven and earth.

At this moment, Giratina is lazily lying among the flowers, basking in the sun.

Originally, the flower field was perfectly fine, but now it has been crushed into a clearing by Giratina's enormous body, and all the flowers underneath have been squashed to death.

D: "..."

Giratina glanced at him and, looking like a cranky old dragon starting early retirement life, asked, "What brings you here?"

D "floated" to the Sky Island full of flowers, where sunflowers provided sunshine and warmth, filling the place with the scent of flowers in Dragon language.

D sat cross-legged in front of the crushed flowers in front of Giratina and opened the Poffin Box.

"I'm bringing you some snacks for boredom," D said. "Today is the human New Year, so I wanted to see you, thus I came."

Giratina has no concept of time and never cares about human holidays.

Therefore, it irritably said, "Pokemon food from the real world, I don't want it!"

After speaking, Giratina regretted it.

It couldn't help but wish it could drink some tea that would reverse its personality at that moment.

However, D didn't mind what Giratina said.

He understood this cranky dragon that says one thing but means another.

D smiled faintly, ready to leave: "Happy New Year, Giratina, please take care of me in the coming year!"

With that said, he didn't linger, but got up and left the Reversed World, returning to the pasture through the lake vortex.

Giratina looked at those Poffins in front of it, feeling a bit conflicted inside.

Humans... humans again.

Humans are really such complex creatures!

Giratina closed its eyes, remembering some humans it had encountered in its mind.

The intense desires, the determination to conquer, the emotions of destruction...

And now, it met someone completely opposite to those.

He was so pure, so sincere, even it couldn't help but be moved.

Giratina remained silent for a while, then moved its huge head closer to the Poffin Box and swallowed some Poffins into its stomach.

After leaving the Reversed World, D returned to the living room, took a quick shower, and lay down in bed.

Koraidon was sleeping beside the bed, Pawmot sleeping on his comforter.

The room was well heated, so there was no worry about getting cold.

Once his hair was dry, D turned off the light and lay down.

At this time, everyone in the pasture should be asleep, right?

No matter how windy and snowy it was outside, those cute Pokemon wouldn't be affected, they were tucked into their warm nests, peacefully dreaming on a snowy night.

Thinking of these things, D couldn't help but feel tender inside.

"Koraidon," D suddenly called out.

Koraidon immediately responded: "Ah kaa?"

D grinned: "Nothing, I just wanted to call you."

Koraidon: "..."

"Pawmo?"

Pawmot hadn't fallen asleep either, rubbing its sleepy eyes at D's voice.

Seeing this, D scooped it into his arms and then said to Koraidon:

"Goodnight, Koraidon."

Koraidon lay down again, calmly closed its eyes, resting on its arm and responded:

"Ah ka-sii."

---

---

---

---

# 287: Chapter 284: Super Sheep Milk Comparable to Holy Ash!

In the year 199 of the League, spring has just arrived in Paldea, and the temperature hasn't quite warmed up yet.

As the snow melts away, fresh green shoots are sprouting across the Paldea land.

The breeze blowing through the mountains and the babbling streams through the valleys are announcing to the world the beginning of a new annual cycle.

The pasture, covered by snow for half the winter, is finally seeing the light of day again. Grass begins to sprout, and garden vines slowly climb the trellis.

The Kilowattrel, having migrated to the far south for the winter, have returned, flapping their wings and gently landing atop rooftops, tidying up their slightly disheveled feathers with their beaks, while observing the now lively pasture.

They are Pokémon, already full-bellied, chasing and playing with each other.

The morning sunlight streams through the floor-to-ceiling window into the house, as D leisurely eats breakfast while watching today's Paldea morning news.

On the television screen, a huge and distinctive academy is situated in the bustling city center.

A crowd of boys and girls in school uniforms, accompanied by Pokémon, have already gathered in front of the academy gates.

Right in front of them, a person dressed like a principal is giving a speech:

"Welcome back to Paldea Academy, everyone! From today, Paldea Academy's famous [Treasure Hunt] will continue!"

"Go forth, children, and seek out treasures unique to yourselves all across Paldea!"

…

"It seems that treasure hunting is truly a tradition at Paldea Academy." Watching this scene, D thought to himself.

Last year, quite a few treasure hunters came to his pasture; he's unsure whether the same situation will occur this year.

In fact, D is quite hopeful that Trainers might come here.

Not just him, Dragonite and Pawmot are also eager to conduct practical training with those Trainers, turning them into experience points and becoming stronger Pokémon as a result.

"There's no rush with that point, if we follow the schedule, those students are just setting out from school now," D thought to himself.

Moreover, since Paldea Academy encourages a free spirit, there are bound to be some students who prefer to stay at school for classes and Pokémon cultivation, even if others like to venture out.

By the time someone ventures here, half a month might have passed.

Thinking of this, D finished the last mouthful of white porridge in his bowl, then got up to tidy the table.

Today, he has more important things to do.

Lately, the Gogoat have consumed a large amount of World Tree leaves and have constantly been nourished by the World Tree.

D is curious if this will have any effect on the milk produced by the Gogoat.

He put away the cleaned tableware, then grabbed the milking machine and had Koraidon help carry the milk bucket to the World Tree.

Early in the morning, the Gogoat started their daily "milking tree action."

They formed a triangle around the World Tree, with a faint green energy exuding from their bodies.

This Grass Energy transformed into tiny green light particles emanating from the Gogoat, then surged into the trunk of the World Tree.

The leaves at the World Tree's top also emitted a sprinkle of green light.

Seeing D approaching with tools, the Gogoat stopped and joyously bleated: "Maa~"

"How are you feeling today?" D patted the head of one Gogoat, then placed the milk bucket underneath it and began milking.

The other two Gogoat lined up behind, while the one being milked looked happy:

"Maa!"

"I see!" D said, glancing at the bucket filled with milk that exuded a rich creamy aroma.

The next moment, information about this bucket of milk surfaced in his mind.

[Gogoat's Milk (Ultra Perfect): Fresh milk enhanced by Nature Power, with quality surpassing perfection, containing nutrients dozens of times greater than ordinary milk.

Consuming it provides immense benefits to the body; it's said that no matter how serious the injuries, Pokémon can recover immediately after drinking it, and it can be used to make top-notch cheese, top-quality butter, and other foods.]

"Indeed…" Observing this information, D murmured to himself: "Indeed, the World Tree's leaves and aura influence Gogoat's milk."

Previously, Gogoat's milk was already enhanced by Berry Milk, which improved its quality and nutrients, reaching top-tier level.

Now, after consuming World Tree leaves, the milk's quality has upgraded again.

No matter how severe the injuries, immediate recovery is possible... this effect rivals that of a Revive and Max Revive.

What frightens D even more is that this is clearly not the limit for Gogoat and its milk.

Once Gogoat fully grows in the future, D dares not even imagine what effects the milk might possess.

It might rival the Holy Ash that can revive the dead and restore flesh to bones!

Holy Ash is a mythical item born from Ho-oh.

It can restore full health to a Pokémon that is on the verge of death.

Legend has it that Ho-oh once used Holy Ash to resurrect three Pokémon that perished in the Brass Tower fire.

These three Pokémon later became the Legendary beasts: Entei, Lei Gong, along with Suicune, the incarnation of the north wind.

---

Upon thinking about this, D's first thought wasn't how astounding the effect would be, but: No way! He has to raise the price!

"I need to have Zack recheck the quality of the sheep milk and then readjust the pricing," D thought.

This sort of thing happens frequently on the ranch and farm.

The physical condition of one Pokemon isn't the same as another. Take Miltanks, some Miltanks are innately weak, producing thin milk of poor quality.

In such cases, this MooMoo Milk would be sold very cheaply.

However, some Miltanks are strong, and coupled with high-quality pasture, they produce milk of exceptional quality.

This milk often fetches a high price.

Zack and the others, responsible for collecting fresh milk at the ranch, do not consistently purchase it at a single price. The actual price fluctuates depending on the milk's quality.

After pondering for a moment, D milked two more Gogoats.

Speaking of which, these three Gogoats are the heroes of their ranch!

D Ranch relied on these three to gradually produce the milk!

D felt deeply moved, seeing the Gogoats still looking at him, waiting for his next command, D couldn't help but smile:

"Alright, that's it, today's work is done..."

Yet before he could finish his sentence, D suddenly remembered there was another task requiring the Gogoats' assistance.

He had Koraidon store the milk and then led the Gogoats to the ranch warehouse.

It was now filled with saplings and grass seeds he had previously ordered from the town.

Today, D was set to officially begin the rear mountain reconstruction plan.

"Do you remember the Pokemon we rescued from the rear mountain?" D asked.

The Gogoats thought carefully, then nodded repeatedly: "Baa!"

Those Pokemon who escaped the forest fire...

The Gogoats still remembered vividly to this day.

That forest fire consumed everything and many Pokemon lost their lives in the fire.

The once-thriving rear mountain had turned into a desolate place due to the fire, leaving the surviving Pokemon full of sorrow and fear.

Seeing that the Gogoats still remembered, D reached out to pat their heads and continued:

"Today, I plan to help them rebuild their homes and plant these grass seeds and saplings."

Upon hearing this, the Gogoats immediately responded: "Baa!"

They wanted to help too!

"Good kids." His Gogoats were kind-hearted Pokemon too! D spoke softly with a smile: "Then I'll rely on you! Next, you will have to transport these goods to the rear mountain."

"Baa!" No problem!

The Gogoats were full of enthusiasm.

Seeing this, D began to hang grass seeds and saplings on the Gogoats' backs.

Soon, the three Gogoats were packed full.

Carrying the hopes of those rescued Pokemon, they followed behind D and Koraidon to the Berry Garden.

There, the Pokemon who had heard about it in advance were already waiting.

Greedent, Tinkatink, Grafaiai, Bellibolt, Maushold, Wattrel, Petilil, Hoppip, Surskit, Shroomish.

Except for Cramorant who flew away in advance and Kricketune who joined the band, the Pokemon who survived the fire were all here.

They spent a safe winter at the ranch, and their fur, once burnt black and barren, now looked completely renewed.

Upon seeing D and Koraidon, this adorable group of Pokemon immediately became excited and cheerful.

Their eyes were fixed on D, filled with anticipation.

Seeing this scene, D also felt a strong emotion, exhaling a deep breath, he spoke loudly: "Finally, the moment has come, are you excited?"

"Squeak squeak!"

Greedent joyfully jumped up on the grass.

D smiled lightly, saying nothing more, just: "If that's the case, then let's set off!"

"Wah!"

"Gumo!"

The Pokemon cheered, quickly transforming into little wagging tails following D, ready to rebuild their home together with him.

Before setting off, D habitually counted the number of Pokemon.

"One, two, three... thirteen, huh? Why is there one missing?"

Seeing this, D stopped in confusion.

The faces of Greedent and the others showed perplexion at his action.

D said: "One is missing, who hasn't come over yet?"

Upon hearing this, the Pokemon group looked left and right, until Greedent slapped its paw, finally remembering who hadn't come.

"Squeak squeak!" (It's the Slakoth that loves sleeping!)

Greedent said while turning around and running towards the depths of the Berry Garden.

D instructed the Gogoats to wait here while he followed the Pokemon group deeper into the Berry Garden.

Soon, he found that due to eating the Dye Berry, the Slakoth had become a different color while sleeping soundly in the middle of the grass in the orchard.

At this moment, Slakoth was still sleeping loudly there.

D: "..." As expected of Slakoth!

Greedent quickly ran up to Slakoth and shook it with its little paw.

Slakoth was awakened, its eyes half-opened, looking drowsy.

Greedent shouted at Slakoth: "Squeak squeak!"

Slakoth looked at Greedent with a confused look.

Then... lazily it yawned.

D: "..." All the Slaking family are like this.

Greedent sighed helplessly.

D: "Forget it, since it wants to sleep here, let it sleep here!" Anyway, the ranch isn't missing its mouth to feed.

Greedent, now eagerly awaiting the rebuilding of its home, disregarded Slakoth and immediately followed D back.

On the way, Greedent started fantasizing about its future life.

Then, once the trees in the forest had grown tall, it would find the tallest and largest tree and hollow out a spacious tree hole to store all the berries it found, packed full!

When returning to the group of Pokemon, D suddenly saw that Pawmot, Dragonite, Alcremie, Cyclizar, Glaceon, Dolliv, Indeedee Sibling, Butterfree and other ranch Pokemon had come to this side.

While behind the group of Pokemon, Calyrex was elegantly levitating, feet off the ground.

Seeing D look over, Calyrex spoke with a gentle voice: "I had promised you before, when rebuilding the rear mountain, I would also come to help."

D smiled and nodded: "Then thank you! It's not too late, let's set off!"

D took up his tools and led a group of Pokemon towards the rear mountain.

*

Meanwhile, in the Pokemon Rescue Center of Porto Marinada Town, the Paldea League was also planning to carry out an ecological restoration plan in the rear mountain.

That forest fire had caused quite a stir, garnering the League's special attention.

To expedite recovery, they recruited forest rangers and experts in planting trees from all parts of Paldea.

Even the reigning Champion Geeta came here, both to handle League tasks and visit the surviving Pokemon.

Nearby, Miss Joy looked admiringly at the Champion comforting the Pokemon, saying to Miss Jenny:

"It feels like the Champion is surprisingly approachable!"

Miss Jenny looked at Geeta over there, having heard from League staff that the Champion's daily work was exceedingly busy.

Sometimes, Geeta was so exhausted from work that she wouldn't realize Glimmet was tangled in her hair, bringing it directly to the League office.

However, now Geeta reserved special time to check on the aftermath of the fire.

Miss Jenny nodded lightly, agreeing with what Miss Joy said.

Their Champion was indeed a reliable person.

At that moment, Miss Jenny's walkie-talkie emitted a sound.

She conversed briefly with the person on the other end, then looked up and said to everyone: "Alright, the forest protection team is ready. We should set off now!"

Geeta heard this too, she straightened up, hands behind her back, preparing to go together and have a look.

Upon seeing this, the League staff beside her was startled, quickly straightening up to salute: "Ch- Champion!"

Geeta reassured them: "I will go along too, don't be nervous, just act as if I'm not here."

League staff: "Yes... yes!"

Geeta smiled slightly: "Well then, let's go!"

---

---

---

---

# 288: Chapter 285: Rebuilding Our Home
