Winter passes and spring arrives. After experiencing the forest fire, the back mountain, once covered in snow, has completely changed its appearance.

The branches and leaves devoured by the flames have turned into nutrients for the earth, rejoining the cycle of nature.

This gives the barren land a faint sign of recovery.

On the dirt path leading to the back mountain, D is advancing while observing the surrounding situation with a group of Pokemon.

Three Gogoat carrying bags of seedlings and grass seeds follow them.

After several months, everyone returns to this place.

At first, the Pokemon living in the forest leaped and frolicked, eager for the rebuilding of their home.

But as the distance shortened, and upon seeing the desolate and barren scene, Greedent and the others were reminded of past memories.

That night, the fire that engulfed the whole back mountain.

Within the crimson flames, countless Pokemon figures were desperately running.

Some Pokemon succeeded, while others failed.

Everyone... those Pokemon that once lived here with them were mercilessly swallowed by the fire.

Thinking of this, Greedent and the others couldn't help but lower their heads in sadness.

Upon seeing this, Alcremie, understanding the encounter of these Pokemon, stood up to cheer them on.

"Mwah!"

Greedent and other Pokemon looked up.

Soon after, Pawmot flew up to Alcremie, saying passionately, "Bamoo!"

They will help together!

"Awoo!"

"Gaho!"

"..."

As these words echoed, other Pokemon in the ranch responded one after another.

They voluntarily stepped forward, standing behind Pawmot and Alcremie, telling the group of unfortunate Pokemon: Everyone will work hard together to restore this place to its original state!

"Woow…" Tinkatink gazed at this scene, its eyes gradually glistening, then tears of emotion welled up, and it earnestly expressed gratitude to the group of Pokemon helping them: "Woow!"

"Squeak!"

"Gumo!"

"..."

Greedent, Maushold, Bellibolt, and other Pokemon began expressing their thanks noisily.

Walking ahead, D looked back at this scene, and a smile appeared on his face.

They resembled the wild Pokemon he had seen in the movie "Zarude and Coco," collaborating to protect the forest from the evil hands.

Even though they might have conflicts and quarrels daily, when a real crisis arises, they will unite regardless of race or type to protect their home together.

Rejuvenated, Greedent and the other Pokemon caught up, and after walking for about ten more minutes, D and his companions officially entered the back mountain area.

"Alright, let's start from here!"

D extended his hand, signaling the Pokemon to stop.

Then, he got close to the Gogoat and, together with Koraidon and Cyclizar, unloaded the grass seeds and seedlings from them, placing them on the land.

Looking at the multitude of Pokemon before him, D pondered slightly, then started distributing grass seeds to each of them.

D: "Pawmot, Dragonite, Daxia, Butterfree, Wattrel, the task of scattering grass seeds is entrusted to you."

"Bamoo!"

Upon receiving the instructions, Pawmot promptly stepped forward to take a pack of seeds from D's hand, while the remaining Pokemon did the same, awaiting further directions from D.

D then turned his gaze to the Indeedee Sibling: "Use telekinesis to survey this area, then divide it into small plots, with each Pokemon responsible for one."

Upon hearing this, the siblings immediately understood D's intention.

They stepped forward and began assigning plots to Pawmot and the others as instructed.

D looked at Grafaiai: "Grafaiai, you can use your paint to mark the ground, helping everyone to distinguish."

Grafaiai promptly nodded in agreement.

Next, D turned to the remaining Pokemon.

"I will plant these seedlings with Cyclizar. Diglett, you team up with Koraidon; use the Dig move to dig holes, and then have Koraidon plant the seedlings. Can you manage that?"

"Squeak!" Diglett straightened its body confidently.

"Very good, let's start the work!"

With D's command, the group of Pokemon dispersed in all directions.

Butterfree and Dragonite kept a low flight while carrying grass seeds, opening their small packs and skillfully scattering seeds onto the ground below.

D observed the scene with satisfaction and nodded.

Once everything is ready, they can ask Calyrex to help the grass and seedlings grow quickly.

He thought it was time for him to start helping too!

With this in mind, D was about to grab a shovel to dig holes for planting trees, when he noticed the remaining Pokemon looking at him expectantly.

D: "..."

Alcremie: "Mwah?" What work will they be doing?

D thought carefully, and then he came up with a good idea: "We're still missing a cheerleading team!"

As he spoke, D's gaze swept across the idle Pokemon in front of him:

"Alcremie, Dolliv, Maushold, Glaceon, Tinkatink, Petilil, Surskit, Hoppip, and Shroomish, you guys form a cheerleading squad to cheer everyone on!"

---

"Wow..." Tinkatink felt that this was too easy, it wanted to do some heavy work.

Seeing this, D said earnestly, "Actually, cheerleading is very important. Your shouts and cheers can provide motivation for everyone working hard and ease their fatigue!"

Hearing this, Alcremie immediately widened its eyes, excitedly expressing its willingness to join the cheerleading team.

"Maa maa!"

Petilil and Dolliv also nodded their heads in agreement.

Only Glaceon sighed helplessly. Shouting and cheering for everyone, it felt a bit awkward to do so...

It was truly a dilemma for Eevee.

But thinking about how everyone was working hard, it couldn't just sit idly by.

So, Glaceon put those thoughts aside.

It followed behind the socially fearless Alcremie and Dolliv, weaving among the busy Pokemon.

When they arrived in the area where Dragonite was working, Alcremie immediately raised its head and shouted loudly, "Maa maa!!!"

Dolliv also softly said, "New!"

Only Glaceon quietly said, "Bui..."

"Maa maa?" Alcremie looked at Glaceon, shook its head indicating that the voice was too soft and Dragonite wouldn't hear it, then demonstrated, "Maa—maa!!!"

"Bui?!" Glaceon was shocked; that was too loud!

Alcremie and Dolliv looked at it expectantly.

Glaceon opened its mouth and could only say, "Bui..."

"Maa maa!" That's it!

Alcremie nodded and said to it, "Maa maa!" That's right! Shout it out!

With a flushed face, Glaceon, in a sort of reckless abandon, loudly shouted, "Bui—!"

"Maa maa!" Alcremie was satisfied.

And after shouting, Glaceon suddenly felt that doing this wasn't as hard as it thought?

After the demonstration, the cheerleading team led by Alcremie continued to cheer for the next Pokemon.

"I think I just heard Glaceon's shout." D, who was planting seedlings with Cyclizar, looked up, somewhat surprised, in a certain direction.

Cyclizar also looked over, nodding, "Grrr!" It had heard it too.

"I can guarantee that was the loudest shout I've ever heard from Glaceon," D said.

Glaceon had made such a big decision to shout, it seemed it really wanted to do something for these surviving Pokemon!

Cyclizar nodded in agreement, "Grrr!"

D planted this seedling, then moved with Cyclizar a few meters away to start planting the next one.

"I wonder when the Paldea League will come over to rebuild the ecology of the rear mountain," D pondered.

The area of the rear mountain was large, and their energy was limited, unable to encompass everything, so the rest would have to be handled by the Paldea League.

Although the time might be staggered, it didn't matter.

At most, they could wait until the Paldea League planted the trees and then come back to help those trees grow fast.

As time passed, Dragonite and they worked very efficiently, and in less than two hours, they finished sowing all the grass seeds D bought.

The seedlings D was planting were almost done, just enough to form a small forest.

Aside from these normal seedlings, D also separately took out a package.

Opening the package, it was unexpectedly full of Berry seeds.

After reconstruction, the rear mountain couldn't do without food, so D was planning to plant these Berry seeds throughout the forest for the Greedent to eat.

Just as D was about to start planting Berry trees, Cyclizar beside him seemed to suddenly discover something and turned its head to look in a direction.

D turned his head and saw several figures had appeared there at some point.

Some of these people were wearing blue work clothes, holding shovels, accompanied by Pokemon like Sandshrew and Trapinch.

When D looked over, those Sandshrews were using Dig on the ground.

Then, the people in blue work clothes planted the seedlings they brought into the holes.

Not far from these people were a few more individuals.

The person on the left was wearing a white uniform, a nurse cap, and had long pink hair.

The middle person was wearing a smart police uniform, and by her side was a Growlithe, looking around and seemingly observing the surroundings.

The person on the right had their hands behind their back, with an elegant demeanor and a gentle expression, chatting with the people beside them.

Seeing this scene, D was momentarily stunned.

That was... Miss Joy, Miss Jenny, Geeta—why were they here too?

Meanwhile, Miss Joy on the other side also noticed D in the distance and exclaimed, "Huh? Is that D from D Ranch?"

Hearing this, Miss Jenny and Geeta immediately turned to look.

From afar, Geeta saw the figure she had met several times before.

—It was that Trainer with Champion Level strength, yet chose to run a ranch in the countryside!

An extraordinary talent! Geeta felt a surge of excitement and a warm smile appeared on her face as she began walking over.

Seeing this, Joy and Jenny also followed.

The three arrived in front of D, Geeta smiled slightly, greeting him, "We meet again."

D: "Long time no see, Chief, are you here today to plant trees too?"

"That's right." Geeta nodded.

---

Then she saw the tools in D's hands and the saplings he had just planted behind him.

Noticing her gaze, D smiled and explained, "I previously took in some surviving Pokemon and promised them that I would help them rebuild their home in the spring, so I've come to plant some trees today."

"I see." Within Geeta's heart, she formed a new impression of this young man—he was not only a rare talent but also a kind-hearted person.

Miss Joy, standing beside them, clasped her hands together in a prayer-like gesture, moved to say it directly, "Mr. D is truly a kind-hearted person!"

Miss Jenny stood by, smiling with her lips pressed together; she had known about this for quite some time.

Being able to open a tavern specifically for Pokemon, not for profit, and proactively helping Pokemon in need, is something normal people cannot do.

D: "…"

Seeing the three people in front of him, he suddenly didn't know what to say.

According to plan, he originally intended to have Calyrex use its power to make the planted seeds and saplings grow.

But now, with Geeta's arrival, it meant that Calyrex's miracle would inevitably be witnessed by them.

"The news can't be hidden anyway; it will spread sooner or later," D thought. "Just as well, we can use this opportunity to boost Calyrex's faith."

He opened his mouth, about to speak, when he suddenly saw Miss Joy reacting as if she had discovered something, and she exclaimed.

"That Pokemon!"

Jenny and Geeta followed her gaze, and then saw a very special Pokemon among the group.

It had an extremely large head, emanating a faint blue glow, floating mid-air with its feet off the ground.

Seeing this Pokemon, Geeta was slightly taken aback and somewhat surprised, thinking it was another Pokemon she didn't recognize.

The Pokemon seemed to perceive her gaze and turned slightly, meeting her eyes in the air.

In the next second, Geeta saw a gentle smile appear on the Pokemon's face.

Before she could react, she heard Miss Joy exclaim loudly, "Oh my! Could that Pokemon... be The King of Bountiful Harvests?!"

Seeing someone recognized it, Calyrex turned its gaze toward this human. It could feel that this human was also providing faith to it.

Prompted by Miss Joy's reminder, Miss Jenny, who had been serving as a police officer in Porto Marinada Town, immediately remembered.

It was the legend of The King of Bountiful Harvests that had been passed down from D Ranch... it was said that this Pokemon could bless the crops of those who believed in it with abundance, and flowers would bloom profusely.

Many people in town had purchased statues of The King of Bountiful Harvests to place in their homes.

And after that, Porto Marinada Town indeed experienced a year of favorable weather and abundant crops.

For this reason, Mayor Thomas even commissioned a giant statue of Calyrex to be placed in the town's Festival Plaza.

And now... The King of Bountiful Harvests appeared here! Miss Jenny was utterly astonished.

Noticing the expressions of Joy and Jenny, Geeta couldn't help but feel utterly puzzled.

Had she missed something?

Thinking of this, Geeta inquired, "The King of Bountiful Harvests?"

Miss Joy was already excited: "That's right! It's the legendary God of Agriculture, capable of making crops abundant and flowers bloom! Many people in Porto Marinada Town have its statue at home. I even bought one from the store to keep in the Pokemon Center!"

Hearing this, Geeta showed an interested expression.

The God of Agriculture? That sounds fascinating!

Judging by its appearance, did D capture it?

Seeing that Calyrex was recognized, D was not surprised. He timely spoke:

"Yes, that's right, it's Calyrex. To be frank, I came to the back mountain today to ask it for help to restore this place to its original state."

"Restore it to its original state?" Geeta was confused.

D nodded, "That's right. Calyrex has the power to make plants grow instantly. With it here, the back mountain can be restored to its original state in just a moment."

Hearing this, Geeta had a look of disbelief.

To be honest, she found it hard to believe, as she had never seen it before.

Miss Joy and Miss Jenny, standing to the side, were also full of shock.

They had seen Pokemon with the power to shatter boulders and Pokemon that could destroy everything with a sandstorm, but had never seen one that could make flowers, grass, and trees grow into large trees in an instant.

To make the entire back mountain's flowers, grass, and trees grow out...

Such great power...

Although The King of Bountiful Harvests could ensure that crops grew smoothly, could it really do so for such a large back mountain?

---

---

---

---

# 289: Chapter 286: Miracle Revealed, Life Abounds!

This answer is affirmative.

D looked at Geeta, and said to her helplessly, "To be honest, we originally planned to restore this area today. Since the League has also chosen today, let's just do it together!"

Together...

Without needing an explanation, Geeta understood the meaning.

She looked at the young man in front of her, saw his determined expression, and after a brief thought, she quickly responded:

"That's indeed something to look forward to!"

To quickly restore the Pokémon ecosystem of this place, the Paldea League dispatched many staff members to plant trees here.

Most of these people were forest rangers. No one understands planting trees better than them, and with the assistance of many Pokémon, the ecological restoration work was completed ahead of schedule before nightfall.

The head ranger brought his Pokémon to report the work to Geeta.

After listening, Geeta nodded, then turned her gaze toward D and the mysterious Pokémon known as Calyrex beside him.

"Next, I'll leave it to you," she entrusted with the authority of Paldea League's chief.

D turned his gaze to Calyrex: "I'm counting on you, Calyrex."

Calyrex nodded slightly: "Leave it to me!"

As the words fell, it slowly floated forward.

At this moment, the forest rangers who had worked all day were gradually returning, planning what to eat for dinner, when they saw a luminous Pokémon with a large head floating into the sky.

"That Pokémon is?!"

"It-It's flying!"

"What's it going to do?"

On the other side, Miss Joy and Miss Jenny were also looking up at the sky.

Miss Joy was somewhat worried: "Can Calyrex really do it?"

Miss Jenny said nothing, her eyes hinting at anticipation.

Gradually, Calyrex rose above the forest.

Time unknowingly reached dusk, and under the beautiful sunset, Calyrex gently gazed at this land, its people, and Pokémon.

It could feel that its presence was spreading across this unfamiliar land, with streams of warm energy coming from afar, flowing into its body.

This energy came from people who knew its name.

Additionally, it sensed an emotion called expectation.

Pokémon rescued by D were hopeful, hoping their home could be rebuilt.

Then...

"As you wish," Calyrex said softly.

The next second, it waved its right hand.

A surge of rich and vibrant energy burst forth from it, then merged into the ground below.

Instantly, the entire land glowed with blue light.

The light rapidly spread into the distance, eventually enveloping the entire barren hillside.

At this moment, Calyrex's energy reached its peak, and the whole hillside erupted in a brilliant light.

Amongst the light, people and Pokémon were looking around in disbelief, gazing at the scene around them.

At some point, a cry of surprise emerged from the crowd.

"Flowers...flowers are blooming!"

His voice seemed to open some kind of floodgate, and the next second, the ground was awash in vibrant green light.

Grass sprouted visibly, transforming into a sea of green that quickly covered the hills and valleys.

The saplings planted by people also underwent rapid transformations.

From a tender young seedling, each grew into a towering tree with thick trunks and lush branches. Together, these trees formed a verdant, life-filled forest, with some trees bearing large, plump berries on their branches.

Witnessing such a miracle, everyone was speechless.

Someone murmured, "Oh my! What kind of power is this?!"

"The trees grew in an instant. Was it that Pokémon who did it?"

"What kind of Pokémon is that? It's amazing!"

Until a voice filled with excitement and awe rang out: "A miracle! This is a miracle!"

Hearing this voice, Geeta was instantly awakened as if from a dream.

Even as Paldea's chief champion, her face was filled with shock.

"There truly exists such a Pokémon in this world... truly worthy of the title 'The King of Bountiful Harvests'!"

Miss Joy and Miss Jenny were also visibly moved.

Joy: "This great power, Calyrex is incredible!"

Miss Jenny did not speak, but she felt a premonition that one day, the name of The King of Bountiful Harvests would spread throughout the entire Paldea Region, with everyone placing its statue in their homes!

Not only people, but the Pokémon who witnessed all this were also deeply moved.

They stood dazed, watching the scene.

Their home was back...

Seeing this familiar place after an unfamiliar spectacle, some Pokémon couldn't help but shed tears.

Their home was finally back!

No one knows how long it took for the light that enveloped the hillside to gradually transform into starlight and dissipate.

Beneath the light was a completely renewed forest.

The tree trunks were thick, their dark green canopies intertwined, and the setting sun cast mottled light and shadows on the grass through the gaps in the canopy.

In the fertile grass, countless small white flowers bloomed, all showing a vibrant scene.

Looking around, the whole forest was lush and green, with no trace of the fierce disaster it had once gone through.

Calyrex slowly floated back to D's side, nodding to him gently.

"Thank you for your hard work!" D said, and then turned his gaze toward the excited and tearful Greedents: "Alright, the promise to you has been fulfilled. Now enjoy your new life!"

"Squeak, squeak!"

The Greedent, coming back to its senses, quickly wiped its tears with its small paws and then jumped into D's arms in front of everyone.

The rest of the Pokémon saved by D also gathered around, each in their way expressing their gratitude to him.

Seeing this, D smiled slightly and patted each of their heads: "I can feel your gratitude, but there's someone even more deserving of your thanks!"

Upon hearing this, the group of Pokémon looked back at Calyrex and quickly ran to its side, expressing their gratitude to it.

Calyrex looked at these children with kind eyes.

D stood up and took a deep breath, feeling accomplished.

This matter was finally finished!

At this moment, Geeta walked forward, her eyes on Calyrex and the Pokémon rescued by D, murmuring:

"I never thought such a Pokémon would exist in this world..."

D nodded gently: "Indeed!"

Geeta, still a bit shaken and in awe, suddenly recalled what Miss Joy had previously mentioned — that Calyrex is the God of Agriculture, capable of blessing humans who believe in it with bountiful crops.

Thinking of this, a thought briefly stirred within Geeta.

If... just if.

If everyone in the Paldea Region were to believe in this Pokémon, would the land beneath their feet become even more bountiful?

---

---

---

---

# 290: Chapter 287: Friends in the Forest and the Berry of Friendship!

In the lush, green mountains.

The forest rangers sent by the Paldea League stared in shock at the gigantic-headed Pokemon, and the young man beside it.

What happened today had far exceeded their understanding.

What kind of Pokemon could instantly turn seeds planted in the soil into a fully grown forest?

"Hey, hey! What exactly happened? How did the forest grow instantly?"

At this moment, hurried footsteps approached from behind the crowd.

Some rangers who arrived late due to planting trees elsewhere ran over, faces full of disbelief, gazing at their comrades.

"That Pokemon helped us!"

Someone raised a trembling hand, pointing towards the distant Calyrex.

The late-arriving rangers looked up and saw a Pokemon with a huge head, wearing a gentle smile on its face, standing on the grass.

They had never seen such a Pokemon before.

But from its levitating feet, the glow emanating from its body, and its elegant demeanor, it was clearly not an ordinary Pokemon.

Thinking of this, the group of rangers was eager to know the name of that Pokemon.

"In this world, there actually exists a power that can make plants grow instantly!"

"What kind of Pokemon is that?"

As the words fell, people instinctively turned their gaze to the young man beside the Pokemon.

He seemed to be the trainer of that Pokemon. As a trainer, he must know about the Pokemon!

D immediately felt the eyes resting on him.

He smiled slightly and slowly began to speak: "He is Calyrex, the Pokemon that once ruled an entire region."

"Caly…rex?"

"King Pokemon…"

People murmured in disbelief.

Upon hearing D's words, Calyrex's face displayed a gentle smile.

His little devotee was once again diligently spreading his name.

However, the King of Galar had become a thing of the past. Now, he was just an ordinary farmer Pokemon.

"Alright, Chief, the matter here is settled. We shall return first," D said, looking at Geeta.

Geeta finally snapped out of it, her eyes sparkling with extraordinary brilliance, and said:

"Once again, the Paldea League owes you, as the chief, I must sincerely express our gratitude to you and Calyrex."

D smiled indifferently: "Both humans and Pokemon are part of this world, helping them is also helping humanity itself. No need to thank me."

After all, the mountains behind us were also a natural treasure trove for ranchers like us, much like the wild berries and vegetables in spring and summer, and mushrooms and pine cones in autumn.

Thanks to the poisonous mushrooms growing in those mountains, I was able to meet Giratina, the legendary Pokemon.

Yet Geeta shook her head: "No matter what, you've helped the Paldea League solve the challenge of restoring the region's ecology. At normal speed, this mountain would take a year to recover."

"Not only that, during this time it would require considerable human and material resources to repair the natural ecology here. But now, thanks to you, the Paldea League can save this expense and invest in more meaningful things."

Upon hearing this, D did not continue to decline.

However, in the next second, Geeta extended an olive branch to him.

"The Paldea League welcomes outstanding talent, trainers like you. If you're willing, I will apply to the League to specially invite you to join directly."

D: "..."

Here we go again.

D understood Geeta's meaning. Working for the Paldea League was somewhat akin to being a public servant in his past life — a secure job.

Such that when one gets older and retires, every month one can receive a generous pension from the Paldea League.

For some professional trainers, this was an invaluable opportunity.

After all, every young trainer will eventually grow old.

But he really does not need it!

Seeing this, Geeta couldn't help but sigh in regret.

D smiled slightly, did not say much more, turned to bid farewell to Miss Joy and Miss Jenny, and then left with the Pokemon from the ranch.

Geeta watched their figures fade away into the dense mountains, then turned and said:

"Alright, thank you all for your hard work today!"

The group of forest rangers instantly felt like waking from a dream. The sky had darkened, and the day's work had come to an end.

Thus, they began packing up their tools, heading down the mountain, ready to recount today's occurrences to their friends and family once back.

The mysterious Pokemon Calyrex, restoring the entire mountain at once, was truly unbelievable!

With this, the matter regarding the mountain was perfectly resolved.

Watching the evening glow at the horizon, Miss Jenny and Miss Joy felt their moods relax as well.

"Calyrex is really amazing!" Miss Joy exclaimed.

Miss Jenny nodded; originally, she held a skeptical attitude towards the truth of such matters, but now, having witnessed the miracle performed by the King of Bountiful Harvests, all doubts in her heart vanished.

---

"I really didn't expect D to capture such a Pokemon..." Miss Jenny murmured.

Geeta also overheard the conversation between the two. She didn't interrupt but silently pondered in her heart how they should thank D.

After thinking for a long time, Geeta still couldn't come up with an answer.

After a moment of silence, she said to herself, "Forget it, I'll leave this matter to Rika afterward. She's better at these things than I am."

*

In the forest near the ranch, D was bidding farewell to Koraidon and the others, along with the Greedent.

"Alright, from today on, this will be your new home," D said with a gentle smile. "Pick a spot you like and settle down!"

"If you get hungry, feel free to stroll around the forest. I've planted many Berry trees here, and they're bearing large, plump Berries. You can pick and eat them as you like."

"Choo-choo!" Greedent responded excitedly.

It couldn't wait to look around, eager to find the tallest, largest tree to build its nest.

The other Pokemon were also very happy at that moment.

Seeing this, D breathed a sigh of relief. Although they hadn't spent much time together, he had gotten to know the temperaments and personalities of these little guys very well.

D glanced over each Pokemon and advised them one by one:

"Greedent, it's best not to stash Berries in a single hollow because if anything goes wrong, you'll end up hungry."

"Maushold, I wish that your children grow up healthy and become a strong, white Mouse, eventually finding their mate and evolving into Tandemaus."

"Wattrel, I saw some other Wattrel flying towards Casseroya Lake earlier. If you want to find companions, you might want to check there, but be careful."

"Tinkatink... Thank you so much for the hammer you made for Pawmot before. Although you've lost contact with the other Tinkaton, I believe you've made new friends by now, so you shouldn't feel lonely anymore, right? Get along well with them!"

"..."

The Pokemon listened intently to these farewell words. At that moment, they finally snapped out of the joy of rebuilding their home and realized that the time to part with D had come.

The faces of the Pokemon showed reluctance and sadness.

Wattrel still remembered when it was injured and unable to fly. It was D who diligently treated it every day and provided it with food.

Grafaiai also lowered its head sadly, recalling how D had saved them and taken them in at that time.

If it weren't for D, they wouldn't have been able to survive the cold winter.

"Waaa..."

Tinkatink held its hammer, tears welling up in its eyes as it looked up at D.

Witnessing this scene, D couldn't help but feel a multitude of emotions.

He comforted them by saying, "It's okay, it's not like we'll never see each other again. This place is close to the ranch, so if you miss us, you can visit the ranch anytime."

"Oh, by the way, from today on, we're neighbors! If you come to collect mountain mushrooms from the back hill in the future, I'll rely on you to help me find them!"

Hearing this, Greedent wiped away its tears, then nodded earnestly and let out a growl.

No problem, when the time comes, it will definitely lead D to find all the mushrooms in the mountains!

The other Pokemon also nodded one after another.

Seeing this, D smiled slightly, waved to the group of Pokemon, and said, "Well then, see you later!"

"Waaa!"

"..."

The Pokemon responded loudly.

D nodded gently, then turned around and descended the mountain with Koraidon and the others, returning to the ranch.

By the time they got back to the ranch, it was already 6:30 PM.

This time had already passed Zack's regular pickup time.

Yet, neither humans nor Pokemon were present at the ranch, so Zack must have made a futile trip today.

Looking at the few buckets of milk placed under the eaves, D pondered briefly, then moved them to the cellar for storage, planning to have Zack test them the next day and reprice accordingly.

Considering the Super Sheep Milk's effects, akin to a Max Potion, D wondered if he should not sell it and keep it for himself instead.

But then he thought, with Gogoat producing milk every day, they couldn't possibly drink it all.

Rather than letting it accumulate in the cellar, it's better to sell it to those in need.

It might help some Pokemon in need or save severely injured Pokemon.

Just like the Champion of Unova Region, Alder.

Alder, during a period when protecting the Pokemon League, had his partner Pokemon Volcarona become severely injured from overexertion in battle, weakening it to the point that even the Pokemon Center couldn't treat it, resulting in its demise.

This also led to Alder losing interest in battling later on.

If only Alder had given Volcarona a bottle of Super Sheep Milk at the time, perhaps a miracle might have occurred, and Volcarona wouldn't have died.

Selling this milk could help many Trainers and Pokemon like Alder and Volcarona.

And for D, it wouldn't hurt to earn some money, would it?

With this thought, D couldn't help but feel more at ease.

Having been busy all day in the back mountains, both he and the Pokemon were quite exhausted.

---

After a simple dinner, D and his Pokemon soon fell into a slumber.

Meanwhile, a festive evening party was taking place in the back mountain.

Pokemon like Greedent, Tinkatink, Bellibolt, Maushold, and Grafaiai gathered together.

The bright moonlight shone through the gaps in the leaves, illuminating the forest, where the group of Pokemon sat in the moonlight, each holding a fresh Berry.

Greedent cheerfully exclaimed, "Cheer!" (I'm going to dig in!)

However, after it spoke, it realized no other Pokemon responded. They were all staring blankly at the Berries, lost in thought.

Greedent was puzzled, "Cheer?" (Why aren't you eating?)

Bellibolt blinked its yellow eyes and said, "Blep..." (D has done so much for us, I want to give him a gift to thank him, but I don't know what to give...)

Tinkatink nodded, "Wawa!" (I also want to give a gift to thank D...)

But didn't know what to give.

The forest had just been rebuilt, and they had nothing.

Thinking about this, the other Pokemon also sighed in low spirits.

Seeing this, Greedent blinked its eyes, looked at the Berry in its hand, and then thought of the Berries it had treasured in the tree hole.

An hour ago, it had successfully dug out a tree hole in a tall, thick tree and hid the Berries it had worked hard to find inside.

"Cheer!" I've got it!

At that moment, a light bulb went off in Greedent's mind.

"Cheer!" (I know what to give!)

"Blep?" (What to give?)

The other Pokemon looked over.

Greedent stood up, raising the Berry in its hand high. Under the moonlight, the ordinary Pecha Berry looked so beautiful.

"Cheer!" (Berries!)

Berries? Hearing this, the other Pokemon looked puzzled.

Berries... D's ranch also has many. Would he like them?

Greedent nodded repeatedly, "Cheer!" (He definitely will! As long as we give D those big, round Berries we treasure, he will surely be happy!)

To Greedent, these Berries were its most precious treasures.

Now, it wanted to give these treasures to D to thank him.

"Wawa..." Tinkatink was stunned.

Big, round Berries?

That's right!

Tinkatink's eyes suddenly lit up.

They could give the biggest and best Berries they found to D!

So, early the next morning, when D opened the door, he saw Greedent struggling to carry a bundle made from a large leaf sitting on the ground.

Seeing D come out, Greedent's eyes lit up, and its little paw loosened, causing the large leaf to unfold automatically, with the pile of Berries rumbling and rolling to the eaves.

"Cheer!"

Greedent was startled, quickly using its large tail to scoop up the rolling Berries.

D also squatted down to help, feeling quite surprised to see Greedent so early: "Why are you here so early?"

Greedent's small eyes were bright and shiny, "Cheer!"

It picked up a Berry and pushed it toward D.

D then realized Greedent's intention: "Is this for me?"

"Cheer!" That's right!

Greedent nodded repeatedly and then added, "Cheer!" It's from everyone in the forest together!

Watching Greedent imitate Tinkatink waving a hammer, D roughly understood its meaning.

"This was prepared by you and Tinkatink together."

"Cheer!" Greedent nodded its little head, looking at D expectantly, hoping he would like it.

This was all they had for now...

Seeing Greedent's expectant little eyes, D smiled slightly and then nodded gently, "Thank you, I really like it."

Upon hearing this, Greedent's eyes immediately became bright.

It wagged its big tail, turned around, and ran off. When it reached the meadow, it stopped again, turned around, and waved its little paw at D.

"Cheer!"

It's going back to tell everyone that D liked their gift!

"Wait!" D wanted to call it back to give it some Poffins, but Greedent ran so fast that it disappeared in a flash.

"Oh dear," D sighed in resignation.

Were these little ones' gifts for him?

Lowering his head to look at the Berries, D's face suddenly broke into a look of astonishment upon seeing the information about these Berries.

[Friendship Berry: Special ingredient, a Berry filled with the gratitude of Pokemon, symbolizing friendship.

When used in cooking, the dish rank is increased by 1 level, ineffective for dishes already at the level cap.]

---

---

---

---

# 291: Chapter 288: The Legendary Pokemon—Calyrex and Arceus

Friendship Berry?

Looking at this pile of ordinary-looking berries in front of him, D felt a bit baffled.

He stood there dumbfounded until his sister Indeedee noticed the unusual situation and came out to check, waking D up with a start.

Berries that can enhance cooking levels... This is incredible!

He was certain that these things couldn't possibly grow under normal circumstances.

Could this be because of Greedent and their feelings of gratitude towards him?

These emotions infused into the berries, causing them to undergo some magical mutations.

This truly is...

For a moment, D didn't even know what to say.

In helping those Pokémon, he never thought of getting anything in return.

Looking at it now, this is indeed an unexpected gift!

If these berries were used properly, they could greatly enhance the ranch and some of the Pokémon within it.

Like the Leaf Cake he made last winter with the leaves from the World Tree.

D recalled the Leaf Cake's level being S-.

If a Berry were added to it, wouldn't it mean the Leaf Cake's level would increase to S?

As we all know, when the level of a dish increases, its effects also become more pronounced.

This means, after eating the enhanced Leaf Cake, Calyrex and Gogoat will be able to cultivate the World Tree even faster!

Thinking of this, D's heart leapt, and he carried the Friendship Berries back to the living room.

Sister Indeedee noticed the pile of berries D was holding and asked, puzzled, "Indeedeedee?"

D: "Greedent came by, and these berries are gifts from it and the Pokémon in the forest."

In his mind, D could vividly imagine the scene last night where the group of Pokémon in the forest were scouring for berries.

Just now, he noticed that these berries were larger and fuller compared to ordinary ones.

Obviously, they were carefully selected by Greedent and the others as gifts to give away.

In his heart, D thanked that adorable group of Pokémon.

After breakfast, he couldn't wait to request a leaf from the World Tree, then took out a Friendship Berry and began working in Alcremie's Cream Workshop.

An hour later, with a ding, the freshly baked Leaf Cake came out piping hot.

D quickly put on oven mitts, took it out from the oven, and then evenly spread cream on top before garnishing with sliced Berry pieces.

Once everything was done, D looked down to check the details of this Leaf Cake.

[Leaf Cake (Incomplete)(S): A cake made by mixing leaves from the World Tree with eggs, MooMoo Fresh Milk, and other ingredients. Listen carefully, and one can feel the breath of nature from it.

Cooking Effect: Breath of Nature.

1. Permanently increases the power of Grass Type Moves by 25%, limited to Grass Type Pokémon, effects cannot stack.

2. After consuming, Grass Type Pokémon can respond to the rhythm of nature, significantly reducing the growth time of crops when using Grass Energy to cultivate, only applicable to Grass Type Pokémon, effects cannot stack.

Additional Effect: Master of the World Tree, you have been recognized by the World Tree, as an entity that nurtures the Gogoat that cultivates the World Tree, you may enter all areas inside the World Tree in the future, this effect only applies to D.

Evaluation: Listen to the breath of nature! Perhaps as the World Tree grows, dishes made with its leaves will have even better effects.]

"Sure enough, the level increased from S- to S!" D felt a thrill in his heart.

"The power of Grass Type Moves has even been increased by an additional 5%, making a total of 25%, one-fourth, which is higher than the power increased by carrying Miracle Seeds!"

"I wonder if the effects of the Leaf Cake and Miracle Seeds can stack. If they can stack, then Gogoat's strength will be further enhanced."

If they unleash Grassy Terrain... Ordinary Pokémon probably won't be able to withstand a single Grass Sacred Sword from Gogoat.

D couldn't help but take a cold breath, terrifying indeed!

But more than this enhancement, what concerns him is whether the ability to cultivate crops has improved.

"From minor to moderate, it's indeed improved!" A weight was lifted off D's heart.

This way, the growth speed of the World Tree is even faster, probably not even requiring four years to grow into a towering tree.

"Truly an unexpected gift!" D's grin nearly stretched to his ears.

He took this Green Grass Cake to the ranch and divided it into five parts, feeding three to the Gogoats, Dolliv, and Calyrex.

In the Glass Greenhouse, D watched Calyrex enjoy the dessert among the flowers, pondering what other dishes could utilize the Friendship Berries.

Berry Platter with refining techniques? Would adding Friendship Berries further increase the chance of Pokémon learning rare moves?

Lucky Pudding? Further enhance fortune?

Or Translation Nuts? Perhaps increase the translation word count.

However, what D regretted most was that the Storm Steamed Bun and Lightning Steamed Bun couldn't be made now. If Friendship Berries were added to those, they might create even more powerful dishes.

---

"No, these berries should be used for brewing wine." D remembered the berry wine he brewed before, which could increase poison resistance and paralysis resistance.

One Friendship Berry per barrel of berry wine, then let every Pokémon in the ranch have a cup, granting them permanent poison and paralysis resistance.

Simply perfect!

D was pleased with his brilliant idea.

Calyrex gracefully picked up the Leaf Cake, placing it in front to slowly savor it.

Noticing the cheerful expression of his little believer, Calyrex spoke:

"D, I feel that there are more people who believe in me now."

D was slightly taken aback, then recovered and contemplated before asking, "Do you not like this?"

Calyrex gently shook his head and slowly said, "It's not that I don't like it, I... I just feel a bit emotional."

"I was once a forgotten king. Since then, I assumed I would no longer be moved by human actions. Yet now, seeing the admiration and gratitude in the eyes of both humans and Pokémon, I still feel happy."

"That's very normal." D said, "Actually, I think to us humans, Calyrex is a Pokémon that is completely different from the other Legendary Pokémon."

"Oh? Why do you say that?" Calyrex asked with a hint of curiosity. Then, as if having understood something, he inquired, "Other Legendary Pokémon? Humans have categorized Pokémon?"

D nodded: "Yes, like Zacian and Zamazenta, they are also considered legendary Pokémon."

He explained, "With legends, existing in this world for a long time, incredibly powerful, such Pokémon are often called legendary."

Calyrex roughly understood.

D continued the previous topic: "As for why I say Calyrex is a Pokémon completely different from other Legendary Pokémon, it's because you are very, very close to humans."

Calyrex looked over.

D: "Other Legendary Pokémon rarely appear in front of humans. They are powerful, proud at heart, perhaps that's why they disdain interacting with humans."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex realized: "I understand what you're saying. I once ruled the Galar Region, lived alongside those who revered me as king, and responded to their prayers, making the land plentiful."

"That's right!" D nodded, "Giratina is also a legendary Pokémon, living constantly in the Reversed World, maintaining the balance between the Reversed World and the real world."

"It seems you are quite knowledgeable about Legendary Pokémon." Calyrex recalled the earliest day: "Did you also hear my legend, which led you to carve a statue and build a shrine for me?"

It turns out that from that time on, his little believer had unwavering faith in his existence?

Calyrex was deeply moved.

D was silent.

Could he say it was to capture Spectrier and Glastrier?

That can't be said to Calyrex, so D tactfully switched the topic.

"Do you want to hear about other Legendary Pokémon?" he asked.

In the morning sunshine, still warm, Calyrex elegantly sipped the tea poured by Polteageist and then gently nodded: "Alright."

Seeing this, D began to chat leisurely with Calyrex.

"This world is actually created by a Pokémon known as the Creator God Arceus."

At the first sentence, Calyrex's face showed an astonished expression: "The world we live in was created?"

D: "According to legend, the Creator God Arceus created the world."

"Such a thing exists..." Calyrex murmured in disbelief: "Then we must also be creations of him?"

"No." Fearing Calyrex might think overly chaotically, D quickly explained: "Both humans and Pokémon were slowly born from this world, not created by anything."

"I see!" Calyrex realized: "A god created the world... What kind of power is needed to do such a thing! If possible, I would really like to meet him! See what the god who created the world looks like."

D: "...Probably like an alpaca?"

"An alpaca?" Calyrex was slightly taken aback, never having heard that name before— is it a Pokémon?

D then realized that this world does not have creatures like alpacas.

He explained, "Just like Spectrier and Glastrier, with four legs, white, looking very sacred."

While saying this, D suddenly recalled a picture he had seen before.

In that picture, Arceus replaced the positions of Glastrier and Spectrier, ridden under Calyrex.

D looked at Calyrex, wondering if Calyrex might not have such rebellious thoughts?

Meanwhile, Calyrex was pondering, thinking that the god who created the world looks like his beloved horse...

It was quite horrifying! From then on, he wouldn't be able to look directly at his two beloved horses!

But just then, Calyrex suddenly noticed how detailed his little believer's description was, as if he had seen the Creator God with his own eyes.

Calyrex couldn't help but ask: "Have you met the Creator God?"

D: "..."

"In the Sinnoh Region, there is a legend about him. It's the origin of the Creator God's culture." D explained.

---

Calyrex understood: "I see!"

"Besides the Creator God, there are many, many Pokémon in this world that humans call gods, like the Earth God Groudon, the Sea God Kyogre, the Time God Dialga, and the God of Space Palkia." D explained one by one.

After listening, Calyrex felt like his worldview had completely changed: "I never thought this world could be so vast! There's so much I didn't know."

"Indeed!" D nodded.

If he weren't a transmigrator, without a god's perspective, he would probably have felt the same as Calyrex did the first time he heard all this.

As for why he transmigrated...

It might be to meet these Pokémon!

Thinking of this, D bid farewell to Calyrex, and the first glance he took when pushing open the door of the Glass Greenhouse, he saw Cyclizar and Koraidon guarding the grass in front of the entrance.

These two Pokémon are sometimes surprisingly in sync about certain things!

Perhaps he came to this world just to meet Cyclizar and Koraidon.

D smiled, reaching out to pat Cyclizar and Koraidon's heads and said to them:

"Alright, let's go home. I want to start developing new culinary dishes!"

"Gyaoo~"

"Aga-screech~"

Cyclizar and Koraidon made happy growls, following D with pitter-pattering footsteps towards home.

D returned to the living room and went straight to the kitchen. He opened the fridge, checking the ingredients inside, preparing to see which dishes could be enhanced with the Friendship Berry.

The issues on the hillside had been resolved, and now he needed to get busy with his own tasks.

Spring had arrived, and the fields in the pasture needed to be cultivated again.

Although the profitability of livestock farming in the ranch was higher, the fields still needed to be planted for those ordinary crops that mutated into magical fruits and vegetables.

Moreover, he and the Pokémon at the ranch could also eat them.

Thinking of this, D already had a target in mind for enhancement with the Friendship Berry.

That would be the A-Class culinary dish, Green Grass Cake.

After Grass Type Pokémon eat this cake, when they use Grass Type Energy to cultivate crops, there is an extremely small chance that the crops will mutate.

For example, the previously mentioned size-variable pumpkins and glowing pineapples.

"With the enhancement from the Friendship Berry, the grade of the Green Grass Cake should reach A+ class, which should increase the chance of crop mutation a bit."

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but feel a bit regretful.

Unfortunately, the Friendship Berry cannot be used for continuous enhancement, or else he could have directly enhanced the Green Grass Cake to S-Class.

Compared to A-Class, every S-Class cooking effect experiences a qualitative leap.

At this point, D suddenly thought of the Black Truffle he found on the hillside last year.

That item doesn't inherently provide benefits, but it can significantly boost the effect of a dish.

"If I make Green Grass Cake using both the Friendship Berry and Black Truffle, will the two enhancements stack?"

"I wonder if any Black Truffle shot up when Calyrex made the plants on the hillside grow instantly before."

In retrospect, it would be worthwhile to take a stroll on the hilltop and ask the Greedent living there; they might provide some help, D mused.

If Greedent can't help, he would have to consider raising a few Lechonk and Oinkologne on the ranch.

This Pokémon's sense of smell is extremely sharp, perfect for searching for truffles in the mountains.

---

---

---

---

# 292: Chapter 289: Heroic Expedition Team in Action!

On a warm spring afternoon, the room was filled with the sweet aroma of cream.

D took the freshly baked cake base out of the oven, cut it in half with a knife, then added Berry chunks inside, and finally spread black chocolate and Alcremie's cream on top.

Thus, a brand new Green Grass Cake was completed.

[Green Grass Cake (A+): A cake made with mature summer grass, flour, MooMoo Fresh Milk, special Berries, and Alcremie's cream.

Cooking Effect: Flower Trick. When given to a Grass Type Pokemon, there is a small chance that the crops it nurtures with Grass Type Energy will undergo slight variations.

Review: Perfectly blends the flavors of different ingredients, popular among Grass Type Pokemon. A Grass Type Pokemon eating this may bring you a delightful surprise with gratitude.]

"As I expected, the level of the Green Grass Cake has increased from A to A+, and the probability of variation has changed from extremely small to small. This should be considered a substantial improvement, right?"

D murmured thoughtfully.

After all, there's no specific probability of variation marked, but it doesn't matter. He'll plant a bit more, and mutant crops will eventually appear.

Thinking of this, D divided the Green Grass Cake into five portions, just like before, and sent them to Dolliv, the Gogoats, and Calyrex.

In the Glass Greenhouse, Calyrex wore a helpless expression on its face as it looked at the dessert emitting a grassy aroma in front of it.

Its little follower really is...

If it continues eating like this, it will be turned into a plump Calyrex.

But seeing the expectant gaze of its little follower, Calyrex could only sigh helplessly, pick up a fork, and begin to slowly savor the Green Grass Cake in front of it.

"Take your time eating, I won't disturb you!"

Said D, ready to turn and leave. However, he suddenly thought of something and turned back to say:

"By the way, the flowers in the flower field have bloomed, and I set up a pergola and a tea table there while you weren't around. It's a good time to enjoy some sunlight now."

This season, as the temperature slowly rises, the Combees and Butterfrees have all returned to live outside.

Not long ago, D had moved the Combees' hive outside to make it easier for them to gather nectar.

The flower field? Calyrex nodded slightly, indicating that it would go have a look.

Seeing this, D grinned and left, quickly finding Cyclizar sunbathing in the grass.

"Will you accompany me to the town?"

"Gyaoo~"

Cyclizar immediately stood up, shook the pollen off its body, then ran up to D and knelt down, making it easy for him to ride on.

Shortly after they left, Calyrex's figure flashed and appeared above the flower field on the pasture, overlooking everything below.

The flower field was square, with a circular area specially cleared in the middle, where indeed as D said, a pergola had been set up.

Green flower vines climbed up the pergola, forming a natural little garden.

Warm sunlight poured into it, surrounded by a thriving flower field. The industrious Combees happily collected nectar above, while the Butterfrees perched on the pergola enjoying the sunshine with closed eyes.

Looking at this delightful and leisurely scene, Calyrex's face broke into a smile.

It slowly landed in the garden, sitting on a wooden park bench and gazing at the verdant pastoral scenery in the distance.

Suddenly, as if thinking of something, Calyrex gently raised its hand, and the figures of Polteageist and Sinistea appeared in the garden.

Looking at them, Calyrex softly said, "You may also bask in the sunlight in this garden!"

*

Porto Marinada Town, Elo Seed Store.

D, guided by Elo, was choosing the seeds he needed, while Cyclizar squatted beside the shelf, looking listlessly at the clock on the wall.

"You've been here for a year without noticing! Time really flies!" Elo remarked, then asked, "How is it? Compared to your previous traveling life, have you gotten used to country life?"

D smiled slightly: "To be honest, I originally thought I couldn't handle this farming life. On the first day of plowing, I almost broke my back. If Cyclizar and Koraidon hadn't been by my side, I wouldn't have known what to do."

"Hahaha!" Elo laughed heartily: "That's how it is for the first time. It gets much better once you get used to it!"

"Yeah!" D nodded gently.

As they spoke, D had already chosen the seeds he needed.

There were garlic, potatoes, strawberries, radishes, cucumbers, cabbages, and turnips.

Garlic can be made into garlic paste to spread on grilled meat, making it taste better.

Potatoes can be used to make mashed potatoes, French fries, etc.

Cucumbers can be pickled into sour, crisp snacks, perfect for enhancing meals when you lack appetite.

Elo packed all these seeds and handed them to D: "It totals 4,200 League Coins, please accept them!"

D took the seeds, paid the money, then bid farewell to Elo and rode Cyclizar back to the pasture.

He stood by the farmland and shouted into a Diglett Hole. In no time, Diglett poked its head out from the hole.

---

D handed over a Poffin and said with a smile, "I'll leave these fields to you then!"

Diglett opened its mouth and swallowed the Poffin in one gulp, then replied spiritedly, "Squeak squeak!"

Leave it to him!

Next, Diglett transformed into a swirling little top and swiftly shuttled through the farm fields.

Wherever it passed, the soil immediately became looser.

Taking advantage of the moment, D started following behind Diglett, scattering seeds.

However, just when Diglett had tilled half the field, an unexpected change occurred.

It suddenly stopped, then burst forth with a dense white light.

"Gah-ow?!"

Cyclizar was attracted to this scene.

Upon hearing its Growl, D turned his head and saw Diglett's appearance transforming rapidly.

It changed from one head to three, with all three heads looking identical except in height.

Is it… evolving into Dugtrio?

D felt a bit surprised.

Moments later, the light faded, and Dugtrio looked back with tiny eyes full of excitement.

"Squeak squeak squeak!"

D quickly responded, smiling and saying to it, "Congratulations, Dugtrio."

"Squeak squeak squeak!"

Dugtrio made a Growl, then continued with its work.

After evolving, Diglett's speed in tilling the fields became faster, with all three heads burrowing into the ground together, and in the blink of an eye, Dugtrio had already tilled a large area.

"Pokemon's power is really astonishing!"

What surprised D even more was Diglett's sudden evolution.

But then he thought, perhaps such mundane evolutions are normal in this world, and the kind seen in crises in anime is the minority.

He had assumptions.

Mundane, yet pretty good.

D smiled and continued to follow behind Dugtrio scattering seeds.

Thanks to Diglett's sudden evolution, his work efficiency was enhanced considerably, and he completed planting all the fields in just over two hours.

At dusk, as the sun set with colorful clouds across the sky.

D stood by the farm and gave Dugtrio a thumbs-up, "Amazing!"

"Squeak squeak squeak!" Dugtrio responded excitedly.

D: "Now go and rest! I'll bring over your favorite sandwich later."

Diglett agreed happily, then burrowed into the ground and returned to its nest.

*

5:00pm.

Zack arrived at the ranch with a large truck right on time.

D hurriedly brought out the Gogoat's goat milk and suggested to Zack the idea of rechecking the milk's quality.

"Reexamine? Of course!" Zack laughed heartily, then, as if pondering something, exclaimed in shock, "Don't tell me the Gogoat's milk quality has improved again?"

D nodded with a smile, "I believe it has."

"This… this… is too amazing!" Zack widened his eyes.

He initially wanted to ask D how he cultivated the Gogoat, but then thought, such Pokémon farming methods must be a secret to a rancher!

Thus, Zack refrained from asking and instead started testing the quality of the goat milk.

Upon testing, he was so shocked his jaw dropped, as if his mouth could fit a lightbulb.

Looking at the instrument showing the nutrition content off the scale, Zack's eyes widened like Tauros's eyes:

"This… this… is too incredible! In all my years in the industry, I've never seen milk of such high quality!"

He remembered, previously, drinking one sip of milk from D Ranch could restore one's energy, and now, if he had another sip, Zack couldn't even imagine what benefits it would bring to the body!

"I'm afraid I can't price it," Zack shook his head, "Because I've never encountered this kind of goat milk."

D: "..."

Could it be?

Zack was still in shock.

At this moment, many thoughts crossed his mind.

The markets sell fresh milk that appeals to both humans and Pokémon.

Everyone knows the milk from Pokémon like Miltank is nutritious; humans drink it to grow healthy, and injured Pokémon heal upon drinking it.

After pondering for a while, Zack finally spoke, "I can't reprice it now, I need to further evaluate the specific effects of this bottle of goat milk."

D: "..." Alright.

Eventually, Zack took only two bottles of goat milk from the ranch, saying he'd conduct some tests at home before setting a price.

Watching Zack's figure fade into the distance, D sighed helplessly and then turned to go back to the house.

It was getting late, and it was time to prepare dinner for everyone.

Tonight, D made a hearty dinner.

Grilled steak, sandwiches, stew, shiitake mushrooms with greens, Berry Juice...

During dinner, Teddiursa suddenly gave D a reluctant look.

"Ei ma…"

D noticed its gaze and realized that Teddiursa seemed like it had something to say, so he ate two pieces of Translation Nuts Cake and asked, "What's up?"

Teddiursa then told D about its desire to continue the journey: "Ei ma ei ma!" (D, I've discussed with Heroic Expedition Team, we want to continue traveling to help Pokémon in need!)

D froze for a moment, then came to senses, "Is that so... When do you plan to set off?"

---

Teddiursa replied: "Yimo." (Tomorrow morning.)

Though excited for the upcoming journey, Teddiursa felt somewhat reluctant about leaving D and the ranch again.

D noticed Teddiursa's thoughts and comforted, "Go with peace of mind!"

"Yimo?" Teddiursa looked over.

D smiled, "There's nothing to worry about here at the ranch, Dugtrio will help me with farming, Indeedee will help with chores, and Koraidon and Cyclizar are very capable as well."

"Feel free to travel outside, do what you want, if you feel exhausted or wish to rest, you can always return to the ranch, and I'll make Honey Pudding and brew Hondew Berry Tea for you."

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa was incredibly moved.

"Yimo!" (D is the best!)

Dream, standing nearby, also felt touched.

"Miu..."

D turned to Dream, named Heroic, and said to it: "And you, Heroic, the ranch always welcomes you. Once the World Tree has been planted, you can call this place home and settle down."

"Miu..." Hearing this, Dream felt touched and a bit shy.

Because a few days ago, it had used Secret Power somewhere on the ranch to build a temporary secret base and had hidden its favorite toys inside.

"But there's one thing," D added.

Dream and Teddiursa looked over simultaneously.

D: "While traveling outside, be sure to maintain your safety, avoid doing anything overly dangerous, and steer clear of drawing too much attention from humans."

Remembering the Kanto Region from their previous travels...

Recalling that still made D somewhat fearful.

If Team Rocket and Giovanni found out about Dream's existence, D couldn't imagine what kind of situation they'd face.

Though this Dream, named Heroic, is very powerful, and Teddiursa possesses the ability to Run Away.

But even Legendary Pokemon like Suicune, Lugia, Dialga, and Palkia have stumbled at the hands of humans.

Hearing these words, Dream and Teddiursa nodded their little heads, indicating they understood.

"That's good." D sighed in relief.

Fortunately, compared to other Legendary Pokemon, Dream could freely transform into any Pokemon's appearance.

If it didn't want to be discovered, people wouldn't notice it's Dream.

The next morning, under the watchful eyes of D and the Pokemon in the ranch, Dream and Teddiursa left the ranch, embarking again on their journey.

They flew over mountains, lakes, and seas, finally arriving in a city with a harbor.

Numerous ships were docked at the harbor.

Dream, transforming into a Wattrel, landed with Teddiursa on one of the ships.

With hopes for a new journey in their hearts, they gazed at the blue ocean ahead.

Teddiursa joyfully said to Dream, "Yimo!" (D said we could invite friends we meet along the way to stay at the ranch!)

"Miu~" Dream felt very happy about this as well.

It had met many Pokemon on past journeys, but after short meetings, each went their separate ways, residing in various places, becoming hard to see again.

If there was a place to invite everyone, then meeting up would be easy!

Teddiursa thought of Zapdos residing in the power plant, craving peace but often disturbed by humans.

"Yimo!" (Let's first go see Zapdos! We can invite it to live with us in the World Tree!)

Dream readily agreed, "Miu~" (That sounds great~)

After pausing, it added, "Miu!" (And other friends! Farfetch'd, Pidgeotto, Krabby...)

Dream named each friend it hoped to see.

Then it thought of Mewtwo, and its face turned somber: "Miu." (It's a pity Mewtwo can't be found... If Mewtwo, Meowth, and Pikachu were here, it would be wonderful.)

Seeing this, Teddiursa tilted its head at Dream, blinked, then extended its paw to gently pat Dream's shoulder, comforting it:

"Yimo!" (D said if we keep traveling, we'll eventually meet them again!)

Upon hearing this, Dream's eyes sparkled: "Miu!" (Then let's set off!)

Heroic Expedition Team, move out!

Just then, the ship beneath them let out a melodious horn sound.

"Attention passengers, Brave Olivine is about to set sail, next stop, Johto Region, Olivine City!"
