# 293: Chapter 290: Mew's Secret Base, and the Shock Caused by Life Sheep Milk!

Mew and Teddiursa left, and life at the ranch returned to its usual calm.

However, this morning, Glaceon suddenly came rushing in a panic, with a look of terror: "Vee!"

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised, what could scare the usually aloof Glaceon like this?

He casually ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake before speaking: "Don't rush, take your time and explain!"

Glaceon managed to calm down for a moment, swallowed, and with a face full of terror, began to recount what happened last night.

"Vee Vee! Vee..." (There's a ghost in the cave! Last night I was sleeping on an ice rock, and around midnight, I suddenly heard a terrifying sinister laugh coming from the walls...)

Recalling this event still made Glaceon feel extremely scared, its cheeks were shrouded in a chilling fear.

"Vee... Vee!" (The walls of the cave are clearly made of stone, it's impossible for a person or Pokemon to be in there... it must be a ghost!)

The laughter was sharp and piercing, sometimes faint and sometimes strong, unlike any sound humans or Pokemon could make.

"A ghost?" D was quite surprised. How could there be ghosts in the ranch? He had lived here for over a year, and aside from Gimmighoul and Spectrier, he hadn't seen any other Ghost-type Pokemon.

Was Glaceon referring to a ghost or a Ghost Type Pokemon?

Seeing D seemingly skeptical, Glaceon anxiously assured: "Vee!" (It's true! The ghost is still laughing non-stop!)

Nani? Is that really the case?

D immediately stopped his work and brought Koraidon and a few other Pokemon from the ranch to the Ice Cave in the back of the old house, which Glaceon considered its home.

As soon as they got close, D heard the sharp and piercing laughter coming from within the walls.

Really hearing the ghostly laughter, his little sister Indeedee hid behind her brother in fear: "Dee..." (There's really a ghost laughing...)

Glaceon was shaking: "Vee?" (See, I wasn't lying, right?)

The brother Indeedee sighed helplessly.

D, however, didn't feel too scared. He thought the sound didn't seem like something a normal Ghost-type Pokemon could produce.

After some thought, he stepped into the ice cellar, following the sound as he pressed his ear against the wall to carefully identify the direction of the laughter.

Koraidon stood aside, waiting for a while, "Aghas?"

"It's not a ghost," D said, then went back outside, searching for the source of the sound from outside.

Soon, he pinpointed where the laughter was coming from.

D pointed to the spot and instructed Koraidon, "Right here, use your fist to hit this spot!"

Koraidon obediently complied.

It clenched its fist and struck the mountain wall in front of it, resulting in a loud crash, and a hole suddenly appeared in the mountain wall.

D stepped forward, clearing away the debris, and then looked inside.

To his surprise, the inside of the wall wasn't solid but had a small space.

Inside the space was a mess of things, including plush toys, plastic Pidgeotto figures, and toy cars.

And the laughter was coming from the fluffy bear toy.

Seeing this, D was a bit stunned.

What was going on? Who created this place?

He reached his hand in, just enough to take out the laughing plush bear toy.

He unzipped the back and found the sound component inside.

It seemed like the battery was running low, which was why the laughter sounded so weak, almost like a ghostly wail.

"It's not a ghost, it's the toy making the sound." D showed the toy to Glaceon and his little sister Indeedee.

As soon as he removed the battery from the back of the toy, the laughter stopped immediately.

Upon seeing the cute bear toy, Glaceon was momentarily stunned, then realized the situation, and her face instantly turned crimson.

She had made a fool of herself again! How embarrassing for an Eevee!

Glaceon weakly responded, "Vee..." (I get it now...)

The little sister Indeedee also breathed a sigh of relief, but then she noticed a problem: "Dee?" (Who placed these toys here?)

Koraidon thought for a moment and guessed: "Aghas? Aghas..." (Was it Mew? I saw it come here and use Psychic on the wall...)

It recalled that among the Pokemon in the ranch, only Mew liked these toys and loved placing them in its nest.

It definitely wasn't Teddiursa, because Teddiursa gave all the treasures it picked up to D and didn't keep any for itself.

Upon hearing Koraidon's words, a move came to D's mind.

That is Secret Power!

This is a very special move; not only can it be used to attack, but it can also be used to build secret bases in grass, big trees, and caves.

For Pokemon, this secret base is equivalent to a nest.

So this place was a little nook that Mew made using Secret Power?

And it hid all the toys here.

It was clear that Mew really liked this place; it probably just forgot to turn off the toy's switch before leaving.

With the toy in an active state, the battery kept draining, and when it was almost out of power, the laughter turned into ghostly moans.

---

So that's why the Glaceon thought this sound was a ghost...

It was all a misunderstanding.

After the truth was uncovered, D shared his hypothesis with Glaceon, then placed Mew's bear plushie inside and blocked the hole Koraidon had made with stones.

Once Mew and Teddiursa returned, he'd explain it to Mew.

"Alright, it's fine now, everyone can go back!"

*

Meanwhile, in Porto Marinada Town, at the Porto Marinada Market Headquarters.

Zack brought the two bottles of Gogoat milk here for comprehensive testing.

The milk produced by Pokemon is very special, much like the berries found in nature, and even has the effect of healing injured Pokemon's stamina.

For humans, long-term consumption makes the body stronger, with many studies showing that children raised on Miltank milk are healthier and stronger compared to those who had human milk.

Additionally, a single Miltank can produce twenty liters of nutrient-rich milk in a day, making MooMoo Milk an essential food in the human world alongside rice and flour.

Compared to Miltank, female Gogoats can also produce milk, but the amount is far less than Miltank, and the nutritional content isn't as rich as MooMoo Milk.

This limits the sales of goat milk, with only a small portion of people purchasing it, while the majority choose the more delicious MooMoo Milk.

Zack had always thought this way.

That was, until D Ranch began selling goat milk.

The exceptionally high-quality goat milk contained nutrients that far exceeded ordinary MooMoo Milk, and long-term consumption could invigorate frail elderly people.

And now... after a year of refining, the quality of D Ranch's goat milk seemed even more perfect.

Thinking of this, Zack handed one of the bottles of goat milk to the examiner at Porto Marinada Market Headquarters.

Due to the abundance of nearby ranches, Porto Marinada Market is equipped with very professional fresh milk testing instruments.

The examiner took the bottle of milk, poured it into the instrument, and casually asked, "Which ranch did this milk come from this time?"

"No, it's not cow's milk," Zack explained. "It's goat milk from D Ranch."

"Oh? The famous magical goat milk in town?" The examiner was immediately intrigued.

Zack grinned and replied, "Yes!"

"Then I must thoroughly test the nutrients inside!" The examiner's interest was piqued, having heard the legends about the goat milk before.

It's said that Mrs. Dante's husband, bedridden due to weakness, became increasingly energetic after consuming medicinal meals made from the goat milk, and now he could be seen jogging every morning.

The instrument slowly started, and the computer in the room quickly analyzed the components and nutrients within the goat milk.

As the testing proceeded, the data graph on the computer screen representing the milk's quality shot upwards.

"It reached the top!" The examiner was visibly shocked. "Everything's full, it's ultra-perfect quality goat milk!"

On hearing this, Zack also quickly looked down.

The next second, he saw the data graph that had reached the tip burst through the top.

Witnessing this, both of them showed expressions of amazement.

The scale was blown!

This indicated that the nutritional content of this bottle of goat milk exceeded their imagination!

"Over eight hundred nutritious substances beneficial to the human body! Truly astonishing!"

The examiner's fingers tapped swiftly on the keyboard, and soon she discovered something significant.

"The goat milk contains a very special energy!"

"What kind of energy?" Zack quickly asked.

"I don't know!" the examiner shook her head. "But I can guarantee that it's harmless to the human body! Wait a minute—such rich active factors!"

The woman was astounded.

She stared at the bottle of goat milk in disbelief and said to Zack, "If I'm not mistaken, this bottle of goat milk has incredibly potent healing powers!"

"Similar to MooMoo Milk?" Zack asked eagerly.

The examiner nodded, "But its effect is dozens or even hundreds of times stronger than MooMoo Milk! No, I need to specifically test its healing capability!"

The two of them exchanged a glance and simultaneously arrived at an answer in their hearts.

To the Pokemon Center!

Fifteen minutes later, at the Pokemon Center in Porto Marinada Town.

Zack hurried there with the remaining bottle of goat milk.

They were in luck; as soon as they entered, they saw a young Trainer rushing in, holding a gravely injured, unconscious Wattrel at death's door.

"Miss Joy, Miss Joy! Please save this Wattrel!" the young Trainer pleaded.

Miss Joy checked the Wattrel's condition and immediately frowned, "Such severe injuries! How did this happen?"

The Trainer, full of guilt, said with a sob, "It was me... I shouldn't have gone to such a dangerous place. Wattrel protected me and was attacked by a powerful Garganacl... Miss Joy, please save it!"

Upon hearing this, Zack rushed forward, "I have a way to heal this Wattrel!"

Seeing Zack, Miss Joy was momentarily stunned and a bit confused, "Mr. Zack?"

Without another word, Zack rushed forward and poured the bottle of goat milk into Wattrel's mouth.

---

The next second, a miraculous scene unfolded. As the Wattrel drank the Life Sheep Milk, its wounds began to heal and recover at a visibly rapid pace.

While everyone was in shock, this Wattrel, who was severely injured and near death, slowly opened its eyes and flapped its wings, flying up from the stretcher.

In that state, it seemed completely uninjured.

Seeing this, the Trainer, overwhelmed with emotion, hugged the Wattrel tightly, crying, "Wonderful... Thank goodness, Wattrel!"

Miss Joy stared blankly at the scene before her, incredulous. "What on earth happened?"

From her professional experience, Wattrel's injuries were unlikely to recover so quickly, and it might even have sustained irreversible damage.

But now... suddenly it recovered?

Zack and the inspector were both utterly shocked.

"Such terrifying healing power!"

Miss Joy turned her head towards Zack, whom she knew as the guy who drove a delivery truck to Porto Marinada Market: "Mr. Zack, what was that just now?"

Zack, still recovering from the shock, replied, "That... was Life Sheep Milk!"

"Life Sheep Milk?" Miss Joy was slightly taken aback.

Zack: "It can instantly restore the stamina of a near-death Pokemon, healing all its injuries. There's no name more fitting than Life Sheep Milk."

The inspector next to them nodded in awe, "Yes! Life Sheep Milk... milk that can bring new life to severely injured Pokemon!"

The Trainer had also calmed down a bit by then and immediately thanked Zack, "Thank you, sir!"

Zack, coming back to himself, shook his head and said to the young man in front of him:

"No need to thank me. If you want to express gratitude, go to D Ranch and thank the owner there!"

Miss Joy paused for a moment, D Ranch? How is this related to Mr. D?

The young man, having never heard of the name, also showed a puzzled expression, "D Ranch?"

Zack nodded: "Because the milk the Wattrel just drank was produced by the Gogoat that D raised using a secret method."

"I see!" Miss Joy recalled the three Gogoat happily running on the Grassy Terrain.

"Mr. D?" the young man stammered, then nodded earnestly, "I'll remember!"

To verify the milk's effect, Zack further requested Miss Joy, "Miss Joy, could you please perform a body check on this Wattrel?"

"No problem!" Miss Joy smiled, pressing her lips together.

She took the Wattrel to the examination room, and in no time, returned to the lobby.

Facing the worried Trainer and hopeful Zack about Wattrel's potential untreated injuries, Miss Joy softly said,

"Don't worry, Wattrel's body has fully recovered, and even its old battle injuries have been completely healed. The effect of the Life Sheep Milk is excellent!"

Not surprising for Mr. D! The Gogoat he bred is truly extraordinary! Miss Joy thought admiringly.

Even the chief holds Mr. D in high regard and personally invited him to join the Pokemon League!

Though it's unknown why Mr. D declined... However, for someone like Mr. D, who even managed to capture The King of Bountiful Harvests, perhaps he doesn't care about the Paldea League's salary?

Truly impressive! Miss Joy mused.

Hearing this, Zack inwardly said to himself, indeed, he thanked Miss Joy and then hurriedly departed.

After confirming the quality and effects of the milk, the only thing left was the pricing issue.

But what troubled them was that they had never encountered such milk before and didn't know what would be a suitable price.

Ten thousand? Twenty thousand? Thirty thousand? Forty thousand? Or fifty thousand?

---

# 294: Chapter 291: Abundant Shrine, Mysterious Visitor from Afar (4k)

When Zack arrived at the pasture with the milk inspection report, D was grooming the fur of Glaceon and Pawmot.

In the spring grass, a lot of burdocks had grown. When the long-haired Pokemon rolled around in the grass, the burdocks would stick to their fur.

Some burdocks would stick to areas they couldn't reach themselves, so they had to return pitifully to find D to help them remove it.

After plucking the last burdock from Pawmot's occiput, D patted its head: "Alright, go play!"

"Pawmo~"

Pawmot happily ran off.

D then stood up and looked at Zack, asking: "How did it go?"

Zack was extremely excited: "It exceeded my imagination! This milk contains a massive amount of nutrients beneficial to the body, and besides that, it possesses an unknown energy that can quickly restore stamina and heal injuries!"

Unknown energy? D was slightly stunned, then speculated in his heart: "Could it be the Natural Energy mentioned by Calyrex? Or the Grass Type Energy from Gogoat?"

"Moreover, I visited the Pokemon Center again."

Zack narrated in detail to D how an injured, unconscious Wattrel recovered after drinking this milk, and then excitedly added:

"This is truly astonishing! Even the Full Restore developed by the Pokemon Center doesn't have this effect!"

"I see..." Compared to Zack, D was much calmer, as he already knew about the milk's effects, which rival the rare item, Ho-oh's Holy Ash.

Zack: "After discussing with Porto Marinada Market, we've decided to set the price at thirty thousand League Coins per bottle for this milk! What do you think?"

Thirty... thirty thousand per bottle?

With Gogoat's production, wouldn't he be able to earn a million League Coins in one go?

D was slightly surprised, but upon reflection, in the game the Holy Ash sells for only 50,000 League Coins, even under the advertising of Ho-oh.

This price is just right; if it's too expensive those in need might not afford it.

After all, his intention is simply to save trainers and Pokemon like Alder and Volcarona, thus reducing tragedies in the world.

He just wasn't sure if Alder's Volcarona had passed away by this time, if not, perhaps he still could save it.

Thinking of this, D nodded in agreement to the price: "Alright."

Zack's eyes lit up: "Then it's settled! I'll take today's goods!"

He specifically brought enough money this time, and after careful calculation and weighing, Zack paid D for the milk and other rock salt, then drove off contentedly from the pasture with his large truck.

With nearly a million suddenly in the account, D looked at the pile of large-denomination bills in front of him, feeling a surge of emotion.

So much money!

Unfortunately, the pasture had already officially gotten on track, and there was basically nowhere to spend it now.

"Never mind, I'll save it for now! I can look for somewhere selling villas in the future, this way I can occasionally bring Koraidon and them on vacation." D thought.

Then, he took out his travel backpack from home, packed the money inside, and rode Koraidon to Porto Marinada Town to deposit the money in the bank.

By the time they returned to the pasture, the sky had gradually darkened outside.

As the temperature slowly warmed up, these Pokemon wouldn't just stay home, nestling in front of the fireplace for warmth.

Even after dark, they were still outside running around playing.

D stood under the eaves, looking helplessly at this scene. He turned back to the room, thinking about Alder and Volcarona, and then used the video phone to contact Geeta.

Maybe Geeta, the Chief Champion of the Paldea League, would know something about champions of other regions.

As the call connected, Geeta's face appeared on the screen.

"Oh? It's D? What's up? Have you finally decided to come work at the Paldea League?" Geeta asked with a smile.

D: "..."

Perhaps his expression was too obviously speechless, Geeta couldn't help but smile: "Alright, let's not talk about that. What's the reason for contacting me this time?"

D nodded and asked: "By the way, do you know the champions of other regions?"

"Champions of other regions?" Upon hearing this, Geeta was slightly surprised, though she works at the League, communications with other regions are not frequent.

And each region is managed and maintained by its own Pokemon League, other regional Pokemon Leagues won't intervene casually.

Therefore, her understanding of champions from other regions is merely on a namesake level.

Thinking of this, Geeta nodded: "I know some, but not many, like Champion Diantha of the Kalos Region, she is also a very famous actress!"

"Then there's Alder from the Unova Region, Cynthia from the Sinnoh Region, Leon from the Galar Region, beyond that I'm not sure, because those places are too far from Paldea."

Saying this, Geeta suddenly remembered something: "Oh right, the strongest champion of the Galar Region, Leon, had recently submitted a visit request to Paldea, not sure why he hasn't arrived yet."

---

Leon? Coming to Paldea? D was quite surprised, could he have gotten lost?

"Why are you suddenly asking about these things?" Geeta asked.

D gave the excuse he had prepared in advance: "Because I heard from a Trainer passing by the ranch that the Champions from other regions are all very strong."

Hearing this, Geeta became interested: "You want to battle them?"

D: "…No, I'm just a rancher, I don't have their kind of strength."

Geeta thought to herself, not believing it for a second, "You're the Trainer who even managed to capture the legendary The King of Bountiful Harvests!"

At this moment, Geeta suddenly remembered something and said, "It might be possible with other Champions, but not with Alder."

"Hmm?" D was puzzled and asked, "What's going on with Mr. Alder?"

Geeta: "Alder's main Pokemon, Volcarona, got injured in an accident and is beyond even the Pokemon Center's ability to heal. Alder currently has no heart for battling and rarely deals with Pokemon League matters. I've heard that the affairs of the Pokemon League there are now handled by the Elite Four."

Speaking up to this point, Geeta sighed with regret: "It's a pity for such talent to disappear just like that."

"I see…" D nodded thoughtfully, but he was a bit curious about why Geeta knew so much about the Unova League.

Geeta seemed to notice his thoughts and smiled as she explained:

"Because of the academy's establishment, in recent years, the Paldea League has established connections with the Unova League."

"Not long ago, they established the sister school Blueberry Academy of Orange Academy and Naranja Academy over the ocean. Every year, the two schools send exchange students to each other's academies for study!"

D was stunned upon hearing this.

Is there such a thing? Why hadn't he heard about it?

At this moment, other voices came through from Geeta's side.

Geeta responded and then said to D: "Alright, there are some matters in the business department that I need to handle. I'll contact you if I have time later!"

Having obtained the information he needed, D thanked Geeta: "Thank you very much for telling me all this."

Geeta smiled slightly, she was about to hang up the call when she suddenly remembered something:

"Oh, by the way, regarding Calyrex using its great power to restore the ecology around Porto Marinada Town, the Paldea League is fully aware."

"To express gratitude and commemorate the deeds of Calyrex, the Paldea League has decided to build an Abundant Shrine in its honor. The person responsible for this task should arrive at your location soon, and she will handle it."

An Abundant Shrine?

D was taken aback, watching as Geeta hung up the phone, feeling completely bewildered.

*

At the same time, deep within the Paldea Giant Pit, inside the Area Zero Research Institute.

The Time Machine was still operating, continuously transmitting Pokemon from distant ancient times and the future to the present.

However, not every moment would a Paradox Pokemon be transported here.

Only those Paradox Pokemon that meet the conditions and satisfy the transfer requirements are transported over.

For example, those who are at the same location as the Time Machine and exhibit curiosity about its activities, responding to its call.

At this moment, within the large structure housing the Time Machine, a dense white light flashed, and a Pokemon with long hair, resembling a large Misdreavus, was leisurely flying in mid-air inside the Time Machine.

Below, Professor Sada and Professor Turo looked at the Pokemon, its name immediately coming to their minds.

It's the Paradox Pokemon known as Flutter Mane documented in the "Book of Scarlet and Violet"!

"Be careful, Flutter Mane is highly aggressive and combative!" Professor Turo first warned.

Professor Sada nodded calmly, having plenty of experience dealing with such situations.

——If a Paradox Pokemon shows an intention to attack, they would forcibly use a Poke Ball to capture it, then seal the Poke Ball, trapping the Pokemon inside.

However, that Flutter Mane seemed to have no intention of attacking.

It just floated calmly in mid-air, watching the two below with its calm, blood-red eyes.

Seeing this, Professor Sada hesitated slightly in her action to throw the Poke Ball.

The next second, Flutter Mane's figure gradually faded into the shadows and vanished from its original spot.

"It's gone?!" Professor Sada felt a sudden tightness in her heart.

Professor Turo also took out a Poke Ball, preparing for a contingency, should Flutter Mane launch a surprise attack from the shadows.

But they waited a long time without any sign of Flutter Mane taking action.

Seeing this, Professor Turo instantly made a judgment: "It escaped!"

"Is there such a thing…" Professor Sada was a bit surprised, as they had never encountered such a situation before.

Fortunately, there were no researchers in the large building housing the Time Machine, so even if Flutter Mane ran out, it wouldn't harm anyone.

And for the fleeing Flutter Mane, there's only one destination—to go to the upper layers of the Paldea Giant Pit and integrate into the ecosystem there.

"What a shame to lose such a rare, calm-natured ancient Paradox Species Pokemon." Professor Sada felt a deep sense of regret.

---

Professor Turo consoled his wife, "Alright, don't worry. Our previous research shows that these Paradox Pokemon, summoned from the ancient and future times, are adapting to this era and humanity. As long as we keep waiting, one day we will realize that dream!"

Professor Sada also came to her senses at this moment and nodded, "That's right!"

It seemed the Time Machine would not be calling any more Pokemon today.

The two glanced at the time and seeing it was late, they turned around and left the place.

In the unnoticed shadows, Flutter Mane silently observed the two unfamiliar figures.

A moment later, it slipped back into the shadows, following them.

Professor Sada and Professor Turo returned to their laboratory.

Professor Turo went to check on the Paradox Species of Pokemon that were gradually adapting to the modern era.

Meanwhile, Professor Sada sat at her desk, writing today's research notes.

"Today, Turo and I ventured deeper, discovering a massive void in the lower depths of Zone Zero.

And Terapagos..... The Hidden Treasure of Area Zero lies dormant at the deepest part of that void.

Its body, turned into Crystal, protects it from external threats.

However, it seems to require some time to awaken.

Perhaps influenced by Terapagos, an unprecedented Terastal phenomenon is occurring within the Underdepths.

Encompassing all Tera Types...

Turo and I decided to temporarily name this Tera Type as Stellar."

Pausing there, Professor Sada turned the page in her notebook and continued writing:

"After discussion, Turo and I decided to refer to the region beneath Zone Zero as Area Zero Underdepths.

Judging from the strata, I estimate this void formed around two million years ago.

Traces suggest that until the void formed, it was filled with countless something.

The depth of the collapse is about 1000 meters, with signs of repeated collapses, initially attributed to tectonic activity and erosion by rainwater and groundwater.

Eventually, after the largest-scale collapse, the terrain within the Underdepths has remained stable to this day."

Professor Sada ceased writing, pondering the connection between the Pokemon named Terapagos and all of this.

Undoubtedly, Terapagos is the source of the Terastal phenomenon.

However, according to her investigation, this Pokemon seems to have been a tribal Pokemon in ancient Paldea, always residing deep within the Earth's crust.

Reflecting on the terrifying collapse traces discovered inside the Underdepths earlier, Professor Sada speculated:

"Could it be that tectonic movements resulted in the extinction of all other Terapagos, leaving only this one, trapped in slumber?"

The contemplative Professor Sada did not notice Flutter Mane lurking in the shadows behind her.

Flutter Mane turned its head to survey the surroundings.

Various documents filled the laboratory, messily piled together.

Beside that pile of documents stood a whiteboard.

Stuck on the whiteboard were various photos, mostly pictures of Paradox Pokemon taken by Professor Sada and Professor Turo.

Additionally, there were quite a few photos of Koraidon and Miraidon, including one of D with the Koraidon already integrated into the human world.

Flutter Mane gazed calmly at these photos, its eyes slowly scanning over them, finally resting on a picture of D stroking Koraidon's head.
