---

---

---

# 295: Chapter 292: Koraidon's Friend

This Pokemon has successfully found that person.

Flutter Mane looked at the familiar Koraidon in the photo, recalling past events in its heart.

Back then, this land was occupied by a group of fearsome and terrifying Koraidon.

Even its territory was taken over by a powerful Koraidon.

After losing the territorial battle, it intended to flee, but just then, a special Koraidon arrived here.

This Koraidon engaged in a life-and-death battle with the Koraidon occupying its territory and successfully defeated it.

Just when Flutter Mane thought its territory would change hands again, that Koraidon gave it a glance and then quietly left.

After this parting, Flutter Mane was full of confusion. In its understanding, it could not grasp what had happened or why that Koraidon acted as it did.

After watching the other leave, Flutter Mane once again took over this land.

However, not long after, another terrifyingly powerful Pokemon arrived.

After a fierce battle with this new opponent, its territory was occupied once again and it fled in humiliation.

Not long after roaming outside, one day, Flutter Mane encountered that special Koraidon again.

It saw it roaming this land, struggling to survive, invading different Pokemon territories, and engaging in battles, even falling into danger multiple times.

All along, a ghost accompanied that Koraidon.

It was an unknown "Pokemon" with white hair on its head and appeared quite elderly.

However, Koraidon seemed unaware of its presence.

Then one day, this special Koraidon encountered another powerful Koraidon.

That Koraidon was the ruler of this area. Even after using all its strength, Koraidon was no match for it.

It was knocked to the ground, and just as the opponent was about to end its life with a punch, the "ghost" stood in front of it at the crisis moment.

Seeing this, Flutter Mane was shocked to its core.

The "ghost" exhausted all its soul power and was struck to shreds, allowing Koraidon to escape and achieve victory from defeat.

At this moment, something in Flutter Mane's heart that once only understood survival and battle was deeply moved, as if something had taken root and begun to sprout within.

It felt curious, wanting to know what it was.

Therefore, it decided to follow this special Koraidon.

From that day on, its life was no longer solely about brutal attacks, savage hunting, and fierce territory battles with other Pokemon. Something else had emerged.

Soon, this Koraidon discovered its presence.

In a brief exchange, Flutter Mane learned from it that it seemed to be searching for someone very important to it.

To this end, it had traveled through nearly every corner of this land.

To understand the reason for the changes in its heart, Flutter Mane decided to help it in its search.

Eventually, they arrived above a large underground cavern.

Koraidon sensed the dangerous aura below and instructed Flutter Mane to stay while it descended.

Flutter Mane agreed but waited a long time without Koraidon's return.

Feeling a growing concern, it hurriedly flew into the cavern to search.

But by then, there was no trace of Koraidon, only a group of innocent, sparkling little turtle Pokemon.

At that moment, Flutter Mane felt something calling out to it.

Following this elusive call, it pressed forward and finally saw a bright white light.

Flutter Mane flew into it, disappearing within the white light and when it opened its eyes again, it had arrived here, facing two unfamiliar creatures with hairy heads.

They were identical to the ghost that once protected Koraidon long ago, clearly of the same race.

According to what the Koraidon had said, these should be a Pokemon called humans, the important people Koraidon had been searching for.

At this moment, looking at Koraidon's smiling face in the photo, a strange feeling resurfaced in Flutter Mane's heart.

—How wonderful.

Flutter Mane thought, genuinely feeling happy for Koraidon from the bottom of its heart.

Thinking of these things, Flutter Mane's usually calm face appeared a little dazed.

Suddenly, a gasp came from behind it.

Flutter Mane was startled awake, just as it saw a human had spotted it.

It didn't stay any longer, instantly merging into the shadows and disappearing from the spot.

Professor Sada looked at that place in shock.

Her face showed an expression of disbelief.

Had Flutter Mane been looking at a photo just now?

At this time, Professor Turo hurriedly came over after hearing the commotion: "What happened?"

Professor Sada detailed her recent discovery to him.

Professor Turo pondered for a moment, then raised his head and looked towards a security camera in the corner of the room.

Three minutes later, the two professors arrived in the surveillance room to review what had just happened in the room.

Through the screen, they clearly saw Flutter Mane emerging from the shadow, attracted by the photos on the whiteboard.

Its gaze scanned the whiteboard and eventually rested on the photo of Koraidon and D, watching for a long time.

Witnessing this, Professor Turo furrowed his brow.

Professor Sada, on the other hand, was full of disbelief: "Was it looking at the number one Koraidon?"

---

What's going on? Could it be that this Flutter Mane and Koraidon come from the same time? Do they know each other?

If that's really the case...

Professor Sada's eyes were filled with excitement; that would be a major discovery!

"Calm down." Seeing his wife so excited, Professor Turo couldn't help but say, "It might just be that the photo caught its attention."

After all, Paradox Pokemon summoned from a distant ancient era have never seen a photo before.

Just like some Pokemon who get scared after seeing themselves in the mirror for the first time.

"I know." Professor Sada nodded, she thought for a moment, planning to conduct some small experiments first.

If this Flutter Mane really knows D's Koraidon, then they might have to invite D over here.

But more important than this, they had more crucial matters to attend to.

Deeper in Zone Zero, the Area Zero Underdepths held many mysteries.

Even Professor Sada and Professor Turo, who discovered the place first, couldn't explain them.

These past days, they had been delving deeper into exploring the Underdepths.

After a night's rest, at the break of dawn, the two professors reorganized their equipment, preparing to take the elevator back into the Area Zero Underdepths.

However, just as they were about to depart, they suddenly received a report from the canteen chef.

"Bad news, professors, the lab is haunted! Yesterday, a pile of meat and Berries I stored in the warehouse disappeared completely!"

Hearing this, Professor Sada was slightly taken aback, "What happened?"

The chef shook his head, "I don't know! The warehouse door was perfectly locked, so there's no way anyone or any Pokemon could have snuck in and eaten it! And they were all uncooked raw meat..."

Professor Sada thought for a moment before saying, "Let's talk about this when we get back."

"Yes!"

After sending off the chef, the two professors arrived at the elevator leading to the Area Zero Underdepths.

Hiding in the shadows, the Flutter Mane observed the actions of these two humans.

It quietly followed along, hiding in the shadows, as they took the elevator deeper down.

As the elevator doors slowly opened, Professor Sada and Professor Turo once again began their investigation of the Underdepths.

And looking at this unfamiliar place, Flutter Mane couldn't help but feel a sense of familiarity.

If it remembered correctly, it was in this very place that it first saw that group of little turtle Pokemon.

Back then, they lived here in groups, enjoying coiling on the shiny Gems, stretching their necks to drink droplets falling from the stalactites above the Underdepths.

But now, it was completely empty, not a hint of a little turtle in sight.

This astounded Flutter Mane.

While the two humans investigated, it quietly flew out and began searching in this vast cave system.

Flutter Mane couldn't understand what had happened; it had just flown into the white light, and when it came out, everything outside had changed drastically.

Even the air was different from before.

The further in it flew, the more anxious it became inside.

Finally, it got completely lost in the depths of the Area Zero Underdepths.

Not until it saw the two humans again did Flutter Mane finally breathe a sigh of relief.

It didn't dare run around anymore and quietly followed behind the two humans, hoping to leave here with them and return above ground.

However, the two humans did not leave; they continued on, and after who knows how long, they arrived at a cavern filled with Crystals.

Here, Flutter Mane saw again the faintly breathing turtles, turned into Gem-like forms, sleeping attached to the glowing rocks.

*

In the blink of an eye, a whole month had passed since the last acceleration of the World Tree.

Today, it was time again for Calyrex to nurture the World Tree.

That morning, D gathered the Pokemon from the ranch around the World Tree.

Once everyone was present, Calyrex nodded slightly to D, then waved its arm.

The next moment, a powerful surge of Grass Type Energy gushed from its body, then poured entirely into the World Tree in front of it.

The World Tree immediately reacted to this power; every leaf began to glow with green light.

The leaves rustled, moving without wind, producing a crisp, rustling sound under the warm spring sunlight.

Its trunk grew even sturdier, its form taller, and the leaves on its crown even more lush.

D's eyes were fixed on the World Tree in front of him.

The specifics of the World Tree appeared in his vision, and the numbers on it continued to change.

[World Tree Seedling: A seedling that has absorbed a large amount of Power of Grass. Due to Gogoat's influence, it underwent a mutation during growth; a vast Life Force is brewing within, eventually breaking through the earth, needing 95,995 more years to mature.]

[94,563 years.]

[94,336 years.]

"..."

Ultimately, it halted at 93,995 years.

Just when D thought it was finished, in the next second, the number twitched again.

It changed from [93,995 years] to [91,220 years].

A critical hit triggered!

An additional 2,775 years were deducted from the growth time; is this the effect of the "Leaf Cake" enhanced with a Friendship Berry?

D was utterly astonished.

And with the "Leaf Cake" effect of "shortening growth time" triggered, the already ceased-growing World Tree emitted a buzzing sound once more.

---

The dense thicket suddenly unfurled, transforming into a massive green canopy, shading D and a group of Pokémon beneath it in the sunlight.

In the blink of an eye, the World Tree shot up from two meters to over three meters.

In this form, it had already grown into a towering tree!

"Maa-ma…"

"Ba-moo…"

"..."

The group of Pokémon around gazed up at the World Tree, whispering in astonishment.

Calyrex was also taken aback by this scene, then, as if realizing something, used telepathy to confirm with D: "Is this the effect of that Leaf Cake?"

D nodded: "Yes."

Calyrex murmured: "How miraculous."

At this moment, a voice suddenly came from behind.

"Is this the power of The King of Bountiful Harvests? Truly as incredible as Geeta said."

D turned his head to see a girl with long green hair, wearing brown dungarees and a khaki collared shirt on top, looking up at the World Tree in shock.

Noticing D's gaze, the neatly dressed woman diverted her attention, nodded, and greeted him:

"Hi, superstar favored by the Champion! I'm Rika, here representing the Paldea League to express gratitude to you and The King of Bountiful Harvests."

Rika?

Isn't she the Ground Type Elite of the Paldea Region? Why is she here?

D was initially surprised, but thinking of Larry being squeezed by Geeta, he wasn't stunned anymore.

He nodded and responded politely: "Hello, I'm D, the Rancher of D Ranch."

"The Champion told me everything, you are truly remarkable! I've heard you're incredibly strong!" Rika said with a smile.

"Uh…" D really didn't know what to say for a moment, and could only reply: "It's not that exaggerated." Just an ordinary rancher.

"Hardly something to say out loud!" Rika raised an eyebrow: "You're too modest. Okay, although I'd like to chat with you, there's more important business to attend to now."

Saying this, Rika walked over to a Pokémon with a huge head.

Though amazed by the size of this Pokémon's head, she seriously said:

"D, The King of Bountiful Harvests, on behalf of the Paldea League, I express deep thanks to you both."

D kept silent, and Calyrex spoke with authority: "No need for formality."

The Pokémon spoke?! Rika's pupils slightly contracted, looking a bit shocked.

Is this the legendary telepathy?

Calyrex is a Psychic Type Pokémon?

Rika had seen much of the world and wasn't too surprised by a talking Pokémon, she continued:

"To show our gratitude to you both, the Paldea League will construct an Abundant Shrine on the hill in honor of The King of Bountiful Harvests, marking this aid."

Having heard beforehand from Geeta, D wasn't too surprised.

He simply found it interesting.

With the construction of the Abundant Shrine, maybe centuries later, the story of Calyrex instantly growing the flora of the hill will become legendary.

By then, perhaps it will attract a new generation of Trainers to explore the legend.

Just as people in the Johto Region built the Bell Tower to commemorate Ho-oh.

And Calyrex will become akin to Ho-oh, its legend bound to spread throughout the vast Paldea Land.

---

---

---

---

# 296: Chapter 293: Shrine and Cemetery, The Legend of Houndstone!

The actions of the Paldea League are astonishingly swift.

When Rika mentioned building a shrine for Calyrex, a truck drove into the back mountain that very morning.

Out of curiosity, D followed Rika over to watch.

He saw several burly men in blue work uniforms open the truck door and jump out, hammering away at the mountaintop.

Soon, a super luxurious shrine was completed.

The statue of Calyrex was placed in the center of the shrine, and the statue's face was smiling, exuding an aura of gentleness and kindness.

D was stunned.

"Isn't this a bit over the top?"

Rika, standing beside him, smiled slightly. Perhaps the two had become more familiar through their chats because her tone was not as formal as it was initially.

"Yes~ That's the speed of the Paldea League's operations! Every member of the League is working hard!"

"And this is just the beginning!" A glint of anticipation flickered in Rika's eyes: "Next, these gentlemen will build stairways leading down the mountain on the path, constructing a large shrine!"

"Huh?" D was dumbfounded: "Isn't this too extravagant?"

He initially thought only a small shrine would be built, like the one deep in the Glimwood Tangle.

"Haha!" Rika smiled and said earnestly: "But compared to what Calyrex has done, these are just minor gestures!"

The ecosystem of this mountain was severely damaged. Restoring it would require a lot of manpower and a considerable amount of money.

Calyrex used its great power to restore everything here, saving the Paldea League a lot of expenses.

Therefore, whether on the surface or deep in their hearts, they sincerely thank Calyrex and D.

"The Chairwoman told me that Calyrex once lost its faith, was forgotten by the world, and wandered alone on the vast snowy plains... Such legends are also circulating in Porto Marinada Town."

At this point, Rika paused and then softly asked: "With this, it should help Calyrex, right?"

Helping it gain belief, restoring its power.

D nodded: "That's correct."

But Calyrex's power has already returned to its peak now, regarding these beliefs...

If there are more, it could accelerate its energy recovery and help the World Tree grow faster, but it's okay if there aren't any.

Hearing the definite answer from D, Rika nodded gently: "Ah~ that would be the best!"

"Calyrex should also be very happy to know that," D said, regretting that Calyrex remained at the ranch and did not come along.

"Ah~" Rika sighed, then remembered something and turned to ask: "Do you wish for the Paldea League to tell the world about Calyrex being by your side?"

D had already considered this matter before.

Hence, he shook his head and refused: "Let's just let it be."

For instance, if Trainers hear about the legend of Calyrex and become interested in it, they might visit the shrine on the hillside in Porto Marinada Town.

Rather than rushing to the ranch to challenge Calyrex just because of its title as a Legendary Pokemon.

If that happened, Calyrex, who transitioned from a King to a farmer, would surely be very troubled.

Upon hearing this, Rika nodded: "Because of the previous forest fire incident, many people are concerned, and they have sent many supplies for the surviving Pokemon."

"Therefore, the Paldea League plans to tell the people living in this place through television news—a Legendary Pokemon with a loving heart couldn't bear to see such a tragic situation, so it used its great power to rejuvenate the back mountain and restore the forest. What do you think of this?"

D nodded, approving.

In that case, the deeds of Calyrex will forever be passed down in the Paldea Region?

Now is no longer the era of ancient kingdoms but a more enlightened era of the League, not to mention that newspapers and television can record the legend of Calyrex.

Even as the Chairwoman of the League, Geeta would find it difficult to erase the traces of Calyrex's existence under such circumstances.

This way, no matter where Calyrex goes, it will no longer fall into its past plight.

Forgotten by the world, abandoned by its beloved horse, and lonely wandering on the Crown Tundra.

This was all D could do for Calyrex, he thought.

At that moment, the men finished building Calyrex's shrine.

D originally thought they would turn and leave, but unexpectedly, they drove deeper into the forest.

Seeing this, D was slightly puzzled, "Where are they going?"

Rika sighed: "The Paldea League intends to build a Pokemon cemetery deep in the forest to mourn those that perished in the fire, hoping that the Pokemon that lost their lives can rest in peace... It's quite sad when you think about it."

D also remembered those deceased Pokemon they found while rescuing in the forest last year.

At that time, he dug a pit to bury them, offering prayers for those Pokemon alongside Pawmot and the others.

---

The Paldea League must have thought of these things too, right?

The two followed along and eventually came to a clearing in the forest.

In the clearing, some burly workers were digging the earth, burying tombstones engraved with the names of deceased Pokemon in the ground.

Rika gazed at this scene and murmured, "I've heard that after a Pokemon passes away, it doesn't just disappear; it transforms into a ghost, wandering this land, searching for the way home."

D remained silent, knowing a bit more about this than Rika did.

After death, both humans and Pokemon become ghosts, returning to the Spirit Realm to rejoin the cycle of the world.

If a ghost with too strong an attachment is unwilling to leave, the gatekeeper Dusknoir will appear and take it into the Spirit Realm, guiding it to the underworld.

"I wonder if, after I die, my foreign soul will be taken to the Spirit Realm by Dusknoir," D murmured to himself.

Seeing his pensive expression, Rika comforted him, "Don't be too sad. Have you also experienced the death of a Pokemon?"

D thought to himself that he wasn't sad, and upon hearing Rika's words, he shook his head.

"Birth, aging, sickness, and death are all normal parts of this world," Rika said, looking up at the cloudy sky. "Have you heard of the Pokemon called Houndstone?"

D froze for a moment. "Houndstone?"

"Yes!" Rika nodded with a smile, "Houndstone is the form taken by a deeply mourned Pokemon when it reincarnates."

"People say that as long as you deeply remember a Pokemon that has passed away, one day in the future, you'll meet a Houndstone."

"That Pokemon, feeling the Trainer's sentiments, has reincarnated into a Houndstone and returned to their side."

"That's really something..." D was momentarily at a loss for words to describe it.

Speaking of this, Rika laughed softly, "But if you raise your Pokemon healthy and strong, they'll live a long, long time!"

"I've seen a Butterfree that lived nearly sixty years! Its Trainer took it out for exercise every day, feeding it all kinds of berries for nutrition, extending a lifespan usually only three or four decades by more than twenty years!"

D had never considered this path: "Is that really possible? You're not joking, right?"

Rika: "Of course not! When do I ever joke...? This is something I've personally encountered!"

D: "..."

He's decided! Once he returns to the ranch, he would start training everyone, preparing all sorts of nutritional supplements to let those Pokemon live long lives!

D made up his mind internally.

Looking at Rika, who seemed younger than him, D suddenly remembered something.

If he wasn't mistaken, the Paldea League's Elite Four were quite unique.

The Earth Elite Rika, Dragon Type Elite Hassel, Flying Elite Larry, and the youngest, the nine-year-old Steel Elite Poppy.

At this time, Poppy must have just been born...

Out of curiosity, D couldn't help but ask, "By the way, who are the Elite Four of the Paldea League?"

His topic shifted too quickly, and Rika didn't react immediately. It took her a while to realize what D was talking about.

Rika curiously asked, "Elite Four? Were you invited by the chief to join the Paldea League as one of the Elite Four? If that's the case, it sounds pretty interesting!"

"Not really," D shook his head. "The chief just invited me to work at the League."

"Is that so?" Rika thought for a moment and then replied, "Currently, the Paldea Elite Four has only three members: me, specializing in Ground Type; Hassel, specializing in Dragon Type; and Larry, temporarily assigned by the chief to fill in as the Flying Elite."

"There's a vacancy for one Elite position. Since the last Elite retired, no one has filled it, and it's remained like this ever since."

D asked in confusion, "What happens if a Trainer challenges the Elite Four?"

Rika: "They temporarily pull someone from the Champion-level Trainers to fill in!"

D: "..."

As expected of the Paldea League, holding multiple positions is all too common here.

For example, Larry, primarily an employee in the Pokemon Operations Department, not only serves as a Gym Leader but also as the Flying Elite.

It's not just Larry; Rika also serves as an interviewer at the Pokemon League.

Even Geeta isn't idle; she not only handles League affairs but also inspects Gyms everywhere, searching for talents to join the Paldea League.

Busy indeed!

In comparison, life at the ranch is much more leisurely.

*

In the afternoon, after receiving a phone call, Rika hurriedly left the ranch.

According to her, a Trainer had collected eight Gym Badges and was heading to League Headquarters to challenge the Elite Four.

As an interviewer, she had to return to work promptly.

D: "..."

He could only watch Rika's figure fade into the distance.

From the current progress, the construction of the Abundant Shrine would not be completed in a day or two.

Besides building the shrine, the workers also had to lay the staircase leading down the mountain.

From what Rika implied, the Paldea League and the mayor of Porto Marinada Town, Thomas, had discussed plans to create a complete shrine and to set the day Calyrex performed miracles as the Abundant Festival.

On that day every year, the town would hold a festival at the shrine to commemorate Calyrex's great deeds.

Regarding the festival... D could only recall scattered memories from anime he'd watched before.

Like girls in festival attire, sake chewed on by mouth, and elders presiding over the festival.

At that time, people would light the mountain path with torches and proceed from the foot of the mountain to the shrine, where they would pay respects to the deities enshrined there.

Dusk approached, and the sky was filled with the colors of twilight.

The ranch echoed with the happy laughter of Pokemon.

They chased and played on the grass or picked flowers in the flowerbeds.

When D returned to the ranch, he was greeted with a bunch of blooming yellow flowers from Cyclizar.

Seeing this, D immediately remembered how last spring Cyclizar had given him a bunch of flowers, and then Koraidon, not to be outdone, also picked a bunch of flowers to give to him.

D reached out to take the bunch of flowers from Cyclizar, planning to place them in a vase in his room, then patted Cyclizar's head:

"Good boy, I really like these flowers."

"Rawr~" Cyclizar let out a happy growl and wagged its tail joyfully.

D was curious about what Koraidon would do this time.

He turned to look, and noticed that Koraidon, who had been napping under the eaves, was nowhere to be seen.

D was a bit surprised and turned around to see Koraidon diving headfirst into the fields of lush flowers.

After a while, Koraidon returned.

Its hands were clutching a colorful bunch of flowers.

"..."

As expected, Koraidon was still Koraidon!

D reached out to accept them, knowing that if he accepted Cyclizar's flowers but not Koraidon's, Koraidon would get jealous and upset.

"Alright! Go play!" D said.

However, Koraidon didn't leave. It had just seen D pat Cyclizar's head, so it leaned in too.

D helplessly patted its head, and only then was Koraidon satisfied.

"Ahh gah-see~"

Such a childish creature!

D turned around, intending to go back to the room to prepare dinner for everyone.

Koraidon and Cyclizar followed him, one on each side.

While taking out ingredients from the refrigerator, D thought of the information Geeta had previously told him.

Geeta said that Alder was no longer involved in League affairs and now likely wandered throughout the Unova Region.

Therefore, finding Alder would be an extremely difficult task.

Looking at the bottle of Life Sheep Milk in front of him, D regretted, even if only a little bit.

He didn't see how he could abandon everything at the ranch to travel to the Unova Region to find Alder.

As night approached, the Pawmot and their group gradually returned home from playing outside.

Seeing them, D was reminded of what Rika had said earlier today.

— Raise your Pokemon healthy and strong, so they live long lives.

No one can be left out, whether it's Pawmot, Cyclizar, Alcremie, Combee, or Butterfree!

---

---

---

---

# 297: Chapter 294: Living to a Hundred

Early the next morning, D began organizing the Pokemon on the ranch for a morning run and workout.

Even the calm Dolliv was forcibly dragged along, starting from the doorstep, passing by the Glass Greenhouse and Berry Garden, and running around the edges of the ranch.

Faced with D's sudden raid, the group of Pokemon were both bewildered and confused.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmot, Gogoat, and Dragonite, who were already skilled at running, found it quite easy.

Whereas for Alcremie, Glaceon, Dolliv, Miltank, and Naclstack, who didn't get much exercise daily, it was somewhat torturous.

Miltank was so tired it was gasping for breath, eventually resorting to Rollout.

It curled its body into a ball and rolled quickly around the ranch, swiftly moving from the back of the group to a forward position.

In the horse stable, Spectrier and Glastrier watched the lively race while munching on carrots.

After one lap, Alcremie was exhausted and collapsed on the grass, with a very aggrieved look on its face.

"Mamaa..."

D stood in front of the group of Pokemon and finally explained in response to their queries:

"Everyone must be wondering why I made you start exercising today, right?"

"Roar." Dragonite nodded its big head; it didn't mind working out, it was just quite surprised.

"That's because I heard that the stronger and healthier a Pokemon's body is, the more powerful it is, and the longer it can live in this world." D explained.

Live longer?

Upon hearing this, Dragonite and the other Pokemon blinked, showing expressions of puzzlement.

To be honest, they didn't have much concept of lifespan.

The group of Pokemon looked at D puzzled, so D had to explain the specific meaning of lifespan:

"Lifespan is the time we can spend in this world. Most organisms have limited lifespans, but due to different races, there can be differences in length."

At this point, D intended to explain a deeper meaning.

But he thought again, the Pokemon didn't need to know these things; even if they knew, it'd only increase their sadness.

So, he changed his words: "Once our bodies get stronger, we can keep living together."

Upon hearing this, the group of Pokemon blinked as if they understood it, though many parts of it were confusing, they understood the last sentence.

As long as they grow stronger, they can be with D forever!

D's gaze swept across the Pokemon in front of him.

Each Pokemon's expression was different; some looked blank, clearly not understanding.

While others had clear eyes, indicating they understood his words.

Like the Indeedee Sibling.

D nodded at them, all was understood without words.

At this point, the clever Pawmot asked, "Bamor?"

The Translation Nuts Cake at home had been used up, so D couldn't directly understand the Pokemon's meaning now.

Seeing this, Pawmot looked around, then quickly ran to the Glass Greenhouse to bring out Calyrex.

With Calyrex's translation, D finally understood Pawmot's question.

It was asking, "How long is D's lifespan?"

D was slightly startled, then replied with a smile, "Me? I'll live to be a hundred!"

Seeing the Pokemon unable to understand, D explained, "It means living to more than a hundred years old."

Pawmot persisted, "Bamor?" (What happens after a hundred?)

D thought carefully, then said, "In human terms, it would be passing away! Passing away means dying, leaving this world."

Upon hearing this, the group of Pokemon in front of him suddenly showed sad expressions.

They knew what dying meant; they'd seen Pokemon lose their lives in a fire before.

Dying means never being able to open your eyes to speak again, forever buried in the soil.

"But no need to worry." D said with a smile: "Even if I pass away, it doesn't matter; I'll become a ghost and accompany you, always be with you."

After all, in this world ghosts do exist. If Dusknoir, the gatekeeper of the Spirit Realm, comes to take him away, he believes everyone will protect him.

By then, the World Tree should have grown, and he can happily live with the Pokemon on it.

Pawmot's eyes sparkled with tears, "Bamor?" (Really?)

D nodded, "Of course it's true!"

Upon hearing this, Dragonite immediately hugged D tightly.

"Now we can train happily, right?" D pat Dragonite's big claw, asking humorously.

"Roar!" Dragonite nodded enthusiastically.

"Then, you all keep running!" D took a deep breath, said with a smile.

However, the Pokemon in front of him did not move.

They looked at each other and then let out a series of growls that D couldn't understand.

Koraidon stepped forward to grab his hand, and the other Pokemon gathered around. In the company of them, D was drawn into this workout activity.

---

The nearby Calyrex, having witnessed everything, also translated the Pokémon's words for him with a smile:

"They said [We also want D to live longer, so D, come train with us!]"

Just like D wanted to be with them forever, they also wished for D to live a long life, always staying together.

D: "..."

So, did he dig a pit for himself?

*

Training is not just about strengthening the body, it also requires proper nutrition.

D used Life Sheep Milk from the Gogoats to prepare various nutritious meals for the Pokémon.

The effects of Life Sheep Milk rival the Holy Ash of Ho-oh.

In essence, the Pawmots were eating something equivalent to Holy Ash.

Nourished by these supplements, it took only a week for all the Pokémon on the ranch to undergo transformations.

This afternoon, D called everyone playing around the ranch over to check on their current status.

Butterfree looked spirited and energetic, its appearance becoming even more beautiful, with those large white wings featuring black veins becoming more resilient, glittering brightly under the sunlight.

D observed carefully and then noted Butterfree's condition in his notebook.

[Butterfree, which has been consuming Life Sheep Milk long-term, seems to have undergone secondary development. Its wings are not as fragile as they appear; they are resilient enough to support it in creating more powerful storms.]

After completing the records, D moved before Cyclizar for examination.

"Gah-ow~"

Cyclizar cooperatively stretched its arms and lifted its legs.

"Hmm, Cyclizar's physique has clearly become more robust, with a noticeable increase in muscle, and it seems to have grown taller. If we were in a game, it would surely be a Cyclizar wearing a Big Guy Badge."

D noted these changes in his notebook.

Then, he moved before Koraidon for a check.

Comparatively, Koraidon's changes were not so apparent, perhaps because its power was already at a top-tier level?

Those all-red muscles might make the neighboring Machamp envious.

"Dragonite..." D approached Dragonite, reaching out to poke at its scales.

Hmm, the texture has become harder. In terms of enduring damage, there should be significant enhancement.

"Just right, I can discuss in detail the Storm Trial and Thunder Trial with Dragonite and Pawmot later. Judging by the time, Paldea Academy's students should arrive here soon."

Because Rika told him earlier that the Paldea League planned to broadcast the follow-up of the forest fire that shocked the region to the people living in this land today.

By then, surely many Trainers lured by Calyrex would come, and he must make preparations in advance.

At this moment, the Miltanks beside him were already restless.

D turned to look.

The Miltanks also looked at him, brimming with spirit and shouted, "Moo!"

D: "...Go! Remember to take care and not bump into other Pokémon!"

"Moo!" The three Miltanks stretched out their strong arms and then transformed into round balls, rolling excitedly across the grass.

Watching this scene, D wrote in his notebook:

"After drinking Life Sheep Milk, the Miltanks seem to have overflowed energy. They're always full of vitality, exercising by rolling around."

This situation is somewhat like what happened previously when Skiddo consumed lots of Grassy Seed and disco-danced on the Grassy Terrain to expend energy.

Only, the Miltanks' method is much straightforward.

Roll, roll, continuously roll without stopping.

If given more time, D thinks they might even surpass Whitney's Miltank.

He suddenly remembered Whitney's Miltank in games that often troubled players.

Then the question arises, could he replicate it in the ranch?

This matter depends on the Miltanks' proficiency with Rollout in the future.

While formulating the training plan for Miltanks in his mind, D checked the changes on other Pokémon.

Now in the ranch, excluding Teddiursa and Mew on travel outside, there are thirty Pokémon in total residing in the ranch.

Included are three Gogoats, three Miltanks, five Naclstacks, and three Combee.

The rest are Cyclizar and Koraidon, Alcremie and Dolliv, Glaceon and Pawmot, Indeedee Sibling, Butterfree, Dragonite, and Diglett.

There are also Calyrex and its two steeds, along with its official teawares Sinistea and Polteageist.

Besides, there are some Dragonite and Dragonair who came from Dragonite Island to work but won't reside in the ranch, and the Gimmighoul hiding in the tavern not coming out.

This number, when calculated carefully, is quite impressive, as most people cannot remember each Pokémon's moves and preferences.

Luckily, D prepared an observation notebook long ago, dedicating it to recording the moves, dietary preferences, and physical conditions of Pokémon in the ranch.

By the time all Pokémon's information was fully recorded, it was already evening.

---

Today is the weekend, the Pokemon Tavern is closed, so after dinner, D sat on the sofa with a group of Pokemon.

He turned on the TV and tuned in to the Paldea news channel.

After a few inconsequential appetizers, the screen finally switched to images from Porto Marinada Town.

A female reporter holding a microphone faced the camera, introducing the audience to the forest fire situation that happened earlier.

"... It's been five months since that heartbreaking disaster on January 22nd. I'm sure the viewers at home are curious about the fate of the Pokemon who survived the forest fire, so today I'll take you through the recovery situation here."

On TV, the female reporter took out a red-and-white Pokeball, releasing a Kilowattrel, then rode on its back, flying toward the back mountain.

As the Kilowattrel flew, the back mountain that had once experienced a forest fire finally appeared before all the viewers.

However... it looked somewhat different from what they imagined.

At this moment, seeing the lush, vibrant mountain forest full of tall, robust trees and thriving dense bushes, all the viewers watching the news showed expressions of surprise.

"What's going on? Wasn't this area completely burned by the fire? Why does it look like this now?"

"It's so strange, shouldn't the growth of the trees take more time?"

"What exactly is happening?"

While the audience was puzzled, the female reporter descended slowly on the Kilowattrel at the mountain's foot.

At this moment, a wild Oddish walked past the camera from the bushes.

Seeing this scene, the female reporter smiled and said, "It seems like the recovery here is pretty good!"

"I'm sure everyone is curious about how, despite such a terrible fire happening in this place, it's as if nothing happened now."

"That's right," viewers at home nodded.

The female reporter didn't keep them in suspense.

She walked slowly up the newly constructed mountain path, explaining as she went:

"The reason this has happened is because on the day the Paldea League sent people over to rebuild the forest,

a legendary Pokemon with a compassionate heart arrived. Unable to bear seeing such a tragic situation, it used its mighty power to rejuvenate the back mountain, restoring the forest to its original state."

"H-How is that possible?" People showed expressions of shock.

To restore the entire forest to its original appearance, what kind of Pokemon could possibly accomplish such a feat?

On the screen, the female reporter continued: "It's truly incredible. There's even a legend circulating around Porto Marinada Town about that Pokemon!"

"Some say it is the King of Bountiful Harvests, who blesses the crops planted by the farmers with great yields. Others say it was a king from ancient times with a compassionate heart who healed wounded Pokemon."

"Mysterious, isn't it? I was also shocked when I first heard of it! But it's said that there were many witnesses when that Pokemon performed the miracle, including our chief Champion!"

"With that in mind, I'm sure everyone's curious about that Pokemon."

At this point, the female reporter reached the end of the mountain path. She passed through the torii gate and arrived in front of a brand-new shrine.

The shrine housed a statue of a Pokemon.

The Pokemon on the statue had a gentle expression and a kindly smile on its face.

Looking at the shrine, the female reporter spoke softly: "Ah! This is the Pokemon that restored the forest's original appearance! It is called Calyrex, also known as the King of Bountiful Harvests in the legends that circulate around Porto Marinada Town. Doesn't it seem friendly?"

The camera gently moved through the female reporter, coming straight to the front of the shrine, capturing a close-up of the statue inside.

The statue of Calyrex faced the center of the screen, a smile on its face, as if it was smiling at the vast people of Paldea.

---

---

---

---

# 298: Chapter 295: Surging Faith (Bonus Chapter)

Meanwhile, in the Glass Greenhouse, Calyrex, who was resting with eyes closed, suddenly opened them as if sensing something.

What on earth... has happened?

How could there be so much Power of Faith?

Feeling the warm energy continuously pouring into its body, Calyrex's pupils trembled.

This sudden surge of faith left it somewhat incredulous.

With a slight mental shift, Calyrex's body appeared in the pasture.

It looked into the distance, trying to find the source of the power, but when it saw the surrounding situation clearly, it stood there dumbfounded.

Because that faith was not coming from any specific person but from all around the land simultaneously!

It was the people living on this land...

While moved at heart, Calyrex was also very surprised.

Because this amount of faith was so great that it far exceeded the faith it once received in the Galar Region!

This wave of faith, as vast as mountains and seas, transformed into a pure energy that surged into its body.

In just an instant, Calyrex felt its power becoming stronger than ever before.

Even exceeding its peak period!

"How could this be?" Calyrex couldn't help but murmur.

To explore the truth of the matter, its form disappeared from the spot and reappeared beside D, recounting the changes it experienced.

"I... I feel that more and more people have faith in me, and as a result, my power is rapidly increasing!"

Compared to the shocked Calyrex, D was much more composed.

He smiled and invited Calyrex to sit down, then made it a cup of tea and began explaining what was happening.

"Because you previously used your abilities to restore the forest on the back hill, the Paldea League... which is akin to the king of this land, they built a shrine for you on the back hill to thank you."

"This news reached some reporters, and they came to tell the people living on this land about what you did before."

The "old antique" Calyrex suddenly felt somewhat out of touch with the times.

It asked puzzledly, "What is a reporter? How can they spread my deeds to so many people in such a short time?"

"Uh..." D thought for a moment, then gave a detailed explanation to Calyrex: "A reporter is a kind of profession, similar to an ancient messenger."

"They specially look for some memorable or other important events to report, record them as programs, and then play them on television, which is this."

Saying this, D pointed to the television that was playing Pokemon mini-cartoons.

The so-called Pokemon mini-cartoons are just some small dramas of daily interactions between Pokemon and humans.

For instance, "The Slugma-Powered Home," "Pancham Who Wants to be a Hero," "Snowy Summer Vacation," and "Song of Jigglypuff."

Pawmot and Glaceon were watching intently.

Following the direction of D's finger, Calyrex gazed over there.

Although living on the farm, it had seldom interacted with these unfamiliar things and never asked about them. It had always stayed in the Glass Greenhouse that D prepared for it, admiring flowers and enjoying tea.

At this moment, since leaving the Crown Tundra, Calyrex felt for the first time how much the world had changed.

D continued: "That's a television, and the programs played on it are animations created by other humans and Pokemon. If they are broadcast on television, then any family with a television can see them."

Hearing this, Calyrex roughly understood.

"So the reporter broadcast my deeds on television, and then those people watching television saw it," he said.

"Exactly!" D nodded, worthy of being Calyrex; it understood in just one go!

"I had never seen such things before." After a brief moment of stupefaction, Calyrex remarked with an amazed tone, "Who would have thought that in just a few thousand years, humanity would develop to such a degree? Truly admirable!"

D smiled.

The era when Calyrex was active was still during the ancient, backward kingdom era.

At that time, there were no televisions, no internet, and no telephones; the speed of information transmission took a lot of time, incredibly closed off.

Even if Calyrex used its powers to make plants bloom and grow, there was probably no way to quickly spread the news.

Besides, Calyrex clearly wasn't the type to deliberately show miracles in front of people.

Whether it was searching for Zacian and Zamazenta or transferring the power to the forest, it was certainly done quietly behind the scenes.

Even if someone witnessed it with their own eyes, there probably wouldn't be many people.

In that era when communication with distant friends relied on sending letters, it was hard to spread such news.

Thinking about it like this, D could roughly understand why the Galar Royal Family could so smoothly erase Calyrex's achievements.

But such a situation will never occur again!

Even without him, Calyrex would not fall into the previous predicament again.

The plan to pave the way was perfectly realized, and D couldn't help but feel very happy inside.

Pokemon like Calyrex truly deserve respect.

He hoped Calyrex could have a better future, so he was willing to do these things for it.

It wasn't in vain to have the identity of the Great Preacher of the Calyrex Crew!

At this moment, Calyrex also came to understand everything.

It knew in its heart that behind all of this, there was undoubtedly this little believer pushing things forward.

Calyrex was deeply moved and slowly said, "Thanks to that faith, my power has now reached a new level."

D nodded and then said, "There should be considerable growth following this."

After all, this news had just been broadcast, and in the future, it would undoubtedly appear as headlines in various newspapers. Then, through word of mouth, more people would definitely know about Calyrex's existence.

This was just one Paldea Region. If Calyrex's deeds were promoted to other regions, making more people aware of its existence...

Then the kind of power Calyrex would possess was something D didn't dare imagine!

This is the greatness of divine faith!

The more faith there is, the stronger it becomes.

Moreover, Calyrex is different from traditional deities of faith. It does not need others to truly worship it; as long as someone knows of its existence and name, it can derive power from it.

Impressive!

"For now, the internet isn't that developed, and Calyrex's deeds can only circulate in the Paldea Region."

"When the live broadcasting industry emerges and online forums thrive, then Calyrex's name can be spread worldwide," D silently thought.

Whether it's the Sinnoh Region, Hoenn Region, Unova Region, or the even farther Kanto Region and Johto Region, the tale of Calyrex will eventually reach there.

---

---

---

---

# 299: Chapter 296: The Slugma-Powered Home

Upon hearing about the television's function, Calyrex became deeply intrigued by the square box that could emit sound and display images.

Seeing this, D invited Calyrex to sit on the couch with everyone and watch TV.

Calyrex gladly accepted.

Since the spots next to D were taken by Pawmot and Koraidon, Calyrex could only sit on the single-seater sofa on the other side.

But this did not affect Calyrex's TV watching.

At this moment, a documentary called "The Slugma-Powered Home" was playing on the television.

The film told the story of a girl named Anna returning from the big city to the countryside during her winter break to visit her grandparents.

The elderly couple lived together with a group of Pokemon.

There was the Haxorus that split firewood with its teeth, the Snom serving as a refrigerator, the Galvantula providing electricity, and the Ariados providing silk for knitting sweaters...

And Slugma lived in specially made heating pipes, providing warmth to the house and helping to light fires and cook meals.

Anna adored Slugma and wanted to become good friends with it, so she kept chasing Slugma around the house, but Slugma kept running around in the heating pipes.

Grandpa told Anna that when Slugma is happy, it would make a "bang bang bang" sound and emit smoke from its body.

Slugma sensed Anna's wish to be friends, so on the day she left, it made a "bang bang" sound in the house and used Lava Plume to bid her farewell.

Although the documentary was short, the atmosphere was very warm.

The elderly couple, though not Trainers, lived together with Pokemon, and in this era, such families were not rare.

After thousands of years of development, humans and Pokemon could finally coexist peacefully and live together.

After watching the entire program, Calyrex couldn't help but exclaim: "Television is truly amazing!"

D chuckled and nodded beside him: "Humans have never stopped progressing. Perhaps, in the near future, humans will be able to fly in spaceships to other planets."

Calyrex admired: "That's something even I can't do!"

During the commercials, Pawmot was still enchanted by the documentary.

With dreams in its heart, it tugged at D's sleeve with its paw.

D turned his head: "What's up?"

Pawmot: "Pawmo!"

Calyrex helped translate simultaneously: "It said [Slugma is such a great Pokemon!]"

Hearing this, D was slightly stunned and asked in surprise: "You really like Slugma?"

"Pawmo!" Pawmot nodded its little head.

It also wanted to build a Slugma pipeline at home and befriend the Slugma living inside!

"Is that so..." D pondered carefully and thought it was not infeasible, but Slugma generally inhabited hot volcanic areas and was hard to find elsewhere.

However, there was still some way.

For instance, he could take some time to fly to Hoenn Region and look for Slugma at the famous Mt. Chimney.

After pondering a bit, D said, "I will see if I can encounter a Pokemon like Slugma later on! If I do, I will invite it to come live with us in our home, okay?"

If Slugma were here, the home's natural gas could be replaced by Slugma, as Slugma's high body temperature could be placed under the pot to help cook meals.

Moreover, this kind of Pokemon could also provide heating for the house.

However, the problem was that if Slugma were to come over, the home's fireplace, ventilation ducts, and heating pipes would need to be remodeled.

D was planning his future plans in his mind.

And upon hearing D's answer, Pawmot immediately let out a joyful growl: "Pawmo!"

But right after, it thought of another question, and with little paws, it clung to his thigh, looking expectantly up at D:

"Pawmo? Pawmo Pawmo!" (When will D's granddaughter visit the ranch? Just like Slugma, I want to be her good friend!)

D: "…?"

What is this? D was puzzled, Pawmot, do you know what you're asking?

He didn't even have someone he liked now, how could a granddaughter suddenly appear?

And Pawmot had already begun imagining future life: "Pawmo?" (Will D's granddaughter live with us?)

Influenced by Pawmot, the Alcremie beside it also started to express its opinion: "Moomoo!" (She definitely will!)

Calyrex smiled and watched this scene, simultaneously using Telepathy to convey the Pokemon's whispers to D.

D listened, feeling a little overwhelmed, swept Pawmot into his arms, and stopped its wild thoughts:

"...I don't have a granddaughter, so there won't be anyone suddenly visiting me from the big city, and even if there is, they probably won't live with us."

Pawmot was very disappointed: "Pawmo…?" (Why?)

Based on D's understanding of this world, children move out after marriage to live with their spouses.

Just like Mayor Thomas's son.

Previously, D heard Mayor Thomas say that his son moved to Levincia City after getting married, where he worked with his wife, only bringing his wife and children back to visit Mayor Thomas during holidays.

---

D organized his thoughts in his mind, glanced at the curious Pokemon in front of him, and explained to them:

"Because children will eventually have their own lives, they will meet their own Pokemon partners, just like you left your parents and met me, and we live together."

The Pokemon listened with a mixture of understanding and confusion.

Only Koraidon lay calmly beside D's leg, because it held a secret only it knew.

After D's explanation, Pawmot began to miss its mom and dad.

Unlike Glaceon and the others, it didn't leave its parents voluntarily; it was brought here by a Pokemon called Bombirdier.

It wondered if they would be worried about it after it left...

Seeing Pawmot suddenly becoming downcast, D immediately guessed what was on its mind.

"Let me think of a solution..."

Bombirdier, this type of Pokemon, mainly inhabits the mountainous regions in the southwest of the Paldea Region.

They use breast fluff and fallen feathers to make pouches, then fly down to pick up stones, branches, and other things to toss around for fun.

Thinking of this, D returned to his room and took out a map of the Paldea Region from his desk.

He laid the map on the table, and a group of Pokemon immediately gathered around.

D found the location of the ranch on the map, then located a nearby mountain range where Bombirdier could be found and connected it to the ranch with a line, forming a straight path.

Plus, Bombirdiers usually don't stray far from their nest when gathering items.

"So... it's likely around this area."

D circled the expanse of wilderness south of Porto Marinada Town with a pen.

However, he couldn't be sure, so after pondering for a moment, D asked, "Pawmot, do you remember what the place you used to live in looked like?"

Pawmot carefully recalled and then answered, "Pawmo!" (It was in a forest with many trees, and there was one very large tree!)

Beyond that, it couldn't remember anything else.

A forest area?

There are many places that fit that description!

D found himself in a bit of a dilemma.

It would be great if Mewtwo were here now.

Mewtwo's abilities allow it to examine human and Pokemon memories, and it might find more information from deep within Pawmot's memories.

Unfortunately, Mewtwo had left before...

What if he asked the Pokemon visiting the Pokemon Tavern for a drink for help?

Lately, more and more Pokemon guests were being drawn to the tavern.

This included some wild Pokemon.

Like Squawkabilly, who had migrated from elsewhere, stopped by upon hearing about the tavern's reputation from other Pokemon, and then continued its journey.

Thinking of this, D decided to post a missing person notice at the tavern, asking these passing wild Pokemon to help and see if they could meet the pair of Pawmot parents who had lost their child during their travels.

So, D immediately told Indeedee about this case and asked that it be conveyed to everyone at the tavern the next day.

Sister Indeedee readily agreed.

With a gentle and kind heart, it looked at Pawmot and softly comforted, "Indee~" (Don't worry, I'll ask everyone to help~)

"Pawmo..." Pawmot was deeply moved, "Pawmo!" (Thank you!)

Sister Indeedee smiled gently.

The next evening, the Pokemon Tavern was open for business as usual.

Sister Indeedee seized the opportunity to verbally convey the "missing person notice" to the group of Pokemon guests at the tavern, asking them to let them know if there were any discoveries.

D didn't go over, but he still learned the follow-up from Sister Indeedee.

All the Pokemon guests agreed and said they would keep an eye out.

In this way, the missing person notice for Pawmot's parents was effectively posted.

What they needed to do next was patiently wait for a response.

However, during the waiting period, an interesting thing happened.

That morning, while D was weeding the fields, Mrs. Dantes, who lived in the town and ordered a bottle of fresh sheep milk from the ranch every day, came to find him.

Seeing her, D was a bit surprised, "Mrs. Dantes? Is something the matter?"

At the moment, the kindly elderly woman's face showed a trace of a smile, "D, has the quality of the sheep milk you've been selling us improved recently? I see the Porto Marinada Market is also selling a kind of Life Sheep Milk... I wonder if they're sourcing it from you."

"That is indeed the case." D nodded.

Because Mrs. Dantes was his longtime customer and had helped him a lot before, he continued to sell her the sheep milk at the original price.

Upon hearing this, Mrs. Dantes hesitated and asked, "Is there any regular quality sheep milk left at the ranch?"

D was a little taken aback, detecting a hint of something unusual in Mrs. Dantes' tone, and couldn't help but ask, "Has something happened?"

Mrs. Dantes exclaimed in surprise, "Ever since we drank the sheep milk delivered by Dragonite that day, my husband's been bouncing off the walls, and it's like he has boundless energy! He's even had multiple nosebleeds every day!"

"We went to the hospital for a check-up, and the doctor said it was caused by excessive nutrition. I thought we hadn't had any supplements lately! Then I realized it was the sheep milk."

D was flabbergasted, "Is that really the case?"

Usually, he drank Miltank's milk and had only tasted Life Sheep Milk once. The taste of sheep milk could be an acquired one, while MooMoo Milk's flavor was more universally appealing.

Mrs. Dantes nodded, "That's why I'm asking if there's any regular sheep milk left at the ranch, as that sheep milk is just too potent!"

"There's no regular sheep milk left." D shook his head, unless he captured a few more Skiddo and fed them with milk quality enhancing dishes.

After thinking for a bit, D asked, "Would you like to try some fresh milk produced by Miltank?"

The Miltank on his ranch had also consumed milk quality improving dishes in the past, so the MooMoo Milk they produced was also of high quality.

Hearing this, Mrs. Dantes nodded, "Then let's switch to cow milk!"

"Alright." D smiled and replaced the sheep milk with cow milk.

Mrs. Dantes couldn't help but breathe a sigh of relief. She handed a paper bag to D, showing a warm smile, and said:

"These are cookies I baked, give them a try!"

"Thank you very much." D did not refuse, politely reaching out to accept the cookies, then bid farewell to Mrs. Dantes, watching her turn and leave the ranch.

D thought this would be just an isolated case, but over the next few days, he began receiving calls from other milk-subscribing customers.

They all reported that the sheep milk was too nourishing, causing nosebleeds, sleepless nights, and even a noticeable weight gain after drinking it.

Not wanting to turn into big fat people, they all decided to switch from sheep milk to MooMoo Milk.

D was at a loss for words, and after the call, he took a bottle of sheep milk from the fridge, wondering to himself if it could really be that exaggerated.

With this thought in mind, D drank it down.

Immediately, he felt a surge of strength welling up from deep within his body.

The fatigue from working in the fields vanished, and he felt full of energy, as if he had an endless reserve of strength.

This feeling made him want to spin in circles like a Pokemon evolving!

Strength... was pouring out continuously!

"It's really exaggerated!"

D was taken aback.

The next second, two streams of warmth flowed out from his nostrils.

D touched with his hand and found it covered in fresh blood.

Oh no! He had a nosebleed!

Brother Indeedee was startled upon seeing this, quickly grabbed some tissues to help D stop the bleeding.

"Thank you!" D took the tissues, hurriedly wiping away the blood.

"Indee..." Brother Indeedee was very worried.

After a while, the bleeding finally stopped.

But D still felt full of strength throughout his body, unable to stay idle at all.

So under the concerned gaze of Brother Indeedee, he rushed out of the room, picked up an axe, and started chopping firewood with vigor.

---

---

---

---

# 300: Chapter 297: Flutter Mane (Bonus Chapter)

After a few hours, D finally let out a sigh of relief after chopping all the timber.

Putting down the axe, D turned around and went back to his bedroom to take a shower.

At this moment, he felt relaxed and very comfortable.

"Seems like the upgraded sheep milk is indeed not something normal people can handle!"

Its effect was really exaggerated, but admittedly, it indeed was a great supplement for humans.

After calming down, D began to ponder if he had been too reckless just now.

He drank an entire bottle of sheep milk in one go.

If he had only drunk a small part, meeting the needs of the body without excess, perhaps the situation wouldn't have occurred?

Afterwards, he could digest these nutrients through running and other exercises.

Combining the two methods, drinking sheep milk while training, in the long run, would make the body healthier, stronger, and then live a long life.

While pondering this, a girl's voice suddenly came from outside.

D stepped out, only to see a short-haired girl wearing a Naranja Academy uniform, carrying a backpack, standing outside the ranch.

Seeing someone come out, the girl's eyes lit up and she quickly asked: "Um... Hello! I'm a student from Naranja Academy, could you please tell me which direction I should head towards to reach the Abundant Shrine?"

A Trainer heading to the Abundant Shrine? Looks like the news promotion was really successful! People are already drawn in by Calyrex.

D pointed towards the direction of the back mountain: "Follow this path forward, keep walking to the end, and you'll see the mountain path leading to the shrine."

The girl clasped her hands in gratitude: "Thank you very much!"

With that, she turned and ran towards that direction.

D watched the girl's figure thoughtfully.

Clearly, this girl wouldn't be the first nor the last Trainer to come here to explore the legend of Calyrex.

Then, the Thunder Trial and Storm Trial he had planned before should be officially put on the process.

Thinking of this, D immediately called over Pawmot and Dragonite, asking:

"Do you remember the Thunder Trial and Storm Trial I discussed with you before?"

"Pawmot!"

"Roar!"

The two Pokemon nodded repeatedly. They remembered D saying that these trials could help them become stronger and become more powerful Pokemon!

"Very good, now let's review the content of the trials."

As he said this, D looked at Dragonite.

Dragonite blinked with anticipation: "Roar?"

"Your trial is called the Storm Trial," D introduced, "As the name suggests, use your most powerful storm mysteries to create hurricanes to sweep your opponents."

"Rain Dance + Hurricane, and you can use other moves like Thunder, Thunderbolt, Dragon Rage in between, depending on the situation."

"However, keep in mind that our ultimate goal is not to defeat the opponent, but to gain experience, treat the Pokemon you battle as sparring partners, test the moves you've understood on them, and gain practical experience to achieve special training effects, understood?"

"Roar!" Dragonite understood, nodding its head in excitement.

Ever since eating that steamed bun last time, it felt much stronger, but it hadn't had the chance to battle yet.

Now, D says there would soon be challengers coming, does that mean it can finally battle?

Dragonite was exhilarated and expectant.

It flapped its wings, and a gust of wind immediately rose up from all around.

Seeing this scene, D was somewhat surprised.

Looks like Dragonite's compatibility with the Storm Steamed Bun is higher than he expected!

Next, it was Pawmot's turn.

D looked at Pawmot holding a hammer.

Before he could speak, Pawmot swung the Mjolnir in its hand.

As the small hammer spun quickly, streaks of lightning emerged from it.

"Pawmot!"

It wanted to become stronger, become a powerful Pokemon, and then use this power to uphold justice like the Pokemon named Thor, to become a true superhero!

D: "…Looks like you don't need me to say much, you've already mastered the technique of using the hammer skillfully."

"Pawmot!" Pawmot exclaimed happily.

Very good, D thought to himself.

Everything is ready, only lacking the wind. The next step is to wait at the ranch for those challengers to arrive.

*

The League Year 199, February 6.

This morning, D received a letter from Elif Cherry Garden.

The letter mentioned that the Sakura Blossom Wine Party was about to be held this year, reminding D not to forget to bring his Pokemon to participate.

Sakura Blossom Wine Party...

D immediately recalled the experiences from last year's Sakura Blossom Wine Party, and it happened that the Indeedee that came later hadn't been there, so he could take them this time to relax a bit.

However, plans often couldn't keep up with changes.

That afternoon, D received a phone call from Area Zero Research Institute.

Looking at Professor Sada on the screen, D was slightly stunned and asked: "Professor, what's up?"

Professor Sada seemed a bit excited, nodded, and said: "D, the matter I discussed with you before can be realized now!"

"What?" D didn't immediately react.

Professor Sada reminded: "Back then, when you helped me take care of Arven, we promised to find Paradox Pokemon in the Giant Pit that could coexist with humans, and now, we have a new discovery."

Upon hearing this, D immediately understood: "Which Paradox Pokemon is it?"

A Pokemon that's adapted to this era and can coexist normally with humans... Is it the calm Iron Thorns? Or the furry Slither Wing? Or perhaps the one like Santa Claus, Iron Bundle?

Professor Sada answered: "It's the Paradox Pokemon called Flutter Mane, recorded in the Book of Scarlet and Violet! Like Koraidon, it comes from the ancient past!"

"Flutter Mane?"

Upon hearing this name, D was dumbfounded.

He never would have thought it would be this Paradox Pokemon.

After all, according to the contents of the sighting report, Pokemon like Flutter Mane are very aggressive and combative!

"That's right." Professor Sada nodded and meticulously introduced this special Flutter Mane to D: "Not long ago, it was summoned to this world from ancient times with the Time Machine."

"Initially, Turo and I thought it would immediately attack us, but surprisingly, it didn't do so. Instead, it hid in the shadows and secretly followed us, seemingly observing."

"Moreover, it also stealthily consumed a large amount of food stored in the cafeteria warehouse, besides, we have a significant discovery."

D immediately grew interested: "Oh? What is it?"

Professor Sada responded excitedly: "It seems to be very interested in the photo you and Koraidon left at the Area Zero Research Institute!"

"Turo and I, therefore, have formed a bold hypothesis."

"Could that Flutter Mane be from the same time as Koraidon, and have possibly met each other?"

"Therefore, I want to invite you and Koraidon to the Area Zero Research Institute so that Koraidon and that Flutter Mane can meet, to verify our hypothesis."

"If they do recognize each other, then you can bring Flutter Mane back to the ranch with Koraidon and have Koraidon help capture it."

"You don't have to worry about the League side, I'll submit an application to Geeta for you, you just need to come to the research institute!"
